Sanarwar ɗaukar alhaki: Idan ka yi amfani da qFT8, kai ne ke da dukkan alhakin bin doka, tsaro, da lalacewar kayan aiki

Mene ne qFT8?

qFT8 manhaja ce ta rediyon amateur domin wayoyi da tablet na Android, iOS, da kuma kwamfutocin Windows, macOS da Linux. Tana fassara kuma tana watsa saƙonnin dijital na FT8, FT4, FT2, LQ8, JS8, ko WSPR ta hanyar transceiver. Da wannan transceiver da wannan kebul ɗin, tana kuma aiki da murya (SSB), Morse (CW), RTTY da hotuna (SSTV).

Sigogin biyu suna aiki iri ɗaya. Inda suka bambanta, wannan jagora tana faɗa ("A Android ...", "A kwamfuta ..."). Hotunan allo suna nuna manhajar Android, kuma "taɓa" yana nufin taɓa allo a Android ko danna da linzami a kwamfuta.

Ko da yake qFT8 tana aiki kai tsaye (plug-and-play) da QMX da ICOM 7300, kana iya amfani da ita da kowane transceiver: ajiye waya ko kwamfuta kusa da lasifika da makirufo na rediyo tare da kunna VOX, ko yi amfani da kebul na sauti. Kana kuma iya gwada qFT8 ba tare da transceiver ba: tana watsawa ta lasifika, tana sauraron sigina ta makirufo. Wayoyi ko kwamfutoci da dama masu qFT8 suna iya tuntuɓar juna ta sauti idan suna kusa da juna.

qFT8 manhaja ce ta kyauta kuma ba ta da tallace-tallace. Za a yanke shawarar lasisin lambar tushe (source code) lokacin da aka fitar da lambar tushen (LICENSE).

Luis Quesada Torres (HB9IPH) ne ya ƙirƙiri qFT8 a shekarar 2026, shi ke da haƙƙin mallaka, kuma ya riƙe dukkan haƙƙoƙi.

qFT8 tana ɗauke da sassa daga usb-serial-for-android (Copyright © 2011-2020 Google Inc., Mike Wakerly, Andreas Butti, Kai Morich, BSD-3-Clause), Roboto (Copyright © 2015 Google Inc. / Christian Robertson, Apache-2.0), Roboto Mono (Copyright © 2015 Google / The Roboto Mono Project Authors, OFL-1.1), Natural Earth (Public Domain), hamradio-zones-geojson (Copyright © 2024 HB9HIL, HA8TKS, MIT license), RADE V1 and V2 speech-codec model weights (data) (Copyright © 2024, drowe67, BSD-2-Clause), FARGAN vocoder and pitch estimator model weights (data) (Copyright © 2001-2023 Xiph.Org, Skype Limited, Octasic, Jean-Marc Valin, Timothy B. Terriberry, CSIRO, Gregory Maxwell, Mark Borgerding, Erik de Castro Lopo, Mozilla, Amazon, BSD-3-Clause), Big CTY (Copyright © Jim Reisert AD1C), da Eclipse Temurin / BellSoft Liberica (OpenJDK) Java Runtime (Copyright © Oracle and/or its affiliates and other contributors, GPLv2 with the Classpath Exception). Babu ɗaya daga cikin waɗannan marubuta ko ayyuka na waje da ke goyon bayan qFT8 ko yake da alaƙa da ita.

Muhimman Fasaloli

  • Saiti cikin sauri
  • Tana aiki da rediyo fiye da 50
  • Yanayin CALL da hannu da aikin AUTO
  • Daidaita lokaci ta atomatik da NTP / GPS
  • Kimanin wuri ta atomatik
  • Shigo da fitar da log na ADIF
  • Haɗi da QRZ / LoTW: lodawa, tabbatarwa
  • Tsarin sake kira mai iyaka da ake iya saitawa
  • SOTA / POTA: mai canza CQ, fitar da ADIF mai lamba
  • Murya ta SSB, CW da paddle da mai fassara ga dukkan passband, RTTY da hotunan SSTV
  • Kunna POTA / SOTA tare da fitarwa da taɓawa ɗaya
  • Editan QSO da log mai sauri domin QSO da ka yi da hannu
  • Sabar yanar gizo domin aiki gaba ɗaya daga nesa
  • Yanayin adana baturi: tana aiki allo a kashe

Na'urorin da ake Tallafawa

qFT8 tana da manyan hanyoyi uku na haɗa transceiver ɗinka:

Transceiver na CAT/Sauti kai tsaye (sarrafawa ta kebul na USB)

Ana tallafa wa waɗannan transceiver kai tsaye ta kebul na USB ɗaya. Kebul ɗin yana ɗaukar sarrafawar CAT (canza mita da PTT) da kuma sauti na USB mai inganci:

  • QRPLabs QMX ko QMX+
  • QRPLabs QDX ko QDX-M (amma wannan na'ura ba ta da ma'aunin W ko SWR)
  • ICOM IC-7300 da ICOM IC-7300 MK2
  • ICOM IC-705
  • ICOM IC-7610
  • ICOM IC-7851
  • ICOM IC-7100
  • ICOM IC-7200
  • ICOM IC-9700
  • ICOM IC-7600
  • ICOM IC-7410
  • ICOM IC-9100
  • ICOM IC-7000
  • ICOM IC-706MKIIG
  • ICOM IC-746 / IC-746PRO
  • ICOM IC-756PRO / PRO2 / PRO3
  • ICOM IC-7700
  • ICOM IC-7800 / IC-7850
  • Yaesu FT-891
  • Yaesu FT-710
  • Yaesu FTX-1
  • Yaesu FT-991 / FT-991A
  • Yaesu FT-DX10
  • Yaesu FTDX101
  • Elecraft K4 / K4D / K4HD
  • Elecraft K3 / K3S
  • Elecraft KX3
  • Elecraft KX2
  • Elecraft K2 (yana buƙatar KIO2 module)
  • Xiegu X6100
  • Xiegu X6200
  • Kenwood TS-480 / TS-590 / TS-2000 series
  • FX-4CR
  • QuantumSDR
  • (tr)uSDX / uSDX+ / usdr
  • SDR: RTL-SDR (network), SDR: KiwiSDR (network), SDR: Hermes-Lite 2 / OpenHPSDR (network), SDR: Airspy / RTL-SDR server (network, port 5555), SDR: remote SDR host (network: SDRplay, LimeSDR, Pluto ...), SDR: OpenHPSDR protocol 2 (network: ANAN, Saturn, Orion ...), SDR: TCI IQ stream (network), SDR: multicast RTP receiver (network, port 5006), SDR: web receiver, audio channel (network), SDR: wideband web receiver (network), SDR: SDR++ server (network), SDR: Airspy HF+ (USB), SDR: Airspy R2 / Mini (USB), SDR: HackRF (USB, receive only), SDR: ADALM-Pluto (USB network, receive only), SDR: sound-card I/Q (SoftRock, FUNcube ...): Karɓa kawai: SDR ba ya taɓa aikawa.

A iPhone da iPad ba a sarrafa rediyo ta wayar USB: duba qFT8 a iPhone da iPad.

Bar Saituna > Na'ura a kan AUTO: rediyon da ake tallafawa wanda aka haɗa ta USB ana gane shi kai tsaye. Kana kuma iya zaɓar rediyonka da kanka daga jerin.

Bayani game da interface na waje (Digirig, kebul na USB-serial na gama-gari): An tsara gano na'ura ta atomatik (AUTO) domin transceiver masu USB a cikinsu ko kebul na musamman na model ɗinsu. Idan ka yi amfani da interface na waje na gama-gari (kamar Digirig), gano ta atomatik yana ganin chip na interface kawai, sai ya koma yanayin VOX. Domin amfani da CAT da rediyon da aka haɗa ta interface na waje (misali Lab599 TX-500), zaɓi model na transceiver ɗinka da hannu a cikin Saituna > Na'ura; ana saita tashar serial da sauri a cikin Saituna > TSARIN NA'URA.

Ana tallafa wa ƙarin na'urori ta VOX. Wannan haɗi ne na wani ɓangare, na sauti kawai.

Lura: Saboda tsaro, qFT8 ba ta bari a canza ƙarfin Tx na transceiver. Canza ƙarfin da hannu a kan transceiver ɗinka.

Sauran transceiver

  • Ana tallafawa ta lasifika/makirufo, amma ba shi ne mafi kyau ba
  • Ana tallafawa ta haɗin sauti na gama-gari
↑ Koma zuwa Abubuwan da ke ciki

qFT8 a kwamfuta (Windows, macOS, Linux)

qFT8 na kwamfuta yana yin aiki ɗaya da qFT8 na Android: yanayi iri ɗaya, aikin AUTO da CALL iri ɗaya, log da ayyuka iri ɗaya. Wannan sashe yana magana ne a kan abin da ya bambanta idan ka yi amfani da shi a Windows, macOS ko Linux: yadda ake shigar da shi, yadda aka tsara taga, na'urorin zamiya biyu na ƙarar sauti, yadda ake zaɓar na'urorin sauti da rediyo, da yadda ake amfani da linzami. Sauran wannan jagora tana aiki yadda take; inda wani sashe ya bambanta a kwamfuta, yana faɗa ("A kwamfuta ...").

Shigarwa da farawa na farko

Sauke fayil ɗin tsarinka daga qft8.com. Kowane abin saukewa yana ɗauke da babban fayil ɗaya ko manhaja ɗaya mai shirin, lasisin da wannan jagora. Babu wani abu da ake buƙatar shigarwa: Java runtime da qFT8 yake buƙata yana ciki.

Tsari Windows
Abin saukewa .zip, 64-bit ko 32-bit
Abin da kake buƙata Windows 10 ko sabo fiye da shi. Yi amfani da fayil ɗin 32-bit kawai a Windows na 32-bit.
Tsari macOS
Abin saukewa .dmg, fayil ɗaya ga kowane Mac (Apple Silicon da Intel)
Abin da kake buƙata macOS 10.13 ko sabo fiye da shi
Tsari Linux
Abin saukewa .tar.gz: PC 64-bit, PC 32-bit, ARM 64-bit ko ARM 32-bit (Raspberry Pi)
Abin da kake buƙata Kwamfuta mai desktop da sound server (PulseAudio ko PipeWire). An gwada a Ubuntu na kwanan nan, a X11 da Wayland (ta XWayland).
Tsari Kowace kwamfuta daban
Abin saukewa .jar
Abin da kake buƙata Java 21 ko sabo fiye da shi, wanda kai da kanka ka shigar

qFT8 yana buƙatar kwamfuta mai aƙalla cores guda 2 na processor (ana ba da shawarar 4) da sarari don taga mai girman aƙalla 960 x 560 pixels. A cores 2 yana aiki, amma a hankali: duba Kwamfutoci masu jinkiri.

Windows

  1. Buɗe .zip ɗin ka jawo babban fayil ɗin qFT8 zuwa wurin da kake ajiye shirye-shirye, misali babban fayil ɗin Documents ko Desktop. Kada ka gudanar da qFT8 daga cikin zip ɗin.
  2. Buɗe babban fayil ɗin ka danna qFT8.exe sau biyu.
  3. Windows na iya nuna "Windows protected your PC" a karon farko. Danna More info, sannan Run anyway. An sanya wa qFT8.exe hannu da takardar shaidar mawallafin; Windows tana koyon amincewa da sabon shiri a hankali.
  4. Idan Windows ta tambaya ko ta ƙyale qFT8 (da ake nunawa a matsayin "OpenJDK Platform binary") ta bi ta firewall, ƙyale shi kawai a cikin hanyar sadarwa ta sirri, kuma kawai idan kana amfani da na'urar sarrafa daga nesa ta yanar gizo ko gada.

macOS

  1. Buɗe .dmg ɗin ka jawo qFT8 zuwa gajeriyar hanyar Applications a cikin taga ɗaya. Sannan fitar da .dmg ɗin.
  2. Fara qFT8 daga Applications. Idan ka fara shi daga cikin taga .dmg, ko daga wurin da burauza ta ajiye shi, qFT8 yana tambaya "A matsar da qFT8 zuwa Applications?": danna MATSAR (macOS na iya tambayar kalmar sirrinka, kamar yadda Finder yake yi) ko BA YANZU BA.
  3. macOS yana tambaya sau ɗaya ko qFT8 zai iya amfani da makirufo. Danna Allow: ta wannan hanya qFT8 yake jin rediyo. Idan ka rasa ko ka ƙi, qFT8 yana nuna kati yana cewa yana jiran izinin makirufo; ba da shi a System Settings > Privacy & Security > Microphone (macOS 13 da sabo fiye da shi) ko System Preferences > Security & Privacy > Privacy > Microphone (macOS na da), sannan ka sake fara qFT8.

Linux

  1. Buɗe .tar.gz ɗin (tar -xzf qFT8-*.tar.gz, ko da manajan fayil ɗinka). Zai ba da babban fayil qFT8.
  2. Gudanar da ./qFT8 a cikin babban fayil ɗin (danna sau biyu yana aiki idan manajan fayil ɗinka yana gudanar da shirye-shirye). Farawa na farko yana buɗe shirin a cikin ~/.cache/qft8, wanda yake ɗaukar daƙiƙa kaɗan; sabuwar siga tana maye gurbin tsohuwa a wurin da kanta.
  3. Port ɗin serial (CAT) na rediyo yana cikin wani rukuni: dialout a Ubuntu, Debian da Fedora, uucp a Arch. Katin sautinsa na USB yana cikin audio. Idan asusunka ba ya cikin rukunin port ɗin, qFT8 yana faɗa a cikin kati: GYARA yana saka ka a cikin rukunin (yana tambayar kalmar sirrinka) ya sake fara qFT8, don haka canjin yana aiki ba tare da ka fita ba. Idan hakan bai yi aiki ba, GWADA A TERMINAL da KWAFI UMARNI suna ba ka umarnin da za ka gudanar da kanka; sannan ka fita ka sake shiga sau ɗaya.

Farawa na farko

qFT8 yana tambayar alamar kiranka, kamar a Android (duba Shigar da Alamar Kira). Nan da nan bayan haka, kati mai suna Tsarin Na'ura yana buɗewa: zaɓi rediyo, sannan na'urorin sauti da aka haɗa da shi. Waterfall ɗin da ke ƙarƙashin shigarwa yana nuna abin da rediyo ke aikawa: idan rediyo yana kunne, ya kamata ka ga hayaniya ko sigina. Za ka iya buɗe wannan kati a kowane lokaci da maɓallin da ke dama da gear a sandar ƙasa, kuma alamar ja Babu sauti da ke kan waterfall tana buɗe shi ma. Duba Na'urorin sauti da haɗin rediyo.

↑ Koma zuwa Abubuwan da ke ciki

Abin da yake iri ɗaya, abin da ya bambanta

Duk abin da ya shafi aiki iri ɗaya ne: QSO ɗinka na farko, yanayin dijital, AUTO, CALL, log, ADIF, QRZ, LoTW da sauran ayyuka, SSB, CW, RTTY, SSTV, iyakar watsawa da ka'idojin tsaro: TX yana kashe a koyaushe idan qFT8 ya fara, maɓallin TX kaɗai ne yake kunna shi, kuma qFT8 ba ya taɓa canza ƙarfin watsawar rediyonka. Hotunan allo a wannan jagora suna nuna manhajar Android; "taɓa" yana nufin danna da linzami. Bambance-bambancen:

Allo
Android Allo ɗaya: spectrogram da shafi ɗaya; Fuskantarwa da Kashi huɗu a Saituna
Kwamfuta Tsare-tsare uku, ana zaɓarsu da maɓallin tsari: duka a taga ɗaya, gefe da gefe kamar a waya, ko kowane sashe a tagarsa. Ƙari
Ƙarar sauti
Android Ƙarar waya, da "Samar da sautin tare da ƙarar" da "Saita ƙarar tsarin zuwa ... yayin aikawa" a Saituna
Kwamfuta Na'urorin zamiya biyu a sandar ƙasa: ƙarar na'urar sauti da kanta da ƙarar tone ta qFT8. qFT8 ba ya taɓa canza ƙarar na'ura da kansa. Ƙari
Na'urorin sauti
Android Na wayar kanta, ko katin sauti na USB na rediyo
Kwamfuta Ka zaɓe su a cikin Tsarin Na'ura: REDIYO da KU (MAI AIKI). Ƙari
Haɗin rediyo
Android USB da akwatin neman izini
Kwamfuta Port na serial (COM); babu akwatin izini, amma a Linux akwai rukuni kuma a macOS izinin makirufo. Ƙari
Shigarwa
Android Taɓawa: taɓa, dogon taɓa, shafawa
Kwamfuta Linzami: danna, danna dama, dabaran linzami. Madannai ba ya sarrafa maɓallan. Ƙari
Saituna da bayanai
Android A cikin manhajar
Kwamfuta A cikin babban fayil a cikin babban fayil ɗin mai amfani; fayil ɗin saituna iri ɗaya da na Android. Ƙari
Baya
Android Foreground service, kashe allo, adana baturi
Kwamfuta Babu ɗaya daga cikinsu: taga tana ci gaba da aiki muddin a buɗe take. Kwamfutoci masu jinkiri suna da nasu yanayi. Ƙari
Sabuntawa
Android Google Play
Kwamfuta Sanarwa mai maɓallin saukewa; kai da kanka ka maye gurbin shirin. Ƙari

Tsarin allo da maɓallin tsari

Maɓallin da ke ƙarshen dama na sandar ƙasa shi ne maɓallin tsari. Kowane danna a kansa yana canza tsarin zuwa na gaba: Mai yawo, sannan A manne, sannan Duka a ɗaya, sannan Mai yawo kuma. Hotonsa yana nuna tsarin da kake ciki yanzu (mai karanta allo yana faɗar sunansa, misali "Tsari: A manne"). qFT8 yana tuna tsarin don farawa na gaba, da girma da wurin kowace taga a kowane tsari daban.

  • Duka a ɗaya (tsarin da qFT8 yake farawa da shi a karon farko). Taga ɗaya mai sassa huɗu daidai: spectrogram a sama hagu, shafin Tashoshi a sama dama, Taswirar duniya a ƙasa hagu da log na QSO a ƙasa dama, a saman sandar ƙasa. Saituna doguwar ginshiƙi ce a dama ta taga ɗaya; maɓallin gear a sandar ƙasa yana nunawa ko ɓoye ta, haka ma X da ke kan taken ta. Sandar ƙasa ba ta da maɓallai na sassa huɗun: koyaushe suna nan.
  • A manne (kamar manhajar Android). Taga ɗaya da spectrogram a hagu da shafin Tashoshi a dama, a saman sandar ƙasa. Ja siririn layin raba tsakaninsu domin ba ɗaya ƙarin sarari (rabon yana tsakanin 20 % da 80 %). Maɓallan Rajistar QSO, Taswirar duniya da Saituna na sandar ƙasa suna buɗe waɗannan uku a matsayin tagogi daban, kuma danna su kuma yana rufe su.
  • Mai yawo. Babban taga yana ƙanƙancewa zuwa sandar ƙasa kawai, kuma kowane sashe taga ne na kansa da za ka sanya inda kake so, ko a wani allo: Spectrogram, Tashoshi, Log na QSO, Taswirar duniya da Saituna. Sandar ƙasa tana da maɓalli ɗaya ga kowanne na biyar ɗin, don buɗewa da rufe su. Yi amfani da shi don shimfiɗa qFT8 a kan na'urorin kallo biyu, ko don riƙe spectrogram da sandar kawai a ƙaramin allo.

Canza tsari yana sake tsara tagogi kawai: babu abin da ya ɓace, watsawa tana ci gaba kuma TX yana yadda yake. Taswirar duniya tana da maɓallin cikakken allo (a sama dama na taswirar): yana cika allo har sai ka sake danna maɓallin ko Esc.

Shawara: Saitunan Android Fuskantarwa, Kashi huɗu da Manhajar tana rufe: (duba Tsarin allo) ba su wanzu a kwamfuta: maɓallin tsari da girman taga suna maye gurbinsu.

↑ Koma zuwa Abubuwan da ke ciki

Aiki da tagogi

  • Shiri ɗaya a taskbar. Ko wane tsari ne, qFT8 yana da shigarwa ɗaya a taskbar, Dock ko Alt-Tab: babban taga. Tagogin sassa na tsarin Mai yawo da A manne ba sa samun nasu. (A Linux an gwada wannan da GNOME, KDE Plasma, Xfce, Cinnamon, MATE da LXQt.)
  • Tagogin sassa suna da ƙaramin firam ɗinsu (iri ɗaya a kowane tsari): siririn gefe da yake canza girman taga daga kowane gefe ko kusurwa, ɗan layin take mai sunan sashe da kake ja domin motsa taga, da X da yake rufe sashe kamar yadda maɓallin sandar ƙasa nasa yake yi. Esc shi ma yana rufe taga ta sashe, muddin ita ce taga mai aiki.
  • Babban taga ana iya canza girmanta da ƙara mata girma yadda aka saba. A tsarin Duka a ɗaya ba za a iya ƙanƙance ta ƙasa da 960 x 560 pixels ba, kuma tana buɗewa a 85 % na allo a karo na farko.
  • Saituna tana riƙe faɗi ɗaya. Taga (da ginshiƙin) Saituna tana riƙe faɗin da shafin saitunan Android yake da shi, don kowane layi ya dace; za ka iya canza tsayinta.
  • Girman komai. Saituna > AIKACE-AIKACE tana da na'urorin zamiya biyu na girma (Siffar mai amfani da girman rubutu: da Girman sassan mai amfani (ba rubutu ba):) kamar a Android. A allo masu ƙuduri mai girma (Retina Mac, Windows a 125 % zuwa 200 %) qFT8 yana bin ma'aunin tsarin.

Ƙarar sauti: na'ura da sautin tone

Ƙananan na'urorin zamiya biyu suna a sandar ƙasa, tsakanin maɓallin Tsarin Na'ura da maɓallin tsari, ɗaya a saman ɗaya. Suna sarrafa abubuwa biyu daban, kuma duka biyun suna da muhimmanci wajen watsawa.

  • Na'urar zamiya ta sama, mai hoton lasifika: ƙarar na'ura. Ita ce ƙarar na'urar sautin da qFT8 yake watsawa ta cikinta: katin sauti na USB na rediyo, ko lasifika ko belun kunne idan can ne kake aika sautin. Matakin ɗaya ne da mai sarrafa ƙarar tsarinka yake nuna wa wannan na'ura, kuma idan ka canza ɗaya, ɗayan yana bi (na'urar zamiya cikin daƙiƙa kaɗan).
  • Na'urar zamiya ta ƙasa, mai hoton igiyar ruwa: ƙarar tone. Ita ce yadda qFT8 da kansa yake samar da sautin watsawa da ƙarfi (tones na FT8, FT4, WSPR, JS8 da LQ8, da sautin TUNE), daga 0 zuwa 100 %. Ana canza ta yayin da kake watsawa: kana ji kana gani sakamakon a watsawa ɗaya. Tana farawa a 100 %. Kowace rediyo tana tuna ƙarar tone ɗinta. Na'urar sarrafa daga nesa ta yanar gizo tana nuna na'urar zamiya iri ɗaya kuma tana motsa wannan.

Yadda na'urar zamiya ta na'ura take aiki.

  • Idan qFT8 ya fara, yana karanta ƙarar na'urar ya nuna ta; yana sake karanta ta kowane daƙiƙa biyu, don haka idan ka canza ƙarar a tsarin, na'urar zamiya tana bi. Idan ka canza na'urar fitarwa, na'urar zamiya tana nuna ƙarar sabuwar na'urar.
  • qFT8 yana canza ƙarar na'ura ne kawai idan ka motsa wannan na'urar zamiya. Ba ya saita ta da kansa kafin watsawa. Wannan ya bambanta da Android, inda qFT8 zai iya saita ƙarar waya zuwa darajar da ke cikin Saituna idan zai yi watsawa; a kwamfuta waɗannan saituna biyu ba su wanzu.
  • Bayan ka motsa ta, qFT8 yana karanta ƙarar baya, don na'urar zamiya ta nuna abin da na'urar ta karɓa da gaske.
  • Na'urar da ba ta da sarrafa ƙara (wasu fitarwa a macOS) tana nuna na'urar zamiya da launin toka kuma ba tare da ƙulli ba.
  • A karon farko da ka yi watsawa ko ka danna TUNE bayan ka fara qFT8, hoton lasifika da ƙullin na'urar zamiyar na'ura suna sauyawa daga launin toka zuwa fari su koma har na tsawon daƙiƙa biyar, don tuna maka cewa wannan shi ne matakin da yake yanke shawarar yadda ake tuƙa rediyo da ƙarfi. Taɓa shi yana dakatar da hakan.
  • Yayin da kake jan na'urar zamiya, darajarta a kashi-kashi tana nuna kusa da ita.

Shawara: Na'urorin zamiya biyun suna yin aikin shawarar ƙara ta Android a cikin [QMX kawai] Duba Sauti: idan rediyo ya nuna cewa sautin ya yi ƙasa ko ya yi sama (dige biyu na QMX maimakon sanda), fara gwada wasu wurare na na'urar zamiyar ƙarar na'ura (misali 70 %, 80 %, 60 %, 100 %), sannan ka yi amfani da ƙarar tone don daidaitawa. In ba haka ba, bar ƙarar tone a daidaitaccen matakinta (100 %). qFT8 ba ya taɓa saita ƙarfin watsawar rediyonka: gyara wannan a rediyo.

↑ Koma zuwa Abubuwan da ke ciki

Na'urorin sauti da haɗin rediyo

Katin Tsarin Na'ura

Buɗe shi da maɓallin da ke dama da gear (yana kuma buɗewa da kansa a farawa na farko kuma daga alamar Babu sauti). Yana da rabi biyu:

  • REDIYO (hagu): rediyon, da katin sautin rediyon: Shigarwa daga na'urar rediyo (abin da rediyo ke ji: abin da qFT8 yake decode) da Fitarwa zuwa na'urar rediyo (abin da qFT8 yake watsawa), kowanne da sample rate, bits da channels ɗinsa. Bar su a daidaitaccen tsarin su sai dai idan rediyo yana buƙatar wani abu daban. Ƙaramin waterfall mai rai a ƙarƙashin shigarwa yana nuna ko sauti yana zuwa.
  • KU (MAI AIKI) (dama): makirufonka da lasifikokinka ko belun kunne, don SSB da CW (duba Lasifika da makirufo, wanda abu ɗaya ne), maɓallin GWADA, da Kunna sautin rediyo a lasifika a yanayin dijital. Waɗannan kada su taɓa zama katin sautin rediyon da kansa.

Zaɓuɓɓuka iri ɗaya, da ƙari (girman buffer, gargaɗin katsewa), suna cikin Saituna > TSARIN NA'URA. Layukan Android "Tsarin sauti" da "Tushen shigar sauti" ba su wanzu a kwamfuta. Bar na'urorin a kan AUTO: yana fifita katin sautin rediyon don rediyo, kuma gare ka na'urar da ba ta rediyo ba.

Sanarwoyin da za ka iya gani

  • Fitarwa zuwa rediyo (alama): fitar da sauti ta asali ta kwamfutarka ita ce katin sauti na rediyo, don haka sautin tsarin, burauza da faɗakarwa suna shiga rediyo kuma suna iya fita iska idan yana watsawa ta VOX ko shigarwar bayanansa. qFT8 yana ba da damar yin amfani da wata fitarwa (YI AMFANI DA ...), buɗe saitunan sauti, ko daina tambaya. Sautin watsawar qFT8 shi ma yana bin fitarwa ta asali: fara zaɓar katin sautin rediyo a matsayin fitarwar qFT8, sannan ka canza ta asalin kwamfutar.
  • Babu shigarwa daga rediyo (alama, Linux): katin sautin rediyo ba shi da shigarwa a tsarin sauti saboda yana kan bayanin fitarwa kawai. KARANTA REDIYO KAI TSAYE (an ba da shawara) yana bar qFT8 ya karanta katin da kansa; CANZA ZUWA PRO AUDIO yana canza bayanin katin ga kowane shiri.
  • Babu na'urorin shigarwa: tsarin ba ya ba da shigarwa; haɗa rediyo ko katin sauti ka duba ko ya bayyana a matsayin shigarwa a saitunan sauti na kwamfuta.
  • Ba a shigar da direban USB na rediyo ba (Windows da macOS): Icom IC-7300 da sauran rediyo masu chip na USB na Silicon Labs na iya buƙatar direban CP210x. Sauke shi daga shafin direban mai ƙerawa, shigar da shi ka sake haɗa rediyo.
  • Wani rediyo daban? (alama): rediyon da aka haɗa ba shi ne wanda aka zaɓa a qFT8 ba.

Ta tsari

  • Windows: qFT8 yana jin rediyo ta injin sauti na Windows da kansa (WASAPI), wanda yake ba da kwararar sauti mai kwanciyar hankali fiye da tsohuwar hanya. Rediyo yana bayyana a matsayin port na COM (COM3, ...) da na'urar sauti "USB Audio CODEC" ko makamancinta. Windows tana bar shiri ɗaya kawai ya yi amfani da port na COM: idan qFT8 ya ce an hana shiga, rufe ɗayan shirin (logger, shirin yanayin dijital, software na rediyon da kansa) ka danna SAKE GWADAWA.
  • macOS: port na rediyo yana kama da /dev/cu.usbmodem... (QMX) ko /dev/cu.SLAB_USBtoUART (IC-7300). macOS ba ya buƙatar rukuni ko izini don port, sai izinin makirufo da aka ambata a sama. Idan an ƙi port, wani shiri ya buɗe shi.
  • Linux: port yana kama da /dev/ttyACM0 ko /dev/ttyUSB0 kuma yana buƙatar rukunin da aka ambata a sama. Na'urorin sauti su ne na sound server (PulseAudio ko PipeWire), don haka lasifikar rediyo, belun kunnenka da makirufonka kowanne na iya zama na'ura daban. Na'urar zamiyar ƙarar na'ura tana amfani da pactl, daga kunshin pulseaudio-utils (yana aiki da PipeWire ma).

Zaɓar rediyo da port ɗinsa

Kamar a Android, bar Saituna > Na'ura a kan AUTO: ana gane rediyon da ake tallafawa idan an haɗa shi. Don rediyo a bayan interface na USB-serial na gama-gari (Digirig, misali), zaɓi rediyon da hannu, sannan ka zaɓi port ɗinsa a Saituna > TSARIN NA'URA > Saitunan port na sadarwa > Na'urar USB; baud rate, data bits, stop bits, parity da hanyar keying (DTR, RTS ko CAT) suna nan ma, qFT8 ne yake saita su don rediyon da ka zaɓa sai dai idan ka sa alama a Bada izinin canza saituna. Rediyo da suke amfani da sauti kawai (VOX) ba sa buƙatar port ko kaɗan. Layin umarni yana iya lissafa ports da na'urorin sauti da qFT8 yake gani.

↑ Koma zuwa Abubuwan da ke ciki

Linzami da madannai

Ana sarrafa shirin kwamfuta da linzami. Don tsaro, madannai ba ya taɓa danna wani abin sarrafawa: Tab ba ya motsawa tsakanin abubuwan sarrafawa, kuma Space da Enter ba sa danna maɓalli, sa alama a akwati ko kunna TX. (Maɓalli da aka danna bisa kuskure ba zai iya watsawa ba.) Abin da madannai yake yi:

  • Filayen rubutu suna karɓar kowane maɓalli kamar yadda aka saba: rubuta alamar kira, saƙo ko bincike. Enter yana tabbatar da akwatin rubutu na akwatin magana. Kwafi da liƙa suna aiki: Ctrl a Windows da Linux, Cmd a macOS (Ctrl shi ma yana aiki a can).
  • Esc yana rufe akwatin magana, taga ta sashe, taswira ta cikakken allo, da editan QSO ko logger.
  • Ctrl-S (Cmd-S a macOS) yana adanawa a editan QSO.
  • CW: madannai naka na iya zama key ko paddles (Saituna > CW); waɗannan maɓallai, har da Space da Tab, suna aiki yayin da allon CW shi ne taga mai aiki (duba CW).
  • SSB: Space shi ne push-to-talk muddin ana riƙe shi kuma sashen SSB yana da hankali (duba SSB).

Linzami yana maye gurbin motsin taɓawa:

A Android Taɓa
A kwamfuta Danna hagu
A Android Dogon taɓa (shafin tasha, shafin rukuni, ma'aji a SSB)
A kwamfuta Danna dama
A Android Taɓa saƙo a hirar JS8
A kwamfuta Danna sau biyu
A Android Zaɓi QSO da yawa a log
A kwamfuta Ctrl-danna, ko Shift-danna don jeri
A Android Shafawa a kan dabaran daidaitawa; jan a kan waterfall
A kwamfuta Dabaran linzami a kan spectrogram a SSB da CW yana motsa sandar daidaitawa (Shift: ƙananan matakai); danna ka ja kamar a waya
A Android Matsewa da yatsu biyu don ƙara girman taswirar duniya
A kwamfuta Dabaran linzami a kan taswira yana ƙara girma a wurin mai nuni; ja yana motsa ta

Babu sandar menu: komai yana a sandar ƙasa, sassan da Saituna.

↑ Koma zuwa Abubuwan da ke ciki

Saituna: inda suke, da mayar da su daga waya

qFT8 yana ajiye duk abin da yake adanawa a babban fayil ɗaya, babban fayil ɗin profile: saituna, kalmomin sirri, log na QSO, kwafin ajiya da hotuna.

  • Windows: C:\Users\<kai>\qFT8
  • macOS da Linux: .qft8 a babban fayil ɗin gidanka (~/.qft8; a ɓoye a Finder da a yawancin manajan fayil)

Babban fayil ɗin yana nan idan ka sabunta ko ka share shirin, don haka sabuwar siga tana samun saitunanka da log ɗinka. A ƙasan babban shafin Saituna, a ƙarƙashin siga, Babban fayil na saituna: yana nuna babban fayil ɗin (danna shi don buɗe shi), kuma maɓallin Buɗe babban fayil na log da ke kusa da BA DA RAHOTON MATSALA yana buɗe babban fayil ɗin da logs na qFT8, wanda yake da amfani idan ka aika rahoton matsala. Siga na Windows na farko suna ajiye shi a babban fayil settings kusa da qFT8.exe: farawa na farko na sabuwar siga yana motsa shi, yana duba kwafin, kuma yana riƙe tsohon babban fayil a matsayin settings.old.

Mayar da saitunanka daga waya (ko zuwa gare ta)

FITAR DA SAITUNA da SHIGO DA SAITUNA, a babban shafin Saituna (a ƙarƙashin Fayilolin saituna), suna yin kuma suna karanta fayil ɗaya wanda yake iri ɗaya a Android da a kwamfuta. Fitar a waya, kwafi fayil ɗin zuwa kwamfuta (wasiƙa, cloud, kebul), shigo da shi a can; haka kuma ta wata hanyar tana aiki.

  • Fayil ɗin yana ɗauke da maɓallin API na QRZ, takardar shaidar LoTW da kalmomin sirrinka. Kada ka raba shi.
  • Ba ya ɗauke da QSO: motsa log ta ADIF, ko ka bar sabobin log na qFT8 su kwafa shi.
  • Ba ya ɗauke da abin da ya shafi wannan kwamfutar kawai: zaɓin na'urorin sauti (na'urori daban ne), tsarin da tagogi. Sake zaɓar su a kwamfuta.
  • Shigo da shi yana tambayarka ka rubuta yes don tabbatarwa, yana buƙatar TX ya kasance a kashe, yana riƙe QSO naka, kuma ba ya taɓa kunna TX.

A kwamfuta, fitarwa ba ta taɓa maye gurbin fayil da ya riga ya wanzu ba (duba Shigo/Fitar da ADIF).

↑ Koma zuwa Abubuwan da ke ciki

Kwafi da yawa, gada, na'urar sarrafa daga nesa ta yanar gizo, layin umarni

  • Kwafi ɗaya ga kowane babban fayil na profile. Idan ka fara qFT8 yayin da wani kwafi yake amfani da babban fayil ɗaya, yana faɗa Ana Gudanar da shi Tuni yana ba da Sake Gwadawa ko Fita: kwafi biyu a babban fayil ɗaya na iya lalata saitunanka da log. Don gudanar da kwafi da yawa a lokaci guda, misali ɗaya ga kowace rediyo, ba kowanne nasa babban fayil idan ka fara shi: qFT8 --qft8_profile D:\qft8-radio2 (Windows: qFT8.exe). qFT8 yana tsayawa da kuskure idan ba zai iya rubutu a wannan babban fayil ɗin ba.
  • Na'urar sarrafa daga nesa ta yanar gizo. Shirin kwamfuta yana da Sabar Yanar Gizo Ta Kula Daga Nesa iri ɗaya da na Android (a kashe ta asali, port 13497) kuma yana nuna shafuka iri ɗaya, har da na'urar zamiyar ƙara. Yi amfani da ita don aiki daga waya ko wata kwamfuta a hanyar sadarwarka.
  • Gada. Kwamfuta ita ce gada ta yau da kullum: rediyo a haɗe da ita, da qFT8 a waya, iPhone ko wata kwamfuta yana sarrafa ta. Tana kuma iya aiki ba tare da taga ba (qFT8 --bridge), don kwamfuta da ba ta da wani aiki.
  • Layin umarni. qFT8 --help yana lissafa zaɓuɓɓuka (a Windows ƙaramar taga tana nuna su): --list-devices yana lissafa kowane port na serial da na'urar sauti da qFT8 yake gani sannan ya fita; --port <na'ura> yana saita port na serial na asali (COM3, /dev/ttyACM0); --qft8_profile <babban fayil> yana zaɓar babban fayil ɗin profile; --bridge yana fara gada; --opengl yana zana taga ta OpenGL (duba Idan wani abu bai yi aiki ba).

Kwamfutoci masu jinkiri

Saituna > AIKACE-AIKACE > Yanayin na'ura mai jinkiri / ƙarancin baturi yana sa qFT8 ya yi sauƙi: kusan hotuna 30 a daƙiƙa, ana sake zana waterfall da ƙarancin yawa, kuma taswirar duniya tana aiki ne kawai idan ana nuna ta. Yana canza abin da ake zana ne kawai, ba decoder ba: qFT8 yana decode iri ɗaya a kunne ko a kashe.

Yanayin jinkiri yana kunnawa ne kawai idan ka sa alama a akwatinsa, ba ya kunnawa da kansa. A kwamfuta mai core ɗaya ko biyu na processor, qFT8 yana tambaya idan ya fara ko zai kunna shi: KUNNA YANAYIN JINKIRI yana sa alama a akwatin, RUFE yana sake tambaya a farawa na gaba, kuma KADA KU SAKE TAMBAYA yana daina tambaya. A irin wannan kwamfuta qFT8 yana kuma ba decoder ɗinsa rabon da ya dace na processor da kansa, ko da yanayin jinkiri ko babu, don wani shiri mai aiki ya kasa matse shi.

Sabuntawa da cirewa

Sabuntawa

Idan an sa alama a Duba sabbin siga lokacin farawa (an riga an sa; duba Duba sabbin siga lokacin farawa), qFT8 yana tambaya sau ɗaya a rana ko akwai sabuwar siga kuma yana nuna Akwai sabon siga da RUFE da SAUKE YANZU, wanda yake buɗe shafin saukewa a burauzarka. qFT8 ba ya sabunta kansa. Don sabuntawa, sauke sabon fayil ɗin ka maye gurbin tsohon shirin:

  • Windows: rufe qFT8, maye gurbin babban fayil ɗin qFT8 (ko kawai qFT8.exe) da sabon.
  • macOS: rufe qFT8, jawo sabon qFT8 zuwa Applications ka zaɓi Replace.
  • Linux: rufe qFT8 ka gudanar da sabon qFT8. Yana buɗe kansa kuma yana cire tsohuwar siga daga cache.

Saitunanka da log ɗinka suna nan a babban fayil ɗin profile.

Cirewa

Rufe qFT8 ka share shirin: babban fayil ɗin qFT8 (Windows, Linux) ko qFT8 a Applications (macOS); a Linux kuma ~/.cache/qft8. Wannan yana riƙe saitunanka da log ɗinka. Don cire su ma, share babban fayil ɗin profile (a sama). Hakan yana goge log ɗin QSO naka: fara fitar da shi ta ADIF.

Idan wani abu bai yi aiki ba

  • Waterfall ba komai kuma alamar tana cewa Babu sauti. Buɗe Tsarin Na'ura: shin katin sautin rediyo ne shigarwa, kuma rediyo yana kunne? A macOS duba izinin makirufo; a Linux alamar Babu shigarwa daga rediyo da rukunonin.
  • Rediyo yana karɓa amma ba ya watsawa, TUNE ba ya keying komai. An ƙi port: wani shiri yana da shi (Windows, macOS) ko asusunka ba ya cikin rukunin (Linux). qFT8 yana faɗa wanne; rufe ɗayan shirin ka danna SAKE GWADAWA, ko ka bi katin Linux. Duba kuma Waya ko kwamfuta ba ta gane transceiver ko haɗin yana katsewa.
  • Rediyo yana da ƙaramin sauti, ko babba, ko yana nuna dige biyu maimakon sanda (QMX): motsa na'urar zamiyar ƙarar na'ura (Ƙarar sauti). Duba cewa ƙarar kwamfutar kanta ga wannan na'ura ba a yi mata shiru ba.
  • Ana jin sautin kwamfuta a iska. Fitarwa ta asali ita ce katin sautin rediyo: amsa alamar Fitarwa zuwa rediyo, ko ka zaɓi wata fitarwa ta asali a tsarinka.
  • "Ana Gudanar da shi Tuni". Wani kwafi yana amfani da babban fayil ɗin profile. Rufe shi, ko ka fara wannan da babban fayil ɗinsa na --qft8_profile.
  • Taga baƙi ce ko ta cakude (tsoffin direbobin graphics, virtual machine, remote desktop): sabunta direban graphics. Idan ya ci gaba, fara qFT8 da --opengl (qFT8 --opengl), wanda yake zana taga ta wata hanya; idan hakan ma bai taimaka ba, bayar da rahoto da BA DA RAHOTON MATSALA (Saituna), kana faɗa wane tsari da katin graphics kake da shi.
  • Taga tana waje da allo (an cire allo): qFT8 yana duba wurin da aka adana na kowace taga idan ya buɗe ta, kuma wadda allonta ya ɓace, ko wadda za ta fi rabi daga allo, tana buɗewa a tsakiyar babban allo maimakon haka. Idan taga har yanzu tana waje, rufe sashenta da maɓallin sandar ƙasa ka sake buɗe shi, ko ka sake fara qFT8.
  • Waterfall ya daskare, katsewar sauti, ko nauyi mai yawa a kwamfuta mai core ɗaya ko biyu: duba Kwamfutoci masu jinkiri, rufe sauran shirye-shirye, ka aika rahoto da BA DA RAHOTON MATSALA idan ya ci gaba.
↑ Koma zuwa Abubuwan da ke ciki

qFT8 a iPhone da iPad

qFT8 na iPhone da iPad yana aiki kamar qFT8 na Android, kuma sauran wannan littafin jagora ya shafe shi; yana bukatar iOS 17 ko sabo. Abin da ya bambanta shi ne yadda yake kaiwa ga rediyo.

iPhone ko iPad ba za su iya sarrafa rediyo ta waya ba: babu CAT, don haka qFT8 ba zai iya saita mitar rediyon ko ya kunna watsawa ta USB ba. Yana jin rediyon kuma yana aika sautukansa ta mahaɗar sauti ta USB ta rediyon, wata mahaɗar sauti ko wayar sauti. Don rediyon ya watsa, yi amfani da ɗaya daga cikin waɗannan:

  • VOX na rediyon: rediyon yana shiga watsawa da kansa idan sautukan qFT8 suka isa gare shi. Zaɓi VOX a cikin saitunan qFT8.
  • Mahaɗar sauti da ke kunna rediyon daga sautin kansa: tana sa rediyon ya shiga watsawa duk lokacin da sauti ya fito daga qFT8.
  • qFT8 bridge: kwamfuta, Raspberry Pi ko wayar Android ko tablet kusa da rediyon, da rediyon a haɗe da shi ta USB. Sannan qFT8 a iPhone ko iPad yana sarrafa rediyon ta hanyar sadarwarku kamar an haɗa shi kai tsaye, har da mita da watsawa. Kwamfutar da ba ta da wani aiki, har da sabar da ba ta da allo, za ta iya gudanar da bridge daga layin umarni, ba tare da taga ba.
↑ Koma zuwa Abubuwan da ke ciki

Saiti cikin Sauri

Ka tabbata kana amfani da wutar lantarki da ta dace da transceiver (misali ≤12V ga QMX), in ba haka ba za ka lalata shi.

Ka kuma tabbata cewa kebul na USB da adafta da kake haɗa waya ko kwamfuta da transceiver da su suna iya ɗaukar bayanai (data). A Android, na'urorin da ba su da USB-C galibi suna buƙatar kebul na OTG (on-the-go) na musamman. Yawancin matsalolin haɗi, inda qFT8 ba ta ganin transceiver, suna faruwa ne saboda kebul ko adafta da bai dace ba.

A Android, wasu na'urori suna kashe USB OTG tun asali. Dole a kunna shi a saitunan na'urarka (zaɓin yana iya kasancewa a ɓoye a saitunan advanced, system, ko additional). Idan kana da matsalar haɗi — musamman idan qFT8 tana karɓar sigina amma ba ta iya watsawa, kuma umarnin CAT ba sa aiki duk da cewa ana tallafa wa na'urar sosai (tana cikin jerin na'urori, ana gane ta a yanayin AUTO, da sauransu) — to wannan ne mai yiwuwa dalilin.

Ba da Izinin Farko

A Android, idan ka buɗe qFT8 a karo na farko, za ta nemi wasu izini:

  • Bari qFT8 ta ɗauki sauti: Wannan izini wajibi ne. qFT8 tana buƙatarsa domin karɓar sigina daga transceiver. qFT8 ba ta adana, ba ta rabawa, kuma ba ta loda kowane sauti da aka ɗauka.
  • Bari qFT8 ta san kimanin wurin wannan na'ura: Ba wajibi ba ne, amma ana ba da shawara. qFT8 tana amfani da shi domin samun lokata na grid na Maidenhead naka, wanda ake aikawa a musayar FT8. qFT8 ba ta duba kuma ba ta raba ainihin wurinka.
  • Bari qFT8 ta aiko maka da sanarwa: Ba wajibi ba ne, amma ana ba da shawara. qFT8 tana amfani da shi domin ta yi aiki a matsayin foreground service lokacin da aka rage ta ko allo a kashe, domin adana baturi. Sanarwa suna aiki ne kawai idan an haɗa da transceiver.
  • Bari manhaja ta ci gaba da aiki a bango koyaushe: Ba wajibi ba ne, amma ana ba da shawara. Ana buƙatarsa domin qFT8 ta yi aiki yadda ya kamata allo a kashe.
  • Bari qFT8 ya nemo na'urori da ke kusa: Ba wajibi ba ne, ana tambaya ne kawai idan ka zaɓi rediyon Bluetooth a saitin na'ura (Android 12 da sama). qFT8 yana amfani da shi ne kawai don haɗawa da rediyon da ka zaɓa; komai yana aiki ba tare da shi ba.

A kwamfuta irin waɗannan izini ba su da yawa. A macOS, tsarin yana tambaya sau ɗaya ko qFT8 za ta iya amfani da makirufo: ka yarda, domin ta haka ne qFT8 take jin transceiver. A Linux, amfani da port na serial (CAT) na rediyo yana buƙatar asusunka ya kasance a cikin rukunin port ɗin (galibi dialout); idan ba haka ba, qFT8 tana bayyana shi kuma tana ba da damar gyarawa.

Babbar ƙa'ida: qFT8 ba ta aika kowane bayani zuwa wata sabar da aka keɓe domin wannan manhaja. Ana sarrafa dukkan bayanai a cikin na'urarka. Kana iya ba da izini a fili a raba alamar kira, kwanan wata da lokaci, da kuma (idan ka so) kimanin wuri, idan ka shigar da Mabuɗin API na QRZ mai inganci.

Domin ƙarin bayani, duba Manufar Sirri - Lasisi na qFT8.

Shigar da Alamar Kira

Shigar da alamar kira wajibi ne domin qFT8 ta yi aiki. Idan ba ka da niyyar haɗawa da ainihin transceiver, kana iya shigar da kowace alamar kira domin gwaji, misali XX1XXX.

qFT8 za ta nemi ka shigar da alamar kiranka kuma ka amince da waɗannan:

  • Dole ka kasance da lasisi mai inganci domin amfani da wannan manhaja idan an haɗa da transceiver.
  • Kada ka bar manhajar tana aiki ba tare da kana wurin ba.
  • Sanarwar ɗaukar alhaki: Idan ka yi amfani da qFT8, kai ne ke da dukkan alhakin bin doka, tsaro, da lalacewar kayan aiki.

Ba da Izinin Haɗin USB

A Android, idan an saka transceiver kuma qFT8 a buɗe take, wata tambaya ta tsarin za ta bayyana (kwamfuta ba ta tambaya: a can, duba bayanin Linux na sama):

"Buɗe qFT8 domin sarrafa Transceiver."

  • Wannan izini wajibi ne domin sadarwa da transceiver.
  • Idan ba a bayar da shi ba, qFT8 za ta iya karɓar sigina amma ba za ta iya watsawa ba.
  • Domin sauƙi, kana iya zaɓar "Koyaushe a buɗe qFT8 idan an haɗa Transceiver".

Idan kana da matsala wajen haɗa transceiver, duba sashen magance matsaloli Waya ko kwamfuta ba ta gane transceiver ko haɗin yana katsewa.

qFT8 tana saita transceiver ta atomatik duk lokacin da aka haɗa shi.

↑ Koma zuwa Abubuwan da ke ciki

[QMX kawai] Duba Sauti

Kafin ka fara aiki, yana da muhimmanci ka tabbata siginar sauti tsakanin wayarka ko kwamfutarka da QMX tana aiki daidai. Danna maɓallin TX ka jira QMX ya fara watsawa a ɗaya daga cikin jerin na gaba.

Komai yana aiki

Idan maɓallin TX ya nuna ƙarfin fita (misali 5W), komai yana aiki daidai. A QMX LCD, za ka ga layi a ƙarƙashin alamar VFO "A" a sama ta hagu na allo — wannan yana nufin QMX yana karɓar siginar sauti mai kyau daga qFT8. Ka kuma tabbata babu "S" bayan mita a LCD; idan akwai, kariyar SWR ce ta kunnu.

An nuna "S" bayan mita

Idan QMX LCD ya nuna "S" bayan mita, kariyar SWR ta kunnu. Ba a daidaita eriyarka yadda ya kamata ga wannan zango ba. Kana iya amfani da zaɓin duba SWR a menu na QMX domin auna SWR, kuma za ka buƙaci daidaita eriyarka.

qFT8 yana da nasa binciken kuma: idan SWR da yake karantawa ya wuce iyakar da ke cikin Dakatar da watsawa idan SWR ya wuce, yana dakatar da watsawa kuma yana mayar da TX zuwa OFF, tare da gargaɗi (a waya, kwamfuta, iPhone da na'urar sarrafa daga nesa ta yanar gizo). Ba za a aika komai a slot na gaba ba: gyara eriya, sannan ka danna TX ON da kanka.

  • Firmware 1_04_004+: qFT8 tana sake saita kariyar SWR ta atomatik a ƙarshen zagaye.
  • Firmware 1_03_000+: Kana iya saita "SWR Protection reset options" zuwa TX->RX a menu na Protection na QMX, domin ya sake saituwa da kansa.
  • Tsohon firmware (kafin 1_03_000): Dole ka shiga ka fita daga menu na TUNE a QMX, ko ka kashe shi ka sake kunna shi, domin sake saita kariyar SWR.

Ɗigo biyu maimakon layi

Idan QMX ya nuna ɗigo biyu maimakon layi a ƙarƙashin alamar VFO "A" a sama ta hagu na allo, ƙarar sauti ta yi ƙasa sosai ko ta yi yawa sosai.

Gwada: Je zuwa Saituna na qFT8, nemo zaɓin "Saita ƙarar tsarin zuwa [X] yayin amfani da rediyo", ka gwada lambobi daban-daban (misali 70%, 80%, 60%, 100%). Ɗaya daga cikinsu zai mayar da ɗigo biyun layi.

A wasu wayoyi, wannan zaɓi ba ya aiki. Idan haka ne, cire alamar zaɓin, sai ka canza ƙarar wayar da hannu zuwa lambobi daban-daban (100%, 90%, 80%, da sauransu) har layin ya bayyana.

Haka kuma, common-mode current yana iya shafar aikin QMX. Saka common-mode choke a eriya yana iya taimakawa ga matsalolin sauti.

Babu komai a ƙarƙashin "A"

Idan babu layi kuma babu ɗigo a ƙarƙashin alamar VFO "A" a sama ta hagu na allon QMX LCD, mai yiwuwa qFT8 ba ta sarrafa transceiver yadda ya kamata. Galibi wannan yana nufin ba a ba da izinin USB ba, ko an rufe tambayar bisa kuskure. Karɓa za ta yi aiki, amma watsawa ba za ta yi ba.

Gwada: Cire QMX ka sake saka shi — a Android, tambayar izini za ta sake bayyana. Mai yiwuwa kuma kebul ɗin bai dace da QMX ba; gwada wani kebul na USB-C.

Da zarar ka ga layi a ƙarƙashin alamar VFO "A" a sama ta hagu na allon QMX LCD da ƙarfin fita a maɓallin TX, to an saita QMX ɗinka daidai kuma a shirye yake!

↑ Koma zuwa Abubuwan da ke ciki

[Na zaɓi] Shigo da QSO

Yanzu kana iya shigo da QSO da kake da su; akwai hanyoyi da dama. Dukkansu suna buƙatar ka je saituna da maɓallin da ke ƙarshen dama a sandar ƙasa ta qFT8.

Hanyar hannu: Je zuwa sashen "Shigo da ADIF File" a saituna, danna maɓallin "SHIGO DA QSOS DAGA ADIF", ka zaɓi fayil na .adi da za a shigo da shi.

Hanyar atomatik ta QRZ: Tana buƙatar biyan kuɗin QRZ mai aiki. Je zuwa sashen "Daidaita QSO" a saituna, shigar da Mabuɗin API na QRZ na alamar kiranka, danna "TABBATAR", sannan danna maɓallin "SHIGO DA QSOS DAGA QRZ". Wannan zai kuma saita lodawa ta atomatik zuwa QRZ da duba tabbatarwa.

Hanyar atomatik ta LoTW: Kyauta ce. Je zuwa sashen "Daidaita QSO" a saituna, shigar da Alamar kira da Kalmar sirri ta LoTW, ka kunna daidaitawa a bango idan kana so. Wannan zai kuma ba ka damar yin "SHIGO DA QSOS DAGA LoTW" na farko.

↑ Koma zuwa Abubuwan da ke ciki

QSO ɗinka na Farko da qFT8

Lura: Daidaitaccen lokaci yana da matuƙar muhimmanci ga FT8. qFT8 tana daidaita lokaci ta atomatik idan manhajar ta fara, ta NTP (intanet) ko GPS (tauraron ɗan adam). Idan kana aiki a waje ba tare da intanet ba, kana iya amfani da maɓallin DAIDAITA GPS a shafin Saituna domin daidaita agogonka kai tsaye daga tauraron GPS. Wannan yana buƙatar izinin ainihin wuri da sararin sama a buɗe.

A wannan lokaci, ya kamata an haɗa transceiver da wutar da ta dace da kuma eriya, kuma an ba da dukkan izini. Ka tabbata an daidaita eriya ga zango mai SWR mai kyau (zai fi kyau ya kasance ƙasa da 2 koyaushe) wanda ba zai kunna kariyar SWR ba. Kashe kariyar SWR a QMX ba shawara ce mai kyau ba; yana iya lalata transistor na ƙarshe (finals) ko tuner na eriya.

Yanzu kana iya zaɓar zangon da kake so a sandar ƙasa ta qFT8 ko a transceiver. Idan ka saita shi a ɗaya, ɗayan zai bi shi.

A wannan lokaci, spectrogram zai nuna sigina kuma za a fara fassara saƙonni, kuma shafin Tashoshi zai riƙa sabuntawa da tashoshin da kake ji.

Danna maɓallin TX (maɓallin da ke ƙarshen hagu a ƙasa) zai kunna watsawa.

Idan komai daidai ne, bayan ɗan lokaci maɓallin TX zai haskaka da "TX Transmitting" kuma zai nuna ƙarfin watsawa da SWR (misali "5.0W SWR 1.2").

Tun da yanayin "AUTO" ne aka zaɓa tun asali, qFT8 za ta fara yin QSO kuma ta rubuta su a shafin Log na QSO.

Kana iya taɓa spectrogram domin canza bambancin mitar Tx (haka kuma, taɓa ɓangaren sama na spectrogram, inda alamun kololuwar sigina suke, yana ba ka damar shigar da mita da hannu). Kana iya canza jeri ta taɓa maɓallan 0 da 1 a sandar ƙasa. Kana iya taɓa maɓallan "CALL" domin kiran wasu tashoshi. Ko kuma kana iya gyara sandar da ke da maɓallin "AUTO" a shafin Tashoshi domin zaɓar dabara. Ƙarin bayani game da waɗannan fasaloli yana cikin sassan da ke gaba.

Lura cewa qFT8 za ta fara kiran tashoshi; idan ba a iya kai wa ko ɗaya ba, za ta fara kiran CQ. A ƙarshe, idan an saita komai daidai kuma propagation yana da kyau, qFT8 za ta sami QSO.

Idan qFT8 ba ta karɓar sigina masu kyau kuma ba ta fassara saƙonni, wannan alama ce a fili cewa akwai matsala: mai yiwuwa propagation a wannan zango a rufe yake, ko ba a daidaita eriya ba, ko ba a haɗa eriya da transceiver daidai ba, ko ba a haɗa transceiver da waya ko kwamfuta daidai ba, ko wani kayan aikinka ba ya aiki daidai. Duba sashen magance matsaloli a ƙasa domin matsalolin da aka saba gani.

↑ Koma zuwa Abubuwan da ke ciki

Gabatarwa ga yanayin dijital

Yanayin dijital na rediyon amateur suna mayar da rubutu da bayanai zuwa sautuka na frequency-shift keying (FSK), waɗanda ake watsawa ta rediyon single-sideband (SSB). Suna haɗa ƙaramin faɗin zango (bandwidth), forward error correction (FEC) mai ƙarfi, da daidaita lokaci sosai. Saboda haka suna ba da damar sadarwa a duk duniya ko da sigina suna ƙasa da hayaniya sosai. qFT8 tana aiki da manyan yanayin dijital na zamani — FT8, FT4, FT2, JS8, WSPR, da LQ8 — kowannensu an tsara shi domin wata manufa: daga beacon na propagation da sigina mai rauni sosai, zuwa gasa cikin sauri, da hira ta rubutu tsakanin mutane biyu.

FT8

FT8 (tsarin Franke-Taylor, 8-GFSK) shi ne protocol na dijital na sigina mai rauni da aka fi amfani da shi a duniya. An tsara shi domin yanayi mai wuya na HF/VHF, sporadic-E, da zanguna masu cunkoso. Yana aiki da saƙonni masu tsari, daidaita lokaci na daƙiƙa 15, da gyaran kuskure mai ƙarfi.

Zagaye da Jeri

FT8 yana aiki a zagaye na daƙiƙa 15. Kowane zagaye yana farawa daidai a daƙiƙa 00, 15, 30, da 45 na kowane minti. Saboda haka, dole agogon na'urarka ya daidaita sosai da lokacin UTC.

Akwai jeri biyu a cikin protocol ɗin:

  • Jeri 0 (Even): Zagayen da ke farawa a daƙiƙa 00 da 30.
  • Jeri 1 (Odd): Zagayen da ke farawa a daƙiƙa 15 da 45.

Tashoshi galibi suna musanya tsakanin watsawa da karɓa. Tashar da ke watsawa a Jeri 0 za ta saurari amsa a Jeri 1, haka kuma akasin haka. A cikin kowane zagaye na daƙiƙa 15, ana watsa saƙo tsakanin kimanin +0.5s da +13s. Wannan yana barin lokaci a ƙarshen zagaye domin fassara, da a farkonsa domin shirya watsawa ta gaba.

Siffofin Sigina

Ana watsa kowane saƙon FT8 a matsayin jerin sautuka masu ɗan canjin mita. Siginar FT8 tana da ƙanƙanta sosai: tana ɗaukar kimanin 50Hz na bandwidth kawai. Saboda haka gomman hirarraki suna iya shiga cikin zangon rediyo na 3kHz ɗaya a lokaci guda.

Matakai 6 na QSO

QSO na FT8 na yau da kullum galibi yana bi ta matakai shida. Ga misalin musaya tsakanin HB9IPH da XX1XXX:

  1. 1/6 CQ: Tasha tana neman kowa domin yin magana.
    Misali: CQ HB9IPH JN47
    Lura: JN47 shi ne lokata na grid na Maidenhead, wanda yake nuna kimanin wurin tashar. Ana iya samun Mai canza CQ kuma, wato saƙo ga sauran masu aiki (misali CQ SOTA HB9IPH domin shirin Summits On The Air, ko CQ US HB9IPH idan ana neman wani a Amurka). Mai canzawa da wuri ba koyaushe suke shiga saƙo ɗaya ba; idan haka ne, ana cire wurin. Idan alamar kira ta yi tsawo sosai, mai yiwuwa wuri da mai canzawa duka ba za su shiga CQ ba!
  2. 2/6 CALL: Wata tasha tana amsa CQ ɗin.
    Misali: HB9IPH XX1XXX JO21
    Lura: Mai amsawa ma yana aika wurinsa (JO21); ana iya cire shi idan alamomin kira sun yi tsawo.
  3. 3/6 RPT: Tasha ta farko tana aika rahoton sigina.
    Misali: XX1XXX HB9IPH -10
    Lura: Rahoton sigina (misali -10) yana nuna ƙarfin sigina idan aka kwatanta da hayaniya, a dB.
  4. 4/6 RRPT: Tasha ta biyu tana karɓar rahoton kuma tana aika nata.
    Misali: HB9IPH XX1XXX R-12
    Lura: "R" a farko yana tabbatar da cewa an karɓi rahoton da ya gabata; bayansa sai rahoton sigina na tasha ta farko.
  5. 5/6 RR73: Tasha ta farko tana tabbatar da komai kuma tana aika "Roger-Roger 73".
    Misali: XX1XXX HB9IPH RR73
  6. 6/6 73: Tasha ta biyu tana aika "73" na ƙarshe domin rufe QSO.
    Misali: HB9IPH XX1XXX 73

Saboda Matsalar Janar-janar Biyu (Two Generals' Problem), a ka'ida ba zai yiwu ka san tabbas cewa ɗayan tashar ta karɓi saƙonka na ƙarshe ba, sai dai idan ta tabbatar. Amma sai kai ma ka tabbatar da tabbatarwarta, ita ma ta tabbatar da taka, haka dai ba iyaka. FT8 yana rage wannan matsala da matakai masu tsari: ana iya ɗaukar QSO a matsayin cikakke bayan mataki na 4/6 (lokacin da aka yi musayar rahotannin sigina biyu). A aikace, domin tashoshin biyu su yarda cewa musayar ta faru, ana rubuta QSO idan tasha ta karɓi RR73 kuma ta aika 73. A taƙaice, watsa RR73 ko 73 ne ke sa a rubuta QSO.

Lura cewa tashoshi suna iya kiran wasu tashoshi ko da ba su fara watsa CQ ba. Misali, tashoshin da ke wurare masu wuya suna iya samun "pile-up", wato tashoshi da yawa suna kiransu a lokaci guda; idan haka ne, tashar tana iya tafiya daga QSO ɗaya zuwa na gaba ba tare da kiran CQ ba.

Wasu tashoshi suna amfani da RRR maimakon RR73 a mataki na 5. Idan haka ne, QSO yana buƙatar ƙarin musaya ɗaya: tasha ta biyu ta aika 73, sannan tasha ta farko ta sake aika 73 domin gamawa.

FT8 yana kuma da saƙonnin rubutu na kyauta (free text) (har haruffa 13), amma a yanzu qFT8 ba ta aika irin waɗannan saƙonni.

Iyakokin Protocol da Hanyoyin Maye

Ana matse saƙonnin FT8 sosai zuwa bit 77. Wani lokaci, bayani mai rikitarwa (kamar dogayen alamomin kira waɗanda ba na yau da kullum ba) ba ya shiga tsarin saƙo na yau da kullum.

  • Alamomin kira masu hash: Idan alamar kira ta yi tsawo sosai, ana iya aika ta a matsayin hash. Idan tashar da ke karɓa ta ga wannan alamar kira tana kiran CQ kwanan nan, manhajarta tana iya mayar da hash ɗin zuwa ainihin alamar kira. In ba haka ba, za a nuna ta a matsayin <...>. Samun QSO ya fi wuya sosai idan alamar kiranka ba ta yau da kullum ba ce.
  • Komawa free text: Ana iya tilasta wasu saƙonni su shiga tsarin free text na haruffa 13, wanda bai kai ƙarfin saƙonni masu tsari ba.
  • Alamomin kira waɗanda ba na yau da kullum ba: Idan tashoshi biyu duka suna amfani da dogayen alamomin kira waɗanda ba na yau da kullum ba, mai yiwuwa ba za su iya sadarwa da juna da FT8 kwata-kwata ba.
  • Tsallake mataki: Idan alamomin kira sun yi tsawo sosai, protocol ɗin ba zai iya ɗaukar rahotannin sigina ba (matakai na 3/6 da 4/6). A irin wannan hali, musayar tana iya tsallakewa kai tsaye daga CALL (2/6) na farko zuwa tabbatarwar ƙarshe (5/6 da 6/6).
  • The red note under your callsign in Settings (phone, computer and iPhone; it follows the mode, LQ8 has its own wording) says what a callsign that is not standard for FT8 costs: your messages cannot carry a CQ modifier or your grid locator; in signal reports your callsign is sent as a short code, which other stations can show only after receiving it in full (in your CQ or your call); reports cannot be exchanged with another non-standard callsign or one ending in /P or /R.

Saƙonnin da za ka gani amma ba za ka taɓa aikawa ba

Wasu tashoshi suna aika nau'ikan saƙo waɗanda qFT8 take nunawa amma ba ta aika su da kanta:

  • Saƙo ɗaya ga tashoshi biyu: Expedition da ke aiki da tashoshi da yawa a lokaci guda yana iya rufe QSO ɗaya ya fara na gaba a watsawa ɗaya: K1ABC RR73; W9XYZ <VP8XYZ> -08. Ma'anarsa "K1ABC, QSO ɗinka ya cika" da "W9XYZ, rahotonka -08 ne", duka daga VP8XYZ. qFT8 tana nuna shi yadda aka aika shi, a jerin tashoshi da sau ɗaya a waterfall. Idan kai ne ɗaya daga cikin tashoshin biyu, ana ɗaukarsa a matsayin RR73 naka ko rahotonka, kamar dai expedition ɗin ya aika maka kai kaɗai, kuma QSO ɗin yana ci gaba ko ana rubuta shi kamar yadda aka saba. Idan ba kai ba ne, babu abin da za ka yi, kuma AUTO ba ta yin komai da shi.
  • Musayar gasa: A wasu gasa, tashoshi suna aika class da section (Field Day) ko rahoto tare da jiha ko lambar jeri (gasar RTTY) maimakon lokata da rahoto. qFT8 tana nuna waɗannan saƙonni domin ka ga abin da ke kan zango, amma ba ta taɓa amsa su: ba ta aika musayar gasa.

Ƙayyadaddun bayanai: S. Franke, B. Somerville, J. Taylor, “The FT4 and FT8 Communication Protocols”, QEX, July/August 2020.

FT4

FT4 yanayin dijital ne da aka tsara musamman domin gasar rediyon amateur, inda saurin yin QSO yake da muhimmanci:

  • Sauri ninki biyu: Yana aiki a zagaye na daƙiƙa 7.5 (ninki biyu na saurin FT8), yana watsawa na kimanin daƙiƙa 4.48, da lokutan watsawa huɗu a kowane minti.
  • Bandwidth ninki biyu: Yana ɗaukar kimanin 90 Hz na bandwidth na RF (kimanin ninki biyu na ~50 Hz na FT8).
  • Ragin sensitivity: A madadin saurin ninki biyu, FT4 yana iya fassara har kimanin -16.4 dB zuwa -17.5 dB SNR (ragin kimanin 3.5 zuwa 4 dB idan aka kwatanta da FT8).
  • Amfani a aikace: Yana amfani da modulation na 4-GFSK da saƙo mai tsari na bit 77, wanda yake ba masu aiki damar kammala musayar gasa a rabin lokaci idan propagation ya yarda.

Ƙayyadaddun bayanai: S. Franke, B. Somerville, J. Taylor, “The FT4 and FT8 Communication Protocols”, QEX, July/August 2020.

FT2

An tsara FT2 domin musaya mai sauri sosai idan sigina suna da kyau, domin meteor scatter a VHF/UHF, da gasa mai sauri:

  • Ninki biyu na saurin FT4 (4x FT8): Yana aiki a zagaye na daƙiƙa 3.75 da kimanin daƙiƙa 2.24 na watsawa, wato har zagaye 16 a kowane minti.
  • Ninki biyu na bandwidth na FT4 (4x FT8): Yana ɗaukar kimanin 180 Hz na bandwidth na RF (ninki biyu na FT4, kusan ninki huɗu na FT8).
  • Ƙarin ragin sensitivity: Yana iya fassara har kimanin -13 dB zuwa -14 dB SNR (ƙarin ragin ~3 dB idan aka kwatanta da FT4, ko ~6 zuwa 7 dB ƙasa da FT8).
  • Amfani a aikace: Ya dace da QSO masu sauri idan zango ya buɗe da sigina masu ƙarfi, da propagation na ɗan lokaci kaɗan, inda damar QSO take gajeriya kuma sigina suna sama da hayaniya sosai.

Ƙayyadaddun bayanai: babu ƙayyadaddun bayanai da aka buga.

JS8

An ƙirƙiri JS8 (tsarin Jordan Sherer, 8-GFSK) domin hira, rubutu tsakanin mutane biyu, da kafa network na ɗan lokaci, ba domin musayar gasa mai tsari ba:

  • Yanka saƙo da sanya shi cikin firam: Yana amfani da 8-GFSK kamar FT8, amma firam ɗinsa suna ɗauke da bit 75, kuma wani layin jigilar saƙo yana yanka saƙo zuwa firam sannan ya sake haɗa su. Don haka saƙonnin rubutu na kyauta masu tsawon kowane iri suna gudana ta cikin slot-slot na lokaci masu bin juna.
  • Gudu iri-iri: Gudu biyar da suka dace da yanayin zango:
    • Slow: zagaye na daƙiƙa 30, faɗin zango kusan 25 Hz, yana fassara har zuwa -25 dB SNR.
    • Normal: zagaye na daƙiƙa 15, faɗin zango kusan 50 Hz, yana fassara har zuwa -21 dB SNR.
    • Fast: zagaye na daƙiƙa 10, faɗin zango kusan 80 Hz, yana fassara har zuwa -18 dB SNR.
    • Turbo: zagaye na daƙiƙa 6, faɗin zango kusan 160 Hz, yana fassara har zuwa -15 dB SNR.
    • Ultra: zagaye na daƙiƙa 4, faɗin zango kusan 250 Hz, don sigina masu ƙarfi kawai.
  • Damar hanyar sadarwa: Saƙonni zuwa tasha ɗaya ko zuwa ƙungiya, amsoshin tambayoyi daidaitattu da ake bayarwa ta atomatik, da heartbeat. qFT8 yana nuna saƙonnin da aka isar, amma ba ya isar da saƙonni na wasu tashoshi kuma ba ya adana su (duba Hira ta JS8).

Ƙayyadaddun bayanai: babu ƙayyadaddun bayanai da aka buga.

WSPR

An tsara WSPR (Weak Signal Propagation Reporter) domin binciken hanyoyin propagation na rediyo da beacon na atomatik kawai, ba domin QSO na hira ta hanyoyi biyu ba:

  • Zagaye na minti biyu: Yana aiki a zagaye na minti 2 (daƙiƙa 120) da aka daidaita sosai, yana watsa jerin sautuka na 4-FSK masu symbol 162 a cikin daƙiƙa 110.6.
  • Bandwidth ƙanƙani sosai: Yana ɗaukar kimanin 6 Hz na bandwidth na RF kawai, wato yana tattara ƙarfin da ake watsawa a ƙaramin yanki na spectrum.
  • Ƙwarewa sosai ga sigina mai rauni: Yana iya fassara sigina masu rauni har -28 dB zuwa -31 dB SNR a bandwidth na 2.5 kHz, wanda yake ba masu watsawa na milliwatt (QRPp) damar kaiwa ko'ina a duniya.
  • Rahoton propagation ta atomatik: Yana watsa alamar kiran tasha, lokata na grid na Maidenhead (haruffa 4 ko 6), da ƙarfin mai watsawa a dBm. Ana iya tura spots da aka fassara zuwa bayanan WSPRnet na duniya, wanda yake taswirar propagation a ainihin lokaci (duba Spots zuwa WSPRnet da PSKReporter).

Ƙayyadaddun bayanai: J. Taylor (K1JT), “WSPRing Around the World”, QST, November 2010.

LQ8

LQ8 yanayin dijital ne na zamani na sigina mai rauni wanda Luis Quesada (HB9IPH), marubuci kuma mai ƙirƙirar qFT8, ya ƙirƙira:

  • QSO cikin sauri, hankali iri ɗaya: LQ8 yana riƙe matakin raunannun sigina na FT8, -21 dB SNR, kuma yana kammala tabbataccen tuntuɓa cikin watsawa 4 (daƙiƙa 60), inda FT8 ke buƙatar 5 ko 6 (daƙiƙa 75 zuwa 90). Tashoshin biyu har yanzu suna musayar alamun kira, lokatoci da rahotannin sigina.
  • Musayar saƙonni huɗu: CQ, sannan CALL (lokacin da rahoton tashar mai amsawa cikin saƙo ɗaya), sannan REPORT+73 (rahoto da tabbatarwa cikin saƙo ɗaya), sannan 73; babu wani matakin RR73 na daban. A cikin pileup, MULTI-REPORT+73 yana tabbatar da tashoshi biyu masu amsawa lokaci ɗaya a kan carrier ɗaya, kuma MULTI-73 yana rufewa da su biyun.
  • Tsari na zamani: Zagaye na daƙiƙa 15, juyawar 8-GFSK mai alamomi 79 cikin daƙiƙa 12,64 (faɗin band 50 Hz), saƙonni na bit 77 waɗanda ake rubuta irinsu da prefix masu tsawo daban-daban (Huffman), CRC na bit 14 da gyaran kuskure LDPC(174,91). Tsarin daidaitawarsa na kansa yana hana LQ8 da FT8 karanta juna.

Ƙayyadaddun bayanai: L. Quesada (HB9IPH), “The LQ Digital Mode Family: Protocol Architecture and Reference Specification for Weak-Signal Communications”, arXiv:2609.06427 (2026); ɗakin karatu na tunani: lq_lib.

Domin ƙarin bayani, specifications, da kayan aiki, ziyarci https://lq8.org, shafin yanayin LQ.

Q65

Q65 hanyar QSO ce ta sigina masu rauni don hanyoyi masu wuya: watsuwa a sararin ionosphere da troposphere a 6 m da 2 m, da tunkuɗa sigina daga wata (EME) daga 2 m zuwa sama. A qFT8 har yanzu ta gwaji ce:

  • Ƙananan hanyoyi: Kowace ƙaramar hanya lokaci ce (daƙiƙa 15, 30, 60, 120 ko 300) da tazarar sauti (A zuwa E), ana rubuta ta kamar 30A ko 60C. Lokaci mai tsawo ya fi ji; tazara mai faɗi ta fi jure sigina da watsuwa ko wata suka bazar. Dole tashoshin biyu su yi amfani da ƙaramar hanya ɗaya.
  • Saƙonni iri ɗaya da FT8: Q65 tana ɗauke da saƙonnin 77-bit iri ɗaya, don haka QSO na da matakai shida iri ɗaya kamar a FT8.
  • Haɗa matsakaici: Watsawa masu maimaituwa da suka yi rauni a fassara su ɗaya-ɗaya ana iya tara su har sai an fassara su.

Zaɓin ƙaramar hanya, bandodi da duk zaɓuɓɓuka an bayyana su a Saituna > Q65; jerin tashoshi a Q65 an bayyana shi a Shafin Tashoshi.

MSK144

An yi MSK144 don watsuwar taurarin wuta a 10 m, 6 m, 2 m da 70 cm. A qFT8 har yanzu ta gwaji ce:

  • Ping: Hanyar taurarin wuta tana tunkuɗa sigina na ɗan lokaci kaɗan kawai. Watsawa tana maimaita saƙonta tsawon lokacin gaba ɗaya, kuma duk ping da ya isa tsawo ya ɗauki kwafinsa guda ɗaya ana fassara shi.
  • Lokuta: Daƙiƙa 5, 10, 15 ko 30; daƙiƙa 15 shi ne zaɓin da aka saba.
  • Kullum a 1500 Hz: Kowace tasha tana watsawa a sautin 1500 Hz, da sautuka a 1000 da 2000 Hz.
  • Gajerun saƙonni: Ana iya aika rahotanni, RRR da 73 a matsayin gajerun saƙonni da suke shiga cikin ping mafi gajarta sosai.

An bayyana zaɓuɓɓuka a Saituna > MSK144; jerin tashoshi a MSK144 an bayyana shi a Shafin Tashoshi.

FST4

FST4 hanyar QSO ce ta sigina masu rauni don ƙananan bandodi (2200 m, 630 m, 160 m) da HF lokacin da sigina suka yi rauni sosai. A qFT8 har yanzu ta gwaji ce:

  • Lokuta: Daƙiƙa 15, 30, 60, 120, 300, 900 ko 1800. Yawan tsawon lokaci, yawan siraran sigina da jinta: daga kusan -21 dB a daƙiƙa 15 zuwa kusan -43 dB a daƙiƙa 1800.
  • Saƙonni iri ɗaya da FT8: FST4 tana ɗauke da saƙonnin 77-bit iri ɗaya, don haka QSO na da matakai shida iri ɗaya kamar a FT8, sai dai a hankali sosai.

An bayyana zaɓuɓɓuka a Saituna > FST4; jerin tashoshi a FST4 an bayyana shi a Shafin Tashoshi. Abokiyar fitilarta, FST4W, tana aiki kamar WSPR.

JT65 da JT9

JT65 da JT9 tsofaffin yanayoyin QSO ne na sigina masu rauni na minti ɗaya. Duka biyun na gwaji ne a qFT8:

  • Lokutan minti ɗaya: aikawa tana farawa daƙiƙa ɗaya bayan farkon minti na UTC mai lamba biyu-biyu ko mara biyu-biyu kuma tana ɗaukar kusan daƙiƙa 47 (JT65) ko 49 (JT9); kana zaɓar biyu-biyu ko mara biyu-biyu kamar a FT8.
  • Saƙonni: bit 72: CQ, alamomin kira biyu tare da locator, rahoto (-NN, R-NN), RRR, RR73, 73, ko rubutu na kyauta har haruffa 13. QSO yana tafiya kamar a FT8 (AUTO, jerin tashoshi, log). Ba za a iya aika alamar kira da ke buƙatar prefix da suffix duka biyu ba.
  • Ƙananan yanayoyin JT65: A (HF), B da C (VHF zuwa sama, nisan sautuka mafi faɗi), ana zaɓarsu kusa da yanayin a ƙaramin mashaya ko a Saituna; dole tashoshin biyu su yi amfani da iri ɗaya. JT9 shi ne JT9-1, faɗin 16 Hz kawai.

An bayyana zaɓuɓɓuka a Saituna > JT65 da Saituna > JT9.

JT4

JT4 yanayin QSO ne na sigina masu rauni na minti ɗaya na bandodin VHF, UHF da microwave (EME, beacons na microwave). A qFT8 na gwaji ne:

  • Lokutan minti ɗaya, kamar JT65: aikawa tana farawa daƙiƙa ɗaya bayan farkon minti na UTC mai lamba biyu-biyu ko mara biyu-biyu kuma tana ɗaukar kusan daƙiƙa 47.
  • Saƙonni: saƙonnin bit 72 iri ɗaya da na JT65 da JT9, da tafiyar QSO iri ɗaya da ta FT8 (AUTO, jerin tashoshi, log).
  • Ƙananan yanayoyi daga A zuwa G: sautuka 4, nisan 4.375 Hz a A kuma har ninki 72 a G (kusan 950 Hz), don watsuwar Doppler ta manyan bandodi. Ana amfani da A a 2 m da 70 cm, masu faɗi a bandodin microwave. Ana zaɓa kusa da yanayin a ƙaramin mashaya ko a Saituna; dole tashoshin biyu su yi amfani da iri ɗaya.

An bayyana zaɓuɓɓuka a Saituna > JT4.

↑ Koma zuwa Abubuwan da ke ciki

Fuskar qFT8 ga Mai Amfani

Manhajar qFT8 ta kasu kashi uku:

  1. Sandar ƙasa: Ana ganin ta koyaushe; ana duba da sarrafa watsawa da ita, da kuma sarrafa qFT8.
  2. Spectrogram: Ana ganin sa koyaushe; ana ganin saƙonnin da aka fassara, lokacin UTC da mitar da aka saita, kuma ana sarrafa bambancin mitar watsawa da shi.
  3. Yankin shafuka: Yana nuna ɗaya daga cikin shafuka huɗu (Tashoshi, Log na QSO, Taswirar duniya, da Saituna). Maɓallan da ke ƙarshen dama na sandar ƙasa ne ke zaɓar wanda ake nunawa.

Sassan da ke gaba suna bayyana dukkan waɗannan dalla-dalla.

Sandar ƙasa

Sandar ƙasa tana nuna muhimman bayanai: ko an shirya watsawa ko ba a shirya ba, ko qFT8 tana watsawa, ƙarfin watsawa, Standing Wave Ratio (SWR), saƙon da ake watsawa, jeri na yanzu da wanda aka zaɓa, mai zaɓar zango, da maɓallan zuwa shafuka daban-daban.

Ga sassan sandar ƙasa dalla-dalla:

  1. Sandar ci gaban zagaye: Sandar rawaya tana farawa a daƙiƙa 0 na zagaye (ƙarshen hagu) kuma tana ƙarewa a daƙiƙa 15 (ƙarshen dama).
  2. Alamar fara Tx: Ɗigon ja a ɓangaren hagu na sandar yana nuna lokacin fara Tx. Idan Tx yana ON (duba na #4) kuma jeri na yanzu shi ne wanda aka zaɓa (duba na #6), watsawa tana farawa a nan.
  3. Alamar lokacin fassara: Ɗigon fari a ɓangaren dama na sandar yana nuna lokacin fassara, wato lokacin da ake fassara saƙonni daga siginar da aka karɓa sannan a sarrafa su.
  4. The TX button: Can be disarmed (OFF) or armed (ON). It also shows when transmission is happening, and the transmit power and SWR; while transmitting, the power and the SWR are the average of the radio's last 2 seconds of readings, so they do not flicker. The button's words stay in English in every language. It reads NO TX / SDR while a receive-only SDR is the receiver, NO TX / I/Q while a QMX is in I/Q mode, NO TX / SWL in SWL mode and NO TX / RX while a receiver (marine VHF, airband, a beacon or time-signal receiver ...) has the radio: nothing can transmit then, and TX stays OFF afterwards until you press TX ON again. With the Ham features off it reads NO CALLSIGN on red; tapping it explains that transmitting needs an amateur licence, a callsign and the Ham features, and SAITA asks for your callsign and switches the Ham features on (TX stays OFF). In QMX firmwares above 1_04, this button will show "HOLD: TUNE". Holding the button will enable tuning mode and report on SWR live.
  5. The message selector: It shows the message selected to be sent or being sent (if that is the case, highlighted in yellow). In AUTO mode, it is managed automatically. In CALL mode it is also managed automatically by default, but it can be tapped on to select the next message to send manually. In FT8, FT4, FT2 and LQ8 the list ends with Rubutu kyauta...: type a short message of your own (in FT8 / FT4 / FT2 up to 13 characters, A-Z, 0-9, space and + - . / ?; an accented letter is sent as its plain letter; in LQ8 the editor shows how full the message is). A CQ or QSO message is refused there (pick it from the list). Picked while AUTO is selected, it switches to a manual CQ.
  6. Mai zaɓar jeri: Layin rawaya a saman maɓallan yana nuna jeri na yanzu (0 ko 1). Taɓa 0 ko 1 yana zaɓar wannan jeri domin watsawa.
  7. Mai zaɓar yanayi: Yana canza yanayi (an jera kowane yanayi a Mai zaɓar yanayi, a ƙasa). Canzawa daga yanayin dijital zuwa yanayin da ke da nasa allo (SSB, CW, RTTY, SSTV, da sauran waɗanda ke canza sandar ƙasa da shafin Tashoshi kamar yadda sassansu suka bayyana), komawa, ko tsakaninsu yana mayar da TX zuwa OFF: sake kunna shi idan ka shirya. Canzawa tsakanin yanayin dijital yana barin shi yadda yake.
  8. Mai zaɓar bandi: Yana buɗe jerin bandodi tare da mitocinsu kuma, idan an haɗa transceiver, yana canza bandi a cikin transceiver (duba Mai zaɓar bandi, a ƙasa).
  9. Maɓallin shafin Tashoshi: Yana buɗe shafin da yake jera dukkan tashoshin da ka ji ko za ka iya tuntuɓa.
  10. Maɓallin shafin Log na QSO: Yana buɗe shafin da yake jera dukkan QSO da ka yi. Za a sa ɗigon rawaya a wannan maɓalli idan kana da QSO da ba a loda zuwa QRZ ba.
  11. Maɓallin shafin Taswirar duniya: Yana buɗe shafin da yake nuna taswirar duniya.
  12. Maɓallin shafin Saituna: Yana buɗe shafin saita qFT8.
↑ Koma zuwa Abubuwan da ke ciki

Spectrogram

Spectrogram yana nuna kololuwar sigina, da waterfall mai sigina da saƙonnin da ke shigowa, sigina da saƙonnin da ke fita, alamun canjin jeri masu lokacin UTC da lokacin fassara, da bambancin mitar da aka zaɓa domin watsawa.

Ga sassan spectrogram dalla-dalla:

  1. Waterfall: Yana nuna tarihin siginar da aka karɓa a ɗan lokacin da ya gabata. Taɓa shi yana canza bambancin mitar da aka zaɓa.
  2. Kololuwar sigina na ainihin lokaci: Suna nuna ƙarfin sigina na yanzu a dukkan mitoci. Taɓa wurin yana buɗe akwati domin shigar da ainihin bambancin mita.
  3. Masu raba jeri: Waɗannan layukan ja na kwance suna raba zagaye, kuma suna ɗauke da lokacin UTC da lokacin da aka ɗauka wajen fassara.
  4. Bambancin mitar da aka zaɓa: Wannan layin rawaya na tsaye a kan spectrogram yana nuna bambancin mitar da aka zaɓa domin watsawa.
  5. Saƙonnin da ke shigowa, da launuka daban-daban:
    • Kore: Saƙonnin CQ.
    • Cyan: Saƙonni tsakanin wasu tashoshi.
    • Rawaya: Saƙonnin da ka watsa.
    • Orange: Saƙonnin da aka aiko maka.
  6. Lokacin UTC da jeri na yanzu: Ana nuna su a saman waterfall.
  7. Mita da zangon da aka saita: Mita da zangon da transceiver yake a kai yanzu.
  8. Layin mita: Yana nuna ma'aunin mita a Hz a cikin zangon da aka zaɓa.
↑ Koma zuwa Abubuwan da ke ciki

Shafin Tashoshi

Shafin Tashoshi shi ne cibiyar duba abin da ke faruwa a zango da sarrafa qFT8. A sama akwai kayan tacewa da na atomatik, sannan jerin tashoshin da ke aiki.

Ga sassan shafin Tashoshi dalla-dalla:

  1. Maɓallin yanayin kira na AUTO: Kunna "AUTO" zai yi ƙoƙarin kiran tashar da ta fi dacewa bisa sharuɗɗan da mai amfani ya zaɓa.
  2. Maɓallin yanayin CALL da hannu: Kunna "CALL" ga wata tasha zai ci gaba da kiran wannan tasha har a sami QSO, sannan ya koma "AUTO".
  3. Manufa: Yana saita sharuɗɗan yanayin AUTO:
    • Kowa: Kowace alamar kira da ba ka da QSO da ita tukuna.
    • Kusa: A fifita tashoshin da suka fi kusa, bisa nisan grid na Maidenhead.
    • Nesa: A fifita tashoshin da suka fi nisa, bisa nisan grid na Maidenhead.
    • Karfin sigina: A fifita tashoshin da suka fi ƙarfin sigina (SNR).
    • Kira CQ: A kira CQ kawai, a amsa kowace tashar da ta kira tasharka.
    • Mai neman POTA: A fifita tashoshin Parks on the Air.
    • Mai neman SOTA: A fifita tashoshin Summits on the Air.
    • Mai neman POTA + SOTA: A fifita tashoshin Parks on the Air da Summits on the Air duka, sannan tashoshin da ba ɗaya daga cikinsu ba, bisa SNR.
    • DXCC: A fifita sabbin ƙasashe/entities.
    • Grid: A fifita sabbin grid na Maidenhead.
    • Yankin ITU: A fifita sabbin yankunan ITU.
    • Yankin CQ: A fifita sabbin yankunan CQ.
    • I/C/D/G: A fifita yankin ITU da farko, sannan yankin CQ, sannan DXCC, sannan Grid.
    A kowane hali, ana jera tashoshin da suka cika sharaɗi ɗaya bisa ƙarfin sigina. Misali, Kowa zai fifita sabbin alamomin kira da farko, amma a cikinsu sigina mafi ƙarfi za su kasance a saman jerin. Tashoshin da suka cika ka'idojin manufa koyaushe ana lissafa su da farko; ɗan rata mai layin "Tashoshin da ke ƙasa ba su cika sharuɗɗan manufa ba" yana raba su da sauran. Idan babu wata tasha da ta cika ka'idojin, ana nuna layin "Babu tashar da ke manufa – duba su a ƙasa" a saman jerin.
  4. Sau nawa: Sau nawa za a tuntuɓi wannan manufa:
    • sau ɗaya: Sau ɗaya a rayuwa.
    • kullum: Sau ɗaya a kowace ranar UTC.
    • mako-mako: Sau ɗaya a kowane kwana 7 masu bin juna.
    • wata-wata: Sau ɗaya a kowane wata mai bin juna.
    • shekara-shekara: Sau ɗaya a kowace shekara mai bin juna.
  5. Ƙa'ida: Yadda ake amfani da waɗannan iyakoki:
    • kowace alamar kira
    • a band daban-daban
    • a yanayi daban-daban
    • har sai an tabbatar da yankin
    • kowace band + har yankin
    • kowane yanayi + har yankin
    • a band ko yanayi daban-daban
    • kowace band/yanayi + har yankin
  6. Yanayin Leƙen asiri: Idan an kunna, qFT8 za ta kuma yi ƙoƙarin kiran tashoshin da ba ka ji suna kiran CQ ba tukuna (masu alamar DIRECT). Wannan yana da amfani domin samun ƙarin tashoshi da shiga pile-up.
  7. Yanayin Mai aiki: Idan an kunna, qFT8 za ta kuma yi ƙoƙarin kiran tashoshin da suke cikin QSO da wani (masu alamar BUSY). Wannan yana da amfani domin shiga pile-up.
  8. Maɓallin +: Kira kowace tasha da hannu ta shigar da alamar kiranta.
  9. Bayanin tasha: Yana nuna cikakken halin kowace tashar da aka fassara.

Kowace tasha tana da layuka huɗu masu bayani:

Layi na farko yana nuna bayanin tasha (daga hagu zuwa dama):

  • Matsayin QSO: Yana nuna tarihin QSO ɗinka da wannan tasha (✗ = babu QSO, ✓ = an rubuta a na'ura, ★ = an tabbatar ta QRZ ko LoTW).
  • Alama: Alamar kiran tashar.
  • DXCC: Ƙasa ko entity da alamar kiran wannan tasha take ciki.
  • Grid: Grid na Maidenhead da wannan tasha take bayarwa.
  • Yankin CQ: Yankin CQ da alamar kiran wannan tasha take ciki.
  • Yankin ITU: Yankin ITU da alamar kiran wannan tasha take ciki.
  • Band: Zangon da aka ji tashar a ƙarshe.

Layi na biyu yana nuna halin sadarwa (daga hagu zuwa dama):

  • Hali: Matakin sadarwa na yanzu:
    • DIRECT: An ji ta kuma da alama ba ta da aiki yanzu, amma ba ta taɓa kiran CQ ba.
    • 1/6 CQ: An ji ta tana kiran CQ kuma da alama ba ta da aiki yanzu.
    • 2/6 CALL: Ta kira ka.
    • 3/6 RPT: Ta aiko maka da rahoton sigina.
    • 4/6 RRPT: Ta amsa rahotonka da nata.
    • 5/6 RR73: Ta tabbatar da rahotonka da RR73.
    • 6/6 DONE: QSO ya cika (an yi musayar 73).
    • BUSY: An ji ta cikin QSO da wata tasha.
    • GONE: Ba a ji ta ba a zagaye biyu-uku na ƙarshe.
    • TOO LONG: Alamar kiranka da tata sun yi tsawo sosai tare, ba za a iya kafa sadarwa ba bisa iyakokin FT8.
  • Karfin sigina: Ƙarfi a dB (yana nuna "????" idan ba a ji ta kwanan nan ba).
  • Jeri: Jeri na ƙarshe da aka ji wannan tasha tana watsawa a ciki (0 ko 1).
  • Bambancin lokaci (Δt): Bambancin daidaita lokaci, a daƙiƙa, tsakanin lokacinta da naka.
  • Nisa: Kimanin nisa tsakanin grid na tasharta da na tasharka.

Layi na uku da na huɗu suna nuna musayar saƙonni da wannan tasha: lokacin UTC, bambancin mita, da rubutun saƙo (da RX: a farko ga saƙonnin da aka karɓa, ko TX: ga waɗanda aka watsa). Bisa yanayin nuna Tashoshi da aka saita a Saituna → Aikace-aikace → Tsarin mai amfani (na asali shi ne A taƙaice - Rx na ƙarshe, da zaɓuɓɓukan A taƙaice - Rx/Tx na ƙarshe mai canzawa, A taƙaice - Rx/Tx na ƙarshe, da A faɗaɗe - Dukansu Rx da Tx na ƙarshe), katin tasha yana nuna layuka uku, ko layuka huɗu masu saƙonnin Rx da Tx duka.

Muhimman alamun da za ka lura da su:

  • Saƙon Rx mai launin coral (UTC, bambancin mita & rubutu): An aiko wani saƙon Rx daga wannan tasha kai tsaye zuwa tasharka / alamar kiranka (har da alamomin kira masu ƙari kamar /P da alamomin kira masu hash) a zagayen yanzu ko wanda ya gabata. Wannan alama ce nan take cewa tashar ta kira ka ko ta amsa watsawarka yanzun nan. Ba a taɓa haskaka kiran CQ na kowa da saƙonnin da aka aika wa wasu tashoshi, kuma rubutun yana komawa launin toka da kansa idan saƙon ya girmi zagayen da ya gabata. Saƙonnin da aka watsa (TX:) koyaushe launin toka ne.
  • Alamar kira ja: Yanayin AUTO ya wuce iyakar sake kira ga wannan tasha. Za a bar ta na ɗan lokaci sai dai idan ka danna "CALL" domin sake saita ta da hannu.
  • Alamar kira mai launin purple: Wannan alamar kira manufa ce bisa sharuɗɗanka na yanzu.
  • Alamar kira mai launin toka: Alamar kiran ta dace da prefixes ɗin da ka ce a ware (waɗanda aka raba da waƙafi). Yanayin AUTO ba zai taɓa nufar wannan tasha ba.
  • Yankin da aka haskaka da purple: Wannan yanki/entity (DXCC, Grid, Yankin CQ, Yankin ITU) manufa ce bisa sharuɗɗanka na yanzu.
  • Lambar jeri ja: Tashar tana kan jeri ɗaya da wanda ka zaɓa, wanda yake kawo karo. Danna "CALL" zai canza jerinka ta atomatik.
  • Zango ja: An ji tashar a ƙarshe a wani zango daban.

Ana jera tashoshi ta atomatik bisa muhimmancinsu. Canza sharuɗɗan manufa ko iyakoki zai sake jera su nan take.

Tsarin jerin

Daga saman jerin zuwa ƙasa:

  1. Tashoshin da kuka gama QSO da su yanzu (DONE).
  2. Tashar da kuka zaɓa da CALL.
  3. Tashoshin da ke cikin QSO da ku, waɗanda suka fi ci gaba da farko: masu jiran 73 ɗinku, sannan waɗanda suka aiko muku rahotonsu, sannan waɗanda suka kira ku.
  4. Tashoshi a bandon ku da suka cika sharuɗɗan manufa, mafi dacewa da farko. Idan dacewa ɗaya ce, sigina mafi ƙarfi da farko; da manufar nisa, nisa ne ke yanke hukunci da farko.
  5. Layin “Tashoshin da ke ƙasa ba su cika sharuɗɗan manufa ba”, sannan duk sauran tashoshi, a tsari ɗaya.
  6. Tashoshin da ba a ƙara jinsu (GONE), kuma a ƙasan ƙasa tashoshin da alamar kiransu da taku ba za su shiga saƙo ɗaya tare ba (TOO LONG).

A cikin kowane rukuni, tashoshin da aka ji su na ƙarshe a wani bando suna zuwa bayan na bandon ku. Ba a nuna tashoshin da aka ji a wata hanya; suna dawowa a karon farko da aka ji su a hanyar yanzu. Tashoshin da kuka ƙara da maɓallin + kullum suna zama a jerin. Layin da ke tsakanin manufofi da sauran tashoshi iri ɗaya ne a waya, kwamfuta, iPhone da na'urar sarrafa ta yanar gizo; lakabi ne kawai, taɓa shi ba ya yin komai.

Shafin Tashoshi a kowace hanyar QSO

FT8, FT4, FT2, LQ8, Q65, MSK144 da FST4 duka suna amfani da wannan shafi ɗaya: sarrafawa iri ɗaya a sama, layukan tashoshi iri ɗaya da tsari ɗaya. Jerin yana nuna tashoshin da aka ji a hanyar da kuke amfani da ita kawai. Abin da ke canzawa daga hanya zuwa hanya:

FT8
Lokaci 15 s
Matakan QSO 1/6 – 6/6
FT4
Lokaci 7.5 s
Matakan QSO 1/6 – 6/6
FT2
Lokaci 3.75 s
Matakan QSO 1/6 – 6/6
LQ8
Lokaci 15 s
Matakan QSO 1/4 – 4/4
Q65
Lokaci 15 – 300 s
Matakan QSO 1/6 – 6/6
Ana zaɓa a sandar ƙasa ƙaramar hanya (30A ...)
MSK144
Lokaci 5 – 30 s
Matakan QSO 1/6 – 6/6
Ana zaɓa a sandar ƙasa lokaci (15s ...)
FST4
Lokaci 15 – 1800 s
Matakan QSO 1/6 – 6/6
Ana zaɓa a sandar ƙasa lokaci

Mai zaɓi kusa da hanya a sandar ƙasa yana a waya, kwamfuta, iPhone da na'urar sarrafa ta yanar gizo. Jeri 0 da 1 su ne lokuta masu lamba biyu-biyu da marasa biyu na hanyar, komai tsawonsu. Da lokuta masu tsawo, ana ɗaukar tasha a matsayin wadda ba a ƙara ji (GONE) sai bayan adadin lokuta shiru masu dacewa.

FT4 / FT2: Komai yana aiki kamar a FT8, sai dai da sauri. Kowane lokaci gajere ne, don haka QSO na tafiya da sauri: a FT2 QSO gaba ɗaya na iya ƙarewa ƙasa da minti ɗaya sosai.

LQ8: QSO na LQ8 na da matakai huɗu maimakon shida, kuma ginshiƙin matsayi yana nuna su:

  • 1/4 CQ: An ji ta tana kiran CQ, misali CQ DL1XYZ JO62.
  • 2/4 CALL: Ta kira ku, da lokaitanta da rahotonku a saƙo ɗaya, misali HB9IPH DL1XYZ JO62 -11.
  • 3/4 RPT73: Ta aiko rahotonta tare da tabbatarwa; ana nuna shi kamar HB9IPH DL1XYZ R-11.
  • 4/4 DONE: QSO ya kammala (an musanya 73).

Babu matakin rahoto daban kuma babu RR73 a LQ8: haka aka yi yarjejeniyar. DIRECT, BUSY, GONE da TOO LONG ma'anarsu ɗaya ce da a FT8. Idan kun danna CALL a tashar da ba za a iya aika alamar kiranta a LQ8 tare da taku ba, qFT8 zai faɗi haka kuma ba zai kira ba.

Q65: Zaɓi ƙaramar hanya kusa da hanya a sandar ƙasa kafin ku kira; ba a fassara tashoshin da ke amfani da wata ƙaramar hanya. Da ƙananan hanyoyi masu tsawo, kowane matakin QSO yana ɗaukar lokaci gaba ɗaya (har zuwa minti 5), don haka QSO na daɗewa sosai, kuma layukan tashoshi suna canzawa sau ɗaya a kowane lokaci. Idan haɗa matsakaici a kunne, tasha na iya bayyana sai bayan an tara watsawarta da dama. Ƙananan hanyoyi masu tsawo suna buƙatar Iyakar watsawa ba tsayawa mai girma isasshe.

MSK144: Zaɓi lokaci kusa da hanya a sandar ƙasa. Tasha tana bayyana idan aka fassara ɗaya daga cikin ping ɗinta, don haka layuka suna zuwa suna tafiya tare da taurarin wuta; ƙarfin siginarta na ping ɗin ne, kuma Δt ɗinta lokacin ping ɗin ne a cikin lokacin, ba kuskuren agogo ba. Ana nuna gajeren saƙo da alamun kira biyu, kamar dogon salo, misali K1ABC W9XYZ R+03; tashoshin biyu na wannan QSO ne kaɗai za su iya karanta shi. Idan gajerun saƙonni a kunne a Saituna, qFT8 yana aika rahotanninku, RRR da 73 haka; CQ ɗinku, kiranku da RR73 ɗinku kullum suna fita a matsayin saƙonni na yau da kullum. Duk tashoshi suna watsawa a 1500 Hz, don haka gurbin tasha ba ya faɗin abu mai yawa.

FST4: Zaɓi lokaci kusa da hanya a sandar ƙasa; dole ya zama ɗaya da na ɗayan tashar. Da lokuta na daƙiƙa 900 ko 1800, QSO ɗaya na iya ɗaukar awa ɗaya ko fiye, kuma kowace watsawarku tana riƙe rediyo a watsawa har zuwa minti 30: saita Iyakar watsawa ba tsayawa don ya yarda da haka, in ba haka ba qFT8 ba zai watsa ba.

Saitunan AUTO, manufofi da sake gwadawa iri ɗaya ne a duk waɗannan hanyoyi; duba Aiki na atomatik (AUTO).

↑ Koma zuwa Abubuwan da ke ciki

Mai zaɓar yanayi

Maɓallin da ke da sunan yanayi a sandar ƙasa yana buɗe mai zaɓar yanayi. A sama akwai shafuka biyu, HAM da SWL, waɗanda ke tsayawa a wurinsu yayin da jerin ke gungurawa, kuma a damansu maɓallin PROBE (duba a ƙasa). Mai zaɓen yana buɗewa a shafin da ka yi amfani da shi na ƙarshe, yana riƙe girmansa idan ka canza shafi (shafi mafi tsawo yana gungurawa a cikinsa) kuma yana rufewa idan ka taɓa wajensa. Idan fasalolin Ham kawai ke kunne babu layin shafuka (sai HAM kawai); idan fasalolin SWL na kunne shafukan biyu suna bayyana, kuma idan fasalolin Ham a kashe shafin HAM yana nan, don karɓa kawai.

  • HAM: sassa masu lamba na maɓallai daidai-wa-daida waɗanda ke daidaita da faɗin allo da girman rubutu: sigina mara ƙarfi, fitilu (beacons), madannai da hira, rediyo na gargajiya, hotuna, murya ta dijital da packet. Yanayin da aka fi amfani da su (FT8, LQ8, FT4, FT2, WSPR, JS8, RTTY, JTTY, SSB, CW, SSTV da Packet (APRS)) suna cikin akwati mai ɗan haske da suna mai kauri; yanayin da ake amfani da shi yana riƙe kamanninsa. Yanayin gwaji suna faɗin haka a ƙaramin layi na biyu. Layi na biyu ƙarami yana nuna kalmar gwaji da ja. Akwatin zaɓi na Gwaji kusa da Nuna duka (a kashe da farko, ana adana shi a saitunan) shi ke yanke shawarar ko za a jera su kwata-kwata: a kashe, babu yanayin gwaji, babu sashe maras komai, babu shafin SWL idan ba shi da komai, babu kuma PROBE; a kunne, dukansu. Yanayin da kake amfani da shi yana nan a jerin har sai ka bar shi. Cikin na gwaji akwai AM, airband, marine VHF, muryar M17, DMR, D-STAR, Fusion da bayanan M17, da kowane mai karɓar SWL.
  • SWL: masu karɓa, maɓalli ɗaya kowanne, tare da abin da suke fassarawa a ƙarƙashin suna: yaɗa labarai, ayyukan HF da siginonin lokaci, sama da teku, yanayi, balan-balan da tauraron ɗan adam, IoT da mesh. An bayyana su a Amfani da qFT8 don SWL.
  • Families of modes have one button: PSK (PSK31, PSK63, PSK125, PSK250, QPSK31, QPSK63) and Iyalin MFSK (MFSK, THOR, DominoEX, Throb); Olivia, Contestia, MT63 and the other keyboard modes too. The variant is picked in the bottom bar beside the mode, as the sub-modes of JT65 are (A, B, C): the closed picker shows short names (31, Q63, M16, TH22, 8/250, 32/1K) and the open one the full names. Each family remembers the variant you used last, and every variant is still logged by its own name.
  • A mode your radio cannot do is hidden. The check box Nuna yanayin da rediyona ba ta goyon baya at the bottom shows them greyed; tapping one says why (for example “Your radio has no I/Q output”).
  • A mode that has a local-law notice where you are (by your callsign or your location) has a reddish button with a wider outline (a lighter red for the most used modes, a dark red when your radio cannot do it either). Tapping it shows the notice first, with An ba ni izinin karɓar wannan a nan and BACK; then, if your radio cannot do it, why. Each notice comes once per session.
