Where the mode is not allowed at all (see Listening and the law) the button is still listed in red, the notice has only BACK and the mode does not start.

giya da ke dama da take a dama da taken yana buɗe Zaɓin yanayi (haka ma a Settings > MODES): yi alama a kan yanayin da kake so a jerin, tare da DUKA da BABU ga kowane sashe. Mai zaɓe zai nuna yanayin da aka yi wa alama kawai; sashe ko shafi ba tare da alama ba yana ɓacewa, kuma yanayin da ake amfani da shi koyaushe yana cikin jerin. A farko an yi wa komai alama; SAKE SAITA ZUWA NA ASALI yana sake yi wa komai alama. Akwatin Gwaji yana nan yana aiki.

PROBE (mode detector, experimental; ana jera shi idan akwatin Gwaji yana kunne) opens the free AUTO MODE DETECTOR, whatever is switched on (the bottom bar then reads PROBE). It names the signals on the spectrogram as you watch; the ones qFT8 decoded get a check mark. Tap a label and qFT8 asks “Go to FT8 on 14075.250 kHz?” (the mode and the frequency of that signal): YES switches to it. It is experimental: it can be wrong, and it never names room noise, voices, music or hum. In Settings > Spectrogram the check box Sanya sunayen sauran sigina a kan spectrogram (mai gano yanayi) lets it keep listening while you operate.

Mai zaɓar bandi

The band button of the bottom bar opens the band selector: the bands one per row, each with the frequencies of the current mode (for example FT8 on 20 m: 14.074 Standard and 14.090 DXpedition; on 6 m: 50.313 and 50.323 Alternate). Tap a frequency to go there; the one you pick is remembered for each mode and band. A band in red has no frequency in this mode (60 m in FT4, for example) and cannot be picked. Below the bands are your custom bands A to E (an empty one opens the settings to fill it), a box for any frequency (in FT8 and the other band modes it becomes that band's frequency; in SSB, CW, RTTY and SSTV it tunes the radio), a gear to the band settings and the check box Ajiye rediyo a mitar da aka zaɓa: on, a radio turned by hand is put back on the frequency you chose; off, qFT8 follows the radio. It closes when you tap outside it. In marine VHF, airband, the broadcast radio and the signals receivers the button only shows the receiver's frequency (for example 156.8) and does not open the list; pick an amateur mode to change band. In the web remote each band of the list shows the same frequency buttons.

Shafin Log na QSO

Shafin log yana nuna QSO da aka samu.

Ga sassan shafin Log na QSO dalla-dalla:

  1. Matatar rubutun alamar kira: Tana tace QSO bisa ko alamar kira tana ɗauke da rubutun da aka shigar.
  2. Matatar hali: Matatar QSO da take nuna:
    • QSO / SWL: once something has been heard, or the SWL features are on, the top of the log has one dropdown, QSO / SWL, in the colour of the log you are looking at (QSO blue, SWL red). QSO, your own log, is the default; SWL shows what you heard, newest first, in its place. The choice is remembered. The SWL page has the same filter bar (Ya ƙunshi, Nuna, na baya-bayan nan), which searches the whole SWL log, and a tap on an entry opens it to edit or delete (nothing is ever deleted on eQSL or any online service). Beside the + a file button asks IMPORT QSO LOG / EXPORT QSO LOG, or IMPORT SWL LOG / EXPORT SWL LOG, for the log shown (the same as Settings > Log files; the web remote only exports).
    • Duka: Tana nuna dukkan QSO da aka rubuta.
    • An tabbatar: Tana nuna QSO da aka tabbatar kawai.
    • Mai harbi POTA / Mai harbi SOTA: Tana nuna QSO da ke da wurin shakatawa ko kololuwa.
    • Ba a tabbatar ba: Tana nuna QSO da ba a tabbatar ba tukuna.
    • Ba a ɗora ba: Tana nuna QSO da ba a loda ba tukuna (duba Yaushe ake ɗaukar QSO a matsayin an loda?).
    • Kwafi: Tana nuna QSO da suka bayyana fiye da sau ɗaya a cikin log.
    • An ƙi: Tana nuna QSO da littafin rajista na kan layi ya ƙi.
    • Wata alamar kira: Tana nuna QSO da aka rubuta da alamar kira daban da wadda aka saita yanzu.
  3. Matatar na kwanan nan: Tana nuna QSO 100 na ƙarshe kawai masu sharuɗɗan da aka bayar.
  4. Jerin QSO: Yana nuna QSO da waɗannan ginshiƙai (daga hagu zuwa dama):
    • QRZ: Yana nuna tarihinka da wannan tasha:
      • ✗: Babu wani QSO da aka rubuta.
      • ✓: An rubuta a na'ura / Ana jiran lodawa.
      • ✓🕒: An loda zuwa LoTW, ana jiran tabbatarwa.
      • ✓: An loda zuwa QRZ.
      • ★: An tabbatar a QRZ ko LoTW (ga QSO da aka loda zuwa QRZ) ko da hannu.
      • ⟳ / ♻️: Kibiya mai zagaye: QRZ ya ce kwafi ne (ana nunawa ne kawai idan akwai ainihin kwafi a log; QSO guda ɗaya da suke kan QRZ tuni ana yi musu alamar an loda da koren tick ta atomatik).
      • ❕: Alamar kiran tasha ba ta dace ba, an riga an loda.
      • ⚠️: Alamar kiran tasha ba ta dace ba, ba a loda ba.
      • ⛔: An ƙi.
    • Alama: Alamar kiran tashar.
    • DXCC: Ƙasa ko entity da alamar kiran wannan tasha take ciki.
    • Grid: Grid na Maidenhead da wannan tasha take bayarwa.
    • Yankin CQ: Yankin CQ da alamar kiran wannan tasha take ciki.
    • Yankin ITU: Yankin ITU da alamar kiran wannan tasha take ciki.
    • Band: Zangon da aka ji tashar a ƙarshe.

Muhimman alamun da za ka lura da su:

  • Rubutu rawaya a ƙarƙashin alamar kira: Wannan QSO na wata alamar kira ne daban da wadda aka saita.
  • Yankin da aka haskaka da kore (DXCC, Grid, Yankin ITU, Yankin CQ): Wannan shi ne QSO na farko da ba a tabbatar ba a log ɗinka na wannan yanki.
  • Yankin da aka haskaka da rawaya (DXCC, Grid, Yankin ITU, Yankin CQ): Wannan shi ne QSO na farko da aka tabbatar a log ɗinka na wannan yanki.

Gyaran QSO: Taɓa kowane QSO a jerin yana buɗe editan QSO. A can ana iya gyara kowane filin QSO, ana iya sa masa alamar an tabbatar ko ba a tabbatar ba da hannu, a sake loda shi, ko a goge shi. Goge QSO yana goge shi a manhaja kawai, ba a QRZ ko LoTW ba, kuma ba a iya dawo da shi sai dai idan ka ajiye kwafin fayil na log da ka fitar, ko an loda shi zuwa QRZ.

Rubutawa da hannu da zaɓi: Maɓallin + yana buɗe log na hannu, domin rubuta QSO da ka yi da kanka. Dogon danna a kan QSO yana fara zaɓin QSO da dama.

↑ Koma zuwa Abubuwan da ke ciki

Shafin Taswirar duniya

Shafin Taswirar duniya yana nuna taswirar duniya ta ainihin lokaci wadda ake iya motsawa. Tana nuna wuraren tashoshi, saƙonnin da aka fassara, QSO da ka samu, da yanayin propagation na rana.

Ga sassa da fasalolin shafin Taswirar duniya dalla-dalla:

  1. Taswira & kayan sarrafawa:
    • Motsawa: Ja domin motsa taswira a hankali a duk duniya; tana ci gaba a kwance ta antimeridian. Matse da yatsu biyu domin ƙara girma ko rage shi.
    • Salon taswira: Zaɓi tsakanin salo huɗu (Bright Future, Console, Earth, da Midnight Navy) daga sandar kayan aiki ta sama.
    • Coordinates na HUD: Yana nuna coordinates (latitude, longitude) da lokata na grid na Maidenhead na wurin da ka taɓa, a ainihin lokaci.
  2. Sandar kayan aiki ta hagu (kunna/kashe overlay & bayanai):
    • INFO: Yana kunna ko kashe katunan bayanai a kan allo (lokaci, layin rana da dare, wurin da rana take a tsaye, da coordinates na tsakiya).
    • MSGs: Yana kunna ko kashe hanyoyin saƙonni da alamominsu: orange ga saƙonnin da aka karɓa (da aka aiko wa tasharka) da rawaya ga saƙonnin da aka aika (daga tasharka). Taɓa tsakiyar kibiya yana buɗe rubutun saƙon.
    • QSOs: Yana kunna ko kashe alamun QSO na kwanan nan na wannan zama (amber ga wanda aka rubuta, emerald ga wanda aka tabbatar). Idan an kashe MSGs da SPOT duka biyu, siririn layi yana haɗa tasharka da kowace tashar QSO a taswira (ba kibiya), don ka ga da kallo ɗaya inda QSO-n ka suka je; hoton taswirar da aka raba shi ma yana nuna waɗannan layukan.
    • SPOT: Yana kunna ko kashe spots na saƙonnin da tasharka take ji a zango yanzu (cyan ga RX/CQ) da masu rahoton siginarka a PSKReporter / WSPRnet (jajayen lu'u-lu'u).
    • SOTA: Yana kunna ko kashe lambobin kololuwa na Summits On The Air da masu kunna kololuwa da ke aiki (⛰️).
    • POTA: Yana kunna ko kashe lambobin wuraren shakatawa na Parks On The Air da masu kunna wuraren da ke aiki (🌲).
    • Band: Yana nuna propagation na zango daga rahotannin intanet, da yankin da ba a ji kusa da mai amfani. Ana sabunta shi a ainihin lokaci idan aka canza zango kuma akwai intanet.

    Maɓallan SOTA, POTA, STATS, da SPOT suna iya yin ƙyalli; wannan yana nufin ba a iya sauke sabuntawa ba.

  3. Sandar kayan aiki ta dama (nasarori & iyakoki):
    • STATS: Yana kunna ko kashe allon ƙididdiga, mai nuna jimillar wanda aka rubuta da wanda aka tabbatar na layer ɗin da ke aiki.
    • DXCC: Yana nuna iyakokin ƙasashe da yankuna, masu launi bisa halin log ɗinka (kore ga wanda aka rubuta/ba a tabbatar ba, rawaya ga wanda aka tabbatar). Ƙananan tsibirai da ƙananan entities suna bayyana a matsayin alamomi.
    • GRID: Grid na Maidenhead na duniya (fields da grid na haruffa 4), masu launin nasara.
    • ITU: Yana ɗora yankunan rediyo 90 na ITU, masu launin nasara.
    • CQ: Yana ɗora yankunan WAZ 40 na mujallar CQ, masu launin nasara.
  4. SAT da maɓallin kamfas: tauraron dan Adam a taswira da kamfas ɗin taswira suna da nasu sashe, Tauraron dan Adam da kamfas ɗin taswira.
  5. Katin bayanin entity & kira cikin sauri:

    Taɓa kowace tasha, spot, wurin shakatawa, ko kololuwa yana buɗe katin bayani mai nuna:

    • Alamar kira ko sunan lamba, entity na DXCC, da sunan wurin shakatawa/kololuwa.
    • Rubutun saƙon da aka fassara, mita, zango, da yanayi.
    • Ƙarfin sigina (SNR) ko rahoton sigina (RST).
    • Lokata na grid, nisa (km / mi), alkibla (°), da lokacin da ya wuce.
    • Maɓallin CALL: Fara tuntuɓar tashar nan take (yana canza zango da yanayi ta atomatik idan ana buƙata).
↑ Koma zuwa Abubuwan da ke ciki

Shafin Saituna

Shafin saituna yana ba da damar saita qFT8.

Babban shafin Saituna, daga sama zuwa ƙasa:

  • Alama: Alamar kiranka ta rediyon amateur (wajibi ce domin watsawa). Dole ta dace da lasisinka.
  • Mai canza CQ: Rubutu na zaɓi da ake ƙarawa a kiran CQ ɗinka (misali "SOTA").
  • Wuri da Lokaci: locator ɗin Maidenhead naka da gyaran agogo, kowanne da maɓallin SYNC; gear ɗin da ke kusa da su yana buɗe LOKACI DA WURI (a ƙasa).
  • Rediyo / Eriya: Rediyo da eriya da kake aiki da su. Kowane QSO da ka rubuta yana adana su (ADIF MY_RIG / MY_ANTENNA), kuma PSKReporter yana karɓar su tare da spots ɗinka. Idan Rediyo babu komai, ana amfani da sunan rediyon da aka haɗa.
  • id ɗin SWL (idan siffofin SWL suna kunne): lambar shaidar mai sauraronku, da ake rubutawa a cikin log ɗin SWL; idan babu komai yana nufin "SWL".
  • Saitunan aikace-aikace: shafi mai, a wannan tsari: Aikace-aikace (yanayin na'ura mai jinkiri / ƙarancin baturi kuma, a kwamfuta, tabbatarwa kafin rufewa), Fasaloli (Fasalolin Ham, fasalolin SWL da Lambobin yabo; a waya kuma Haɗin rediyo, Rajista da Taswirar duniya), Fuskar mai amfani (fuskantarwa, launuka, abin da jerin ke nunawa, girman fuskar mai amfani da rubutu, Spectrogram da Taswirar duniya), Log da Ci gaba da aiki idan an rage / allo a kashe.
  • Na'ura: Ana amfani da shi domin saitawa da zaɓar transceiver ɗinka (misali QMX, QDX, Kenwood TS-480 / TS-590 / TS-2000 series, ko VOX/sauti kawai) da bayanan haɗi. A ƙarƙashinsa:
    • Ɗauka cewa ƙarfin watsawa N W ne: ga rediyo da ba sa bayar da ƙarfinsu, watts ɗin da ake rubutawa a log. Ba ya taɓa canza ƙarfin rediyon.
    • Dakatar da watsawa idan SWR ya wuce: da rediyo mai bayar da SWR, qFT8 yana daina aikawa idan ya wuce ƙimar da ka zaɓa. Yana kuma mayar da TX zuwa OFF: sake danna TX ON bayan ka gyara eriya.
    • TSARIN NA'URA: zaɓi na'urar sauti, saita ƙarar aikawa da iyakokin VOX, kuma saita yadda ake kunna rediyo don aikawa (a ƙasa).
    • Lasifika da makirufo: makirufo da lasifikarka ko belun kunne naka, don SSB da CW (duba Lasifika da makirufo, a ƙasa kai tsaye).
    • Samar da sautin tare da ƙarar da ƙarar na'ura yayin aikawa: saituna biyun nan iri ɗaya da na DEVICE CONFIGURATION, an sake nuna su a nan.
    • KUNSURIN CAT: rubuta umarni ga rediyo da kanka (duba Allon umarnin CAT).
    • Yanayin I/Q (fadin bakan) (experimental, while a QMX is the device): the radio sends its raw I and Q over USB (48 kHz) and qFT8 tunes inside that span, for the spectrum and every decoder. Transmitting is refused while it is on (the TX button reads NO TX / I/Q); if the radio stays in audio mode, its firmware may not have I/Q mode.
    • Rediyon hanyar sadarwa (experimental, in DEVICE CONFIGURATION; the web remote shows it read only): choosing Rediyon hanyar sadarwa (na gwaji) in Haɗin rediyo uses a radio reached over the network with its own protocol, with no cable and no sound card: TCI (the SDR programs and radios that serve it: frequency, mode, PTT and the audio both ways) or FlexRadio SmartSDR (6000 / 8000 / Aurora: one slice, DAX audio both ways; found on the network by itself on the phone and the computer). Type the address (name or IP, optionally :port), the receiver or DAX channel and press HAƊA. FT8 and the other timed modes transmit through it; the voice, CW, RTTY and SSTV modes do not yet (“This mode cannot transmit through a network radio yet.”). qFT8 never changes the radio's power, never keys a server that says it cannot transmit, and a lost connection turns TX OFF. It has only been tried against simulated servers so far.
    • Rig servers and SDR programs (experimental, the same rows, another Hanya): Rig server (rigctl protocol) and FLrig (XML-RPC) drive a radio that another program controls (default ports 4532 and 12345): qFT8 tunes, sets the mode and keys it through that program; the audio comes from your sound card, as with a cable, and the transmit power is only read, never set. In FLrig, turn on its own PTT via CAT. SDR++ (rigctl + network audio) is receive only: qFT8 follows and sets its frequency and demodulator through its Rigctl Server module (port 4532) and takes the audio from its Network sink at 48000 Hz, mono or stereo: TCP (qFT8 connects to it; Tashar haɗin kwararar sauti 7355 by default) or UDP (the program sends to this device's address and that port); choose Katin sauti under Sauti to use a cable instead. Works with rigctld, FLrig and SDR++; tried against the real FLrig and SDR++ programs, not yet with a real radio.
    • More network radios (experimental, the same rows, another Hanya): Icom LAN / Wi-Fi (IC-705, IC-9700, IC-7610, IC-7300 MKII, IC-R8600, IC-905, IC-7851 and the Xiegu X6100 / X6200 with open firmware) and Kenwood LAN (TS-890S, TS-990S) ask for the Adireshi, the Rediyo (the model; IC-705 and TS-890S first), the radio's own Sunan mai amfani and Kalmar sirri: Icom LAN also carries the audio over the network, Kenwood LAN takes it from your sound card. Elecraft K4 Ethernet, CAT over TCP (adapter) (a serial-to-network adapter; Rediyo says which radio, Kamar yadda aka zaɓa a Na'ura uses the one chosen in the Device row), X6100 companion server and QuantumSDR (UDP) ask for the address (and the radio) only. qFT8 sends the radio's own CAT commands, never changes the radio's power, and a lost link turns TX OFF and does not turn it on again by itself. Tried only against simulated radios so far; the QuantumSDR format is an assumption until its maker confirms it.
    • Bluetooth radio (experimental): choosing Bluetooth (na gwaji) in Haɗin rediyo reaches a radio whose CAT runs over Bluetooth, with no USB cable. On the phone pair the radio in the system's Bluetooth settings first, then press Zaɓi na'ura under Na'urar Bluetooth and choose it (on the iPhone the list is a few seconds of Bluetooth scan and the system asks for permission the first time; on the computer it lists the serial ports of the paired radios). Under Rediyo, Kamar yadda aka zaɓa a Na'ura uses the radio chosen in the Device row, or pick another model, then press HAƊA. On Android 12 and later qFT8 asks for the Nearby devices permission at that moment, never at start-up; if you refuse it, the connection stays as it was. Audio comes from your sound card, or from a Bluetooth headset chosen as the audio device on Android; Bluetooth audio is narrowband, so a cable decodes weak signals better. Set a TX time-out on the radio itself. qFT8 never changes the radio's power, and a lost link turns TX OFF and does not turn it on again by itself. Not yet tried with real radios.
  • QSO and SWL (the two buttons at the very top of Settings, experimental; QSO with the Ham features on, SWL with SWL on): each shows a star and the stars you have earned; tap the QSO or SWL button to open its live diploma (see Awards).
  • Taruka: kunna POTA da SOTA da ƙirgar QSO (duba Sashen Abubuwan da ke faruwa na Saituna).
  • Fayilolin log: IMPORT QSO LOG and EXPORT QSO LOG, your QSO logbook as ADIF (.adi) files, and the SWL log's own buttons (see The SWL log in Settings) (see ADIF Import/Export).
  • Daidaita QSO: layi ɗaya ga kowane sabis da ake loda QSO ɗinka zuwa gare shi: makunninsa, alama ta yanayinsa da giyarsa (duba Ayyukan loda QSO da Zaɓar ayyuka da sabobi).
  • Bayar da rahoto: PSKReporter, WSPRnet da sanar da ayyukan LQ8 naka.
  • Al'umma: ƙungiyoyin Telegram guda biyu (duba Al'umma).
  • Fayilolin saituna: SHIGO DA SAITUNA da FITAR DA SAITUNA (duba a ƙasa).
  • Ƙarin saituna: YANAYI (shafi mai ɗauke da, bisa wannan tsari AIKI NA ATOMATIK, YANAYIN DIJITAL da ZAƁIN ZANGO, sannan, ƙarƙashin "Saitunan yanayi", maɓalli ɗaya ga kowane yanayi: FT8, FT4, FT2, LQ8, JS8, WSPR sauran yanayin dijital kuma suna buɗe YANAYIN DIJITAL a wannan yanayin; SSB, CW, RTTY, JTTY, SSTV da PACKET shafukansu na kansu; KOMA yana komawa zuwa YANAYI), TUSHEN BAYANAI, SABOBI (abin da wannan na'urar take bayarwa ga sauran shirye-shirye, duba Zaɓar ayyuka da sabobi) da GOGE QSOS, kowanne an bayyana shi a Shafukan Saituna.
  • Duba sabbin siga lokacin farawa da Aika taƙaitaccen bayanin aiki na kullum ba tare da suna ba (duba Rahoton matsala da sabbin sigogi).
  • Harshe: harshen qFT8, ƙarƙashin layi a ƙarshen jerin.
  • A ƙasa: sigar, da hanyoyin haɗi zuwa wannan jagora da manufofin sirri, izini da na'urar USB da aka haɗa, da BA DA RAHOTON MATSALA (duba Ba da rahoton matsala).

    • Lasifika da makirufo

    Yanayin dijital suna buƙatar na'urar sauti ɗaya kawai: ta rediyo. SSB da CW suna buƙatar ta biyu domin kai: lasifika ko headphones domin jin rediyo, kuma ga SSB makirufo domin magana. Waɗannan biyu kada su zama katin sauti na rediyo, domin abin da ka faɗa zai koma kai tsaye.

    • A Android: Saituna > Lasifika da makirufo, a ƙarƙashin TSARIN NA'URA kaɗan.
    • A kwamfuta: katin TSARIN NA'URA yana da rabi biyu: REDIYO a hagu (katin sauti na rediyo: abin da rediyo yake ji da abin da yake aikawa) da KU (MAI AIKI) a dama.

    Akwai zaɓi biyu:

    • Makirufonku (kuna magana, rediyo tana aikawa). A ƙarƙashinsa, ƙaramin waterfall mai rai da ma'aunin ƙara suna nuna abin da makirufo yake ɗauka: yi magana, za ka ga yana motsi.
    • Lasifikarku / belun kunne (kuna jin rediyo), tare da maɓallin GWADA wanda yake kunna gajeren sauti a kansu.

    Duka biyun suna farawa a AUTO, wanda yake zaɓar na'urar da ba ta rediyo ba ce: a waya, headset idan an haɗa ɗaya, in ba haka ba makirufo da lasifikar wayar; a kwamfuta, na'urar sauti ta farko wadda ba katin rediyo ba ne. Canza su kawai idan AUTO ta zaɓi wadda ba daidai ba.

    Shawara: Yi amfani da headphones domin SSB. Idan lasifika tana kusa da makirufo, sautin da aka karɓa yana iya kunna VOX ko a ji shi a watsawarka.

    Allon umarnin CAT

    KUNSURIN CAT (Saituna, ƙarƙashin Na'ura) na magana ne da rediyo da kanka, da harshen umarninsa. Rubuta umarni a akwatin da ke ƙasa, misali FA; a QMX ko Kenwood, ka danna AIKA. Log ɗin da ke sama yana nuna abin da aka aika da abin da rediyo ya amsa. FITAR yana adana ko raba wannan log a matsayin fayil ɗin rubutu, GOGE kuma yana share shi.

    • Akwatin qFT8 a sama yana da alama idan allon ya buɗe: qFT8 yana ci gaba da magana da rediyo kamar yadda ya saba, kuma umarninsa suma suna bayyana a log. Idan ka cire alamar, qFT8 ba ya faɗa wa rediyo komai, don haka umarninka kaɗai ne a kan layi. Muddin an cire alamar, qFT8 ba ya aikawa ko tuning, kuma yana faɗin haka.
    • Rufe allon koyaushe yana mayar da alamar akwatin kuma yana mayar da rediyo ga qFT8.
    • Abin da ka rubuta yana tafiya zuwa rediyo daidai yadda aka rubuta shi, don haka umarni zai iya canza kowane saiti na rediyo, har da ƙarfin aikawa. qFT8 da kansa ba ya taɓa canza ƙarfin rediyo; allon shi ne kaɗai hanyar da irin wannan umarni yake isa gare shi, kuma sai idan ka rubuta shi.
    • Wannan yana aiki a kowane nau'in haɗi: kebul, USB ko cibiyar sadarwa.
    • Idan girman rubutu babba ne, maɓallan da ke sama (FITAR, GOGE) suna komawa ƙarƙashin taken maimakon rufe shi.

    Watsawa da doka

    • qFT8 ba ya sa rediyo ya yi watsawa a wajen band ɗin masu sha'awar rediyo na yankinka (Yankin ITU 1, 2 ko 3), kuma yana faɗa maka; maɓallin TX yana nuna NO HAM BAND.
    • Wasu ƙasashe suna ba da izinin wani ɓangare ne kawai na band, ko suna buƙatar izini na musamman ko sanarwa da farko. A waɗannan band (har da 160 m, 80 m, 40 m da 2 m a Yanki na 3, 23 cm, 70 cm da 13 cm) qFT8 yana nuna sanarwa sau ɗaya ga kowace band, misali “Wasu ƙasashe suna ba da izinin wani ɓangare kawai na 60m, ko suna buƙatar izini na musamman ko sanarwa da farko. Duba lasisinka.”; ba ya taɓa hana ka saboda wannan sanarwa.
    • Da zarar qFT8 ya san ƙasarka (duba Saurare da doka), yana kuma bin tsarin band na wannan ƙasar inda ya fi na yankinka ƙuntata. Ya zuwa yanzu an gina wannan a ciki don Faransa, Jamus, Biritaniya, Suwizalan, Italiya, Sipen, Holan, Belgiyom, Ostiriya, Amurka, Ostareliya da Japan. Inda tsarin ƙasar ya hana wata mita a fili (misali 60 m a wajen 5351.5–5366.5 kHz a Jamus, 40 m sama da 7200 kHz a Japan, 13 cm 2310–2390 MHz a Amurka) TX yana nan a shirye, ba a aika komai kuma maɓallin yana nuna NO HAM BAND; inda tsarin ba shi da tabbas sosai, sanarwar lasisi kawai za ka gani.
    • Kullum kai ne ke da alhakin lasisinka da tsarin band a inda kake aiki.

    Shafukan Saituna

    Kowane maɓalli mai kibiya yana buɗe shafi nasa; ‹ baya a saman shafin yana komawa babban shafin Saituna. Yawancin shafuka suna ƙarewa da SAKE SAITA ZUWA NA ASALI, wanda yake mayar da saitunan wannan shafin kawai (ba asusu, maɓalli, kalmar sirri ko QSO ba).

    TSARIN NA'URA

    • Haɗin rediyo: how qFT8 reaches the radio. Kai tsaye (USB / waya) (the default): the radio is plugged into this device. qFT8 bridge: a radio connected to another device (see qFT8 bridge). Rediyon hanyar sadarwa (na gwaji): a radio reached over the network with its own protocol. Bluetooth (na gwaji): a radio's CAT commands over Bluetooth. Only the chosen connection's rows are shown, right under it; the Na'urar USB row only for Direct. Changing it closes the old connection and connects the radio the new way; it never turns TX on. The web remote's settings page has the same choice.
    • Saitunan sauti: Tsarin sauti, Cikakken duplex, Tushen shigar sauti (a wayoyin da suke bayarwa, Unprocessed shi ne mafi kyau ga sigina masu rauni, tare da AGC na rediyo a kashe), na'urorin sauti na rediyo (Shigarwa daga na'urar rediyo, Fitarwa zuwa na'urar rediyo) da girman buffer ɗinsu, Samar da sautin tare da ƙarar (matakin sautin aikawa), ƙarar na'ura lokacin amfani da rediyo da lokacin amfani da lasifika, da Nuna gargaɗin kuskuren buffer sauti. Bar su a kan AUTO sai dai idan wani abu bai yi aiki ba.
    • Saitunan na'ura, ga rediyo da aka haɗa ta USB: Bada izinin canza saituna yana buɗe Na'urar USB, Gudun Baud, Bits na Bayani, Bits na Tsayawa, Parity da yadda ake kunna rediyo don aikawa (PTT: ta umarnin CAT, ko layin RTS ko DTR), tare da jinkirin PTT kafin aikawa. Saita yanayin transceiver yayin farawa da kowane zagaye yana kiyaye rediyo a yanayin da ya dace. Bayani a babban shafin yana faɗin lokacin da wannan na'urar take da gyare-gyare.
    • Daidaitawa (wannan rediyo): Bambancin daidaitawar spectrogram da Iyakar watsawa ba tsayawa.

    LOKACI DA WURI

    • Tazarar lokaci (ms): yawan gyaran da ake yi wa agogonka. DAIDAITA NTP yana saita shi daga intanet, DAIDAITA GPS daga GPS, kuma Saita lokaci ta kansa yana kiyaye shi daidai da kansa (idan qFT8 ya fara da kowane minti 30, daga intanet, ko kuma GPS). Idan babu intanet ko GPS, Daidaitawar gaggawa daga saƙonni yana saurara a band ɗin kuma yana saita agogo daga lokacin saƙonnin da yake fassarawa.
    • Wuri (Maidenhead grid): locator ɗinka, tare da DAIDAITA GPS, da ko ana sabunta shi daga GPS duk lokacin da qFT8 ya fara.
    • Ƙasa don dokokin karɓa: ƙasar da qFT8 ke amfani da ita don ƙa'idojin abin da za ka iya karɓa da watsawa (duba Saurare da doka da Watsawa da doka). Yana nuna “Ba a zaɓa ba: daga wurinka, locator ko alamar kira” har sai ka zaɓi ɗaya; CANZA yana buɗe jerin ƙasashe (mafi kusa da farko, sannan duka daga A zuwa Z) kuma ana tuna ƙasar da ka zaɓa.

    Saitunan aikace-aikace

    • Yanayin na'ura mai jinkiri / ƙarancin baturi: zane mai sauƙi, mai fassara bai canza ba (duba Kwamfutoci masu jinkiri; haka ma a wayoyi).
    • A kwamfuta: Tabbatar kafin rufewa (yana tambaya kafin babbar taga ta rufe qFT8), da babban fayil inda ake adana saitunansa da log ɗinsa.
    • Tsarin mai amfani: Fuskantarwa, Tilasta tsayayyen fuska a yanayin log (waya), Kashi huɗu (tablet), Fasaloli da Manhajar tana rufe (duka a Tsarin allo).
    • Launuka: Tsarin launuka na manhaja (dukkan manhajar: asalin kamannin duhu, mai haske, masu kama da tasha da na dare), Tsarin launuka na spectrogram da Tsarin launuka na taswira (taswirar duniya: salon haske, duhu, ƙasa da dare).
    • Faɗin spectrogram: rabon allo da spectrogram ke ɗauka.
    • Ga tashoshi, nuna: yawan abin da kowane layi na jerin Tashoshi yake nunawa (layi ɗaya da saƙon ƙarshe da aka karɓa, ko aka aika, ko a musanya, ko layuka masu tsayi da duka biyun). Nuna filaye a cikin tashoshi da log na QSO: ƙarin ginshiƙai, yankin CQ, yankin ITU, yanayi, da RST da aka aika da aka karɓa a log.
    • Ma'aunin nisa: kilomita, mil, ko zaɓin tsarin.
    • Siffar mai amfani da girman rubutu (komai ya fi girma ko ƙanƙanta) da Girman sassan mai amfani (ba rubutu ba) (maɓallai da akwatuna kawai).
    • Log: Log yana bin band da yanayin manhaja muddin sun dace (duba Log na hannu).
    • Ci gaba da aiki lokacin da aka rage/allo a kashe: lokacin da qFT8 ke ci gaba da aiki a bango: yayin da aka haɗa shi da rediyo ta CAT, yayin da bridge yake kunne, yayin da aka haɗa na'urar sauti (VOX), yayin da TX yake kunne, ko kullum.

    SPECTROGRAM

    • Fuskantarwa: a tsaye ko a kwance, kuma a kwance a wane gefe ƙananan mitoci suke. CW da RTTY koyaushe suna nuna shi a kwance.
    • Abubuwan da ake zana a samansa: Nuna lokacin UTC da Seq a kan spectrogram, Nuna saƙonni da aka ɗora, Zana layuka a kan kallon kololuwa da Nuna murfin mitoci don tashar da aka zaɓa.
    • Faɗin spectrogram, Sikeli na rubutun spectrogram da palette na launuka.
    • Ma'auni (matakin baƙi): gaba zuwa dama, ƙarin hayaniyar bango tana zama baƙi. Bambanci: gaba zuwa dama, bambanci mai ƙarfi tsakanin sigina masu rauni da masu ƙarfi.

    TASWIRAR DUNIYA

    • Ko taswirar tana nuna QSO, saƙonni da spots na wasu yanayi da na wasu band ba na yanzu ba.
    • Kamfas a taswira (duba Kamfas ɗin taswira).
    • Tauraron ɗan adam a taswira: tauraron dan Adam na hanya biyu da na saurare kawai, a kowane zango (duba Maɓallin SAT da tauraron dan Adam da ake nunawa).
    • Tsawon lokacin da QSO, spots da saƙonni suke zama a taswira, kowanne da lokacinsa.
    • Hanyar tauraron ɗan adam a gaba da Alamomin hanyar tauraron dan Adam kowane (duba Zaɓar tauraron dan Adam).
    • Palette na launukan taswira.
    • Yaɗuwar band: Nuna yankin makaho kusa da ni, zoben da ke kewaye da kai wanda band ɗin yake tsallakewa.
    • SOTA da POTA: a kan waɗanne band da a waɗanne yanayi ake zana masu kunnawa.

    AIKI NA ATOMATIK

    • Kira tashoshi ko da ba su kira CQ ba da Kira tashoshi ko da suna aiki a yanzu.
    • Guji kiran tashoshin da mai canza CQ ɗinsu bai shafe mu ba (duba CQ masu nufi).
    • Sau nawa ake sake gwada kira, cikin minti nawa, da tsawon lokacin da AUTO yake jira bayan gwajin ƙarshe.
    • Watsar da prefixes (an raba su da waƙafi): ba a taɓa kiran alamun kira da suka fara da su ba.
    • Tsaftace jerin tashoshi: bayan minti nawa na shiru tasha take barin jerin, da MANTA TASHOSHI YANZU.

    YANAYIN DIJITAL

    • Zurfin fassara da yawan threads da fassara za ta iya amfani da su, ga kowane yanayi.
    • Ƙarfin fassara (duba a ƙasa).
    • Don alamomin kira na musamman: a QSO da alamar kira mai haɗe (misali I8/ON8SGA), Fi son aika rahoto (aika gajeriyar alamar kira) ko Fi son cikakkun alamomin kira (tsallake rahoton sigina).
    • Don FT8, FT4, FT2 da LQ8: Lokacin fara fassarawa da Lokacin fara watsawa a cikin kowane zango. Canza su kawai idan na'urarka tana fassara a makare ko rediyonka yana fara aikawa a makare.
    • LQ8: Kunna amsa mai yawa da Kunna multi-73 (duba LQ8).
    • Saitunan JS8, WSPR, Q65, MSK144, FST4 da FST4W, an bayyana su tare da kowane yanayi.

    Ƙarfin fassara yana nuna yawan lokacin da mai fassara ya yi amfani da shi a zango na ƙarshe na yanayin da kake ciki. Sandar tana da sassa biyu, fassara mai sauri da fassara mai zurfi, kuma rubutun da ke samanta yana ba da lokutan biyun da lokacin da aka yarda. A gefenta: An gama (duka biyun sun ƙare a kan lokaci), An yanke zurfi (an dakatar da fassara mai zurfi a iyakar lokaci: al'ada ne a na'urori masu jinkiri) ko An yanke sauri (ko fassara mai sauri ba ta iya ƙarewa ba, kuma wasu sigina ba a fassara su ba). Don ba mai fassara ƙarin lokaci, saita Zurfin fassara ya yi amfani da ƙarancin lokaci, ko Lokacin fara fassarawa na farko.

    ZAƁIN ZANGO

    • Lokacin farawa app da Lokacin haɗa na'ura: ko qFT8 yana kwafin band ɗinsa zuwa rediyo (na asali) ko yana ɗaukar band ɗin rediyon.
    • Tilasta mita zuwa zaɓaɓɓen yanayi lokacin da na'ura ke canza band: idan ka canza band a rediyo, qFT8 yana mayar da shi zuwa mitar yanayin da ake ciki.
    • Nuna waɗannan band a cikin zaɓin band na ƙasa, da har band biyar na Band ɗin da aka keɓance (A zuwa E) da mitarsu a kHz.
    • Tauraron ɗan adam a taswira: waɗanne nau'ikan taurari taswirar take nunawa (duba Tauraron dan Adam da kamfas ɗin taswira).

    TUSHEN BAYANAI

    Abin da qFT8 yake saukewa don taswira, duka a kunne ta asali: spots daga PSKReporter da WSPRnet, ayyukan LQ8, kunnawar SOTA da POTA, da rahotannin yaɗuwa. Yi amfani da ma'ajiyar bayanai ta qFT8 yana ɗaukar waɗannan bayanan jama'a daga sabar qFT8 da farko, daga asalin shafukan kuma idan hakan ya gaza kawai; ayyukan LQ8, abubuwan da ke faruwa da taurari koyaushe suna zuwa daga sabis ɗin qFT8. Ba a nuna shi yayin da taswirar duniya take a kashe.

    GOGE QSOS

    GOGE DUK QSOS, GOGE QSOS NA SAURAN ALAMUN KIRA (QSO da aka yi rajista da alamar kira da ba ta yanzu ba) da GOGE DUK KWAFIN QSOS. Kowanne yana tambaya tukuna. Suna share QSO a wannan na'urar kawai, ba a sabis na kan layi ba.

    Fayilolin saituna

    FITAR DA SAITUNA yana adana dukkan saitunanka a fayil ɗaya, SHIGO DA SAITUNA kuma yana karanta shi, a wannan na'urar ko wata, waya ko kwamfuta (duba Saituna: inda suke, da mayar da su daga waya). Fayil ɗin yana ɗauke da maɓallanka da kalmomin sirrinka: kada ka taɓa raba shi.

    Rajistar saƙonni na asali

    qFT8 na iya adana rubutaccen rikodi na kowane saƙon da yake karɓa, a kowane yanayi: saƙonnin abokan hulɗarka da na kowa. Wannan yana da amfani wajen nuna cewa an yi tuntuɓa lokacin da ba a kammala ta ba ko ba a yi mata rajista ba, misali QSO na DXpedition inda aka rasa 73 na ƙarshe. Watsa shirinka na kanka ba ya cikin waɗannan fayiloli.

    Kunna shi a saman Saituna > Ƙarin saituna > YANAYI: Ajiye duk saƙonnin da aka fassara (a kashe ta tsohuwa). BUƊE BABBAN FAYIL da ke ƙarƙashinsa yana kai ka ga fayilolin. Ba a aika komai ko'ina, kuma ba a goge komai kai tsaye.

    Fayilolin suna cikin babban fayil mai suna raw_logs. Yana da babban fayil ɗaya a kowace rana, mai sunan kwanan watan UTC (raw_logs/2026-11-07/), da fayil ɗaya ga kowane yanayi a cikin kowane babban fayil na rana (FT8.tsv, LQ8.tsv, CW.tsv, ...). Sabuwar rana tana farawa da ƙarfe 00:00 UTC.

    • Android: babban fayil na manhajar da kanta a ma'ajiyar waya, Android/data/com.ft8.app/files/raw_logs. Za ka iya isa gare shi daga kwamfuta ta USB. BUƊE BABBAN FAYIL yana raba fayilolin ranar da ta fi sabuwa: ajiye su a Files ko Drive, ko ka aika ta imel.
    • Windows, macOS, Linux: babban fayil na saituna da rajista (ana nuna shi a Saituna > Aikace-aikace), kusa da rajistar QSO. BUƊE BABBAN FAYIL yana buɗe shi a manajan fayil.
    • iPhone da iPad: manhajar Files, On My iPhone > qFT8 > raw_logs. BUƊE BABBAN FAYIL yana buɗe shi a can.
    • Na'urar sarrafawa ta yanar gizo: saitin nan ɗaya a shafin Saituna nata. BUƊE BABBAN FAYIL nata yana jera kwanaki da fayiloli, kuma kowane fayil yana sauka da dannawa ɗaya.

    Kowane fayil rubutu ne da aka raba da tab. Layi na farko yana ba da sunan yanayi da nau'in qFT8, layi na biyu yana ba da sunayen ginshiƙai, sannan akwai layi ɗaya ga kowane saƙo, ana ƙarawa yayin karɓa. Lokuta suna cikin UTC kuma mitoci suna cikin Hz. Buɗe fayil a cikin spreadsheet (LibreOffice Calc, Excel, Numbers) ko editan rubutu ka nemi sunan kira. Kowane layi yana nuna lokaci, mita, rahoton sigina da ainihin saƙon da tasha ta aika.

    Misali ɗaya gajere ga kowane nau'in yanayi (an taƙaita ginshiƙai a nan):

    • FT8, FT4, FT2 (FT8.tsv): 2026-11-07T14:10:45Z   14074000   20m   1250   4   HB9IPH F4XYZ -07   F4XYZ   HB9IPH   report
    • LQ8, LQ4, LQ2, LQ16 (LQ8.tsv), tare da siffofin saƙo 4 CQ, CALL, REPORT73, 73: 2026-11-07T15:02:30Z   14080000   1500   -23   EA4XYZ HB9IPH JN47 -21   CALL
    • JS8 (JS8.tsv): kowane firam, da cikakken saƙon da zarar ya kammala: 2026-11-07T16:00:30Z   7078000   1210   HEARTBEAT LN05   DL1XYZ: @ALLCALL HEARTBEAT LN05
    • CW, RTTY da sauran yanayin allon rubutu (CW.tsv, RTTY.tsv, ...): layi ɗaya ga kowane yanki na rubutun da aka fassara, tare da lambar kwarara ga kowane sigina: 2026-11-07T14:10:42.9Z   7028500   600   s3   26 wpm   OK1XYZ OK1XYZ K
    • WSPR, FST4W, Q65, JT65, JT9, MSK144: ginshiƙan FT8 da filayen kowane yanayi na kansa (karkata, ƙarfi, ƙaramin yanayi).
    • NAVTEX (NAVTEX.tsv): layi ɗaya ga kowane saƙo, tare da tasha, batu, adadin kurakurai da rubutun a layi ɗaya.
    • Masu fassarar saurare (LAB da SWL), kamar AIS, ADS-B, APRS, na'urorin kira da siginar lokaci: fayil ɗaya ga kowane sigina (AIS.tsv, ADSB.tsv, ...), layi ɗaya ga kowane rahoton da aka fassara. ADS-B da AIS suna maimaita jirgin sama ko jirgin ruwa iri ɗaya sau da yawa a sakan, don haka ana rubuta kowanne sau ɗaya a kowane sakan 30 a mafi yawa, kuma nan take idan sunansa ko inda zai je ya canza.
    • Murya da rubutun watsa labarai (tashar rubutu ta FreeDV, rubutun rediyon FM da AM, rubutun DAB+ da DRM): FREEDV.tsv da BROADCAST.tsv, layi ɗaya idan rubutun ya tabbata da kuma wani idan ya canza. Ba a yin rajistar hotuna (SSTV, faks na yanayi, hotunan tauraron ɗan adam) da sautin murya.

    Rukunin FT8 mai cunkoso yana cika kusan MB 3 zuwa 6 a kowace rana. Ka goge tsofaffin manyan fayilolin rana da kanka idan ba ka bukatarsu kuma.

    Al'umma

    Sashen Al'umma na Saituna yana da maɓallai biyu da ke buɗe ƙungiyoyin qFT8 a Telegram a burauzarka ko manhajar Telegram: qFT8 Telegram group (labarai, taimako da sigogin gwaji) da LQ8 Telegram group (al'ummar yanayin LQ8).

    ↑ Koma zuwa Abubuwan da ke ciki

    Aiki na atomatik (AUTO)

    Yanayin AUTO ita ce babbar hanyar amfani da qFT8. A wannan yanayi, manhaja tana duba saƙonnin da aka fassara a ƙarshen kowane zagaye ta atomatik, kuma tana zaɓar tashar da ta fi dacewa a tuntuɓa bisa sharuɗɗan manufarka.

    Yadda ake Zaɓar Manufa

    Shafin Tashoshi yana nuna jerin tashoshin da ke aiki, an jera su yadda waɗanda suka fi dacewa (bisa sharuɗɗanka) suke a sama. A ƙarshen kowane zagayen fassara, qFT8 tana duba wannan jeri kuma tana ƙoƙarin kiran tasha ta farko da ta cancanta. Idan babu wadda ta cancanta, za ta fara kiran CQ ta atomatik domin jawo masu kira. A yanayin AUTO, qFT8 koyaushe tana amsa kira.

    Sharuɗɗa da Matatu

    Kana iya daidaita aikin atomatik da jerin zaɓi guda uku a saman shafin Tashoshi:

    • Manufa: Yana bayyana "nasarar" da kake nema (Kowa, DXCC, Yankin CQ, Yankin ITU, Grid).
    • Tazara: Sau nawa kake son tuntuɓar entity ɗaya (sau ɗaya, koyaushe, kullum, mako-mako, wata-wata).
    • Ƙa'ida: Tana taƙaita sharaɗin kasancewa na dabam (kowace alamar kira, kowane zango, har a tabbatar da yanki/entity).

    Shiga da hannu: Kana iya shiga tsakani da hannu koyaushe ta taɓa maɓallin CALL na wata tasha. Wannan yana shiga yanayin CALL domin wannan QSO kawai. Idan ya ƙare, qFT8 tana komawa yanayin AUTO.

    CQ Masu Nufi

    Wasu tashoshi suna kiran CQ ga wani ɓangare na duniya kawai: CQ DX, CQ NA, CQ JA. Kalmar da ke bayan CQ ita ce Mai canza CQ nasu. Idan an sa alama a Guji kiran tashoshin da mai canza CQ ɗinsu bai shafe mu ba (Saituna > AIKI NA ATOMATIK, a kunne tun asali), AUTO ba ta kiran tashar da CQ ɗinta ba naka ba ne. Ana gane inda kake daga ƙasar alamar kiranka; idan ba a san ta ba, daga lokata naka.

    Mai canza CQ nasu DX
    AUTO tana kiransu idan muna wata nahiya daban da ta mai kira
    Mai canza CQ nasu NA
    AUTO tana kiransu idan muna Arewacin Amurka (har da Amurka ta Tsakiya da Caribbean)
    Mai canza CQ nasu SA
    AUTO tana kiransu idan muna Kudancin Amurka
    Mai canza CQ nasu EU, EUR
    AUTO tana kiransu idan muna Turai
    Mai canza CQ nasu AS, ASIA
    AUTO tana kiransu idan muna Asiya
    Mai canza CQ nasu AF, AFR
    AUTO tana kiransu idan muna Afirka
    Mai canza CQ nasu OC, PAC
    AUTO tana kiransu idan muna Oceania
    Mai canza CQ nasu AN, ANT
    AUTO tana kiransu idan muna Antarctica
    Mai canza CQ nasu USA, US, W, K
    AUTO tana kiransu idan ƙasarmu Amurka ce (ban da Hawaii, ban da Alaska: ana neman waɗannan da KH6 da KL)
    Mai canza CQ nasu VE, CAN
    AUTO tana kiransu idan ƙasarmu Kanada ce
    Mai canza CQ nasu JA, JPN
    AUTO tana kiransu idan ƙasarmu Japan ce

    Mai canza CQ nasu UK, G
    AUTO tana kiransu idan ƙasarmu Ingila ce, Scotland, Wales, Ireland ta Arewa ko ɗaya daga cikin tsibiransu
    Mai canza CQ nasu DL
    AUTO tana kiransu idan ƙasarmu Jamus ce
    Mai canza CQ nasu EA
    AUTO tana kiransu idan ƙasarmu Spain ce (ban da tsibiran Balearic, tsibiran Canary, Ceuta da Melilla: ana neman waɗannan da EA6, EA8 da EA9)
    Mai canza CQ nasu BY, CHN
    AUTO tana kiransu idan ƙasarmu China ce
    Mai canza CQ nasu UA, RUS
    AUTO tana kiransu idan ƙasarmu Rasha ce (ta Turai ko ta Asiya)
    Mai canza CQ nasu PY
    AUTO tana kiransu idan ƙasarmu Brazil ce

    AUTO tana kiransu idan ƙasarmu Indiya ce
    Mai canza CQ nasu HL
    AUTO tana kiransu idan ƙasarmu Koriya ta Kudu ce
    Mai canza CQ nasu YB
    AUTO tana kiransu idan ƙasarmu Indonesia ce
    Mai canza CQ nasu CAR
    AUTO tana kiransu idan ƙasarmu tana Caribbean
    Mai canza CQ nasu ME
    AUTO tana kiransu idan ƙasarmu tana Gabas ta Tsakiya
    Mai canza CQ nasu Prefix na alamar kira na wata ƙasa mai haruffa ɗaya zuwa uku (F, I, SM, 9A, KH6, EA8...)
    AUTO tana kiransu idan ƙasarmu ita ce wannan ƙasa
    Mai canza CQ nasu Duk wani abu daban (TEST, POTA, SOTA, QRP, FD, WW, lambobin jihohi da larduna, free text)
    AUTO tana kiransu idan koyaushe: ba game da wuri ba ne
    • Kiran farko na AUTO kanta ne kawai ake riƙewa. Tashar da ta kira ka ana amsa ta koyaushe, QSO da ake ciki ana gama shi koyaushe, kuma idan ka taɓa CALL da kanka ana kira koyaushe.
    • CQ na ƙarshe ne ake la'akari da shi. qFT8 tana tuna mai canzawa na CQ na ƙarshe na tasha har sai tashar ta aika CQ ba tare da komai ba.
    • Lambobi masu haruffa biyu waɗanda suke prefix na wata ƙasa kuma jiha ta Amurka ko lardin Kanada (ON, PA, LA, CT, OK, HI, TN, OH) ba a taƙaita su, domin ana amfani da su ta hanyoyi biyu. Idan qFT8 ba ta iya gane inda mai kira ko kai kuke ba, ita ma ba ta taƙaitawa.
    • FT8, FT4 da FT2 ba sa iya ɗaukar kowane mai canzawa: nasu haruffa ɗaya zuwa huɗu ne ko lambobi uku, don haka ba a iya aika CQ KH6 ko CQ EA8 a cikinsu. Waɗannan layuka suna da amfani a LQ8.

    Tsarin Sake Kira

    Domin kada qFT8 ta ci gaba da kiran tashar da ba ta amsawa ba iyaka, qFT8 tana da tsarin sake kira da dakatarwa, wanda yake a kunne tun asali. Kana iya saita shi a shafin Saituna, a AIKI NA ATOMATIK:

    • Iyakar sake kira: Mafi yawan sau da qFT8 za ta maimaita mataki ɗaya na watsawa ga tashar da ba ta amsawa.
    • Lokacin ƙirga: Tsawon lokacin da ake ƙirga waɗannan ƙoƙari a ciki.
    • Lokacin jira: Idan aka kai iyaka, ana "dakatar" da tashar na wannan tsawon lokaci. Yanayin AUTO zai bar tashoshin da aka dakatar har lokacin jira ya ƙare ko tashar ta amsa.

    Misalan Saiti Masu Kyau

    Ga wasu haɗe-haɗen sharuɗɗa da ake ba da shawara domin manufofin da aka saba:

    Lura cewa a dukkan waɗannan, kana iya kunna Leƙen asiri da Mai aiki domin kai wa ƙarin tashoshi da shiga pile-up nasu.

    Lura: Idan alamar kiranka ba ta yau da kullum ba ce (misali mai ƙari ko prefix da aka raba da "/", doguwar alamar kira, ko mai lambobi a wurare da ba a saba ba), zai fi kyau ka saita Manufa zuwa Kira CQ. Dalili: mai yiwuwa za a aika alamar kiranka a matsayin hash, kuma tashoshin da suka gan ka kana kiran CQ ne kawai za su iya gane ta. Kana iya kiran tashoshi har yanzu, amma za su gane saƙon naka ne kawai idan sun ji ka kana kiran CQ kwanan nan.

    ↑ Koma zuwa Abubuwan da ke ciki

    Aiki da hannu (CALL)

    Ko da yake qFT8 tana yin abubuwa da yawa da kanta, kana iya karɓar sarrafawa a kowane lokaci. Taɓa maɓallin CALL kusa da kowace tasha a shafin Tashoshi zai shiga yanayin CALL nan take kuma ya yi ƙoƙarin yin QSO da wannan tasha.

    Da zarar QSO da tashar da ka zaɓa ya ƙare (da nasara ko babu), qFT8 za ta koma yanayin AUTO ta atomatik kuma ta ci gaba da dabararka ta baya (misali kiran CQ).

    Lokaci ga Masu Aiki da Hannu

    Idan kana son ƙarin lokaci a ƙarshen zagaye domin yanke shawara da hannu, kana iya samun ƙarin sarari ta canza Saitunan FT8 a shafin Saituna:

    • Rage "Lokacin fara fassarawa": Ana fara sarrafa sauti da wuri.
    • Ƙara "Lokacin fara watsawa": Ana jinkirta farkon amsarka.

    Gargaɗi: Canza waɗannan lambobi da yawa yana iya sa saƙonninka su yi wa sauran tashoshi wuyar fassara sosai, domin kana rage lokacin da siginar FT8 kanta take da shi.

    ↑ Koma zuwa Abubuwan da ke ciki

    Gyara da rubuta QSO da hannu

    qFT8 tana rubuta QSO da ta yi a yanayin dijital da kanta. Wannan sashe yana magana ne game da abubuwa uku da kake yi da hannu a shafin Log na QSO: gyara QSO, rubuta QSO da ka yi ta wata hanya (murya, Morse, wani rediyo), da aiki a kan QSO da dama a lokaci guda.

    Editan QSO

    Taɓa QSO a shafin Log na QSO, sai shafin ya zama editansa. Kowane fili da aka adana yana nan: alamar kiranka da lokata naka, nasu, yanayi, zango, mitoci, kwanan wata da lokuta, ƙarfi, rahotanni, lambobin POTA da SOTA, da bayani.

    1. QSO da ake gyarawa: alamar kira, yanayi, zango, kwanan wata da lokaci.
    2. Matsayi: ko an tabbatar da QSO. Taɓa shi domin Tabbatar da hannu ko Cire tabbatarwa da hannu (wannan yana canza log naka ne kawai).
    3. An loda: inda QSO yake a QRZ da LoTW. Taɓa shi domin sake loda QSO zuwa sabis ɗaya ko duka.
    4. Filin da aka canza ana haskaka shi, da tsohuwar ƙimarsa a ja kusa da shi da kibiya mai mayar da tsohuwar ƙimar. Taɓa kowace ƙima domin canza ta. Ana zaɓar yanayi da zango daga jerin manhaja, zango yana bin mitar da ka rubuta, kuma ƙimar da ba za ta iya zama daidai ba (alamar kira, lokata, kwanan wata...) ana ƙin ta tare da dalili.
    5. BAYA: Yana barin edita. Idan akwai canje-canje, yana tambaya ko a watsar da su.
    6. Adadin canje-canje da ba a ajiye ba tukuna.
    7. GOGE: Yana goge QSO daga log na wannan manhaja kawai, bayan ka rubuta yes.
    8. ADANA: Yana neman tabbatarwa, sannan ya ajiye.

    Abin da yake faruwa a QRZ da LoTW idan ka ajiye. Tambayar tabbatarwa tana gaya maka, game da QSO da kake gyarawa:

    • QRZ: ana sabunta QSO a can. Idan ka canza alamar kira, kwanan wata, lokaci, zango ko yanayi, QRZ yana ɗaukarsa a matsayin wani QSO daban: ana ƙirƙirar sabo, tsohon kuma yana nan, sai ka goge shi a qrz.com.
    • LoTW: ana sake loda QSO da aka gyara a matsayin sabo; tsohon yana nan a can, ba a tabbatar da shi ba kuma ba ya cutarwa.
    • Canjin da yake karya tabbatarwa (alamar kira, alamar kiranka, kwanan wata ko lokacin QSO) dole a tabbatar da shi ta rubuta yes, domin QSO ba zai ƙara dacewa da log na ɗayan tashar ba.
    • QSO da aka yi da wata alamar kira daban da wadda take a Saituna ba a loda shi.

    qFT8 ba ta taɓa goge komai a QRZ ko LoTW. Goge QSO yana cire shi daga manhaja kawai. Idan wannan QSO yana kan wani sabis, yana iya dawowa a daidaitawa ta gaba: fara goge shi a sabis ɗin. Ba a iya dawo da abin da aka goge sai dai idan ka ajiye kwafin log da ka fitar.

    Log na hannu

    Maɓallin + na shafin Log na QSO yana buɗe log na hannu, fom mai sauri domin QSO da ka yi da kanka. A waya, yana juya allo zuwa tsaye, da siririn spectrogram a sama (ana iya kashe wannan a Saituna > AIKACE-AIKACE > Tsarin mai amfani).

    1. QSO / SWL (filin farko, shuɗi don QSO, ja don SWL): inda shigarwar ke zuwa. SWL yana rubuta tashar a cikin log ɗin SWL, ba a cikin log ɗin QSO ba. Shafin SWL na log yana buɗe shi a SWL kuma shafin QSO a QSO; a ko'ina kuma yana farawa a QSO idan fasalolin Ham na kunne kuma a SWL idan suna kashe.
    2. Alama: Rubuta alamar kira ka danna Enter a madannai: ana rubuta QSO da lokacin yanzu, akwatin ya zama fanko, kuma kana iya rubuta na gaba. Madannai yana kasancewa a buɗe daga QSO ɗaya zuwa na gaba.
    3. RUBUTA: Daidai da Enter.
    4. GOGE / SOKE: Idan an rubuta alamar kira, maɓallin yana cewa GOGE kuma yana share akwatin. In ba haka ba, yana cewa SOKE kuma yana cire QSO na ƙarshe na wannan zama daga log, ya mayar da shi fom domin a gyara shi.
    5. AN GAMA: Yana rufe log na hannu. Idan an rubuta alamar kira amma ba a rubuta ta a log ba, yana tambaya ko a rubuta ta.
    6. Layin bincike: Ƙasar alamar kira, QSO ɗinka na ƙarshe da ita, da ko kwafi ne. DUPE da launin orange yana nufin ka riga ka rubuta wannan tasha a wannan zango da yanayi yau (kana iya rubuta ta har yanzu).
    7. Cikakkun bayanai (shafa a gefe domin ƙari): lokaci (Yanzu, ko taɓa shi domin saita kwanan wata da lokaci), zango, yanayi, mita a kHz, mitar karɓa idan ta bambanta, ƙarfi, rahotannin da aka aika da waɗanda aka karɓa, lambar POTA / SOTA tasu da taka. Suna farawa daga abin da manhaja take a kai, kuma ana riƙe su daga QSO ɗaya zuwa na gaba.
    8. Wannan zama: Adadin QSO da ka rubuta tun buɗe log na hannu. A waya, taɓa shi domin ganin su a jeri.
    9. QSO na wannan zama ana nuna su da launin coral da alamar agogo: sun riga sun shiga log ɗinka, amma ba a loda su zuwa QRZ ko LoTW sai ka danna AN GAMA, domin a iya soke kuskuren rubutu. Taɓa ɗaya daga cikinsu domin soke shi ko gyara shi.

    Maɓallin QSO na allunan SSB, CW, RTTY da SSTV yana buɗe wannan fom a matsayin ƙaramin kati, an riga an cika yanayi, mita da tasha, domin QSO ɗaya a kowane lokaci.

    Zaɓar QSO da dama

    Yi dogon danna a kan QSO a shafin Log na QSO domin fara zaɓi (a kwamfuta, Ctrl-click, ko Shift-click domin zaɓar da yawa a jere). Sannan taɓa wasu QSO domin ƙara su ko cire su.

    1. X: Yana barin zaɓi ba tare da yin komai ba.
    2. Adadin QSO da aka zaɓa.
    3. ZAƁI TSAKANI: Taɓa shi, sannan taɓa QSO inda zaɓin yake ƙarewa: ana zaɓar duk abin da ke tsakani.
    4. DUKA da BABU.
    5. AYYUKA: Abin da za a yi da QSO da aka zaɓa:
      • Tabbatar da hannu / Cire tabbatarwa da hannu.
      • Fitar (ADIF): fayil mai waɗannan QSO kawai.
      • Fitar da lambar POTA / SOTA: iri ɗaya, da wurin shakatawarka ko kololuwarka a rubuce a kowane QSO na fayil (ba a canza log kansa).
      • Ƙara zuwa wani kunna POTA / SOTA da Cire daga kunna nasu (duba Kunna wurin shakatawa ko kololuwa).
    6. QSO da aka zaɓa ana sa musu tick kuma ana haskaka su.

    Kowane aiki yana fara gaya maka adadin QSO da zai canza kuma yana neman tabbatarwa, kuma ana iya soke canji nan take bayansa. Ba a taɓa goge komai a QRZ ko LoTW.

    ↑ Koma zuwa Abubuwan da ke ciki

    Shigo/Fitar da ADIF

    qFT8 tana aiki da tsarin ADIF (Amateur Data Interchange Format) domin musaya da sauran manhajojin log, sabis na intanet, da kuma ajiyar kwafi da hannu.

    Shigo da QSO

    Fasalin shigowa yana ba ka damar kawo bayanai daga wasu wurare (kamar wasu manhajojin log ko kwafi daga wasu na'urori) zuwa log na qFT8 a na'urarka.

    • Zaɓuɓɓukan tacewa: ana shigo da kowane yanayi. Kai ke zaɓar ko a taƙaita shigowar ga alamar kiranka ta yanzu ko a haɗa dukkan masu aiki.
    • Cire kwafi ta atomatik: qFT8 tana haɗa bayanai da hikima. Tana hana kwafi ta kwatanta QRZ Log IDs da lokutan QSO. Idan bayanin da aka shigo da shi yana da tabbatarwa ko Log ID mai inganci wanda naka na na'ura ba shi da shi, qFT8 tana sabunta naka da bayanin da ya fi kyau.

    Fitar da QSO

    Taɓa FITAR DA QSOS yana ƙirƙirar fayil na ADIF 3.1.6. A Android yana buɗe allon Share na tsarin, wanda yake ba ka damar ajiye log a na'urarka ko aika shi ta email/saƙo; a kwamfuta kai ne kake zaɓar inda za a ajiye fayil.

    • Tsawon lokaci: Kana iya fitar da dukkan tarihinka, ko ka yi amfani da masu zaɓar kwanan wata/lokaci na UTC domin ware wani kunnawa ko wata gasa.
    • Matatun hali: Suna da amfani wajen neman awards (fitar da "Tabbatattu kawai") ko lodawa da hannu zuwa wasu sabis (fitar da "Waɗanda ba a ɗora zuwa QRZ ba kawai" domin guje wa kwafi).
    • Sharuɗɗan mai harbi: Tace log ɗinka domin fitar da "Fitar da duk bayanan", "Fitar da mai harbi POTA kawai", ko "Fitar da mai harbi SOTA kawai", domin samun fayilolin neman awards masu ɗauke da lambobin tashoshin (<POTA_REF> / <SOTA_REF>).
    • Lambar SOTA: Kamar yadda aka bayyana a sashen SOTA, qFT8 tana iya saka lambar kololuwa ta atomatik a filin <MY_SOTA_REF> na kowane bayani da aka fitar.
    • Lambar POTA: Kamar yadda aka bayyana a sashen POTA, qFT8 tana iya saka lambar wurin shakatawa ta atomatik a filayen <MY_SIG>, <MY_SIG_INFO>, da <MY_POTA_REF> na kowane bayani da aka fitar. Wurare da dama, waɗanda aka raba da waƙafi, suna ba da fayil ɗaya ga kowane wuri (duba "Wane wurin shakatawa za a rubuta a fayil?").

    Sunayen fayilolin da aka fitar

    • Ba tare da lamba ba: qFT8-YYYYMMDD-HHMMSS.adi, wato kwanan wata da lokacin (UTC) fitarwa, misali qFT8-20261006-181500.adi. Matatun hali suna ƙara _confirmed ko _notuploaded bayan lokaci, don haka fayilolin babban fayil suna jeruwa bisa kwanan wata.
    • Da lambar POTA: CALLSIGN@PARK-YYYYMMDD.adi, misali K1ABC@US-1234-20261006.adi. Wannan shi ne sunan da shirin wuraren shakatawa yake nema. Akwai fayil ɗaya ga kowane wuri da kowace ranar UTC, don haka fitarwar da ta ƙetare tsakar dare ta UTC tana ba da fayiloli biyu.
    • Da lambar SOTA: fayil ɗaya, misali K1ABC@W1_HA-001-20261006.adi ("/" na kololuwa ko na alamar kira ba ya iya zama cikin sunan fayil, ana rubuta shi "_").
    • A kwamfuta, fitarwa ba ta taɓa maye gurbin fayil da yake nan tuni: idan an riga an yi amfani da sunan, ana ajiye sabon fayil a matsayin name (1).adi, sannan name (2).adi, haka dai.

    Lura: Ga manyan log sosai (sama da 100KB), qFT8 za ta nuna gargaɗi, domin cire kwafi da jerawa suna iya ɗaukar ɗan lokaci, musamman a waya.

    ↑ Koma zuwa Abubuwan da ke ciki

    Ayyukan loda QSO

    qFT8 na iya aika kowane QSO da ka yi rajista zuwa littattafan log da dama na yanar gizo ko na network ɗin gida: QRZ, LoTW, Wavelog, Cloudlog, eQSL da wani qFT8 (qFT8log). Kowanne yana da layi a saman Saituna: akwatin zaɓi don kunna shi, da maɓallin gear da ke buɗe shafin sabis ɗin da kansa.

    • Akwatin zaɓin yana kasancewa launin toka har sai an tabbatar da sabis ɗin: buɗe shafinsa, shigar da bayanansa ka danna TABBATAR.
    • Kowane shafi yana farawa da taƙaitaccen bayani da maɓallin ? da ke buɗe wannan littafin jagora a sashen sabis ɗin. Yana kuma nuna QSO nawa ba a loda su ba tukuna, tare da ƊORA YANZU, da maɓallin SAKE SAITA ZUWA NA ASALI (ba ya taɓa share asusu, mabuɗi ko kalmar sirri).
    • Idan ba a iya isa ga sabis tun lokacin da ya yi aiki na ƙarshe ba, layinsa yana zama ja kuma layin ja a ƙasansa yana cewa Ba a iya haɗawa ba tun da kwanan wata da lokaci.

    Yaushe ake ɗaukar QSO a matsayin an loda?

    Alamar kore a cikin log, jerin tashoshi, taswira da na'urar sarrafa daga nesa ta yanar gizo duk suna bin doka ɗaya. Shafin kowane sabis yana da akwatin zaɓi Ɗauki QSO a matsayin an loda idan an loda shi nan. Yana a kunne ga QRZ da LoTW kuma a kashe ga sauran.

    • Sabis yana ƙidayuwa ne kawai idan an saita shi: an tabbatar da shi kuma an kunna shi.
    • Ana ɗaukar QSO a matsayin an loda da zarar kowane sabis da ake ƙidaya yana da shi.
    • Idan babu ɗaya daga cikin sabis ɗin da aka saita da akwatinsa aka yi masa alama, duk sabis ɗin da aka saita ana ƙidaya su. Don haka idan ka saita qFT8log kaɗai, manhajar tana jiran qFT8log kaɗai, ba ta taɓa jiran QRZ ko LoTW.
    • Idan ba a saita kowane sabis ba kwata-kwata, akwatunan da aka yi wa alama ne ke yanke hukunci daga abin da QSO ɗin da kansa yake ɗauke da shi (misali log da aka shigo da shi daga wata manhaja yana riƙe da alamominsa).
    • QSO da aka shigo da shi daga LoTW ana ɗaukarsa a matsayin yana kan QRZ tuni kuma ba a aika shi can. QSO da ya zo daga sabis ana ɗaukarsa a matsayin an loda shi zuwa wannan sabis ɗin kuma ba a taɓa mayar masa da shi ba.
    • Tauraro (an tabbatar) yana buƙatar QSO ya kasance an loda shi bisa wannan doka kuma wani sabis ya tabbatar da shi, sai dai idan ka tabbatar ko ka cire tabbacinsa da hannu.

    Fitar da ADIF na iya zaɓar Ba a loda zuwa sabis ɗaya a lokaci guda; zaɓuɓɓukan sabis ɗin da ba a saita ba launin toka ne. qFT8 ba ta taɓa share QSO a kan kowane sabis ba: share QSO a nan yana gargaɗinka ne kawai cewa har yanzu yana can.

    Zaɓar ayyuka da sabobi

    Daidaita QSO lists every service, one line each: its switch, an icon for its state and its gear, which opens the service's page. There is no icon while the service is not set up (or, for a server, while it is off), a green tick while it sends your QSOs, an amber pause while it is set up but switched off, and a red “!” when it could not connect; only then does a second, red line under the row say what went wrong. The logging programs on your network keep a second line naming the programs they work with; what they cannot promise (UDP, no password) is explained on their own pages.

    A cikin Ƙarin saituna > SABOBI, daidai a ƙasan TUSHEN BAYANAI, akwai abin da wannan na'ura ke bai wa wasu shirye-shirye: sabar yanar gizo ta sarrafawa daga nesa da qFT8 bridge (iPhone ba shi da ko ɗaya), sabobin Wavelog, Cloudlog da qFT8log da masu karɓa don shirye-shiryen log a cikin hanyar sadarwarka. Akwatin zaɓi na layi, misali “Sabar Yanar Gizo Ta Kula Daga Nesa a tashar 13497”, shi ne makunni ɗaya da na shafin wannan sabar, kuma giyar tana buɗe shafin; BAYA daga can yana komawa SABOBI. Sabar da ka kashe tana tsayawa nan take kuma tana dawowa yadda take idan ka sake kunna ta.

    Saukewar da ke ciyar da taswira suna cikin Ƙarin saituna > TUSHEN BAYANAI, daidai a ƙarƙashin YANAYI; duk suna kunne ta asali. Idan taswirar duniya a kashe take, ba a nuna TUSHEN BAYANAI (ba a sauke komai don taswirar a lokacin).

    Kowace sabis da kowace sabar

    Inda qFT8 zai iya aika QSO da rahotannin ka, abin da zai iya bai wa wasu shirye-shirye, da kuma inda aka bayyana kowanne:

    • Daidaita QSO (QSO ɗinka suna fita): QRZ, LoTW, eQSL, Wavelog, Cloudlog, qFT8log (zuwa wani qFT8), HRDLog, HamQTH, World Radio League da Club Log, da shirye-shiryen rajista a cibiyar sadarwarka (FT8 network log da ADIF over UDP).
    • Sabobi (Ƙarin saituna > SABOBI; wasu shirye-shirye suna haɗawa da wannan qFT8): Sabar Yanar Gizo Ta Kula Daga Nesa, qFT8 bridge, sabobin Wavelog da Cloudlog, sabar qFT8log, da masu karɓa don shirye-shiryen rajista a cibiyar sadarwarka (FT8 network log, ADIF over UDP, da mahaɗan AC Log da DXLab).
    • Bayar da rahoto (abin da ka ji yana fita): PSKReporter, WSPRnet da ayyukan LQ8 a taswira kai tsaye.
    • Taruka: kunna POTA da SOTA da ƙirgar QSO.

    Don raba rajista ɗaya tsakanin qFT8 da dama, qFT8log ita ce hanyar da aka ba da shawara: tana aiki iri ɗaya a kowane qFT8, ita ce mafi aminci (kalmar sirri, kowane saƙo an sanya masa hannu kuma, ta asali, an ɓoye shi), kuma ɗayan qFT8 yana tabbatar da kowane QSO, don haka babu wanda ke ɓacewa a hanya. Sabis ɗin da ke tafiya ta UDP ba za su iya alkawarta haka ba, kuma Saituna tana faɗin hakan a ƙarƙashin kowane maɓallinsu (a ƙasa).

    HRDLog, HamQTH, World Radio League da Club Log

    Kowanne yana da shafinsa. HRDLog yana neman Lambar lodawa ta HRDLog da ke cikin bayanan martabarka na HRDLog (ba kalmar sirrinka ba); HamQTH yana neman Alamar kira ta HamQTH da Kalmar sirri ta HamQTH; World Radio League yana neman Mabuɗin API na WRL da ka ƙirƙira a shafinsa (Integrations > Developer API) kuma, idan kana da da yawa, Littafin rajista na WRL; Club Log yana neman Imel na Club Log da Kalmar sirrin manhaja ta Club Log. Sannan danna TABBATAR. qFT8 ba ya taɓa share QSO a can; a Club Log, QSO da ka canza alamar kiransa, kwanan wata, lokaci, band ko yanayi yana barin tsohon shigarwarsa (share ta a shafin yanar gizo).

    HAMLOG.online rajista ce ta yanar gizo da RAZA, RDA da sauran kyaututtuka ke amfani da ita. Shafinta yana da layi ɗaya, Haɗi. Taɓa shi: qFT8 yana nuna kati mai lambar da manyan haruffa, lokacin da lambar za ta ci gaba da aiki (yana ƙirgawa baya) da adireshin a matsayin lambar QR, tare da KWAFI URL da BUƊE. Buɗe shafin a wannan na'ura ko ka duba lambar da wayarka, ka shiga, ka yi alama a sunan kiranka ka amince da qFT8 (yana neman izinin karanta da loda QSO kawai); katin yana rufewa da kansa da zarar ka amsa. Na'urar sarrafawa ta nesa ta yanar gizo tana nuna lamba, adireshi da lambar QR iri ɗaya yayin da qFT8 ke jira. Sai ka taɓa TABBATAR ka yi alama a akwatin. Ana loda kowane QSO yayin da kake rubuta shi, a ƙarƙashin sunan kira da ke saman Saituna (ko sunan kiran QSO na tafi-da-gidanka, misali /P); gyara yana canza QSO a can; qFT8 ba ya taɓa share komai a HAMLOG.online. Kowace na'ura tana haɗa kanta (ba a fitar da haɗin tare da saituna); cire tsohuwar haɗi a ƙarƙashin Connected loggers a shafin yanar gizo. LODA na kunna RAZA shi ma yana nuni zuwa HAMLOG.online: idan wannan sabis yana kunne, QSO na kunnawar sun riga sun kasance a can, kuma kana aika kunnawar zuwa mai kula da RAZA a shafin yanar gizo.

    Shirye-shiryen rajista a cibiyar sadarwarku

    qFT8 zai iya aika kowane QSO da kuka rubuta zuwa shirye-shiryen rajista da na taswira a cibiyar sadarwarku ta gida. A ƙarƙashin Daidaita QSO nemi layin FT8 network log (tashar UDP 2237) ko ADIF over UDP (tashar UDP 2333) sannan ka buɗe giyarsa. A shafinsa rubuta adireshin kwamfutar da ke gudanar da dayan shirin (127.0.0.1 ita ce wannan kwamfutar) da tashar, danna TABBATAR, sannan zabi akwatinsa. FT8 network log na iya aika abubuwan da kuka fassara da yanayin tasharku (Har ila yau aika abubuwan da na fassara da yanayin tashata (don taswira da faɗakarwa)), wadanda taswira da fadakarwar dayan shirin ke amfani da su. Wadannan sakonni ba su da shaidar karba, don haka QSO da aka aika ta wannan hanya ba a taba kirga shi a matsayin wanda aka loda ba. Saitunan suna ambaton, a karkashin kowanne, shirye-shiryen da aka gwada shi da su.

    N1MM Logger+ (Windows, an gwada da sigar 1.0.11469) yana karɓar QSO daga qFT8 a tashar UDP 2237. A tagarsa Config > Configure Ports duba Enable kusa da tashar UDP 2237 sannan a sake kunna shi; bayan haka a qFT8 ƙara FT8 network log, ko ADIF over UDP da tashar da aka canza zuwa 2237, da adireshin wannan kwamfutar. Kowane QSO yana bayyana a littafinsa tare da kira, mitar, yanayi, rahotanni, lokator da lokaci. Ba ya saurare a tashar 2333.

    Log4OM 2 (Windows, an gwada da sigar 2.42.1) yana aiki ta hanyoyi biyu. A saitunan haɗinsa ƙara haɗin UDP mai shigowa da samfurin JT (tashar 2237) don FT8 network log, ko da samfurin ADIF (tashar 2234; saka tashar iri ɗaya a qFT8) don ADIF over UDP. Haɗin ADIF mai fita zuwa tashar 2333 ta kwamfutar qFT8 yana aika QSO da aka rubuta a ciki zuwa mai karɓar ADIF over UDP na qFT8.

    N3FJP AC Log (Windows, an gwada da sigar 7.0.12) yana karɓar FT8 network log a tashar UDP 2237: duba akwatin da ke sauraron shirye-shiryen FT8 a tagar API ta menu ɗinsa na Settings. Ba ya karɓar ADIF over UDP.

    CQRLOG (Linux, an gwada da sigar 2.5.2) yana karɓar FT8 network log a yanayinsa na nesa don shirye-shiryen FT8 (tashar 2237; a abubuwan da ya fi so ɗauki mitar daga ɗayan shirin), da ADIF over UDP a yanayinsa na nesa don ADIF (tashar 2333).

    GridTracker2 (an gwada da sigar 2.261019.5) yana nuna a taswira kuma yana rubuta abin da FT8 network log ke aikawa zuwa tashar 2237; idan karɓar ADIF UDP ɗinsa tana kunne yana karɓar ADIF over UDP ma a tashar 2333. Tura rajistarsa tana aika QSO ɗinsa zuwa masu karɓar qFT8.

    Akasin haka, qFT8 na iya karbar QSO daga wasu shirye-shirye a cikin rajistarsa. A cikin Ƙarin saituna > SABOBI zabi layin mai karba na tsarin da dayan shirin ke aikawa: FT8 network log (UDP 2237), ADIF over UDP (UDP 2333), mahadar AC Log (TCP 1100) ko mahadar DXLab (TCP 52001). Shafinsa yana nuna adireshin da za a sa a cikin dayan shirin kuma yana ba ku damar canza tashar da karbar QSO daga wasu adireshi kawai. Babu abin da aka karba ta wannan hanya da zai iya watsawa, canza rediyo ko share QSO. Masu karba a kashe suke har sai kun kunna su; a iPhone suna aiki ne kawai lokacin da qFT8 ke bude kuma ba sa shiga kungiyar multicast.

    Rajista ta UDP ba cikakkiya ba ce: FT8 network log da ADIF over UDP suna aika kowane QSO sau ɗaya, ba tare da takardar karɓa ba, don haka idan wannan fakiti ya ɓace (misali a kan mummunar haɗin Wi-Fi) QSO ɗin ya ɓace. Babu kalmar sirri a cikin waɗannan tsare-tsare ma: mai karɓa da ka kunna yana bar kowa a cikin cibiyar sadarwa ya aika QSO da sunanka, kuma abokin ciniki yana aika QSO ɗinka ga duk wanda ke saurare a adireshin da ka rubuta. Saituna tana nuna wannan a ƙarƙashin kowane maɓallinsu, a kan layukan da kuma shafukansu. Jerin adiresoshin da mai karɓa zai karɓa yana iyakance wanda zai iya aika masa. Don QSO ɗinka, ana ba da shawarar qFT8log maimakon haka: tana dacewa a tsakanin qFT8, ita ce mafi aminci, kuma an ɓoye ta (duba qFT8log).

    PSKReporter

    PSKReporter yana tattara rahotannin karɓa daga ko'ina a duniya: wace tasha aka ji a ina, a kan wane band da yanayi, da yadda ƙarfinta yake. A cikin Saituna, ƙarƙashin Bayar da rahoto, Loda rahotanni zuwa PSKReporter (a kashe ta asali) yana aika tashoshin da ka warware, a kowane yanayi da qFT8 ke warwarewa, tare da sunan kiranka, locator ɗinka da Rig da eriyarka. Gear ɗinsa yana buɗe shafin:

    • Kuma loda rahotanni lokacin sadarwa ta hanyar lasifika/makirufo: a kashe ta asali, don kada a aika komai yayin da manhajar ke saurare ta makirufon na'ura maimakon kebul zuwa rediyo.
    • Rahotannin da ke jiran fita (A halin yanzu ana adana rubuce-rubuce 12) da lokacin da za su fita (Lodi na gaba a ... UTC). Ana aika su tare kusan kowane minti 5. Ana bayar da rahoton tasha ɗaya a kan band da yanayi ɗaya sau ɗaya kawai a kowane minti 15 a mafi yawa, kuma ana jefar da rahoton da ya girmi minti 10. ƊORA YANZU yana aika su nan take; GOGE CACHE yana share su ba tare da aikawa ba.
    • SAKE SAITA ZUWA NA ASALI.

    A kishiyar hanya, Sauke spots daga PSKReporter (Saituna, Tushen bayanai) yana kawo waɗanda suka ji siginarka ya nuna su a taswirar duniya. WSPRnet, cibiyar sadarwar beacon, an bayyana ta tare da WSPR.

    Ayyukan LQ8 a taswira kai tsaye

    Sanar da ayyukan LQ8 ga sabis na qFT8 (Saituna, Bayar da rahoto, a kashe ta asali): yayin da kake aika sigina a LQ8 ta rediyo, qFT8 yana aika sunan kiranka, locator ɗinka mai haruffa 4, band da mita, yanayi, lokaci da sigar qFT8. Sauran qFT8 da taswirar kai tsaye a lq8.org/live suna nuna shi na minti 10; ana ajiye shi a ƙwaƙwalwa kawai. A karo na farko da ka shirya TX a LQ8 yayin da yake a kashe, qFT8 yana tambaya sau ɗaya ko a kunna shi. Kuma sanar da shi lokacin sadarwa ta hanyar lasifika/makirufo yana yin haka idan babu rediyo da aka haɗa da kebul. Sauke ayyukan LQ8 daga sabar qFT8 (Saituna, Tushen bayanai) yana nuna ayyukan LQ8 na sauran tashoshi a taswirarka.

    ↑ Koma zuwa Abubuwan da ke ciki

    Haɗi da QRZ

    qFT8 tana da haɗi mai zurfi da QRZ.com domin log ɗinka ya riƙa daidaituwa ta atomatik. Lura cewa haɗi da QRZ sabis ne na biyan kuɗi wanda yake buƙatar biyan kuɗin QRZ (XML Logbook Data ko sama). Idan kana son na kyauta, yi la'akari da haɗi da LoTW, wanda ARRL take bayarwa kyauta.

    Yadda yake Aiki

    Domin kunna haɗi da QRZ, dole ka shigar da Mabuɗin API na QRZ naka a shafin saituna. Kana iya samun mabuɗin API ɗinka a shafin QRZ Logbook API na hukuma. Bayan ka shigar da shi kuma ka danna TABBATAR, kana iya kunna "Ɗora QSOs zuwa QRZ da zarar an kammala su, kuma a bango" domin aiki ta atomatik a bango.

    • Lodawa ta atomatik: Duk lokacin da QSO ya cika (ya kai matakin 6/6 DONE), qFT8 tana ƙoƙarin loda shi nan take zuwa log ɗinka na QRZ.
    • Aminci a bango: Idan lodawa ta kasa (misali babu intanet ko sabar ta ƙi amsa), qFT8 ba ta haƙura. Wani aikin bango zai sake gwada lodawar da ta kasa kowane minti 5 ta atomatik. Wannan yana faruwa a ɓoye, kuma yana tabbatar da cewa log ɗinka zai kai intanet idan haɗi ya dawo.
    • Tsallakewa da hikima: qFT8 tana kare log ɗinka ta loda QSO da suka dace da alamar kiran da aka saita yanzu da yanayin FT8 kawai. Ana tsallake Non-FT8, QSO na wasu alamomin kira, ko QSO da aka shigo da su daga LoTW ta atomatik domin kada a yi buƙatun API da yawa.

    Daidaita Tabbatarwa

    Haɗin ya wuce lodawa kawai. qFT8 tana kuma tambayar QRZ ko an tabbatar da QSO naka.

    • Dubawa nan take: Daƙiƙa 30 bayan QSO ya cika kuma an loda shi, qFT8 za ta sake duba QRZ ko ɗayan tashar ma ta loda QSO ɗin; idan ta yi, za a daidaita QSO a matsayin an tabbatar nan take.
    • Daidaitawa ta atomatik: Tun asali, qFT8 tana tambayar QRZ kowane awa 6 (ana iya canzawa), QSO 250 a kowane lokaci, tana zagaya dukkan log ɗinka da ba a tabbatar ba, domin ko tsofaffin QSO ma a duba su.
    • Tauraron tabbatarwa (★): Idan aka tabbatar da QSO da aka loda zuwa QRZ, a QRZ ko LoTW, ana haskaka shi da tauraron zinariya. Kana kuma iya tabbatar da kowane QSO da hannu domin nuna wannan alama. Idan QRZ ya ba da rahoton tabbatarwa, alamar hali a shafin Log na QSO za ta koma tauraron zinariya ta atomatik.
    • Daidaitawa da hannu: Ana kuma iya fara sabunta QSO 250 da suka fi daɗewa ba a daidaita su ba da hannu a saituna.

    Shawara: Kana iya ganin ci gaban daidaitawa a saituna a ƙarƙashin "QSO mafi tsufa da aka daidaita a". Babu gaggawa a kan wannan, domin qFT8 za ta kula da shi a hankali.

    Dabarar Daidaita Log

    A saitunan daidaitawa, kana iya zaɓar yadda qFT8 take gane cewa an "loda QSO gaba ɗaya" ta zaɓin "Yi la'akari da QSO da aka ɗora (✓) lokacin da aka ɗora zuwa:":

    • Kowanne daga LoTW da QRZ: Zaɓi mai kyau idan ka saita sabis ɗaya kawai.
    • Duka biyu: Zaɓi wannan domin mafi yawan tsaro: dole a aika QSO zuwa sabis biyun da nasara kafin a ɗauke shi a matsayin an gama kuma a fara duba tabbatarwa.
    • QRZ kawai (sai dai an shigo da su daga LoTW): Ana ba da shawara idan QRZ ne babban log ɗinka. Lura: QSO da aka shigo da su daga LoTW ana sa musu alamar an daidaita amma ba a loda su zuwa QRZ, domin guje wa iyakokin API (wanda yake barin lodawa ɗaya-ɗaya kawai); ana iya daidaita su a cikin QRZ kansa daga baya.
    • LoTW: Ana ba da shawara idan LoTW ne babban tushenka.
    ↑ Koma zuwa Abubuwan da ke ciki

    Haɗi da Logbook of the World (LoTW)

    qFT8 tana da haɗi mai ƙarfi da ARRL Logbook of the World (LoTW). Ana ba da shawara sosai a haɗa da LoTW domin tabbatar da QSO naka da samun awards kamar DXCC da WAS.

    Abubuwan da ake Buƙata

    Domin kunna fasalolin LoTW gaba ɗaya, har da lodawa ta atomatik da duba tabbatarwa, dole ka shigar da bayanai huɗu a shafin saituna:

    • 1. Sunan mai amfani na LoTW: Alamar kiranka ta hukuma da kake amfani da ita a LoTW.
    • 2. Kalmar sirri ta LoTW: Kalmar sirri da kake shiga shafin yanar gizo na LoTW da ita. Ta bambanta da kalmar sirri ta takardar shaida.
    • 3. Takardar shaida ta TQSL (.p12): Fayil na sa hannun dijital da aka fitar daga manhajar TQSL. Domin yadda ake nema da fitar da wannan takardar shaida, duba shafin taimako na LoTW na hukuma.
    • 4. Kalmar sirri ta takardar shaida: Kalmar sirri da ka saita lokacin fitar da fayil na .p12 daga TQSL (tana iya zama fanko idan ba ka saita ɗaya ba).

    Wurin tasha ta atomatik: Bayanai kamar Yankin ITU, Yankin CQ, da Sunan Tasha ana gane su ta atomatik daga alamar kiranka. Ba a buƙatar saita waɗannan da hannu.

    Fasaloli

    • Lodawa ta atomatik: Idan an kunna Ɗora QSOs zuwa LoTW da zarar an kammala su, kuma a bango, qFT8 za ta sa hannu kuma ta loda kowane sabon QSO zuwa LoTW a bango da zarar an rubuta shi.
    • Duba tabbatarwa daƙiƙa 30 bayan lodin QSO: Idan an kunna, qFT8 za ta yi duba tabbatarwa guda ɗaya, daidai daƙiƙa 30 bayan lodawa mai nasara. Wannan yana ba ka damar ganin ko an tabbatar da QSO ɗinka kusan nan take.
    • Daidaitawa a bango lokaci-lokaci: Saita Daidaita tabbatarwa kowane awa __ domin duba sabbin tabbatarwa a bango a tazarar da kake so (misali kowane awa 6). Wannan yana sa dukkan log ɗinka ya kasance sabo ba tare da ka yi komai ba.
    • Shigo da log: Danna SHIGO DA QSOS DAGA LoTW domin sauke dukkan tarihin LoTW naka zuwa qFT8. Lura cewa waɗannan QSO da aka shigo da su ba a loda su zuwa QRZ ta atomatik, domin guje wa iyakokin API.
    • Alamar jira: Ƙaramin ɗigon rawaya a kan maɓallin Rajistar QSO yana nuna cewa akwai QSO da suke jiran a loda su zuwa LoTW.

    Dabarar Daidaita Log

    A saitunan daidaitawa, kana iya zaɓar yadda qFT8 take gane cewa an "loda QSO gaba ɗaya" ta zaɓin "Yi la'akari da QSO da aka ɗora (✓) lokacin da aka ɗora zuwa:":

    • Kowanne daga LoTW da QRZ: Zaɓi mai kyau idan ka saita sabis ɗaya kawai.
    • Duka biyu: Zaɓi wannan domin mafi yawan tsaro: dole a aika QSO zuwa sabis biyun da nasara kafin a ɗauke shi a matsayin an gama kuma a fara duba tabbatarwa.
    • QRZ kawai (sai dai an shigo da su daga LoTW): Ana ba da shawara idan QRZ ne babban log ɗinka. Lura: QSO da aka shigo da su daga LoTW ana sa musu alamar an daidaita amma ba a loda su zuwa QRZ, domin guje wa iyakokin API (wanda yake barin lodawa ɗaya-ɗaya kawai); ana iya daidaita su a cikin QRZ kansa daga baya.
    • LoTW: Ana ba da shawara idan LoTW ne babban tushenka.
    ↑ Koma zuwa Abubuwan da ke ciki

    Wavelog

    Wavelog shiri ne na littafin rubuta tuntuɓa da kake gudanarwa a sabar yanar gizo taka (ƙaramar kwamfuta a gida ko wurin yanar gizo da aka yi hayar) kuma ka buɗe a mai bincike. qFT8 yana aika kowane QSO zuwa gare shi da zarar an rubuta shi, kuma yana karanta waɗanda Wavelog naka ya tabbatar. qFT8 yana magana da shi ta hanyar mahaɗin Wavelog da aka wallafa: docs.wavelog.org/developer/api.

    Saita shi

    1. A cikin Wavelog, buɗe menu na asusu > API Keys ka ƙirƙiri maɓalli mai haƙƙin karatu da rubutu.
    2. A cikin qFT8, buɗe Saituna, danna gunkin gear kusa da Wavelog ka rubuta Adireshin sabar (adireshin da kake buɗe Wavelog da shi, misali https://log.example.org) da Maɓallin API. Adireshin da aka rubuta ba tare da http:// ko https:// ba ana fara gwada shi da https; idan sabar tana magana da http na yau da kullum kawai (sabar wani qFT8 tana yin haka), qFT8 yana amfani da http kuma shafin yana cewa maɓallin yana tafiya ba tare da kariya ba.
    3. Na zaɓi, domin a aika gyare-gyare ma: ƙirƙiri alamar API v2 a cikin Wavelog (menu na asusu > API Keys, shafin API v2, tare da izinin qso:read da qso:write) kuma rubuta ta a matsayin Alamar gyara. TABBATAR yana duba ta ba tare da canza komai ba.
    4. Danna TABBATAR. qFT8 yana duba maɓallin ya jera wuraren tashar Wavelog naka; zaɓi wanda QSOs naka suke ciki (idan ɗaya ne kawai, ana zaɓar maka shi).
    5. Saka alama a Wavelog a saman Saituna. Daga yanzu kowane sabon QSO yana tafiya zuwa gare shi, kuma waɗanda aka rubuta a baya ma ana aika su.

    Abin da ke faruwa

    • Babu intanet: QSO da ba za a iya aikawa ba yana jira a cikin littafin kuma ana aika shi idan sabar ta sake amsawa (ana sake gwadawa bayan minti 1, sannan ba da yawa ba, har sau ɗaya a sa'a). Yayin da ba a iya isa ga sabar, layinta a cikin Saituna yana zama ja da "Ba a iya haɗawa tun daga ...".
    • Kwafi: QSO da Wavelog naka ya riga ya samu ana lissafa shi a matsayin an ɗora.
    • An ƙi: QSO da Wavelog ya ƙi ba a sake aika shi sai ka gyara shi ko ka sake ɗora shi; editan QSO yana nuna dalili. Maɓallin da ya daina aiki yana dakatar da ɗorawa har sai ka sake danna TABBATAR.
    • Tabbatarwa: sau ɗaya a kowace awa (da kuma lokacin da kuka danna ƊORA YANZU) qFT8 yana tambayar Wavelog waɗanne daga cikin QSOs ɗinku aka tabbatar (LoTW, QSL na takarda, eQSL) kuma yana yi musu alama a layukan An loda da Matsayi na editan QSO, layuka da alamomi iri ɗaya da na QRZ da LoTW.
    • Gyare-gyare: idan an saita Alamar gyara, ana samun QSO da aka gyara a cikin Wavelog ɗinku kuma a sabunta shi a can (canjin mafi sabo yana nasara; ba a taɓa taɓa alamomin tabbatarwa da Wavelog ke riƙewa ba). Ba tare da ita ba, hanyar lodawa ta Wavelog za ta iya ƙara QSOs kawai, don haka ba a aika gyara: canza shi a cikin Wavelog ma.
    • QSOs na wasu alamomin kira: QSO da aka rubuta da wata alamar kira daban da taku (alamar kira ta kulob ko ta taron musamman) ana riƙe shi daga QRZ da LoTW, amma yana tafiya zuwa Wavelog matuƙar an yi alama a Aika har da QSO da aka yi da wasu alamomin kira (na asali).
    • Gogewa: qFT8 ba ya goge komai a Wavelog ko kaɗan. Goge QSO a can da kanka.
    • "Ɗauki QSO a matsayin an ɗora idan an ɗora a nan" a shafin yana yanke ko ɗorawa zuwa Wavelog yana ƙidayuwa ga alamar "an ɗora" (a kashe ta tsohuwa).

    Shigo da QSOs daga Wavelog

    SHIGO DA QSOS DAGA WAVELOG, a ƙasan shafin Wavelog, yana kwafar QSOs na wurin tashar da aka zaɓa zuwa log ɗin qFT8, tare da tabbatarwarsu ta LoTW, QSL na takarda da eQSL.

    • Abin da ake ɗauka: kowane yanayi. Daga, Zuwa, Bands da Alamar kira ta fara da suna taƙaita shi (kowane = babu iyaka). Ana tsallake QSOs da aka yi da wata alamar kira banda taka, sai dai idan an saka alama a Shigo da QSOs don alamun kira banda na yanzu.
    • Ya riga ya kasance a log: QSO da log ɗin ya riga ya samu ana haɗa shi, ba a ƙara shi sau biyu ba (ƙa'idodin iri ɗaya da shigowar QRZ da LoTW); QSO da ka gyara a nan yana riƙe gyaranka kuma yana ɗaukar tabbatarwa kawai.
    • Ba a taɓa mayar da shi: ana lissafa QSOs da aka shigo da su a matsayin sun riga sun kasance a Wavelog. Gyaran da ka yi daga baya a qFT8 yana tafiya can (da Alamar gyara).
    • TSAYAR yana ƙare shigowar bayan ɓangaren da yake kai; abin da ya shigo har yanzu yana nan. Layin ƙarƙashin maɓallin yana ƙidaya abin da aka karɓa, aka ƙara kuma aka haɗa.
    • Ba a goge komai ba, a nan ko a Wavelog. QSO da ka goge a qFT8 yana dawowa tare da shigowa ta gaba.

    Haɗi mai tsaro

    • Fi son adireshin https://. Da http:// maɓallin yana tafiya ba tare da kariya ba: yi amfani da shi a hanyar sadarwar gidanka kawai.
    • Android 6 da 7.0 ba sa amincewa da wasu sabbin takaddun shaida da yawancin irin waɗannan sabobin ke amfani da su; a can, sabar https:// na iya kasa haɗawa. A hanyar sadarwar gidanka adireshin sabar na http:// yana aiki.
    • iPhone yana haɗawa da adireshin https:// kawai.

    Kwatanta Wavelog da Cloudlog

    Ɗorawa, kwafi, jerin jira ba tare da intanet ba

    Cloudlog eh

    Tabbatarwa da ke komawa zuwa qFT8

    Maɓalli na karatu kawai
    Wavelog an ƙi

    Wavelog an karɓa

    QSOs na wasu alamomin kira

    Kunnawa (POTA / SOTA)

    Wavelog yana ɗaukar wurin shakatawa ko kololuwar ka daga wurin tashar da QSO ke zuwa, ba daga QSO kansa ba. Don kunnawa, ƙirƙiri (ko zaɓi) wurin tasha a Wavelog mai lambar wurin shakatawa ko kololuwa a filin POTA / SOTA da kuma cikin sunansa, misali Park US-0001. Yayin da kunnawar ke gudana, qFT8 yana ɗora QSOs ɗinsa zuwa wannan wurin da kansa. Idan babu wurin da yake da lambar, qFT8 yana faɗa sau ɗaya a kowace kunnawa kuma yana ɗora zuwa wurinka na yau da kullum, ba tare da lambar ba.

    ↑ Koma zuwa Abubuwan da ke ciki

    Cloudlog

    Cloudlog shirin ne da Wavelog ya samo asali daga gare shi: littafin rubuta tuntuɓa a sabar yanar gizo taka. Wani sabis ne daban a cikin qFT8, da nasa saituna, ana saita shi daidai kamar Wavelog (a cikin Cloudlog: menu na asusu > API > ƙirƙiri maɓalli mai haƙƙin karatu da rubutu). Mahaɗinsa da aka wallafa: github.com/magicbug/Cloudlog/wiki/API.

    Bambance-bambance daga Wavelog

    • Babu tabbatarwa da ke dawowa: Cloudlog ba ya ba wasu shirye-shirye hanyar karanta waɗanne QSOs aka tabbatar, don haka qFT8 yana ɗora masa kawai.
    • Sunan kiran tasha: Cloudlog yana ƙin QSO wanda sunan kiran tashar sa ya bambanta da na wurin tashar da aka zaɓa. Irin wannan QSO yana bayyana a matsayin an ƙi a cikin edita; zaɓa masa wurin tasha da ya dace a cikin Cloudlog.
    • Ba a aika gyare-gyare; ba a taɓa goge komai; rashin intanet, kwafi, jan bayanin "Ba a iya haɗawa" da bayanan haɗi mai tsaro duk kamar na Wavelog.
    • Shigo da: wasu shirye-shirye ba za su iya karanta Cloudlog ba. Don kawo log ɗinsa cikin qFT8, fitar da fayil na ADIF a Cloudlog ka yi amfani da SHIGO DA QSOS DAGA ADIF a cikin Saituna > Shigo/Fitar da logs (duba ADIF).

    Kunnawa (POTA / SOTA)

    Cloudlog yana ɗaukar wurin shakatawa ko kololuwar ka daga wurin tashar da QSO ke zuwa, ba daga QSO kansa ba. Don kunnawa, ƙirƙiri (ko zaɓi) wurin tasha a Cloudlog mai lambar wurin shakatawa ko kololuwa a filin POTA / SOTA da kuma cikin sunansa, misali Park US-0001. Yayin da kunnawar ke gudana, qFT8 yana ɗora QSOs ɗinsa zuwa wannan wurin da kansa. Idan babu wurin da yake da lambar, qFT8 yana faɗa sau ɗaya a kowace kunnawa kuma yana ɗora zuwa wurinka na yau da kullum, ba tare da lambar ba.

    ↑ Koma zuwa Abubuwan da ke ciki

    eQSL

    eQSL (eqsl.cc) yana musayar katunan QSL na lantarki: kowane QSO da aka aika can yana zama kati a akwatin saƙon eQSL na ɗayan tasha. qFT8 yana ƙara katuna kawai; ba ya taɓa goge komai a eQSL.

    Saita shi

    1. Taɓa giya ta jere na Daidaita QSO da eQSL a saman Saituna.
    2. Shigar da Sunan mai amfani na eQSL naka (yawanci sunan kiranka) da Kalmar sirri ta eQSL. Idan kana da asusun eQSL da yawa (wurare) ƙarƙashin sunan kira ɗaya, shigar da Sunan laƙabi na QTH na wurin yadda aka rubuta shi a eqsl.cc; in ba haka ba, bar shi babu komai kuma eQSL zai zaɓi asusun bisa ranar QSO.
    3. Danna TABBATAR. Yana karanta akwatin saƙon eQSL naka ne kawai don duba shiga: ba a aika kati.
    4. Yi alama a Daidaita QSO da eQSL. QSOs da ba a aika ba tukuna za su tafi nan da nan, ɗaya a kowane daƙiƙa, kuma kowane sabon QSO yana tafiya idan an yi masa rajista.

    Abin da ya kamata ka sani

    • Ɗauki QSO a matsayin an loda idan an loda shi nan a kashe yake don eQSL: kati a eQSL bai isa shi kaɗai ya sa alamar kore ta rajista ba (duba Ayyukan loda QSO). Yi masa alama idan kana so.
    • Ba za a iya canza kati ba. Idan ka gyara QSO da ke a eQSL tuni, babu abin da ake aikawa da kansa. Don aika katin da aka gyara yi amfani da Sake loda zuwa eQSL a editan QSO; tsohon katin yana zama a akwatin saƙon ɗayan tashar har sai ka goge shi a eqsl.cc.
    • LQ8: eQSL yana karɓar hanyoyin ADIF na hukuma kawai, don haka ana aika QSOs ɗinsa a matsayin MFSK, kuma sharhin yana ambaton ainihin hanyar („QSO by qFT8, mode LQ8“). Sauran ayyukan suna riƙe nasu tsarin.
    • QSO da eQSL ke da shi tuni ana amsa shi a matsayin kwafi kuma ana ƙidaya shi a matsayin an aika. Wanda ya ƙi (misali ranar da babu asusun naka da ya rufe, ko hanyar da bai sani ba) yana bayyana a matsayin an ƙi a editan QSO, da kalmomin eQSL da kansa, kuma ba a sake gwadawa har sai ka sake loda shi.
    • Idan babu intanet, ko lokacin da eQSL ke kashe saboda gyara, katunan suna jira kuma ana sake gwadawa daga baya (bayan minti 1, sannan tsawon lokaci, har zuwa awa ɗaya); jan bayanin Ba a iya haɗawa na jere yana faɗin tun yaushe.
