Idan an ƙi kalmar sirri (misali ka canza ta a eqsl.cc), aikawa yana tsayawa har sai ka sake yin TABBATAR.
    • Tabbatarwa: Kusan sau ɗaya a kowace awa (da kuma a UPLOAD NOW), qFT8 yana karanta akwatin saƙonka na eQSL (katunan da sauran tashoshi suka aiko maka). Katin da yake da alamar kira da band iri ɗaya, yanayin da ya dace, da lokaci cikin mintuna 30 daga wani QSO a log ɗinka yana yiwa wannan QSO alama a matsayin eQSL ya tabbatar da shi: layin eQSL na editan QSO yana cewa An tabbatar, kuma alamar tabbatarwa da matattarar log suna ƙidaya shi kamar tabbatarwar QRZ ko LoTW. Katin MFSK yana tabbatar da QSO ɗinka na LQ8, wanda ya ci gaba da zama LQ8. Ana karanta akwatin ne kawai: ba a ƙara komai a log ɗinka ba, kuma ba a canja ko goge komai a eQSL ba.
    ↑ Koma zuwa Abubuwan da ke ciki

    qFT8 a matsayin sabar Wavelog

    A cikin Saituna > Ƙarin saituna > SABOBI > Sabar Wavelog, qFT8 yana amsawa a hanyar sadarwar gidanka kamar shi Wavelog ne: kowane shirin da zai iya aika QSOs zuwa Wavelog (har da wani qFT8) zai iya aika su zuwa wannan qFT8 maimakon haka.

    Saita shi

    1. Kunna Kunna sabar Wavelog, ka riƙe Tashar haɗi (13500) ko ka zaɓi wata, ka rubuta Maɓallin API mai haruffa aƙalla 8 (ka sa shi ya yi tsawo kuma na bazuwar: shi ne kaɗai ke hana wasu shiga).
    2. A cikin ɗayan shirin saita Wavelog da adireshin da shafin ke nunawa, an rubuta shi gaba ɗaya: http://, adireshin wannan na'ura, alamar colon da tashar jiragen ruwa, misali http://192.168.1.20:13500/ (yawancin shirye-shiryen log suna buƙatar a rubuta http:// da tashar; wani qFT8 yana samunsu da kansa), maɓalli ɗaya da tasha 1 (a wani qFT8: TABBATAR yana zaɓarta).

    Ana iya canza tashar a kowane lokaci: sabar tana komawa sabuwar tashar nan take (kuma tana tsayawa nan take idan an kashe ta).

    Kallon log a cikin burauza

    Buɗe adireshi ɗaya (http://192.168.1.20:13500/) a cikin burauza a cibiyar sadarwar gidanku: bayan kun rubuta maɓallin sabar, yana nuna log ɗin QSO ɗinku, na sabo da farko, don karantawa kawai; tsofaffin QSOs suna lodawa yayin da kuke gungurawa, sababbi ko waɗanda aka canza kuma suna bayyana da kansu. Ba a taɓa aika maɓallin ba: burauza yana amsa ƙalubale na sau ɗaya da ƙima da aka yi daga maɓallin, kuma adireshin da ya amsa kuskure ana toshe shi na ɗan lokaci. Sabar Cloudlog da sabar log ta qFT8 suna nuna shafi ɗaya a tashoshinsu, da maɓalli ko kalmar sirri nasu.

    Abin da yake yi

    • Kowane QSO da aka karɓa, a kowane yanayi kuma da kowane sunan kira, yana shiga littafin da jerin QSOs, sannan zuwa sabis ɗin da aka saita a nan (QRZ, LoTW, Wavelog, ...), ba ya komawa inda ya fito. QSO da littafin ya riga ya samu ana amsa shi a matsayin kwafi kuma ba a sake tura shi.
    • Yana amsa wuraren shiga da shirin rubuta tuntuɓa ke amfani da su: api/qso (ɗaya ko da yawa QSOs a matsayin ADIF), api/version, api/station_info, api/check_auth, api/auth, api/get_contacts_adif (domin qFT8 da ke can ƙasa a cikin sarƙa ya iya karanta tabbatarwar) da api/radio (ana karɓa kuma a yi watsi da shi). Babu hanyar canza ko goge QSO ta cikinsa.
    • Tsaro: hanyar sadarwar gida kawai, http na yau da kullum (kamar sabar yanar gizo ta sarrafa daga nesa); ana kwatanta maɓallin ta hanyar da ba ta fallasa shi; adireshin da ya aika maɓallai 10 marasa daidai ana hana shi na minti 10; mafi yawa buƙatu 20 a daƙiƙa ga kowane adireshi, 128 KB da 500 QSOs a kowace buƙata; duk abin da ba a tsara shi daidai ba yana samun amsar kuskure.
    • Inda yake aiki: a Android har a bango idan "gudu a bango" a kunne yake; a kwamfuta muddin shirin a buɗe yake; a iPhone kawai muddin qFT8 a buɗe yake.

    Sarƙoƙi

    Sabobi da sabis suna haɗuwa su zama sarƙoƙi: tasha da yawa (wayoyi, kwamfutoci) suna aika QSOs ɗinsu zuwa qFT8 ɗaya wanda yake tattara su kuma shi ne kaɗai ke ɗorawa zuwa QRZ da LoTW; wannan kuma zai iya aika komai zuwa Wavelog ko wani qFT8 da aka ajiye a matsayin madadin. Tabbatarwa tana komawa ƙasa: qFT8 mai tattarawa yana samun su daga QRZ / LoTW / Wavelog nasa, kuma qFT8 ɗin da ke aika zuwa sabar Wavelog nasa suna karanta su a kowace sa'a. Ba a taɓa tura kwafi ba, don haka madauki a cikin sarƙa yana tsayawa da kansa.

    ↑ Koma zuwa Abubuwan da ke ciki

    qFT8 a matsayin sabar Cloudlog

    Daidai kamar sabar Wavelog, don shirye-shiryen da ke aika QSOs zuwa Cloudlog, a tashar sa (13501) da maɓallinsa. Yana amsawa kamar yadda Cloudlog yake yi: babu api/version kuma babu api/get_contacts_adif (don haka babu tabbatarwar da ke komawa ƙasa ta cikinsa), kuma yana karɓar ɗorawa a tsarin fom ma. Dukan sabobin biyu suna ciyar da littafi ɗaya: QSO da ɗaya ya karɓa kwafi ne ga ɗayan.

    ↑ Koma zuwa Abubuwan da ke ciki

    Summits On The Air (SOTA)

    qFT8 tana da cikakkun kayan aiki na ciki ga masu harbi (Chasers / Hunters) da masu kunnawa (Activators) na SOTA (Summits On The Air): ɗauko spots ta atomatik, bin lambobi a ainihin lokaci, haskaka nasarori, da matatun fitar da ADIF na musamman.

    Ɗauko Spots na Kololuwa ta Atomatik

    Idan akwai intanet, qFT8 tana haɗawa da API na hukuma na SOTA a bango ta atomatik (kowane daƙiƙa 90) domin ɗauko kunna kololuwa da ke gudana a duk duniya.

    • Nunawa a Taswirar duniya: Ana zana masu kunna kololuwa da ke aiki a Taswirar duniya da alamar kololuwa (⛰️), alamar kira, sunan kololuwa, tsayi, da mita.
    • Daidaita lamba ta atomatik: Idan ka ji mai kunnawa a iska, qFT8 tana kwatanta alamar kiransa da spots domin samo ainihin lambar kololuwarsa (misali HB/BE-001, W6/NC-001).
    • Cikewa daga baya ta atomatik: Idan ka kammala QSO da tashar da take kiran CQ SOTA kafin a sauke spot ɗinta ko kafin a karɓi lamba, qFT8 tana duba spots da ke shigowa kuma tana saka lambar kololuwa a QSO da aka rubuta kwanan nan (a cikin awa 1) ta atomatik.

    Aiki a matsayin Mai Harbi na SOTA (Chaser / Hunter)

    Domin harbin tashoshin da suke kunna kololuwa:

    1. A shafin Tashoshi, saita jerin zaɓi na fifikon manufa zuwa Mai neman SOTA (ko Mai neman POTA + SOTA).
    2. Alamomi & ginshiƙin lamba: Tashoshin SOTA da ke aiki (waɗanda aka gani a intanet ko suke watsa CQ SOTA) ana sa musu alamar SOTA a Layi na 2. Haka kuma, sunan ginshiƙin DXCC yana komawa Lambar SOTA, yana nuna lambar kololuwa kai tsaye.
    3. Haskaka kololuwar da aka yi aiki da ita da wadda ake buƙata:
      • Kololuwar da ba a yi aiki da ita ba: Tana da alamar SOTA mai launin purple mai haske da rubutun lamba na purple, wato sabuwar kololuwa ce da za a harba.
      • Kololuwar da aka riga aka yi aiki da ita: Ana nuna ta da alama mai launin toka, domin ka bambanta sabbin kololuwa da waɗanda suke a log ɗinka nan take.
    4. Harbi da AUTO: A yanayin AUTO, qFT8 tana fifita kiran masu kunna SOTA da ba a yi aiki da su ba a kan masu kiran CQ na yau da kullum, bisa sigina mafi ƙarfi (SNR).

    Sarrafawa & Gyaran Lambobin SOTA a Log

    qFT8 tana da kayan aiki na musamman domin duba da gyaran tarihin harbin SOTA naka:

    • Kallon Mai harbi SOTA a shafin Log na QSO: A shafin Rajistar QSO, zaɓi kallon Mai harbi SOTA domin duba lambobin kololuwa. QSO na kololuwa na farko da aka tabbatar ana haskaka su da zinariya, na farko da ba a tabbatar ba kuma da rawaya.
    • Gyaran lambobin kololuwa: Taɓa kowane QSO a log ɗinka domin buɗe editan QSO, sannan taɓa filinsa na SOTA domin ƙara, canza, ko gyara lambar kololuwa (misali HB/BE-001).
    • Daidaita QRZ Logbook ta atomatik (ACTION=REPLACE): Idan an riga an loda QSO zuwa QRZ, gyaran lambar kololuwa yana sabunta QRZ Logbook naka a wurinsa ta atomatik, ba tare da ƙirƙirar kwafi ba.
    • Bayani game da LoTW: Lura cewa ba a sabunta LoTW (Logbook of The World) ta atomatik idan aka gyara lambobi, domin LoTW ba ya barin a canza QSO da aka riga aka loda.

    Aiki a matsayin Mai Kunna SOTA

    Domin yin kunna SOTA daga kololuwa:

    1. Daidaita lokaci: Tabbata agogon na'urarka daidai yake kafin ka fita.
    2. Fara kunnawa: A shafin Saituna, danna KUNNA SOTA ka shigar da kololuwarka, domin a rubuta kowane QSO da ita kuma a ƙirga shi (duba Kunna wurin shakatawa ko kololuwa).
    3. Saita Mai canza CQ: A shafin Saituna, saita Mai canza CQ zuwa SOTA. Kowane kiran CQ zai watsa CQ SOTA [Callsign].
    4. Saita yanayin AUTO: A shafin Tashoshi, saita fifikon manufa zuwa Kira CQ kuma kunna yanayin AUTO.
    5. Fara aiki: Kunna TX. qFT8 za ta kira CQ ta atomatik kuma ta amsa masu harbi da ke shigowa ɗaya bayan ɗaya.

    Shiga da hannu: Idan masu harbi da dama sun kira ka a lokaci guda kuma kana son zaɓar wata tasha, taɓa CALL a layinta. Da zarar wannan QSO ya ƙare, qFT8 tana komawa kiran CQ ta atomatik.

    Fitar da Log na SOTA (Masu Harbi & Masu Kunnawa)

    qFT8 tana iya ƙirƙirar fayilolin ADIF da suka dace da masu harbi da kuma log na masu kunnawa:

    1. Je zuwa Saituna ka taɓa Fitar da ADIF File.
    2. Ga masu harbi (Chasers / Hunters):
      • Saita Sharuɗɗan mai neman zuwa Fitar da mai harbi SOTA kawai domin fitar da QSO da aka tuntuɓi lambar kololuwa kawai (masu <SOTA_REF>).
    3. Ga masu kunnawa (hanya mafi sauri ita ce maɓallin FITAR na kunnawa naka; matakan da ke ƙasa suna yin iri ɗaya da hannu):
      • Zaɓi Zaɓaɓɓen lokaci domin ya dace da lokacin farawa da ƙarewar kunnawarka.
      • A Taro, zaɓi SOTA ka shigar da lambar kololuwar da ka kunna (misali HB/BE-001). qFT8 tana saka lambarka a <MY_SOTA_REF> a kowane bayani.
    4. Summit-to-Summit (S2S): Idan ka yi aiki da wani mai kunnawa yayin da kake kunna kololuwa, qFT8 tana saka kololuwarka (<MY_SOTA_REF>) da kololuwar ɗayan tashar (<SOTA_REF>) a fayil da aka fitar.
    5. Danna FITAR DA QSOS domin raba ko ajiye fayil na ADIF.
    ↑ Koma zuwa Abubuwan da ke ciki

    Parks On The Air (POTA)

    qFT8 tana da cikakken haɗi na ciki da shirin POTA (Parks On The Air) ga masu harbi (Hunters) da masu kunnawa (Activators): ɗauko spots ta atomatik, bin wuraren da aka yi aiki da su, gyaran lambobi, da zaɓuɓɓukan fitarwa.

    Ɗauko Spots na Wuraren Shakatawa ta Atomatik

    Idan akwai intanet, qFT8 tana tambayar API na hukuma na POTA a bango ta atomatik (kowane daƙiƙa 90) domin ɗauko dukkan kunna wuraren shakatawa da ke gudana a duniya.

    • Nunawa a Taswirar duniya: Ana nuna masu kunna wuraren shakatawa da ke aiki a Taswirar duniya da alamar wuri (🏞️), alamar kira, sunan wuri, da mitar aiki.
    • Gano lamba ta atomatik: Idan aka karɓi watsawa daga tashar wurin shakatawa da ke aiki, qFT8 tana kwatanta alamar kira da spots domin gane lambar wurin ta atomatik (misali K-1234, US-0001, VE-0123).
    • Cikewa daga baya ta atomatik: Idan ka rubuta QSO da tashar da take kiran CQ POTA kafin a karɓi lambar wurinta, qFT8 tana duba spots da ke shigowa kuma tana saka lambar wurin a QSO da aka rubuta kwanan nan (a cikin awa 1) ta atomatik.

    Aiki a matsayin Mai Harbi na POTA

    Domin harbin tashoshin da suke kunna wuraren shakatawa:

    1. A shafin Tashoshi, saita jerin zaɓi na fifikon manufa zuwa Mai neman POTA (ko Mai neman POTA + SOTA).
    2. Alamomi & ginshiƙin lamba: Tashoshin POTA da ke aiki ana sa musu alamar POTA a Layi na 2, kuma sunan ginshiƙin DXCC yana komawa Lambar POTA domin nuna lambar wurin.
    3. Haskaka wurin da aka yi aiki da shi da wanda ake buƙata:
      • Wurin da ba a yi aiki da shi ba: Yana da alamar POTA mai launin purple mai haske da rubutun lamba na purple, wato wurin da ba ka yi aiki da shi ba tukuna.
      • Wurin da aka riga aka yi aiki da shi: Ana nuna shi da alama mai launin toka, domin sauƙin fifita wuraren da ba a yi aiki da su ba idan zango yana da cunkoso.
    4. Harbi da AUTO: A yanayin AUTO, qFT8 tana fifita masu kunna POTA da ba a yi aiki da su ba a kan tashoshin yau da kullum, bisa ƙarfin sigina (SNR).

    Sarrafawa & Gyaran Lambobin POTA a Log

    Duba kuma kula da log ɗinka na mai harbin wuraren shakatawa a cikin manhaja:

    • Kallon Mai harbi POTA a shafin Log na QSO: Canza kallon shafin Rajistar QSO zuwa Mai harbi POTA domin nuna lambobin wurare kusa da QSO naka. Nasarorin wuri na farko da aka tabbatar ana haskaka su da zinariya, waɗanda ba a tabbatar ba kuma da rawaya.
    • Gyaran lambobin wurare: Taɓa kowane QSO a log domin buɗe editan QSO, sannan taɓa filinsa na POTA domin ƙara, canza, ko cire lambar wuri (misali K-1234).
    • Daidaita QRZ Logbook ta atomatik (ACTION=REPLACE): Idan aka sabunta lambar wurin wani QSO, qFT8 tana sabunta bayanin da yake kan QRZ Logbook a wurinsa ta atomatik, ba tare da ƙirƙirar kwafi ba.
    • Bayani game da LoTW: Lura cewa ba a sabunta LoTW (Logbook of The World) ta atomatik idan aka gyara lambobi, domin LoTW ba ya barin a canza QSO da aka riga aka loda.

    Aiki a matsayin Mai Kunna POTA

    Domin kunna wurin shakatawa:

    1. Daidaita lokaci: Tabbata agogon na'ura daidai yake kafin ka fara kunnawa.
    2. Fara kunnawa: A shafin Saituna, danna KUNNA POTA ka shigar da wurin shakatawarka, domin a rubuta kowane QSO da shi kuma a ƙirga shi (duba Kunna wurin shakatawa ko kololuwa).
    3. Saita Mai canza CQ: A shafin Saituna, saita Mai canza CQ zuwa POTA domin watsa CQ POTA [Callsign].
    4. Saita yanayin AUTO: A shafin Tashoshi, saita fifikon manufa zuwa Kira CQ kuma kunna yanayin AUTO.
    5. Fara aiki: Kunna TX. qFT8 za ta ci gaba da kiran CQ kuma ta yi aiki da masu harbi da ke shigowa ta atomatik.

    Shiga da hannu: Taɓa CALL a kan kowace tashar da ke jira yana fifita wannan QSO, sannan a koma yanayin AUTO CQ.

    Fitar da Log na POTA (Masu Harbi & Masu Kunnawa)

    Ƙirƙiri fayilolin ADIF da suke a shirye domin lodawa zuwa pota.app:

    1. Je zuwa Saituna ka taɓa Fitar da ADIF File.
    2. Ga masu harbi:
      • Saita Sharuɗɗan mai neman zuwa Fitar da mai harbi POTA kawai domin fitar da QSO da aka harbi wurin shakatawa kawai (masu <POTA_REF>).
    3. Ga masu kunnawa (hanya mafi sauri ita ce maɓallin FITAR na kunnawa naka; matakan da ke ƙasa suna yin iri ɗaya da hannu):
      • Zaɓi Zaɓaɓɓen lokaci domin ya dace da lokacin kunnawarka.
      • A Taro, zaɓi POTA ka shigar da lambar wurin shakatawarka (misali K-1234). qFT8 tana cika <MY_SIG>POTA, <MY_SIG_INFO>, da <MY_POTA_REF> ta atomatik a dukkan bayanan da aka fitar.
    4. Park-to-Park (P2P) & Park-to-Summit (P2S): Idan ka harbi wasu wurare ko kololuwa yayin kunnawarka, ana fitar da wurinka (<MY_POTA_REF>) da lambar ɗayan tashar (<POTA_REF> / <SOTA_REF>) tare.
    5. Danna FITAR DA QSOS domin raba ko ajiye fayil na log ɗinka.
    ↑ Koma zuwa Abubuwan da ke ciki

    Multi-tone: several signals at once

    Settings has a section Watsa na musamman, directly above Taruka. Its ? opens a card with all the explanations and a link to this page. In it, in this order: the button Sautuka da yawa (experimental; blue while on, a tap switches it on or off); under it Yanayin Fox (off by default, usable only while Multi-tone is ticked: multi-tone the way a DXpedition runs it), the red power line, Ƙara saƙon CQ with Koyaushe at its right, and Iyakar sautuka: Sautuka da yawa to Yanayin Fox (greyed while Multi-tone is off, and in Fox mode, which sends as many signals as fit); then the button Yanayin Hound (kira DXpedition). Multi-tone and Hound mode cannot be on together: switching one on switches the other off, with a short message. While Multi-tone is on, each transmission in FT8, FT4, FT2 and the LQ modes can carry that many ordinary signals side by side, each with its own message to its own station. The price is power: to keep the audio from clipping, the signals share it, so each one reaches the other station weaker (2 tones: 6 dB weaker each, 3: 9.5 dB, 4: 12 dB, 5: 14 dB). A cycle that needs fewer messages sends fewer signals, each then stronger. A blue Sautuka da yawa marker on the spectrogram reminds you while it is on; the transmit frequency is drawn as one bar per signal, and every station being worked has its receive bar.

    • Koyaushe (the usual data / USB-D modes of Icom, Yaesu, Kenwood, Elecraft, Xiegu and similar radios). A QMX / QMX+ sends several signals through its SSB mode: qFT8 switches it to USB for an over with several signals and back to DIGI for an over with one, when its firmware is 1_04_001 or newer and its SSB audio source is the USB sound card (qFT8 only reads that setting; set it on the radio). Otherwise, and on a QDX or a (tr)uSDX, which send one measured tone, multi-tone sends one signal and a red line says Wannan rediyon ba zai iya aika sigina da yawa lokaci guda ba. Keep the radio's ALC at zero: several signals show splatter on other frequencies much sooner than one.
    • While multi-tone is on, a red line also says how much weaker each signal is, for example Sautuka 2.
    • Ƙara saƙon CQ (off by default; the same box for multi-tone and Fox mode): off, a CQ is never mixed with other messages: a cycle with stations to answer sends only those, and a CQ goes out alone, as one signal at full level, only when nobody waits. On, a CQ may take a free signal beside the calls. Koyaushe (at its right, off by default, usable only while Ƙara saƙon CQ is ticked): the first signal is always a CQ, even while you are working stations.
    • Yanayin Fox and Hound mode are for DXpeditions: a Fox answers several callers at once, on a frequency announced in advance outside the usual sub-bands; a Hound calls a Fox above 1000 Hz and, when the Fox answers, qFT8 moves to the frequency it gives and finishes the QSO by itself, only while TX is ON.
    • Yanayin Hound (kira DXpedition) 100 Hz apart above your transmit frequency in FT8 and LQ8 (150 Hz in FT4, 250 Hz in FT2); below it when there is no room above. A station keeps the signal it was answered on for the whole QSO. When you switch multi-tone on, choose more tones or change the mode, qFT8 moves your transmit frequency if needed so the whole group stays inside the band (200 to 2800 Hz) and says so briefly (Sautuka da yawa); it stays there when you switch multi-tone off. Only if the group fits nowhere does Settings say for example Dole rediyonka ya watsa sauti a layi (linear); your choice is kept.
    • AUTO fills the further signals by itself with the next stations it would call, under all its usual rules, always in the other slot; in the LQ modes it puts two stations at the same report or 73 step into one signal, so LQ8 with 5 tones reaches up to 10 stations. It never sends two CQs (and, unless Ƙara saƙon CQ is ticked, never a CQ beside other messages). Every station on a signal has its Ƙara saƙon CQ button lit.
    • Koyaushe tap a station's CALL to mark it, tap others to mark them too; tap a marked one to unmark it. Up to one station per tone can be marked in the FT modes and two per tone in the LQ modes (10 with 5 tones); tapping a new station when the most are marked replaces the oldest mark. Choosing fewer tones unmarks the oldest marks and says which (Ƙara saƙon CQ). With more than one station marked the message box shows Ana zaɓar saƙonni kai tsaye and qFT8 sends each station its next message. A station is unmarked by itself when its QSO is done.
    • Multi-tone never switches TX on, never changes your radio's power and changes no message: every signal is a normal frame of its mode that any station decodes. The transmit time limits count a multi-tone transmission exactly like one signal, and every signal must be inside the band.

    Fox da Hound (DXpedition)

    A ƙarƙashin Sautuka da yawa a cikin Saituna akwai wasu akwatuna biyu, duka a kashe ta tsohuwa: Yanayin Hound (kira DXpedition) da Yanayin Fox (don DXpedition kawai). Suna aiki a FT8, FT4, FT2 da yanayin LQ. Ɗaya ne kawai daga cikinsu zai iya kasancewa a kunne, kuma Yanayin Fox ba tare da sautuka da yawa ba: saka alama a ɗaya yana cire ta daga ɗayan, kuma gajeren saƙo yana faɗar haka. ? da ke kusa da taken sashen yana buɗe wannan shafi.

    Fox da Hound ba su ne sautuka da yawa ba. Sautuka da yawa yana aika siginoni na yau da kullum da yawa a lokaci guda ne kawai. Fox da Hound su ne ka'idar DXpedition: Fox yana gaya wa kowane Hound inda zai koma, kuma yana amsa da yawa daga cikinsu da saƙonni na musamman da aka haɗa, kuma qFT8 yana bin waɗannan umarni da kansa ne kawai yayin da ɗaya daga cikin yanayin biyu ke kunne.

    Yanayin Hound: yin aiki da DXpedition

    • Saka alama a Yanayin Hound, taɓa DXpedition (Fox ɗin) a jerin tashoshi sannan ka kunna TX. qFT8 yana kiran sa sama da 1000 Hz (FT8; ɗan sama kaɗan a FT4 da FT2), ba za ta taɓa kasancewa a lokacin Fox ɗin kansa ba. Alamar da ke kan spectrogram tana karanta Hound.
    • Idan Fox ya amsa da rahotonka, qFT8 yana tafiya zuwa mitar da Fox ya kira ka a kai (saƙo yana faɗa, misali "KH7Z ya mayar da kai zuwa 300 Hz") sannan ya aika rahotonka a can; maimaitawa tana tafiya 500 Hz sama ko ƙasa.
    • RR73 na Fox, shi kaɗai ko a cikin saƙonsa na haɗe ("K1ABC RR73; W9XYZ <KH1/KH7Z> -08"), yana kammala QSO: ana adana shi a log, qFT8 yana komawa mitar kiranka kuma ba ya aika wani abu kuma ("QSO da KH7Z ya kammala").
    • Waɗannan motsi suna faruwa ne kawai idan Yanayin Hound yana kunne kuma TX yana ON. Idan TX yana OFF ba a taɓa aika komai ba. Idan an zaɓi AUTO, Yanayin Hound ba ya kiran kowa: kai ne kake zaɓar Fox.
    • A yanayin LQ QSO da Fox QSO ne na LQ na yau da kullum; Yanayin Hound ba ya canza komai a can.

    Yanayin Fox: gudanar da pileup

    • Don DXpedition kawai, a mitar da aka sanar tun da wuri, a wajen ƙananan rukunin FT8 na yau da kullum. Gargaɗi yana faɗar haka idan ka saka alama.
    • Fox yana watsawa a zangon farko daga 300 Hz zuwa sama. Yana amsa Hound ɗin da ke kiransa (sama da bene na Hound) bisa jerin da aka ji su, da sigina masu yawa a lokaci guda kamar yadda yanayin ya ba da dama (har 5) da kuma yawan tashoshin da za a yi wa hidima. Idan babu mai kira yana aika CQ ɗaya, shi kaɗai, da cikakken ƙarfi. Alamar tana karanta, misali, Fox: QSO 2, masu kira 5; kowace tasha da ake aiki da ita tana haskawa, kuma kowace sigina tana da sandar TX ɗinta.
    • Hound da ya aika rahotonsa yana samun RR73, tare da rahoton Hound na gaba a saƙo ɗaya na haɗe idan akwai wanda ke jira, kuma ana adana QSO sau ɗaya. Ana kiran Hound da bai amsa ba sau 3; wanda bai ji RR73 ba yana samunsa sake, har sau 3. Taɓa tasha don sanya ta ta farko; ana iya yiwa da yawa alama.
    • Ƙara CQ (a ƙarƙashin Yanayin Fox, a kashe ta tsohuwa): CQ yana fita kuma a cikin siginar da aka bari a buɗe yayin amsa Hound. A kashe, CQ yana fita shi kaɗai ne kawai, lokacin da babu wanda ke jira.
    • A yanayin LQ Fox yana da sautuka da yawa tare da saƙonnin LQ na kansa: kowace sigina tana amsa tashoshi biyu, har 10 a kowace watsawa.
    • Kowace daga cikin N sigina tana samun 1/N na sauti: ka bar ALC na rediyo a sifili. Yanayin Fox ba ya taɓa kunna TX, ba ya taɓa canza ƙarfin rediyo, kuma watsawa ɗaya ana ƙidaya ta a matsayin zagaye ɗaya a iyakokin lokacin watsawa.

    Kunna wurin shakatawa ko kololuwa

    Idan kana aiki daga wurin shakatawa (POTA) ko kololuwa (SOTA), fara kunnawa a qFT8. Daga nan, kowane QSO da ka rubuta, a kowane yanayi, yana ɗaukar wurinka ko kololuwarka ta atomatik, qFT8 tana ƙirga maka su, kuma a ƙarshe tana rubuta fayilolin da shirye-shiryen suke nema da taɓawa ɗaya.

    Sashen Abubuwan da ke faruwa na Saituna

    Saituna tana da sashe Taruka don abubuwan aiki. A yau yana ƙunshe da:

    • KUNNA POTA da KUNNA SOTA: kunna wurin shakatawa ko kololuwa, kamar yadda aka bayyana a ƙasa. Gilashin ƙara girma kusa da kowanne yana jera kunnawar da ka yi a baya; gear ɗin da ke kusa da POTA yana buɗe saitunan sanarwarsa.
    • FARA ƘIRGA: ƙirgar QSO, don kowane abu dabam (gasa, ranar kulob, tashar abu na musamman).

    An bayyana farautar wuraren shakatawa da kololuwa daga gida a sassan POTA da SOTA.

    Farawa

    1. A shafin Saituna, danna KUNNA POTA ko KUNNA SOTA (a sashen Taruka).
    2. Rubuta lambarka, misali US-1234 ga wurin shakatawa ko W1/HA-001 ga kololuwa. Idan kana cikin wurare da dama a lokaci guda ("two-fer"), raba su da waƙafi. qFT8 tana duba tsarin yayin da kake rubutu, kuma tana nuna lambobin da ka yi amfani da su kwanan nan.
    3. Danna FARA. Maɓallin ya koma shuɗi kuma yana nuna lamba da adadin QSO.

    Kunna wurin shakatawa da kunna kololuwa suna iya kasancewa a buɗe a lokaci guda; sai kowane QSO ya sami lambobi biyun. Domin kiran CQ POTA ko CQ SOTA a yanayin dijital, saita Mai canza CQ kamar yadda aka bayyana a sassan POTA da SOTA.

    qFT8 tana kuma iya ba da shawarar fara maka: idan spots da take saukewa domin taswirar duniya sun nuna alamar kiranka a matsayin mai kunnawa, tana tambaya A fara kunna POTA a can? Kuma idan ka rubuta wurinka ko kololuwarka a akwatin POTA nawa / SOTA nawa da babu komai a ciki na log na hannu, tana tambaya ko a fara kunnawa da ita.

    Sanar da shi, domin mafarauta su same ka

    qFT8 yana sanar da kunnawar POTA; ba ya sanar da kunnawar SOTA (sanar da ita da kanka a shafin yanar gizon SOTA). Idan ka fara kunnawar POTA da kanka (ba daga wani spot da ya riga ya sanar da kai ba), qFT8 yana tambaya: A sanar a kan POTA? kuma yana faɗa abin da mafarauta za su gani, misali FT8 on 20m (14074 kHz). BA YANZU BA ba ya sanar da komai. SANAR yana buɗe ƙaramar taga:

    • Bandeji da Yanayi: zaɓi ɗaya ko fiye daga kowanne. Bandin da yanayin da qFT8 yake kai an riga an zaɓe su. qFT8 yana aika sanarwa ɗaya don kowane wurin shakatawa, band da yanayi da ka zaɓa, kuma yana faɗa yawansu kafin ka danna SANAR.
    • Yanayin da shirin bai lissafa ba ana sanar da su a matsayin DATA, tare da ainihin yanayin a cikin bayanin sanarwar. Band da yanayi da ba su da mitar da aka saba (RTTY a kan 60 m) ana barin su, kuma taga tana faɗa haka.
    • Mitar ita ce inda rediyonka yake don band da yanayin da kake kai, da kuma mitar kira ta yau da kullum ga sauran.

    Daga nan, qFT8 yana sake sanar da kunnawar kowane minti 30 (sanarwa tana ɓacewa daga jerin bayan kusan awa ɗaya; gear ɗin da ke kusa da KUNNA POTA yana saita Sake sanarwa kowane, daga minti 20 zuwa 60, da Sake gwada sanarwar da ta kasa bayan, minti 5 ko 10), muddin kunnawar a buɗe take kuma qFT8 yana aiki: a kan allo, ko a ƙanƙance tare da qFT8 yana gudana a bango kuma rediyo a haɗe. Yana tsayawa idan ka kammala kunnawar, kuma ba ya sanarwa da kansa idan qFT8 a rufe yake: a farawa ta gaba, bayan ka zaɓi ci gaba da kunnawar, yana tambaya: A sake sanarwa?

    Idan sanarwa ba ta isa ba (babu intanet, ko shafin ya ƙi ta), qFT8 yana faɗa abin da ya faru kuma yana ba da SAKE GWADAWA, ko SANAR DA HANNU, wanda yake buɗe shafin spot na shirin a burauzarka domin ka sanar da kanka. Idan babu haɗi yana kuma sake gwadawa da kansa (kowane minti 5 sai dai idan ka zaɓi 10).

    Don sanarwa da wasu band ko yanayi, ko ganin lokacin da aka sanar na ƙarshe, danna SANAR a kan katin kunnawar (taɓa alamar shuɗi) ko a cikin kallon QSO na kunnawar. Taga sai ta kuma ba da DAKATAR DA SAKE SANARWA. Idan ka canza yanayi a lokacin kunnawar da aka sanar, qFT8 yana tambaya ko a sanar da sabon yanayin shi ma.

    Yayin da yake a buɗe: alamar shuɗi

    Alamar POTA ko SOTA mai launin shuɗi tana zama a ƙasa ta dama na waterfall muddin kunnawa yana a buɗe, da adadin QSO na kunnawa kusa da kalmar: POTA 10. Ana ƙirga kowane QSO da ka rubuta sau ɗaya: sake yin aiki da tasha ɗaya a band da yanayi ɗaya kwafi ne kuma ba a ƙirga shi. Adadin yana raguwa idan aka cire QSO daga kunnawa, aka goge shi, ko aka soke shi a log na hannu. A POTA yana sake farawa daga sifili da ƙarfe 00:00 UTC. Da wurare biyu a lokaci guda, adadi ɗaya ne har yanzu, domin kowane QSO yana ƙidaya ga wurare biyun. Inda babu sarari, alamar tana nuna adadi kawai kusa da gunkinta; idan har haka bai shiga ba, ana zana ta ƙarama, tare da jan gargaɗin da suke layi ɗaya. Taɓa ta domin ganin halin kunnawa:

    • Lamba da ƙirga: QSO da aka ƙirga zuwa yanzu, aka nuna su a kan 10 ga wurin shakatawa ko 4 ga kololuwa, da nawa daga cikinsu aka yi da wani wuri ko kololuwa (P2P / S2S).
    • NUNA KUNNA: jerin QSO nasa (a ƙasa).
    • KAMMALA KUNNA: yana gama shi. Koyaushe yana fara tambaya, da CI GABA domin ci gaba.
    • RUFE: yana rufe katin.

    QSO na kunnawa

    1. Koma zuwa dukkan log na QSO.
    2. Kunnawa: shiri da lamba, lokacin da aka fara (UTC), da QSO nasa.
    3. +: Yana buɗe log na hannu domin rubuta QSO da hannu kai tsaye a wannan kunnawa.
    4. ƘARA QSO: Yana ƙara QSO da suke a log ɗinka tuni, bisa tsawon lokaci. Yi amfani da shi idan ka manta fara kunnawa: zaɓi daga da zuwa, sai qFT8 ta faɗi adadin QSO da za ta ƙara kafin ta yi.
    5. FITAR: Yana rubuta fayilolin da za a loda (a ƙasa).
    6. Wurin shakatawa ko kololuwar ɗayan tashar, idan ita ma tana kunnawa.
    7. Alamar kunnawa da yake a buɗe.

    Domin cire QSO daga kunnawa, yi dogon danna a kan ɗaya domin zaɓar sa, taɓa sauran, danna AYYUKA ka zaɓi CIRE DAGA WANNAN KUNNA. QSO suna nan a log ɗinka; lambarka ce kawai ake cirewa daga cikinsu. Kana kuma iya motsa QSO guda ɗaya daga editan QSO, da layinsa na Kunna POTA / Kunna SOTA.

    Gamawa, da ci gaba

    KAMMALA KUNNA yana nuna taƙaitawa: lambobi, lokacin farawa da ƙarewa, da QSO a kowane yanayi. QSO da aka rubuta bayan gamawa ba sa cikinsa.

    • Ka gama bisa kuskure, ko ka dawo bayan hutu? Na awa 24, taƙaitawa, jerin kunnawa da taga farawa ta KUNNA POTA / KUNNA SOTA suna ba da CI GABA DA KUNNA, wanda yake sake buɗe shi kamar babu abin da ya faru.
    • Sabuwar ranar UTC. Ga POTA, kowace ranar UTC kunnawa ne daban. Bayan tsakar dare ta UTC, qFT8 tana ba da FARA SABO a wuri ɗaya maimakon ci gaba.
    • An bar shi a buɗe. Idan an rufe qFT8 na ɗan lokaci, ko dogon lokaci ya wuce ba tare da QSO ba, a farawa ta gaba tana tambaya ko a CI GABA ko a gama.

    Fitarwa

    FITAR (a taƙaitawa da a jerin kunnawa) yana rubuta fayilolin ADIF masu sunan da kowane shiri yake nema, a shirye domin lodawa a shafin yanar gizonsa:

    • POTA: fayil ɗaya ga kowane wuri da kowace ranar UTC, mai suna CALLSIGN@PARK-DATE.adi (misali K1ABC@US-1234-20261006.adi). Two-fer yana ba da fayil ɗaya ga kowane wuri. Tashar da ita kanta take cikin wurare biyu ana rubuta ta sau ɗaya ga kowane wurinta, don haka duka suna ƙidaya a matsayin park-to-park.
    • SOTA: fayil ɗaya ga kololuwa, mai suna iri ɗaya (K1ABC@W1_HA-001-20261006.adi).

    Kana iya ajiye fayilolin ko raba su. Maɓallin gilashin ƙara girma kusa da KUNNA POTA / KUNNA SOTA a Saituna yana jera kunnawarka na baya, sabbi a farko, kuma yana sa wa kowanne alamar YANA AIKI, ANA IYA CI GABA, AN FITAR ko ba a fitar ba; taɓa ɗaya domin buɗe shi da sake fitar da shi. qFT8 ba ta loda zuwa shafukan yanar gizon shirye-shiryen da kanta.

    Wurare da dama a jere

    A ranar da kake da wurare da dama, ɗaya bayan ɗaya, kowane wuri kunnawa ne nasa da fayil nasa:

    1. A wuri na farko, danna KUNNA POTA a Saituna, rubuta lambarsa ka danna FARA.
    2. Yi aiki. Kowane QSO yana samun wurin, kuma alamar shuɗi tana ƙirga su.
    3. Kafin ka tafi, taɓa alamar ka danna KAMMALA KUNNA, sannan FITAR a taƙaitawa, ka ajiye ko raba fayil ɗin (K1ABC@US-1234-20261006.adi).
    4. A wuri na gaba, sake farawa da sabuwar lamba.

    Babu abin da za ka sake rubutawa ko gyarawa a gida. Idan ba ka da lokacin fitarwa a waje, jerin kunnawa na baya (gilashin ƙara girma) yana fitar da kowanne daga baya.

    "Wane wurin shakatawa za a rubuta a fayil?"

    Fayil na wurin shakatawa yana iya ambaton wuri ɗaya kawai. Idan ka fitar da QSO da aka yi daga wurare da dama a lokaci guda ba tare da bi ta kunnawa ba (dukkan log daga Saituna, ko Fitar (ADIF) a kan zaɓi), qFT8 tana tambaya Wane wurin shakatawa za a rubuta a fayil? Zaɓi wurin da wannan fayil yake nasa; sake fitarwa domin ɗayan. Ba a canza log. Fitarwa daga kunnawa kansa ba ya taɓa tambaya: yana rubuta fayil ɗaya ga kowane wuri.

    Domin samun fayil ga kowane wuri ba tare da kunnawa ba, rubuta wuraren da kanka, ka raba su da waƙafi (US-1234, US-5678), a akwatin lambar POTA na FITAR DA QSOS ko a Fitar da lambar POTA a kan zaɓi: qFT8 tana rubuta fayil ɗaya ga kowane wuri, da QSO iri ɗaya a kowanne. Kololuwa koyaushe lamba ɗaya ce.

    Idan tashar da kake aiki da ita tana cikin wurare biyu a lokaci guda, qFT8 tana riƙe duka: tana karanta wuri na biyu daga bayanin spot idan mai rahoton ya rubuta shi a can. Log yana nuna wuri na farko da nawa suka rage (US-1234 +1). Idan wuri ba daidai ba ne ko ya ɓace, gyara filin POTA na QSO a editan QSO; yana karɓar wurare da dama da aka raba da waƙafi. Wurin da aka ambata sau biyu ga tasha ɗaya (a spot da kuma a bayaninsa, ko sau biyu a jerin da aka rubuta) ana ƙirga shi sau ɗaya, a log da a kowane fayil da aka fitar.

    Ƙirgar QSO

    Don kowane abu dabam, FARA ƘIRGA a sashen Abubuwan da ke faruwa yana ƙirga kowane QSO da ka rubuta daga lokacin. Yayin da yake ƙirga, maɓallin yana zama shuɗi kuma yana nuna ƘIRGA 12, kuma alamar shuɗi iri ɗaya tana bayyana a ƙasan dama na magudanar ruwa. Taɓa ɗaya daga cikinsu yana kawo ƙarshen ƙirgar; bayan haka ba a ajiye komai. Ƙirgar tana ci gaba idan aka rufe manhajar aka sake buɗe ta.

    ↑ Koma zuwa Abubuwan da ke ciki

    Abubuwan da ake tallafawa

    POTA da SOTA a kunne suke ta tsohuwa. A Saituna > Taruka, maɓallin ƘIRGA (misali ƘIRGA 12) yana nuna adadin QSO da ake ƙirgawa don ayyukan kunna wuri ko gasar da ke gudana.

    Don kunna sauran, danna ƘARA WASU ABUBUWAN DA KE FARUWA a Saituna > Taruka sannan ka yi alama a kan shirin ko gasar da kake so. An yi musu alama a matsayin na gwaji.

    Shirye-shiryen lambobin yabo

    • WWFF: Wuraren shakatawa da na kare yanayi a duniya.
    • WWBOTA: Bunkoki da katangar tsaro.
    • IOTA: Tsibirai: ana yi wa tashoshin kasashe masu tsibiri ɗaya alama.
    • SiOTA: Rumbunan hatsi (Ostiraliya).
    • HEMA: Tuddai da tuddan ƙasa.
    • LLOTA: Tafkuna da lagoons.
    • WWTOTA: Hasumiyoyin kallo.
    • HOTA: Wuraren tarihi: gidajen sarauta, kufai, coci-coci da abubuwan tunawa. Kunnawa mai inganci shine QSO 5 tare da tashoshi 5 daban-daban a rana ɗaya ta UTC. Idan ka fara kunnawar HOTA, qFT8 da farko yana jera wuraren da ke kusa da lokatarka (jerin yana fitowa daga HOTA sau ɗaya a rana; wurinka yana zama a na'urar), sannan RUBUTA LAMBAR WURI; sunan wuri ma yana aiki. SANAR na iya aika spot ɗinka kai tsaye zuwa HOTA: a karon farko yana tambayar maɓallinka na kanka daga cqhota.app (My account > Integration API key), kuma yana aikawa ne kawai idan ka taɓa shi.

    Gasa (na gwaji)

    • ARRL Field Day: Aji da sashe. Kai ne kake fara kowane QSO: AUTO ba ya yi.
    • Winter Field Day: FT8 da FT4 ba sa ƙirguwa a Winter Field Day; RTTY yana ƙirguwa.
    • ARRL RTTY Roundup: RTTY kaɗai: jiha, lardi ko lambar jeri.
    • EU VHF (IARU Region 1): Rahoto, lambar jeri da locator mai haruffa shida. Maki a kowane km.
    • ARRL International Digital Contest: Grid ɗinka shi ne musayar: saƙonnin FT8 da FT4 na yau da kullum.
    • FT Challenge: Grid ɗinka shi ne musayar: saƙonnin FT8 da FT4 na yau da kullum.

    Danna FARA GASA ka rubuta musayarka. A gasar da dokokinta suka hana QSO na kai-tsaye (duk waɗannan banda Winter Field Day), ana kashe AUTO yayin gasar: maɓallinsa yana juyewa ja, kuma danna shi yana nuna “Ba a yarda da aikin AUTO a wannan gasar ba”. Kai ne kake fara kowane QSO.

    Yanayin SWL (masu sauraron gajeren zango)

    Idan kana saurare kawai ba tare da aika ba, danna YANAYIN SWL (SAURARE KAWAI) a ƙarshen Saituna > Taruka sannan ka rubuta lambar SWL ɗinka (misali R4-123-456) ko sunan kiranka. Yayin da yake kunne, qFT8 ba ya aika sam: maɓallin TX yana nuna NO TX / SWL kuma ba ya yin komai. Kowane cikakken QSO da aka ji tsakanin wasu tashoshi biyu (sunayen kira biyu, lokaci, band, mita, yanayi, rahotanni biyu da grid idan an decode su) yana shiga cikin log ɗin abubuwan da aka ji, ba cikin log ɗin QSO naka ba; ana ɗaukar QSO a matsayin cikakke da zarar an ji tabbatarwarsa (RR73, RRR, ko 73 bayan rahoton ɗayan tashar), a FT8, FT4 da LQ8 duka. Gilashin ƙara girma kusa da maɓallin yana buɗe log ɗin abubuwan da aka ji: FITAR DA ADIF (rikodi ɗaya ga kowace tasha da aka ji, biyu ga kowane QSO: SWL=Y, shaidarka a matsayin mai aiki, tashar da aka ji a CALL tare da rahotonka game da ita, tashar da take aiki da ita a saƙon kati "Heard working ..." da kuma cikin sharhi, tare da duk wani bayanin shiri da aka ji gare ta, misali RAZA), FITAR DA CSV da SHARE WAƊANDA AKA JI. Na'urar sarrafa ta yanar gizo tana nuna QSO da aka ji (karantawa kawai).

    ↑ Koma zuwa Abubuwan da ke ciki

    Hira ta JS8

    JS8 na hirar da ake rubutawa a madannai ne: rubutu na kyauta mai tsawon kowane iri, saƙonni zuwa tasha ɗaya ko zuwa ƙungiya, da gajerun tambayoyi da shirin ɗayan tashar ke amsawa da kansa. Saƙon da ya fi tsawon watsawa ɗaya ana yanka shi zuwa firam kuma ana aika firam ɗaya a kowane slot na lokaci; tashar da ke karɓa tana sake haɗa firam ɗin zuwa saƙo ɗaya.

    Zaɓi JS8 da mai zaɓar yanayi na sandar ƙasa. Shafin Tashoshi yana zama allon hira ta JS8, kuma maɓallin gudu (abu #11) yana ɗaukar wurin maɓallin jerin a sandar ƙasa. Spectrogram, mai zaɓar zango, babban maɓallin TX ON / TX OFF da jan layin mitar aikawarka suna aiki kamar a FT8.

    Allon hira ta JS8

    Daga hagu zuwa dama kuma daga sama zuwa ƙasa:

    1. AUTO: Amsoshi ta atomatik, a kunne (kore) ko a kashe (baƙi); a kunne ta tsohuwa. Idan AUTO yana kunne kuma babban maɓalli yana kan TX ON, qFT8 yana amsa tambayoyin da wasu tashoshi ke aiko maka (duba Amsoshin atomatik). AUTO ba ya kunna na'urar aikawarka ko kaɗan.
    2. Hira JS8: Taken allon.
    3. Nema: Yana buɗe jerin daidaitattun tambayoyi da za a aika zuwa hirar da ke buɗe (duba Tambayoyi ga wasu tashoshi). Yana launin toka a hira da ƙungiyar da ba @ALLCALL ba.
    4. QSO: Yana rubuta QSO da tashar da hirarta ke buɗe (duba Rubuta QSO na JS8). Yana launin toka a [GLOBAL] da a hirarrakin ƙungiyoyi.
    5. CQ: Yana aika kira na kowa zuwa @ALLCALL: CQ sai haruffa huɗu na farko na lokata na grid ɗinka, misali CQ JN47.
    6. +: Yana buɗe Ƙara hira, inda kake rubuta alamar kira ko ƙungiya (@NAME) don fara hira da ita, ko da ba a ji komai daga gare ta ba tukuna.
    7. Saƙonnin: Saƙonnin hirar da ke buɗe, sabon yana ƙasa. A saman kowanne: zango, mitar sauti, ƙarfin sigina (na saƙonnin da aka karɓa), kwanan wata da lokaci na UTC. Saƙonninka kore ne; saƙonnin da aka aiko maka shuɗi ne; sauran shuɗi-toka ne. Saƙon da ke jiran slot ɗinsa rawaya ne mai duhu, wanda ke kan iska yana da gefen zinariya kuma yana farawa da TX (sending 2/5)...: yayin da firam ɗinsa ke fita, sashin da aka riga aka aika yana komawa kore.
    8. Jerin hirarraki: Hirarrakin da za ka iya buɗewa (duba Jerin hirarraki). Hirar da ke buɗe tana da shuɗi; hirar da ke da saƙonnin da ba ka karanta ba tukuna tana launin amber.
    9. Akwatin saƙo: Inda kake rubutawa. Ana mayar da haruffa manya; har haruffa 100.
    10. Kibiyar aikawa: Tana aika abin da ka rubuta zuwa hirar da ke buɗe. Idan akwatin fanko ne ba ta yin komai.
    11. Maɓallin gudu a sandar ƙasa: Slow, Normal, Fast, Turbo ko Ultra (duba Gudu).

    Idan babban maɓalli yana nuna TX OFF, jan layi a saman akwatin saƙo yana cewa: Kunna TX domin fara aikawa idan akwai saƙo yana jira, ko Kunna TX don ba da damar aiki ta AUTO idan AUTO yana kunne. Saƙonnin da aka aika yayin TX OFF suna jira a hirar; babu abin da ke fita a iska har sai ka danna babban maɓalli.

    Gudu

    Kowane gudu yana da slot ɗinsa na lokaci. Gudu masu jinkiri suna amfani da ƙaramin faɗin zango kuma suna fassara sigina masu rauni; masu sauri suna aika rubutu mai yawa a minti ɗaya. qFT8 yana karɓar gudun da aka zaɓa da maɓallin gudu kawai, don haka zaɓi gudu iri ɗaya da na tashoshin da kake son yin magana da su (Normal shi ne na kowa).

    Slow
    Slot daƙiƙa 30
    Faɗin zango kusan 25 Hz
    Normal
    Slot daƙiƙa 15
    Faɗin zango kusan 50 Hz
    Fast
    Slot daƙiƙa 10
    Faɗin zango kusan 80 Hz
    Turbo
    Slot daƙiƙa 6
    Faɗin zango kusan 160 Hz
    Ultra
    Slot daƙiƙa 4
    Faɗin zango kusan 250 Hz

    Sunayen gudu suna zama da Turanci a kowane harshe. Kowane firam yana ɗauke da bit 75; yawan rubutun da ke shiga firam ɗaya ya dogara da kalmomin, domin ana aika kalmomin da aka fi amfani da su da gajeren lamba.

    Jerin hirarraki

    Daga sama:

    • [GLOBAL]: Duk abin da aka fassara a zango da duk abin da ka aika. Saƙon da ka aika daga nan yana zuwa @ALLCALL, sai dai idan ka fara shi da alamar kira ko ƙungiya da kanka; rubuta HB ko HEARTBEAT kawai yana aika heartbeat (duba Amsoshin atomatik).
    • @ALLCALL: Saƙonni ga kowace tasha.
    • Ƙungiyoyinka (@NAME): Ƙungiyoyin da ka shiga, har da waɗanda ba su da saƙo tukuna.
    • Hirarrakin alamar kira: Hira ɗaya ga kowace tasha da ta rubuta maka, ko da ka rubuta mata, ko da ka buɗe da +.
    • Ƙungiyoyin da aka ji: A ƙasan siririn layi kuma a dusashe, ƙungiyoyin da aka ji a zango waɗanda ba ka shiga ba. A saman irin wannan ƙungiya wani banner yana cewa Ba kwa cikin wannan ƙungiyar, tare da Shiga ƙungiya. Aika saƙo zuwa ƙungiyar da aka ji yana shigar da kai cikinta.

    Dogon latsa a kan hira (a kwamfuta: danna dama) yana buɗe zaɓuɓɓukanta: Shiga ƙungiya ko Bar ƙungiya (ga ƙungiyoyi kawai; ba don @ALLCALL ba), Fitar (ajiye a na'ura) da Fitar (raba) (saƙonninta a matsayin fayil na rubutu), Goge (yana share saƙonninta), Goge kuma rufe (yana cire ta daga jerin ma; ana barin ƙungiya), Goge duk hira da Goge kuma rufe duk hira. Ba za a iya rufe [GLOBAL] da @ALLCALL ba.

    Ba sirri ba ne: kowane saƙon JS8 yana fita a iska a fili. Hirar alamar kira tana jera maka saƙonni ne kawai; duk wanda ke kan mitar zai iya karanta su.

    Aika saƙo

    1. Buɗe hirar: tasha, ƙungiya, @ALLCALL ko [GLOBAL].
    2. Zaɓi mitar aikawarka a kan spectrogram, a wuri fanko, kamar a FT8.
    3. Rubuta rubutun ka danna kibiyar aikawa. Saƙon yana bayyana a hirar yana jiran slot na gaba.
    4. Danna babban maɓalli har ya nuna TX ON. Firam suna fita ɗaya a kowane slot; saƙon yana nuna yawan da aka aika. Saƙonni da yawa suna fita ɗaya bayan ɗaya.

    Saƙon da ke jira ko yake kan iska yana da x a gefensa: yana buɗe Soke watsawa? tare da Soke saƙo kuma, idan saƙo fiye da ɗaya yana jira, Soke dukan saƙonni. TX OFF ma yana dakatar da saƙon da ke kan iska.

    Dogon saƙo yana barin rediyo a aika na slot-slot da yawa a jere. Idan zai ajiye rediyo a aika fiye da yadda iyakar watsawa ba tsayawa ta rediyon ta ba da dama, qFT8 yana tambaya sau ɗaya, kafin firam na farko: Aika da hutu (ana barin wasu slot fanko don rediyo ya huta; ɗayan tashar tana karɓar saƙo ɗaya duk da haka), Gajarta (komawa ga rubutu, tare da zaɓar sashin da zai jira) ko Soke. Koyaushe aika dogayen saƙonnin JS8 da hutu a cikin wannan tambaya (da a Saituna) yana hana tambayar.

    JS8 yana buƙatar alamar kira daidaitacciya: a cikin JS8, Saituna suna gargaɗi Wannan alamar kira ba daidaitacciya ba ce kuma ba ta dace da JS8 ba. idan ba za a iya aika naka ba.

    Taɓa saƙo

    Taɓa saƙon da aka karɓa ko aka aika (a kwamfuta: danna sau biyu) yana buɗe cikakkun bayanansa: kwanan wata da lokaci na UTC, wanda ya aika da wanda aka aika wa, zango, mitar, gudu, ƙarfin sigina, lokata na grid da nisa idan an sani, da rubutun. A ƙasansu, ayyukan:

    • Ga saƙon da aka karɓa: AMSA (don tambayar da aka yi maka kawai: yana aika amsar), ƙungiyar da aka aika wa (yana buɗe hirarta), Hira (yana buɗe hira da mai aikawa), Daidaita Lokaci (yana gyara agogonka bisa lokacin tashar; ba a kwamfuta ba) da Goge.
    • Ga saƙonka: Sake aikawa (yana mayar da rubutunsa cikin akwatin saƙo, a hirarsa), Hira ko ƙungiyar, Daidaita Lokaci da Goge.

    Tambayar da aka yi maka wadda ba a amsa ba tukuna tana da ƙaramar kibiya a gefenta: taɓa ta yana aika amsar nan da nan. A kwamfuta, danna saƙo sau ɗaya yana saka alamar kiran mai aikawa cikin akwatin saƙo.

    Launuka a cikin rubutun: alamar kiranka ja ce, sunayen ƙungiyoyi lemu ne, tambayoyi da umarni (SNR?, GRID? ...) rawaya ne, hanyoyin isarwa (>CALL>) kore-shuɗi ne, kuma alamar ◇ da ke ƙare saƙo shuɗin sama ce. ~ mai launin lemu tana nuna firam da ba a karɓa ba, don ka san cewa wani sashi na rubutun ya ɓace.

    Tambayoyi ga wasu tashoshi

    Nema yana aika ɗaya daga daidaitattun tambayoyin JS8 zuwa hirar da ke buɗe (daga [GLOBAL]: zuwa @ALLCALL):

    • SNR? da HW CPY?: yadda ake jin ka a can da ƙarfi.
    • INFO? da STATUS?: rubutun bayanai da na matsayi na tashar.
    • GRID?: lokata na grid ɗinta.
    • HEARING?: tashoshin da take ji.
    • AGN?: yana neman a maimaita saƙon ƙarshe.
    • QSL?: yana tambaya ko an karɓi saƙonka.
    • QUERY CALL: yana tambaya ko tana jin alamar kiran da ka rubuta.
    • QUERY MSGS: yana tambaya ko tana ajiye maka saƙonni.
    • @HB HEARTBEAT: yana sanar da kowa cewa kana kan iska, tare da lokata na grid ɗinka.

    Za ka iya rubuta wasu umarnin JS8 da kanka, misali MSG TO:, ko rahoto ga hanyar sadarwar APRS: @APRSIS GRID JN47AB. Hanyar isarwa kamar K1ABC>W9XYZ HELLO ana aika ta yadda ka rubuta ta; qFT8 ba ya isar da saƙonni na wasu tashoshi, kuma ba ya adana su.

    Amsoshin atomatik (AUTO)

    Idan AUTO yana kunne kuma babban maɓalli yana kan TX ON, idan aka karɓi tambaya zuwa alamar kiranka gaba ɗaya, qFT8 yana saka amsar cikin jerin gwano kuma yana aika ta a slot na gaba:

    • SNR? ko HW CPY?: SNR -08 (yadda ake jin tashar a nan da ƙarfi).
    • GRID?: haruffa huɗu na farko na lokata na grid ɗinka.
    • INFO? da STATUS?: rubutunka daga Saituna (USING QFT8 har sai ka canza su).
    • HEARING?: tashoshi har huɗu da aka ji cikin mintuna 10 da suka wuce.
    • QSL?: QSL. QUERY CALL: YES tare da ƙarfin sigina da tun yaushe, idan an ji tashar cikin awa ɗaya da ta wuce, in ba haka ba NO. QUERY MSGS: NO MSG.
    • heartbeat daga wata tasha (@HB HEARTBEAT): HEARTBEAT SNR -08.
    • SNR? ko GRID? da aka aika wa ƙungiyar da ka shiga; ba a taɓa amsa tambaya zuwa @ALLCALL ko zuwa ƙungiyar da ba ka cikinta.

    Ba a amsa tambaya iri ɗaya sau biyu muddin amsar tana jira, kuma ba a taɓa amsa saƙonninka da kanka ba. qFT8 ba ya aika heartbeat ko CQ da kansa: yana aika su ne kawai idan ka danna CQ ko ka zaɓe su a Nema. AUTO na JS8 daban yake da AUTO na FT8.

    Rubuta QSO na JS8

    JS8 ba shi da tsayayyen jerin saƙonni, don haka ana rubuta QSO idan ka yanke shawarar ya cika. Buɗe hirar tashar ka danna QSO: Rubuta QSO na JS8 yana tambaya A rubuta QSO na JS8 tare da K1ABC? (kuma yana faɗin lokacin da aka rubuta JS8 QSO na ƙarshe da wannan tasha, idan akwai). Rubuta QSO yana rubuta shi a cikin log da yanayin JS8, zango da mitar, lokaci, lokata na grid na tashar idan an ji shi, da rahotannin da aka musanya a hirar a matsayin lambobi masu lamba biyu da alama (+05, -12). Daga log ana loda shi kamar kowane QSO, kuma hirar tashar a jerin tana nuna alamar tick (an rubuta), tick tare da agogo (yana jiran lodawa) ko tauraro (an tabbatar).

    Saituna > JS8

    Sashen Saitunan JS8 na Saituna > Yanayin dijital yana da:

    • Bayanai da Matsayi: rubutun da ake aika a matsayin amsa ga INFO? da STATUS? (har haruffa 20 kowanne).
    • Rufe saƙonni bayan mintuna 1 na rashin aiki: lokacin da saƙon da ba a ji firam ɗinsa na ƙarshe ba ake ɗaukarsa ya ƙare.
    • Haɗa saƙonni a cikin ± 25 na faɗin zangon: yadda firam biyu za su kusanci juna a mitar don su zama na saƙo ɗaya.
    • Ajiye saƙonni har zuwa 1000, da Ajiye saƙonni na tsawon har zuwa wata 1: yawan hirar da ake ajiyewa. Ana ajiye hirar idan an rufe manhajar.
    • Koyaushe aika dogayen saƙonnin JS8 da hutu: duba Aika saƙo.
    • Ga kowane gudu (Gudun a hankali, Gudun al'ada, Gudun sauri, Gudun turbo, Gudun ultra): Lokacin fara fassarawa da Lokacin fara watsawa, a milisekan a cikin slot.

    A kwamfuta, iPhone da na'urar sarrafa ta yanar gizo

    • Kwamfuta: allo ɗaya. Danna dama yana maye gurbin dogon latsa, danna saƙo sau biyu yana buɗe bayanansa, kuma Fitar (raba) yana ajiye fayil ɗin ya nuna shi a mai sarrafa fayil.
    • iPhone: allo ɗaya; fitar da hira yana buɗe takardar rabawa.
    • Na'urar sarrafa ta yanar gizo: shafin JS8 yana da hira ɗaya da jerin hirarraki ɗaya, tare da Fitar da Goge a kansa, da SOKE TX a gefen maɓallin aikawa yayin da saƙo ke jira ko yake kan iska. Saƙon da aka aika daga [GLOBAL] yana fara tambaya Aika saƙon duniya: zuwa @CQ ko @ALLCALL. Ana zaɓar gudu a sandar ƙasarsa.
    ↑ Koma zuwa Abubuwan da ke ciki

    WSPR da FST4W (beacons)

    WSPR ba na hira ba ne: kowace tasha tana aika gajeren beacon da alamar kiranta, lokatarta da ƙarfinta, kuma tana sauraron sauran. Abin da kake samu shi ne jerin tashoshin da kake ji, kuma ta WSPRnet da PSKReporter, taswirar waɗanda suke jin ka. Watsawar WSPR tana ɗaukar daƙiƙa 110.6 kuma tana farawa daƙiƙa ɗaya bayan mintin UTC mai lamba biyu-biyu, don haka zangon yana aiki a zagaye na minti 2. FST4W yanayin beacon ne iri ɗaya mai zagaye masu tsayi (duba FST4W a wannan allon).

    Zaɓi WSPR da mai zaɓar yanayi na sandar ƙasa. Shafin Tashoshi yana zama allon WSPR (a na'urar sarrafa daga nesa ta yanar gizo ana kiran shafin Yanayin WSPR), spectrogram yana motsawa a hankali daidai da zagayen minti 2, kuma ɓangaren spectrogram da ke wajen 1400–1600 Hz yana da inuwa: duk siginar WSPR na zangon tana cikin waɗannan 200 Hz.

    Allon WSPR

    Daga hagu zuwa dama kuma daga sama zuwa ƙasa:

    1. BEACON: Yana kunna beacon ɗinka (kore) kuma yana kashe shi (baƙi). Idan beacon yana kunne, qFT8 yana watsawa a wasu zagaye, yawansu bisa kason watsawa (abu na 2). Beacon ba ya taɓa kunna na'urar watsawarka da kansa: muddin babban maɓallin sandar ƙasa yana nuna TX OFF, ba a aika komai, kuma layi ja yana karanta Kunna TX domin fara aikawa.
    2. TX: 20%: Kason zagayen da beacon yake watsawa a cikinsu. Taɓawa tana buɗe Kason Watsawa na WSPR: 0% (Karɓa Kawai), 10%, 20%, 25%, 33%, 50% ko 100% (Mai ci gaba). Ana zaɓar zagayen ba da tsari ba, zagaye bayan zagaye, domin tashoshi biyu masu saiti ɗaya kada su riƙa watsawa a lokaci ɗaya kullum; 20% yana nufin zagaye ɗaya cikin biyar a matsakaici, kusan watsawa ɗaya kowane minti 10. Ƙimar tana ja idan iyakar watsawa ba tsayawa ta rediyo tana ba da ƙananan zagaye fiye da haka: beacon sai ya jira ya watsa a zagaye na gaba da zai iya amfani da shi.
    3. Ƙarfin (misali 5 W): Ƙarfin da saƙon beacon ɗinka yake sanarwa ga tashoshin da suke jin ka. Taɓawa tana buɗe Ƙarfin da za a ruwaito:, jeri daga 1 mW (0 dBm) zuwa 1000 W (60 dBm) a matakan da WSPR zai iya ɗauka. Saita shi zuwa ƙarfin da rediyonka yake fitarwa da gaske. Lamba ce kawai a cikin saƙonka: qFT8 ba ya taɓa canza ƙarfin rediyo, yana nan a maɓallan rediyon da kansa.
    4. FITAR: Yana ajiye spots na jerin a matsayin fayil na ADIF. A waya akwatin Raba rajistar ADIF ta WSPR yana ba da Ajiye a na'ura da Raba; kwamfuta tana tambayar inda za a ajiye fayil ɗin; iPhone yana buɗe shafin rabawa nasa; na'urar sarrafa daga nesa ta yanar gizo tana sauke fayil ɗin zuwa na'urar da kake amfani da ita.
    5. GOGE: Yana wofintar da jerin.
    6. Jerin: Layi ɗaya ga kowace siginar WSPR da aka fassara, sabuwa a sama: alamar kira, ƙasa (DXCC), lokata (Grid), SNR a dB (an auna a 2500 Hz, kamar yadda kowane mai karɓar WSPR yake bayarwa), ƙarfin da ɗayar tashar ta sanar, da kwanan wata da lokacin UTC na zagayenta (yyMMdd HH:mm). Ana ajiye jerin lokacin da aka rufe manhajar; yawan layuka da tsawon lokaci ana saita su a Saituna > WSPR.
    7. Layin matsayi: Abin da zai faru na gaba: Zagayen Rx yana ƙarewa a 01:12. Tx: Kashe idan beacon ba shi da wani shiri, Aikawa ta gaba cikin 05:40 yayin da yake jiran zagayensa, Ana aikawa — zai ƙare cikin 01:30 yayin da yake kan iska, da An kashe watsawa idan an shirya watsawa amma babban maɓallin yana nuna TX OFF. Layi ja a nan yana nufin ba za a iya aika beacon ɗinka ba: TX: saita alamar kiranku da lokatarku (ana buƙatar duka biyun, a Saituna), ko Alamar kira ta yi tsayi da yawa don aika WSPR / Tx: ba za a iya aika wannan alamar kira a WSPR ba.
    8. Tx NA GABA: Yana watsawa a zagaye na gaba, ko menene kason ya ce; daga baya beacon yana ci gaba kamar da. Yana kore idan zagaye na gaba zai yi amfani (har ma idan beacon ne ya zaɓe shi), kuma taɓawa ta biyu tana ƙin wannan zagaye. Yana toka muddin babban maɓallin yana nuna TX OFF.

    Sandar saƙo ta sandar ƙasa tana nuna saƙon da beacon ɗinka yake aikawa: alamar kiranka, haruffa huɗu na farko na lokatarka da ƙarfin abu na 3, misali HB9IPH JN47 37.

    Karɓa da aika beacon

    1. Zaɓi zangon da mai zaɓar zango: rediyo yana tafiya zuwa mitar WSPR ta zangon (duba ƙasa). Karɓa ba ta buƙatar wani abu: kowane minti 2, 'yan daƙiƙu bayan zagaye ya ƙare, ana ƙara siginar da aka fassara a ciki cikin jerin.
    2. Don aika beacon, tabbatar an saita alamar kiranka da lokatarka kuma Ƙarfin (abu na 3) shi ne abin da rediyonka yake fitarwa. Zaɓi kason watsawa (20% shi ne na kullum), bar BEACON kore, ka danna babban maɓallin sandar ƙasa har ya nuna TX ON. Layin matsayi yana ƙirga baya zuwa watsawarka ta gaba.
    3. Don aika beacon ɗaya yanzu, ko da beacon a kashe, danna Tx NA GABA.
    4. Don tsayawa, sake danna babban maɓallin (TX OFF) ko kashe BEACON. Watsawar da ke kan iska tana ƙarewa idan aka danna TX OFF.

    Watsawar WSPR tana ɗaukar kusan minti biyu: rediyon da iyakar watsawa ba tsayawa tasa ta fi haka gajarta ba zai iya aika ta ba, kuma qFT8 yana gaya maka. Ba a rubuta komai a log a WSPR: spots suna nan a jerin wannan allon kuma ba QSO ba ne.

    Zangon mita da mitoci

    Kowane zango na mai zaɓar zango yana da mitar dial ta WSPR, daga 2200 m zuwa 13 cm: 2200 m 136.000 kHz, 630 m 474.200 kHz, 160 m 1.836600 MHz, 80 m 3.568600, 60 m 5.287200, 40 m 7.038600, 30 m 10.138700, 20 m 14.095600, 17 m 18.104600, 15 m 21.094600, 12 m 24.924600, 10 m 28.124600, 6 m 50.293000, 4 m 70.091000, 2 m 144.489000, 70 cm 432.300000, 23 cm 1296.500000 da 13 cm 2400.048000 MHz (upper sideband).

    Ana aika beacon ɗinka a mitar sauti da aka zaɓa a spectrogram, cikin 1400–1600 Hz. Idan Kunna sauyin mitar bazuwar (±60 Hz) yana kunne (na asali), kowace watsawa tana matsawa zuwa wuri bazuwar har 60 Hz nesa, domin kada ka zauna a kan tasha ɗaya a kowane lokaci.

    Spots zuwa WSPRnet da PSKReporter

    • Loda rahotanni zuwa WSPRnet (a kashe tun asali; Saituna, Bayar da rahoto, da gear kusa da shi): ana aika tashoshin da ka fassara zuwa WSPRnet, kaɗan-kaɗan. Ana aika tasha ɗaya a mita ɗaya sau ɗaya kawai a kowane minti 15 a mafi yawa. Shafin WSPRnet yana da Kuma loda rahotanni lokacin sadarwa ta hanyar lasifika/makirufo (a kashe: babu loda lokacin da manhajar take saurare ta makirufon waya maimakon kebul), Rajistan loda WSPRnet na kwanan nan: mai LODA YANZU da GOGE RAJISTA, da SAKE SAITA ZUWA NA ASALI.
    • Loda rahotanni zuwa PSKReporter (Saituna, Bayar da rahoto): yana aika fassarorin WSPR da FST4W ɗinka ma, kamar kowane yanayi.
    • Sauke spots daga WSPRnet (a kunne tun asali; Saituna, Tushen bayanai): yana ɗauko waɗanda suka karɓi beacon ɗinka kuma yana nuna su a taswirar duniya da maɓallin SPOT nata.

    Saituna > WSPR

    Sashen WSPR na Saituna > Yanayin dijital yana da:

    • Kunna sauyin mitar bazuwar (±60 Hz) (a kunne): duba Zangon mita da mitoci.
    • Ajiye saƙonni har zuwa 1000, da Ajiye saƙonni na tsawon har zuwa Wata 1: layuka nawa jerin yake ajiyewa, kuma na tsawon lokaci nawa.
    • Lokacin fara fassarawa (114000 ms): a wane lokaci, cikin zagayen minti 2, fassara take farawa.
    • Lokacin fara watsawa (1000 ms): a wane lokaci, cikin zagaye, beacon yake fara watsawa.

    FST4W a wannan allon

    FST4W (layin FST4W (gwaji) na mai zaɓar yanayi) yana amfani da allo ɗaya: jeri ɗaya, BEACON, Tx NA GABA, ƙarfi, FITAR da GOGE, da loda iri ɗaya zuwa WSPRnet da PSKReporter. Abin da ya bambanta:

    • Zagayen shi ne Lokaci: daƙiƙa 120, 300, 900 ko 1800, ana zaɓa a sandar ƙasa ko a Saituna. Idan lokaci ya fi tsayi, ana fassara sigina masu rauni (kusan −33 dB a 120 s, −45 dB a 1800 s) kuma kana watsawa na tsawon lokaci: har kusan minti 30, wanda dole iyakar watsawa ba tsayawa ta ba da dama. Dole tashoshin biyu su yi amfani da lokaci ɗaya.
    • Kason watsawa nasa ne daban (Kashi na aikawa, 20 % na asali): canza shi a FST4W ba ya canza na WSPR.
    • Yana fassara ne kawai a kewayen mitar karɓa (yawanci 1500 Hz), cikin haƙurin karɓarsa.
    • A 2200 m, 630 m da 160 m yana da mitocinsa (136.000 kHz, 474.200 kHz, 1.8368 MHz); a sauran zangon yana amfani da mitocin WSPR.

    An bayyana saitunansa a Saituna > FST4W.

    ↑ Koma zuwa Abubuwan da ke ciki

    SSB (murya)

    A yanayin SSB, qFT8 ita ce makirufo da lasifikar transceiver ɗinka: siginar da rediyo yake karɓa tana zama murya a lasifikarka ko headphones, kuma muryarka tana tafiya daga makirufonka zuwa rediyo. Rediyo yana kasancewa a haɗe daidai kamar yadda yake ga yanayin dijital (kebul na USB ɗaya, katin sauti ɗaya), don haka babu abin da ake buƙatar sake sakawa domin canzawa daga FT8 zuwa murya da komawa.

    Ƙayyadaddun bayanai: ajin watsawa J3E na ITU (Dokokin Rediyo na ITU); murya ba ta da ka’idar dijital.

    qFT8 da kansa yake samar da siginar murya kuma yana aika ta ta katin sauti na rediyo. Yana sa rediyo a yanayin bayanai na gefen bandi ɗaya (misali USB-D / LSB-D, DATA-U / DATA-L, DIGU / DIGL; a QMX, yanayinsa na USB ko LSB), don haka allon rediyo yana nuna mita ɗaya da qFT8. Gefen bandi yana bin bandi: LSB ƙasa da 10 MHz, USB daga 10 MHz zuwa sama da kuma a 60 m.

    Zaɓi SSB da mai zaɓar yanayi na sandar ƙasa. Wurin saƙo, maɓallan jeri da sandar zagaye suna ɓacewa (murya ba ta da zagayen lokaci), maɓallin USB / LSB yana bayyana kusa da mai zaɓar yanayi, kuma shafin Tashoshi yana zama allon SSB:

    Ga sassan allon SSB dalla-dalla:

    1. RX / TX / RX/TX: Yana zaɓar abin da ƙafar daidaitawa (na #12) take motsawa. Da RX/TX kana karɓa da watsawa a mita ɗaya. Da RX ko TX, wannan kaɗai ne yake motsi, don haka kana iya saurare a mita ɗaya ka watsa a wata (split). Ba tare da haɗin CAT ba waɗannan maɓallan suna da launin toka kuma suna zama a RX/TX; taɓawa tana cewa Daidaitawa na buƙatar haɗin CAT da rediyo.
    2. AUTO RIT: Yana riƙe tashar da ta ɗan kauce daga mita a tsakiya, domin muryarta ta yi daidai ba tare da taɓa ƙafar ba.
    3. Gear: Yana buɗe Saituna > SSB (duba ƙasa). BAYA yana komawa wannan allo.
    4. QSO: Yana buɗe log na hannu, an riga an cika yanayi, gefen zango (sideband) da mita, domin rubuta QSO da ka yi yanzu (duba Log na hannu). QSO ɗaya a kowane danna. Rahotanni (59 / 59) ma an cike su, kuma bayan hulɗar split, duka mitar da kuka watsa da wadda kuka saurara.
    5. Ma'ajiyoyin murya: Saƙonnin da ka ɗauka (kiran CQ, alamar kiranka...). Har sai ka ɗauki ɗaya, wannan wuri yana nuna Saita ma'ajiyoyin murya a Saituna.
    6. IN: Matakin makirufonka da abin jan gain nasa. Layin da aka rubuta VOX shi ne matakin da dole muryarka ta wuce domin kunna watsawar rediyo idan VOX yana kunne (na #10): ja shi.
    7. OUT: Matakin sautin da aka karɓa da abin jan ƙarar lasifikarka. Maɓallan ƙara na waya suna yin iri ɗaya. A ƙasan sandar gaba ɗaya sauti a kashe yake (MUTE).
    8. FILTER da NR: Matatar karɓa (a kashe, 2.7, 2.4 ko 1.8 kHz) da rage hayaniya (a kashe, 1, 2 ko 3). Kowane taɓawa yana zuwa lamba ta gaba. Waɗannan alamomi iri ɗaya ne a kowane harshe.
    9. PTT: Danna ka yi magana. Yana cewa "riƙe don magana" ko "taɓa don magana" bisa saitin Yanayin PTT, da "ANA AIKAWA" yayin da kake watsawa. Da madannai (kwamfuta, sarrafawa ta yanar gizo, waya ko kwamfutar hannu), maɓallin space yana yin iri ɗaya idan wuri na ƙarshe da ka danna shi ne sandar ƙasa, spectrogram, taswirar duniya ko wannan allon; a cikin akwatin rubutu yana ci gaba da rubuta sarari. Lokacin watsawa yana nuna sauran daƙiƙoƙi idan iyaka tana aiki (duba ƙasa). Idan VOX ta kai iyaka, yana nuna "An dakatar da VOX", kuma "Babu SSB" idan rediyo ba zai iya watsa murya daga manhajar ba.
    10. VOX: Idan yana kunne, muryarka ce take kunna watsawar rediyo maimakon maɓallin PTT.
    11. USB / LSB: Gefen zango (sideband). Ana zaɓa maka shi idan ka canza zango (LSB ƙasa da 10 MHz, USB sama); taɓawa tana canza shi, kuma mitar da kake karantawa tana nan yadda take. A 60 m ma USB ne, kuma canjin bandi da maɓallin rediyo kansa ma yana zaɓar gefen bandi na bandin.
    12. Ƙafar daidaitawa: A SSB, ma'aunin mita na spectrogram ƙafa ce. Shafa a kanta domin daidaita rediyo; shafa mai sauri tana ɗaukar manyan matakai. Taɓawa ba ta yin komai, don haka mita ba ta tsalle bisa kuskure. A kwamfuta, ƙafar linzami ma tana daidaitawa (riƙe Shift domin matakai na 1 Hz). Daidaitawa na buƙatar haɗin CAT da rediyo.
    13. Mita da zango: Mitar da za ka faɗa a iska (carrier), da zango. Layin orange da rubutun RX/TX a ƙarƙashinsa suna nuna ɓangaren spectrogram da kake saurare da wanda kake watsawa a kai. Taɓa mitar yana buɗe Mitar rediyo (kHz) don rubuta ɗaya; mita a wani bandi tana canza bandin. Wannan ma yana buƙatar CAT.
    14. Guntun STEP, a ƙarƙashin mita (SSB, CW, RTTY): taɓa tsakiyarsa yana zaɓar mataki (AUTO, 10, 50, 100, 500 Hz, 1 ko 5 kHz; ana tunawa da shi ga kowanne daga SSB, CW da RTTY); kibiyoyinsa suna kunna mataki ɗaya ƙasa ko sama, zuwa mita mai zagaye ta gaba. Da mataki tsayayye kowane layi na zirin kunnawa na SSB yana motsawa da wannan matakin (AUTO: zamewa da sauri yana ɗaukar manyan matakai). A kwamfuta maɓallan LEFT da RIGHT ma suna yin mataki a SSB. Yana buƙatar CAT.

    Da QMX ko QMX+, layi mai launin toka a saman maɓallin PTT yana tunatarwa cewa tushen sautin SSB na rediyo (menunsa SSB > Input) dole ya zama katin sauti na USB (duba Abin da SSB ke buƙata).

    Magana

    1. Shirya watsawa da maɓallin TX na sandar ƙasa. Kamar a kowane yanayi, babu abin da ake watsawa muddin yana cewa TX OFF: jan layin Danna TX ON don aikawa yana tunatar da kai.
    2. Riƙe PTT ka yi magana, sannan ka sake shi. Ko kuma kunna VOX ka yi magana kawai.
    3. Domin aika ma'ajiyar murya, taɓa ta. Da TX ON ana watsa ta, kuma kana jin ta a lasifikarka ma. Da TX OFF ana kunna maka ita kawai, wanda hanya ce mai kyau ta duba abin da ka ɗauka. Dogon danna (danna dama a kwamfuta) koyaushe yana kunna maka ita kawai.
    4. Idan hulɗar ta ƙare, danna QSO don rubuta ta a log.

    Ba a taɓa mayar da muryarka kai tsaye a lasifikarka. Idan mitar da kake son watsawa a kai tana wajen zangon amateur, qFT8 ba ta kunna watsawar rediyo kuma tana faɗa.

    Iyakar watsawa: Kowace watsawar SSB tana ƙarewa da kanta a iyakar watsawa ba tsayawa ta rediyo (Tsarin Na'ura), ko bayan minti 3 idan rediyo ba shi da iyaka. Da Taɓa don sauyawa da kuma VOX, maɓallin PTT yana ƙidaya sauran daƙiƙoƙi baya (da Riƙe don magana da ma'ajiyoyin murya, sai idan rediyo yana da iyaka). Bayan iyakar, VOX tana zama a dakatarwa na lokaci ɗaya, ko har sai kun taɓa PTT. Wannan yana kare rediyo daga makirufo da ke ci gaba da jin hayaniya.

    Ma'ajiyoyin murya

    Je zuwa Saituna > SSB > Ma'ajiyoyin murya (ko taɓa Saita ma'ajiyoyin murya a Saituna a allon) ka danna NAƊI SABUWAR MA'AJIYAR MURYA. Kana iya ajiye har ma'ajiyoyi 6 na daƙiƙa 30 kowacce. Ga kowacce kana iya ba ta suna, sake ɗaukar ta, saita ƙararta ko danna DAIDAITA, da yanke shiru daga farkonta da ƙarshenta ta jan hannaye biyu a hoton sautin; KUNNA yana kunna ɓangaren da ka bari, kuma babu abin da yake canzawa sai ka ajiye. Ma'ajiyoyi suna cikin fitar da saituna, har da sautukan da aka ɗauka. Ma'ajiyar murya tana naɗar muryarku daga makirufonku, ba sautin rediyo ba. qFT8 ba ya naɗar hulɗoɗinku.

    Saituna > SSB

    • Hanyar PTT: Maɓallin PTT, ko VOX.
    • Yanayin PTT: Riƙe don magana ko Taɓa don sauyawa.
    • Jinkirin PTT: Lokaci tsakanin kunna watsawa a rediyo da aika muryarka. Babu abin da ka faɗa da zai ɓace: kalmarka ta farko tana jiran rediyo.
    • Gefen band: Ta atomatik bisa zango, koyaushe USB, ko koyaushe LSB. Canji yana aiki nan take, har lokacin da kuke cikin SSB.
    • Matata da rage hayaniya lokacin shiga SSB.
    • Kunna sautin da aka karɓa a lasifika da Kashe sautin lasifika yayin aikawa. Na biyun koyaushe a kunne yake yayin da ake amfani da makirufo da lasifika na wayar kanta, don kada wayar ta ji kanta.
    • Ma'ajiyoyin murya da SAKE SAITA ZUWA NA ASALI.

    Abin da SSB yake buƙata

    • Makirufo da lasifika waɗanda ba katin sauti na rediyo ba ne. Waya tana da nata; headset ma yana aiki. Duba Lasifika da makirufo. Idan rediyo da makirufonka ko lasifikarka na'ura ɗaya ce, jan layi yana faɗa, kuma babu abin da ake aikawa ko kunnawa.
    • Rediyo. Idan babu, ana maye gurbin allon da kati mai cewa Haɗa rediyo don amfani da SSB; allon yana dawowa da kansa idan aka haɗa rediyo. Rediyon da ba shi da CAT, wanda VOX nasa yake kunna watsawarsa, ma yana aiki, amma manhaja ba za ta iya daidaita shi ba. Katin yana da maɓalli zuwa Tsarin Na'ura. Ba tare da CAT ba kuna daidaitawa da maɓallin rediyo: maɓallan RX / TX, dabaran da AUTO RIT suna zama da launin toka. Wasu katuna suna faɗi idan ba a zaɓi makirufo ko lasifika ba, idan an kashe Cikakken duplex a Tsarin Na'ura, ko idan sautin wayar ba zai iya naɗa da kunnawa a lokaci ɗaya ba.
    • QMX ko QMX+ yana buƙatar firmware 1_04_001 ko sabo don watsa murya daga manhajar. Da tsohon firmware maɓallin PTT yana cewa Babu SSB, layi ja yana bayyana dalilin, kuma yanayin karɓa ne kawai.
    • A QMX qFT8 yana saita SSB > Input na rediyo zuwa USB yayin da kuke cikin SSB kuma yana mayar da zaɓinku idan kun fita; ba a ajiye komai a rediyo ba. Idan firmware bai yarda ba, layi ja yana roƙonku ku saita shi a menun rediyo. Idan rediyo bai fitar da ƙarfi ba yayin da muryarku ke zuwa gare shi, saƙo ɗaya yana lissafa abin da za a duba (SSB > Input, ribar USB na rediyo, firmware).
    • A wasu wayoyi ba za a iya amfani da VOX da rediyon USB ba, saboda wayar ba za ta iya sauraron makirufonta da rediyo a lokaci ɗaya ba; a can ku yi amfani da maɓallin PTT.
    • A karo na farko da ka je wani zango a SSB, qFT8 tana daidaitawa zuwa mitar murya da aka saba a wannan zango a yankinka na duniya; bayan haka tana komawa mitar ƙarshe da ka yi amfani da ita a can. Zangon 30 m ba shi da ɓangaren murya, don haka ba a ba da shi.
    • Idan ba a yarda qFT8 ya saita yanayin rediyo ba (wannan saitin a kashe yake a Tsarin Na'ura) ko ba a san umarnin bayanai na LSB na rediyo ba, rediyo yana zama a yanayin bayanai na USB kuma qFT8 da kansa yake samar da LSB: a bandunan LSB allon rediyo zai nuna mita 3 kHz ƙasa da wadda qFT8 ke nunawa. Ko ta yaya, ku yi magana a makirufon da aka zaɓa a Lasifika da makirufo kuma ku yi amfani da PTT na qFT8, ba na rediyo ba.

    Ana rubuta QSO a matsayin SSB tare da gefen zangonsu, kuma ana loda su zuwa QRZ da LoTW kamar kowane QSO.

    A kwamfuta, iPhone da sarrafawa ta yanar gizo

    • Kwamfuta da iPhone suna nuna allo ɗaya da maɓallai iri ɗaya. A kwamfuta, danna dama a kan ma'ajiyar murya yana kunna ta gare ku kaɗai, kuma dabaran linzamin kwamfuta a saman spectrogram yana daidaitawa.
    • The web remote cannot switch the app to SSB. While the app is in SSB the page shows the waterfall and three buttons: Saurara plays the radio's audio on the page (while you transmit, what you send and the TUNE tone), Microphone makes the browser's microphone the radio's microphone, and PTT transmits (one tap on, one tap off; the space bar does the same). It transmits only with the app's TX ON. Browsers give a page its microphone only on a secure address: turn on the secure address (https) in the web server settings and open the link the page shows. The radio frequency is tuned from the page as in the app: a tap on the frequency at the right of the bar over the waterfall asks for the radio frequency (kHz), and the STEP chip beside it picks the step (tap its middle) and tunes one step down or up (its arrows). A tap on the waterfall does not tune in SSB (as in the app); the page says so. Without CAT the page shows the app's notice instead of tuning.
    ↑ Koma zuwa Abubuwan da ke ciki

    AM, FM, airband da marine VHF (murya)

    Jerin yanayi yana da ƙarin shigarwar murya da ke buɗe fannin SSB tare da wani mai karɓa a bayansa: AM da FM (bands ɗin masu son rediyo, karɓa da watsawa; kowanne kuma a matsayin layin I/Q, duba a ƙasa), Muryar jirgin sama (jirage da hasumiyai, 118-137 MHz, karɓa kawai) da Marine VHF (jiragen ruwa da tashoshin gaɓa, karɓa kawai). Sandar ƙasa sai ta ambaci yanayin AM ko FM, kuma zaɓin sideband yana ɓacewa: waɗannan sigina ba su da shi.

    Ƙayyadaddun bayanai: azuzuwan emission na ITU A3E (AM, airband) da F3E (marine VHF, narrow FM, tashoshi na 25 kHz); tashoshin ruwa na ƙasa da ƙasa na ITU Radio Regulations, Appendix 18; airband 118-137 MHz da tashoshi na 25 kHz ko 8.33 kHz (ICAO Annex 10).

    • Da rediyo ƙarƙashin CAT qFT8 yana sanya rediyon a cikin yanayinsa na AM ko FM (umarnin yanayi kawai: ba ya taɓa canza ƙarfi) kuma yana mayar da shi yanayin data ɗinsa idan ka fita. Allon rediyon yana nuna ainihin mitar tashar.
    • Da mai karɓa na SDR (misali RTL-SDR dongle) qFT8 yana demodulating da kansa: AM synchronous wanda ke zama mai tsabta idan carrier ya yi fading, da narrow FM mai squelch da ke sa tashar da ba a amfani da ita ta yi shiru.
    • Rediyon da qFT8 ba zai iya canza zuwa AM ba yana zama a yanayin data ɗinsa: za ka ji tashar AM a matsayin upper sideband ɗinta (daidaita daidai da carrier ɗinta), kuma qFT8 ba ya watsa AM ta cikinsa.

    Magana a AM

    Ana aika AM daidai kamar yadda ake aika SSB: TX ON na mai aiki da farko, sannan PTT ko VOX, memory na murya, iyakar watsawa mai ci gaba ta rediyo. qFT8 yana aika AM ne kawai a cikin band na masu son rediyo (dole bangarorin carrier biyu su shiga) kuma ta rediyon da ya sanya a AM kawai. Ana yin log na QSO a matsayin AM.

    Muryar FM: ƙunƙuntacciya da mai faɗi

    FM ita ce muryar FM ta masu son rediyo (10 m, 6 m, 2 m, 70 cm zuwa sama), ana karɓarta da watsa ta kamar AM, kuma ana rubuta ta a log a matsayin FM. A karon farko tana buɗewa a 29.600 MHz (mitar kira ta FM ta 10 m) sannan a mitar ƙarshe da kuka yi amfani da ita. A inda maɓallin AUTO RIT yake a SSB, wani maɓalli yana nuna faɗin tasha; taɓa shi don canzawa:

    • 25 kHz (mai faɗi, zaɓi na farko): karkacewar ±5 kHz, kusan 16 kHz ake amfani da su. Wannan shi ne abin da yanayin FM na yau da kullum na rediyo yake aikawa, kuma mai karɓa mai faɗi yana jin ƙunƙuntacciyar tasha duk da haka.
    • 12.5 kHz (ƙunƙuntacce): karkacewar ±2.5 kHz, kusan 11 kHz ake amfani da su, ƙunƙuntattun tashoshin tsare-tsaren band da masu maimaita sigina da yawa.

    FM mono ce: stereo na FM ɗin watsa labarai ne (allon watsa labarai). Faɗin yana kuma yanke shawarar duba gefen band: qFT8 yana watsawa ne kawai idan duka tashar ta shiga cikin band. Da rediyo a ƙarƙashin CAT, qFT8 yana zaɓar yanayin FM na yau da kullum na rediyon; don watsawa a ƙunƙunce, ka saita kuma yanayin FM-N na rediyon ko matatar ƙunƙunce (qFT8 ba ya canza shi).

    Rediyo ko I/Q: AM / AM I/Q, FM / FM I/Q

    Kamar SSB da SSB I/Q, kowanne daga AM da FM yana da layuka biyu. Layin na yau da kullum yana bar rediyo ya yi demodulation: qFT8 yana sanya shi a yanayin AM ko FM nasa kuma yana kunna sautin rediyon, tare da spectrogram ɗin sauti. Layin I/Q (gwaji) yana ɗaukar I/Q ɗin da ba a sarrafa ba na SDR, ko na rediyon da ke bayar da shi (QMX a yanayin I/Q ɗinsa), yana yin demodulation a cikin manhajar kuma yana nuna spectrogram ɗin I/Q mai faɗi. Haka rediyon da mai karɓarsa faɗin 3 kHz ne kawai zai iya jin tashar FM duk da haka: faɗin I/Q ɗinsa 48 kHz ne. FM I/Q yana buƙatar I/Q na 32 kS/s ko fiye (20 kS/s na KiwiSDR ya yi ƙunci sosai). Rediyon da ba shi da yanayin FM (ko AM) layin yau da kullum nasa yana launin toka, ana nuna shi ne kawai da Nuna duka.

    LOW BANDWIDTH a maɓallin TX

    Karɓa da watsawa tambayoyi ne daban-daban. Idan rediyon da aka zaɓa ba zai iya watsa yanayin layin ba, maɓallin TX yana zama ja yana rubuta LOW BANDWIDTH (da Turanci, kamar duk kalmominsa): na'urar watsawar rediyon SSB / CW / bayanai faɗinta kusan 3 kHz ne, yayin da AM ke buƙatar kusan 6 kHz kuma FM 11-16 kHz. Kuna ci gaba da saurare; babu abin da ake kunnawa, kuma TX yana kasancewa yadda kuka bar shi. qFT8 yana sanin wannan daga takardun masu ƙera kowane rediyo; idan ba su warware wani yanayi ba (FM ƙunƙuntacce, wanda ya dogara da saitin rediyon kansa), ba ya toshewa. Da SDR na karɓa kawai maɓallin yana rubuta NO TX / SDR kamar a kowane yanayi.

    Airband da marine VHF

    Dukansu karɓa ne kawai: fannin ba shi da PTT, ba shi da VOX kuma ba shi da maɓallin QSO. A maimakonsu yana nuna tasha da kibiyoyi biyu don sauka da hawa (dabaran mita ma tana canza tashoshi) da mitar a ƙasanta. Airband yana tafiya 8.33 kHz a Turai, Afirka da Gabas ta Tsakiya (bisa locator ɗinka) kuma 25 kHz a wasu wurare, kuma yana buɗewa a 121.500 MHz a karon farko; marine VHF yana buɗewa a tasha ta 16 kuma yana jin kowace tasha ta duplex a mitar tashar gaɓarta. Kowanne yana riƙe mitar ƙarshe da ka yi amfani da ita. Sauraron waɗannan ayyuka ba a yarda da shi a ko'ina ba: duba dokokin ƙasarka.

    Alamar passband a kan spectrogram tana karanta RX a can, ba RX/TX ba: qFT8 ba ya aikawa a waɗannan ayyuka, kuma tare da rediyo ƙarƙashin CAT maɓallin TX yana karanta NO TX / RX (duba Mai zaɓar yanayi). VHF na sama da na teku tare da SDR ba sa taɓa neman rediyon da ke aikawa. A SSB da AM ba tare da rediyo ba allon yana ci gaba da amfani don saurare, kuma jan layi a ƙasansa, “Haɗa rediyo don amfani da SSB.” (ko “Haɗa rediyo don amfani da AM.”), yana buɗe Tsarin Na'ura idan an taɓa shi. A kwamfuta, ba tare da rediyo ƙarƙashin CAT ba (SDR ko katin sauti kaɗai), ƙafafun linzami a kan spectrogram yana zuƙowa a SSB, AM, VHF na sama da na teku. Zaɓar yanayi ko bandi na mai son rediyo bayan haka yana komawa bandin da kake kai kafin mai karɓa.

    ↑ Koma zuwa Abubuwan da ke ciki

    FreeDV (muryar dijital, na gwaji)

    FreeDV a cikin jerin yanayi (shafin HAM, muryar dijital, layi ɗaya da aka yi wa alama a matsayin na gwaji) muryar dijital ce a HF, mai dacewa da FreeDV 700D, 700E, 1600 da 700C: ana sanya muryarka a code da Codec 2 700C sannan a aika ta a matsayin sigina mai kunkuntar (faɗin kusan 1 kHz) na masu ɗauka 17 wanda tsakiyarsa ke 1500 Hz sama da mitar dial, tare da gyaran kuskure, kuma tashar da aka ji a cikinsa ana mayar da ita magana a lasifikarka. Yana buɗe fane na SSB da irin wannan tuning, matakan sauti, ma'adanai da na'urorin aunawa; kullum shi ne upper sideband, a kowane band. Ban da RADE V1 da RADE V2 (a ƙasa), yana da ƙananan yanayi guda huɗu na gargajiya: 700D (wanda aka fi amfani da shi a cikinsu), 700E (don saurin fading, faɗin kusan 1.5 kHz) da 1600 (tsohon, mai magana mafi bayyana, Codec 2 1300, faɗin kusan 1.2 kHz, yana buƙatar siginar da ta fi ƙarfi) da 700C (magana iri ɗaya da ta 700D ba tare da gyaran kuskure ba: ana aika kowane bit sau biyu, a kan masu ɗaukar biyu; yana kullewa da sauri kuma yana jure saurin dusashewa da kyau a matsakaicin matakan sigina, faɗin kusan 1.5 kHz, babu tashar rubutu). Zaɓi ƙaramin yanayi daga jerin da ke kusa da yanayin a mashaya ta ƙasa (RADE V1 da RADE V2 suna sama), ko danna maɓallin matsayi don zuwa na gaba; ana tuna zaɓin. Dole ne tashoshin biyu su yi amfani da ƙaramin yanayi iri ɗaya.

    RADE V1 shi ne ƙaramin yanayi na farko a cikin mai zaɓe kuma na asali: muryar dijital mai amfani da autoencoder na rediyo wanda yawancin tashoshin FreeDV ke amfani da shi yau. Yana ɗaukar magana mai faɗin band (har zuwa 6 kHz a qFT8) cikin kusan 1.5 kHz na RF kuma yana ci gaba da aiki har kusan -2 dB SNR (3 kHz), da jinkirin 120 ms. Maɓallin matsayi yana nuna RADE V1 NO SYNC yayin nema da RADE V1 SYNC 4 dB (rabon sigina da hayaniya) bayan ya kulle. Mai karɓa, mai aikawa, nazarin magana da na'urar haɗa murya na qFT8 ne da kansa kuma suna amfani da bayanan samfurin RADE V1 da aka wallafa (an ambata a cikin lasisi); ana aika sunan kiran ku (daga Saituna) a cikin firam ɗin ƙarshen kowane over, kamar yadda tashoshin RADE ke yi, kuma sunan kiran ɗayan tashar, da aka karɓa a ƙarshen over ɗinta, yana bayyana a maɓallin yanayi na minti ɗaya (misali RADE V1 NO SYNC HB9IPH) da cikin rajistar saƙonni na asali. PTT, VOX da ma'ajiyoyi suna aiki da TX ON kamar sauran ƙananan yanayi, iyakokin aikawa iri ɗaya suna aiki, kuma dole ne tashoshin biyu su yi amfani da ƙaramin yanayi iri ɗaya. Gwaji ne, kamar FreeDV. RADE V2, kai tsaye a ƙasansa, shi ne sigar gaba ta wannan tunani ɗaya (mafi kunkuntar, kusan 0.9 kHz, kuma ya fi jin sigina da wasu dB); har yanzu ana ci gaba da haɓaka siffar igiyarsa da aka wallafa kuma tana iya canzawa, saboda haka an yi masa alama a matsayin na gwaji kuma yana buƙatar ɗayan tashar ma ta yi amfani da RADE V2.

    Ƙayyadaddun bayanai: FreeDV 700D: OFDM, masu ɗaukar bayanai 17 masu tazarar 55.6 Hz (sauti 1000-2000 Hz), QPSK mai daidaituwa tare da jerin pilot ɗaya a kowane alama 8, alamomi na 20 ms (prefix mai zagayawa na 2 ms), firam na 160 ms, LDPC (224,112), magana 700 bit/s (Codec 2 700C), tashar rubutu ta 25 bit/s (varicode); ana amfani da shi kusan 14.236 MHz da 7.177 MHz USB. FreeDV 700E: masu ɗaukar bayanai 21 masu tazarar 71.4 Hz (sauti 750-2250 Hz), jerin pilot ɗaya a kowane alama 4, alamomi na 20 ms (prefix mai zagayawa na 6 ms), firam na 80 ms, LDPC (112,56), irin wannan codec na magana da tashar rubutu. FreeDV 1600: masu ɗaukar DQPSK 16 masu tazarar 75 Hz (sauti 900-2100 Hz) da pilot na BPSK a 1500 Hz mai ninki biyu na girmansu wanda ke alamar firam, 50 baud (alamomi na 20 ms, root raised cosine 50 %), firam na 40 ms, magana 1300 bit/s (Codec 2 1300) tare da mafi muhimman bit 12 na ta a kare da lambar Golay (23,12), tashar rubutu ta 25 bit/s (varicode). FreeDV 700C: masu ɗaukar 14 (sauti 770-2150 Hz, tazarar kusan 106 Hz), ana aika kowace alamar bayanai a kan biyu daga cikinsu (diversity), QPSK mai daidaituwa tare da alamomin pilot 2 da alamomin bayanai 4 ga kowane mai ɗaukar kaya a kowane firam na 80 ms, 75 baud (root raised cosine 50 %), magana 700 bit/s (firam biyu na Codec 2 700C a kowane firam), babu gyaran kuskure kuma babu tashar rubutu. RADE V1: OFDM, masu ɗaukar bayanai 30 masu tazarar 50 Hz (sauti 750-2200 Hz), jerin pilot ɗaya a kowane jeri 5, jerin na 20 ms (prefix mai zagayawa na 4 ms), firam na 120 ms masu ɗauke da magana a matsayin alamomi masu ƙima mai ci gaba daga samfurin radio autoencoder da aka wallafa, an yanke envelope (peak-to-average ƙasa da 1 dB), firam ɗin ƙarshen watsawa; magana 16 kHz (6 kHz a qFT8). RADE V2: masu ɗaukar bayanai 14 masu tazarar 62.5 Hz (sauti 1062-1875 Hz), babu pilot, alamomi na 16 ms (prefix mai zagayawa na 4 ms), firam na 40 ms.

    • Karɓa: maɓallin da AUTO RIT yake a SSB yana nuna ƙaramin yanayi da NO SYNC, ko SYNC tare da rabon sigina zuwa hayaniya da sunan kira na ɗayan tashar idan rubutunta ya zo (700C ba shi da tashar rubutu). Ba tare da sync ba (ko a firam da ya lalace da yawa don gyarawa) lasifika yana yin shiru.
    • Magana: daidai kamar a SSB: da farko TX ON na mai aiki, sai PTT ko VOX (ko ma'adanar murya), ƙarƙashin iyakar watsawa ta ci gaba ta rediyo. Ana aika sunan kiranka a tashar rubutu (sai dai a cikin 700C). qFT8 ba ya canza ƙarfin rediyo har abada: saita ƙarfin a rediyo (muryar dijital tana da matsakaicin ƙarfi fiye da maganar SSB).
    • Ana rubuta QSO a matsayin DIGITALVOICE / FREEDV (ADIF).
    ↑ Koma zuwa Abubuwan da ke ciki

    CW (Morse)

    A yanayin CW, qFT8 tana karanta kowace siginar Morse a passband kuma tana rubuta rubutunta a spectrogram, kuma tana aika Morse daga paddle na kan allo, maɓallanka, saƙonnin da aka shirya, ko rubutun da ka buga. Da TX OFF komai yana aiki a matsayin gwaji: kana jin abin da kake aikawa a lasifika, kuma babu abin da ke fita iska.

    Ƙayyadaddun bayanai: ITU-R Shawara M.1677-1, “International Morse code” (2009).

    qFT8 yana aika CW a matsayin tsabtataccen sautin murya mai siffa ta hanyar yanayin bayanai na rediyo (misali DIGU ko DIGL, USB-D ko LSB-D), saboda haka rediyon yana canza zuwa wannan yanayi yayin aikawa, karɓa kuma yana nan faɗi. Banda shi ne QRP Labs QMX: ana yin masa makulli da ainihin CW ta hanyar CAT.

    Zaɓi CW da mai zaɓar yanayi na sandar ƙasa:

    Ga sassan allon CW dalla-dalla:

    1. AUTO: Yana barin qFT8 ta yi cikakkun QSO na CW da kanta (duba AUTO a CW).
    2. RX / TX / RX/TX: Yana zaɓar sandar da taɓa waterfall ko ƙafar daidaitawa take motsawa: sandar karɓa, sandar watsawa, ko duka tare. AUTO RIT, bayan su, yana kai sandar karɓa kan tashar da ke kiran ka (duba AUTO RIT).
    3. Gear yana buɗe Saituna > CW, kuma QSO yana buɗe log na hannu, an riga an cika CW, mita da manufa.
    4. Manufa: Alamar kiran tashar da kake aiki da ita. Tana cika ɓangaren [TARGET] na saƙonninka.
