Taɓa shi domin rubuta ɗaya, ko taɓa alamar kira a layin mai fassara (na #10) domin ɗauko ta daga can. Yayin da AUTO ke kunne kuma TX a kashe, layi mai launin toka a ƙasansa yana cewa K1ABC yana kiran ka – danna TX ON idan wata tasha ta kira ka.
    5. Ma'ajiyoyi: Saƙonnin da aka shirya. Taɓawa tana aika ɗaya. Ana nuna alamar kiranka da shuɗi, rahoto kuma da rawaya. Ma'ajiyar da take buƙatar wani abu har yanzu (manufa, sunanka...) ana dusashe ta; taɓa ta domin ganin abin da ya rage. Idan AUTO RIT na kunne, layukan Bi K1ABC +150 Hz? na tashoshin da ke kiran ka suna bayyana a saman wannan jeri.
    6. WPM da FARNSWORTH: Saurin aikawa a kalmomi a minti, da saurin Farnsworth na zaɓi (haruffa suna riƙe saurinsu, tazarar tsakaninsu kuma tana ƙaruwa).
    7. FILTER, NR da SIDETONE: Faɗin matatar karɓa a kusa da sandar karɓa, rage hayaniya, da ƙarar sautin gefe naka (duba Sautin gefe).
    8. Paddle: Na hagu yana aika dit, na dama yana aika dah. Danna duka biyun domin yanayin iambic.
    9. Jan layi: Danna TX ON don aikawa. Muddin yana nan, bugun maɓallinka gwaji ne.
    10. Layin mai fassara: Yana inda mai zaɓar saƙo yake a FT8. Yana nuna rubutun tashar da ke cikin matatarka da fari, abin da kake aikawa kuma da kore, sabo a dama. Taɓa alamar kira tana ba da ita a matsayin manufa; taɓawa a ko'ina kuma tana sa dukkan tattaunawar wannan mita a wurin spectrogram, sabo a ƙasa (ja domin karanta tsohon rubutu), layin mai fassara kuma yana zama ƙaramin waterfall: gajeriyar taɓawa a kansa tana dawo da spectrogram, riƙe shi da ja shi gefe kuma yana canza mita.
    11. Rubutun da aka fassara a waterfall: Kowace sigina tana da rubutunta a rubuce tare da hanyarta, don haka kana karanta dukkan band a lokaci ɗaya: tashar da ke cikin matatarka da kore, manufa da magenta, sauran da fari, abin da kake aikawa kuma da rawaya; kalmar da ake sake karantawa har yanzu ana rubuta ta a karkace. Lakabin da ke ƙarƙashin sandar karɓa yana nuna alamar kira da saurin tashar da kake kai.
    12. Ƙafar daidaitawa: Ma'aunin mita. Shafa a kanta domin motsa sandar da aka zaɓa (da rediyo, idan sandar ta kai gefe). Taɓawa ko ja a waterfall yana motsa sandar kai tsaye.
    13. Je zuwa mita: Taɓa layin da yake da farin layin ƙarfin sigina domin rubuta mita a kHz.

    Aikawa

    Akwai hanyoyi huɗu na aikawa, kuma dukkansu suna amfani da saurin da aka saita da WPM:

    • Paddle na kan allo, da yatsa ɗaya ko biyu.
    • Maɓallanka: maɓallan madannai, maɓallan linzami (a kwamfuta, maɓallin hagu shi ne paddle na hagu, na dama kuma paddle na dama), ko adaftar keyer da aka haɗa ta USB ko Bluetooth wadda take aiki kamar madannai. Duba Paddle da keyer.
    • Ma'ajiya: taɓa ta. Ma'ajiya ta biyu da aka taɓa a wannan lokaci tana jiran nata lokacin. Layi mai TSAYA yana bayyana yayin da ake aika rubutu, kuma taɓa paddle ma yana tsayar da shi.
    • Madannai (a ƙarshen ma'ajiyoyi): rubuta saƙo ka danna AIKA. Layi a ƙarƙashin akwatin yana nuna ainihin abin da za a aika. A Android, Murya yana yin iri ɗaya daga magana: faɗi saƙon, abin da wayar ta fahimta yana buɗewa a akwatin Madannai, da manyan haruffa, domin ka gyara ka aika da AIKA.

    A iska, CW koyaushe sauti ne mai tsafta mai gefuna masu laushi, komai sautin gefe (sidetone) da ka zaɓa ka ji. Domin tsaro, maɓallin da aka riƙe na daƙiƙa 10 ana sakin sa, kuma ana tsayar da watsawa bayan minti 3.

    Gwaji da TX OFF

    Muddin sandar ƙasa tana cewa TX OFF, paddle, maɓallanka, ma'ajiyoyi da akwatin Allon rubutu duk suna aiki, amma a lasifikarka kawai. Abin da kake aikawa ana fassara shi kuma ana nuna shi da kore a layin mai fassara, domin ka ga ko aikawarka tana karantuwa. Babu abin da ke kaiwa rediyo. Ba a buƙatar rediyo ko kaɗan don wannan.

    Paddle da keyer

    A Saituna > CW, sashen Keyer yana saita yadda paddle suke aiki:

    • Yanayin keyer: Maɓalli madaidaici, Iambic A, Iambic B, sigogin yatsa ɗaya na duka biyun, Ultimatic, Bug (semi-automatic) da Cootie (sideswiper).
    • Sauri, Farnsworth, Tazarar kalmomi da Lokaci mai tsauri (tazarar yau da kullum tsakanin haruffa da kalmomi).
    • Juya paddle: yana musanya dit da dah.
    • Jinkirin break-in: tsawon lokacin da rediyo yake kan watsawa bayan dit ko dah ɗinka na ƙarshe.
    • Daidaita sauri: aika da saurin tashar da kake karɓa.

    Sashen Shigarwar paddle yana saita abin da ake ɗauka a matsayin paddle:

    • Paddle: babban maɓallin kunnawa. A kashe yana nufin babu paddle a kan allo, kuma babu maɓalli, maɓallin linzami ko adafta da zai iya kunna mai watsawa.
    • Paddle a kan allo, Maɓallan linzami, Maɓallan madannai da Adaftoci na waje (USB / Bluetooth): ana iya kashe kowane tushe da kansa.
    • Rarraba maɓallai: Na asali yana amfani da jerin maɓallan da aka shirya (a cikinsu akwai [ da ], maɓallan Ctrl na hagu da na dama, F1 da F2). Da Na musamman kana gina jerinka domin paddle na hagu da na dama: danna ƘARA MAƁALLI sannan maɓallin da kake so. Maɓallin da yake kan ɗayan paddle tuni ana ƙin sa.

    Idan akwatin rubutu yana buɗe, maɓallan Ctrl da F1 / F2 kawai ne suke aiki a matsayin paddle, don haka rubuta alamar kira ba ya taɓa aika Morse.

    Buga Morse da adafta, madannai ko linzami

    Ba dole ne ka taɓa allo domin aika Morse ba. Abubuwa huɗu suna iya aiki a matsayin paddle, kuma ana iya amfani da su duka a lokaci guda:

    • Paddle na kan allo, da yatsunka.
    • Ainihin maɓallin Morse ko paddle a kan adaftar USB: ƙaramin akwati ko kebul mai wurin saka maɓallin a gefe ɗaya da USB a ɗayan gefen. Ga waya ko kwamfuta, madannai ne (kowane contact na maɓallin yana danna maɓallin madannai) ko linzami (kowane contact yana danna maɓallin linzami). Adaftocin Bluetooth suna aiki iri ɗaya.
    • Madannai: maɓalli ɗaya ga paddle na hagu da ɗaya ga paddle na dama.
    • Linzami: maɓallansa na hagu da na dama.

    Dukkansu suna bin Yanayin keyer da saurin da ka zaɓa, kuma dukkansu suna aiki da TX OFF a matsayin gwaji (duba Gwaji da TX OFF).

    Haɗa adafta

    1. Saka maɓallinka ko paddle a adafta, sannan saka adaftar a waya, tablet ko kwamfuta. Ba a buƙatar shigar da komai, kuma qFT8 ba ta neman izini: adaftar madannai ne ko linzami na yau da kullum. A wayar da transceiver ya ɗauki wurin USB ɗinta ɗaya tilo, yi amfani da USB hub ko adaftar Bluetooth.
    2. Buɗe allon CW ka fara bugawa. Adaftar da take aiki kamar madannai tana aiki nan take idan tana aika maɓallan jerin na asali da ke ƙasa (maɓallan Ctrl na hagu da na dama, da [ da ], su ne aka saba). An gwada ta da ainihin adaftar VBand.
    3. Idan babu sauti, adaftar tana aika wasu maɓallai daban. Je zuwa Saituna > CW > Shigarwar paddle, danna ƘARA MAƁALLI a ƙarƙashin Paddle na hagu (dit) ka rufe contact na dit na maɓallinka; sannan yi haka a ƙarƙashin Paddle na dama (dah) da contact na dah.
    4. Adaftar da take aiki kamar linzami tana buƙatar paddle na kan allo: bar alamar linzami a kan paddle na hagu ka buga. Haka ma linzami na yau da kullum.

    Maɓallan da suke aiki ba tare da saita komai ba

    Madannai, ko adaftar da take aiki kamar madannai
    Paddle na hagu (dit) [  A  ,  Left arrow  1  0  Num 1  Num 0  L-Ctrl  L-Shift  L-Alt  Num .  F1
    Paddle na dama (dah) ]  D  S  .  Right arrow  3  9  Num 3  Num 9  R-Ctrl  R-Shift  R-Alt  Num Enter  F2
    Iri ɗaya, idan akwatin rubutu yana buɗe
    Paddle na hagu (dit) L-Ctrl  F1
    Paddle na dama (dah) R-Ctrl  F2
    Linzami, da alamarsa a kan paddle na hagu
    Paddle na hagu (dit) maɓallin hagu
    Paddle na dama (dah) maɓallin dama
    Linzami, da alamarsa a kan paddle na dama
    Paddle na hagu (dit) maɓallin dama
    Paddle na dama (dah) maɓallin hagu

    Ana yanke dokar linzami lokacin da aka danna maɓalli na farko: babban maɓalli (na hagu) shi ne paddle da ke ƙarƙashin alamar linzami, ɗayan maɓallin kuma shi ne ɗayan paddle, har sai an saki duka. Maɓallin da aka riƙe ba ya maimaitawa. Dannawa yayin da ake aika ma'ajiya ko rubutun da aka buga yana tsayar da wannan rubutu, kuma shi kansa ba ya aika komai; saki, sannan ka sake bugawa.

    Maɓalli madaidaici, paddle, bug

    Yanayin keyer (Saituna > CW > Keyer) yana yanke abin da gefuna biyu suke yi, komai abin da kake bugawa da shi:

    • Maɓalli madaidaici: maɓalli ɗaya, kuma kai ne kake yin kowane dit da dah da hannu. Gefen paddle na hagu ne kawai yake aiki: haɗa maɓalli madaidaici da contact na dit na adafta, ko saka maɓallinsa a jerin Paddle na hagu (dit). A kan allo, paddle biyun suna zama maɓalli ɗaya.
    • Iambic A da Iambic B: domin paddle mai hannaye biyu. Gefen hagu yana yin dit, gefen dama dah, kuma duka tare suna musanya su. Iambic A, yatsa ɗaya da Iambic B, yatsa ɗaya an yi su ne domin allo, inda wani yanki a tsakiya yake ƙidaya a matsayin paddle biyun (faɗinsa shi ne Yankin tsakiya (yanayin yatsa ɗaya)).
    • Ultimatic: kamar paddle, amma idan aka danna gefuna biyu, wanda aka danna a ƙarshe ne yake nasara.
    • Bug (rabin atomatik): gefen dit yana yin dit da kansa; gefen dah maɓallin hannu ne, kuma yana yin sauti muddin kana riƙe da shi.
    • Cootie (sideswiper): gefuna biyun maɓallin hannu ɗaya ne.

    Saita shi yadda kake so

    Komai yana cikin Saituna > CW, a sashen Shigarwar paddle:

    • Paddle: babban maɓallin kunnawa ga dukkan abin da yake biye.
    • Paddle a kan allo da Tsayin paddle: ɓoye paddle domin ba waterfall ƙarin sarari idan kana amfani da ainihin maɓalli kawai, ko ƙara musu tsayi domin yatsunka. Linzami yana buƙatar a nuna su.
    • Maɓallan linzami: kashe shi idan ba ka son danna paddle ya yi komai.
    • Maɓallan madannai da Adaftoci na waje (USB / Bluetooth): wanda zai iya bugawa. Duba bambancin Android da kwamfuta a ƙasa.
    • Rarraba maɓallai: Na asali shi ne jadawalin da ke sama. A ƙarƙashinsa akwai jeri biyu, Paddle na hagu (dit) da Paddle na dama (dah). ƘARA MAƁALLI yana jiran ka danna maɓalli (ko ka rufe contact na maɓallinka) sannan ya ƙara shi; kwandon shara kusa da maɓalli yana cire shi. Canji na farko yana mayar da saitin zuwa Na musamman, wanda yake farawa a matsayin kwafin jerin na asali. Komawa Na asali ba ya goge jerinka. Kana iya amfani da haruffa, lambobi, F1 zuwa F12, kibiyoyi, Ctrl, Shift, Alt, numeric pad, Space, Enter, Tab da maɓallan alamomin rubutu da aka saba. Maɓalli yana iya kasancewa a paddle ɗaya kawai.

    Akwai ƙarin zaɓuɓɓuka biyu a sashen Keyer: Juya paddle yana musanya dit da dah ga kowane tushe a lokaci guda (domin paddle da aka haɗa wayoyinsa a juye, ko mai amfani da hannun hagu), kuma an bayyana Yanayin keyer a sama. SAKE SAITA ZUWA NA ASALI, a ƙarshen shafin, yana mayar da kowane saitin CW yadda yake, har da jerin maɓallai.

    Android da kwamfuta

    • A Android akwai maɓallan kunnawa biyu: Maɓallan madannai shi ne madannai na na'urar kanta, Adaftoci na waje (USB / Bluetooth) kuma shi ne kowane madannai ko adafta da ka saka ko ka haɗa. Kafin Android 10, kowane madannai na zahiri ana ɗaukarsa a matsayin na waje. Linzami na USB ko Bluetooth yana aiki a kan paddle kamar a kwamfuta.
    • A kwamfuta, adafta ƙarin madannai ne kawai, don haka akwai maɓallin kunnawa ɗaya, Maɓallan madannai, ga duka. Ana danna paddle na kan allo da linzami. Maɓallai suna aiki ne kawai idan tagar qFT8 mai allon CW ita ce tagar da ake aiki a kanta kuma babu akwatin tambaya a buɗe.

    Idan wani abu bai yi aiki ba

    • An musanya dit da dah: sa alama a Juya paddle. Da linzami, duba cewa alamarsa tana kan paddle na hagu, ba na dama ba.
    • Babu abin da yake faruwa idan ka buga: dole allon CW yana nunawa, Paddle da maɓallin kunna tushenka suna kunne, kuma AUTO a kashe (allon yana cewa Kashe AUTO don aikawa da hannu). A kwamfuta, fara danna tagar qFT8. Sannan duba cewa maɓallinka yana cikin jerin: danna ƘARA MAƁALLI ka buga. Idan akwatin bai amsa ba, adaftar tana aika maɓallin da ba a iya amfani da shi, ko ba a haɗa ta ba.
    • Kana jin Morse amma rediyo ba ya watsawa: jan layi yana cewa Danna TX ON don aikawa. Wannan gwaji ne.
    • Maɓalli madaidaici ba ya yin komai a yanayin Maɓalli madaidaici: yana gefen paddle na dama. Mayar da shi na hagu (duba sama).
    • Bugawa tana rubuta haruffa a akwati maimakon aikawa: idan akwatin rubutu yana buɗe, maɓallan Ctrl da F1 / F2 kawai ne paddle, domin rubuta alamar kira kada ya taɓa aika Morse. Rufe akwatin, ko ba adaftarka ɗaya daga cikin waɗannan maɓallai.
    • Bugawa tana rubutu a wata manhaja daban: a kwamfuta, maɓallai suna zuwa tagar da ake aiki a kanta. Danna qFT8.
    • Sauti yana tsayawa yayin da kake riƙe da maɓalli: maɓallin da aka riƙe na daƙiƙa 10 ana sakin sa, tare da sanarwar An riƙe maɓalli tsawon lokaci: an sake shi.

    Ma'ajiyoyi, macros da prosigns

    Gyara saƙonninka a Saituna > CW > Ma'ajiyoyi (ƘARA MA'AJIYA, ko taɓa ɗaya domin canza ta). Ana iya amfani da kalmomi na musamman iri biyu a saƙo:

    • Macros, a cikin square brackets, ana maye gurbinsu idan aka aika saƙon: [MYCALL] alamar kiranka, [TARGET] alamar kiran da ke akwatin Manufa, [RST] rahoton da za a aika, [NAME] sunanka, [QTH] wurinka, [EXCH] musayar gasarka, da [MYPOTA] wurin shakatawa ko kololuwar kunnawa naka da yake a buɗe. Editan yana da maɓalli ga kowanne, kuma yana nuna saƙon daidai yadda za a aika shi.
    • Prosigns, a cikin angle brackets, ana aika su a matsayin harafi ɗaya ba tare da tazara ba: <BK>, <SK>, <KN>, <AR>.

      • Maimaitawa: the button with two arrows in a loop, at the right end of each memory, sends that memory again and again, for a CQ or a beacon-like call. A tap on it asks for the pause between two sends: 15, 30, 60, 90 or 120 seconds, counted from the end of the message (A kashe stops it). While it loops the button is green and counts down the pause. It works only while TX is ON; any touch of the paddles or the key (that touch is not sent), another memory, a typed message, STOP, TX OFF or a station answering you ends it. Every send counts against your radio's transmission limit, like any message.

    Rahotonka, sunanka, wurinka da musayarka ana rubuta su sau ɗaya, a wannan shafin Saituna. Saƙon da aka shirya wanda yake buƙatar ɗaya daga cikinsu yana kasancewa a dushe har sai ka cika shi, kuma taɓa shi yana kai ka wurin cikewa. Yayin da kunna POTA ko SOTA yake a buɗe, ana ba da saƙon CQ POTA a matsayin ma'ajiya ta farko.

    Karɓa

    Fassara ta atomatik ce kuma tana bin saurin kowace tasha. Tashar da take cikin matatarka ita ce ake rubutawa a layin mai fassara na sandar ƙasa; motsa sandar karɓa zuwa wata sigina domin bin ta (abin da aka riga aka karɓa a wannan mita ana nuna shi nan take). Idan tashoshi biyu suna cikin matata, sandar ƙasa tana faɗa. Ana kunna siginar da aka karɓa a lasifikarka a Muryar sa ido da ka zaɓa, daidai da sautin gefe naka, don haka tashar da sautinta ya yi daidai da sautin gefe naka tana kan mitarka daidai.

    Saituna > CW yana kuma da Sautuna (yadda sautin gefe naka yake: muryarsa, ƙararsa, gefuna masu laushi ko masu kaifi, sauti ko ƙarar sounder), Fassara da nuni (Fassara ta atomatik, Mai fassara da yawa domin rubutun kowace sigina a waterfall, spectrogram na kwance, matata lokacin shiga yanayin), Sa ido (lasifika) da Prosign da alamomi.

    Sautin gefe

    Sautin gefe shi ne abin da kake ji na aikawarka a lasifika ko belun kunne na waya ko kwamfuta. Ba ya taɓa zuwa rediyo. SIDETONE, maɓalli na ƙarshe a ginshiƙin dama, yana nuna ƙararsa; taɓawa tana buɗe mai zamewa a gefensa, Ƙarar sautin gefe, wata taɓawa kuma tana rufe shi. Saiti ɗaya ne da na Saituna > CW > Sautuna. Yayin da kake aikawa, maɓallan ƙara ma suna canza sautin gefe.

    A Sautuna kana kuma zaɓar Muryar sa ido (murya ɗaya da ta siginar da aka karɓa), Hawan / saukar sautin gefe, Siffar igiyar sautin gefe da Sautin gefe (Sauti ko Ƙararraki (sounder)). Waɗannan suna canza abin da kake ji kawai: a iska, CW kullum sauti ne mai tsabta mai laushin gefuna. Kiyaye lasifika a farke yana kiyaye wayoyi da belun kunne na Bluetooth a farke tsakanin dannawa domin kada a yanke dit na farko; ƙara shi idan farkon dit yana ɓacewa. Domin kashe sautin gefe ko siginar da aka karɓa, yi amfani da Kunna abin da kuke aikawa (sautin gefe) da Kunna siginar da aka karɓa a lasifika a Sa ido (lasifika).

    A iPhone babu sautin gefe kuma babu maɓallin SIDETONE.

    AUTO a CW

    Maɓallin AUTO a sama ta hagu na allon yana barin qFT8 ta yi cikakkun QSO na CW. Yana kashe duk lokacin da ka shiga yanayin. Taɓa shi domin kunna shi (yana komawa kore), ka sake taɓawa domin kashe shi. Yana aikawa ne kawai idan TX yana ON: AUTO ba ta taɓa shirya watsawa da kanta.

    Yayin da yake kunne, kuma TX yana ON, qFT8:

    • tana kiran CQ idan mitar shiru take, ko tana amsa CQ da ta ji;
    • tana musayar rahotanni, suna da wuri, tana yin sallama, kuma tana rubuta QSO;
    • tana sake tambayar alamar kira ko rahoton da ba ta iya karɓa ba, kuma tana amsa buƙatun maimaitawa ko rage sauri;
    • ba ta taɓa aikawa da sauri fiye da WPM naka, kuma tana rage sauri zuwa na ɗayan tashar;
    • tana bin tashar da mitarta take zamewa, ba ta kiran tashar da aka riga aka yi aiki da ita a wannan rana, kuma ba ta farawa a kan QSO na wani;
    • ba ta taɓa rubuta QSO da ba ta iya kammalawa ba.

    Yayin da AUTO take kunne, an kashe paddle kuma wani layi yana cewa Kashe AUTO don aikawa da hannu. TSAYA ma yana kashe AUTO. Idan ba ka taɓa son ta ba, kashe Nuna yanayin atomatik a Saituna > CW, sai a ɓoye maɓallin.

    AUTO RIT: bin tashar da ke kiran ka

    Tashar da ke amsa CQ naka sau da yawa tana ɗan nesa da mitarka. AUTO RIT, maɓallin bayan RX/TX, yana kula da hakan. A kashe yake har sai ka kunna shi, kuma qFT8 tana tuna zaɓinka. Idan yana kunne yana zama kore, kuma:

    • tashar da ke kiran ka (aikawarta tana da alamar kiranka, kuma ba CQ ba ce ko kiran wani dabam) a cikin matatarka tana jawo sandar karɓa zuwa gare ta da kanta. Sandar watsawa ba ta taɓa motsi: kana ci gaba da aikawa inda ka kira;
    • tashar da ke kiran ka a wajen matatarka ana ba da ita a matsayin layi a saman ma'ajiyoyi, Bi K1ABC +150 Hz?, tare da nisanta daga sandar karɓa. BI yana kai sandar karɓa gare ta, alamar giciye kuma tana cire layin. Ana nuna layuka har uku, sabo da farko; kowanne yana ɓacewa minti ɗaya bayan kiran ƙarshe na wannan tashar;
    • yayin da kake cikin QSO da manufarka, wata tashar da ke kiran ka tana samun layi kawai; ba ta taɓa ƙwace sandar karɓa daga abokin aikinka.

    AUTO RIT yana motsa sandar karɓa kawai. Ba ya taɓa aikawa, ba ya taɓa kunna TX, kuma babu abin da aka ji yayin da kake aikawa da ake ƙirgawa.

    Yadda ake yi wa rediyo makulli

    • Yawancin rediyoyi: qFT8 yana aika CW a matsayin sautin murya mai siffa zuwa yanayin bayanai na rediyo, saboda haka kana saita matakin aikawa da ƙarar TX, kamar a FT8. Rediyon yana canza zuwa yanayin bayanai yayin aikawa, karɓa kuma yana nan faɗi.
    • QRP Labs QMX da QMX+: qFT8 yana yi wa rediyo makulli ta hanyar CAT, guda-guda, rediyon kuma yana yin nasa carrier daidai a mitar aikawarka. Yana zama a yanayin dijital koyaushe, ba a aika masa da sauti, kuma ƙarar TX ba ta da muhimmanci. Sautin gefe naka yana ci gaba da kunnuwa a waya ko kwamfuta, da saurinka da lokacinka.

    A duka biyun, STOP ko TX OFF a tsakiyar dit ko dah yana gama shi da tsabta, rediyon kuma yana komawa karɓa. Yayin da qFT8 ke aikawa, maɓallin TX yana nuna cewa ana aikawa.

    Rubuta QSO na CW a log

    QSO, a saman dama na pane, yana buɗe log da aka riga aka cika: yanayin CW, band da mitar aikawarka (da mitar karɓa idan sanduna biyu sun rabu), alamar kira daga Manufa, da rahotanni. Rahotannin su ne waɗanda aka yi musaya da gaske idan AUTO ya yi aiki da wannan tashar; in ba haka ba, rahoton da kake aikawa shi ne Rahoton da za a aika [RST] naka (ko, idan babu komai, wanda aka auna daga siginar), wanda ka karɓa kuma shi ne wanda aka karanta daga aikawar ɗayan tashar. Ana kuma cika wurin shakatawa ko kololuwar kunnawa da ke buɗe. Duba shi, canza abin da kake buƙata ka adana (duba Log na hannu).

    AUTO yana rubuta kowane QSO da ya kammala da kansa, tare da rahotanni biyu da sunan da wurin ɗayan tashar a sharhi, kuma yana cewa An rubuta K1ABC. Idan Aika gajerun lambobi (5NN) na kunne, ana aika rahotanni a matsayin 5NN, amma log kullum yana adana 599.

    Rubuta QSO da kake buga mabuɗi da kanka a log

    qFT8 yana sauraron bugun mabuɗinka ta rediyo (idan rediyon yana mayar da shi) kuma yana rubuta QSO naka na CW a log. Rediyoyi da yawa suna aika sautin watsawarsu (monitor na TX, ko sautin gefe) zuwa kwamfuta ko waya ta USB. Idan ka buga wa rediyo mabuɗi da mabuɗinka ko paddle ɗinka, qFT8 yana karanta abin da kake aikawa da amsoshin ɗayan tashar da mai fassara ɗaya, yana bin musayar (alamar kira ta ɗayan tashar, rahoto a kowane gefe, suna da QTH idan an aika su) kuma yana rubuta QSO a log da zarar ya ƙare, kamar yadda AUTO ke yi, yana cewa An rubuta K1ABC.

    Ana rubuta QSO ne kawai idan an ji alamar kiranka bayan DE a cikinsa (haka qFT8 ke sanin cewa rediyon yana mayar da bugun mabuɗinka), an karɓi alamar kira ta ɗayan tashar a fili, kuma an yi musayar rahotanni biyu. Duk abin da bai kai haka ba ba a rubuta shi. Kashe shi da Rubuta QSO da na buga mabuɗi da kaina ta rediyo a Saituna > CW. Yana buƙatar Fassara ta atomatik a kunne, kuma yana hutawa yayin da AUTO ke kunne.

    Layin mabuɗin CW (a kwamfuta): rediyon da ake buga wa mabuɗi ta soket ɗin mabuɗinsa, misali da optoisolator a tashar serial ta biyu, ana iya buga masa mabuɗi da layin DTR ko RTS na wannan tashar. Zaɓi layin da tashar a Saituna > CW; ana ajiye zaɓin ga kowane rediyo, kuma a kashe yake sai ka zaɓa. qFT8 yana buga layin ne kawai yayin da kake da TX ON, da iyakokin watsawa iri ɗaya, kuma ana sakin mabuɗin a STOP, TX OFF, a iyakar, lokacin da tashar ta ɓace da lokacin da qFT8 ya rufe.

    A kwamfuta, a iPhone da a sarrafa daga nesa ta yanar gizo

    • A kwamfuta pane ɗaya ne. Maɓallan linzamin kwamfuta da allon rubutu su ne paddle (duba Buga Morse da adafta, madannai ko linzami), ƙafar linzamin kwamfuta a kan spectrogram tana motsa sandar da aka zaɓa, kuma babu Murya.
    • A iPhone pane ɗaya ne, ba tare da sautin gefe ba (duba sama). Murya yana buɗe akwatin aikawa da allon rubutu, domin ka iya yin rubutu da murya.
    • A cikin sarrafawa daga nesa ta yanar gizo (duba Sabar Yanar Gizo Ta Kula Daga Nesa) shafin yana nuna rubutun da qFT8 ke fassarawa, layi ɗaya ga kowace sigina, sabo a farko, da akwati don rubuta saƙo da aika shi; STOP yana dakatar da shi. Maɓallan paddle na madannin kwamfutarka suna sarrafa keyer na qFT8 na kansa, tare da saitunansa. AUTO RIT da layukansa na FOLLOW ma suna nan, da maɗaukin SIDETONE a gefensu: yana saita ƙarar sidetone na wannan burauzar na kansa (yana farawa a ƙarar sidetone na aikace-aikacen kuma ba ya taɓa canza ta). Ƙwaƙwalwa ma suna kan shafin, kamar a aikace-aikacen: taɓawa tana aika ɗaya (sai da TX ON kawai), kuma a CW kowanne yana da maɓallinsa na LOOP tare da hutu iri ɗaya (duba madauki na ƙwaƙwalwa a sama; madauki yana farawa ne kawai da TX ON kuma TX OFF yana ƙare shi). Taɓawa ko ja a kan waterfall yana motsa sandar karɓa, kamar a aikace-aikacen. Taɓa zirin kololuwa yana neman mitar rediyo; guntun STEP da guntun mita suna a damar sandar da ke saman waterfall (duba a ƙasa).
    ↑ Koma zuwa Abubuwan da ke ciki

    RTTY da JTTY

    RTTY (radioteletype) yana aika rubutu a matsayin sautuka biyu masu musanya. A qFT8 yana amfani da allon CW, ba tare da paddle ba: ana fassara kowace siginar RTTY a passband, kowacce da nata sauri da shift, kuma ana rubuta ta a kan layinta a waterfall. Wannan allon kuma yana gudanar da JTTY, wani sabon yanayin rubutu don sigina masu rauni (duba RTTY da JTTY: bambance-bambance).

    Ƙayyadaddun bayanai: ITU-T Shawara S.1, “International Telegraph Alphabet No. 2”; Baud 45.45 da canjin 170 Hz al’ada ce ta masu son rediyo.

    Ga sassan allon RTTY dalla-dalla:

    1. Manufa da Musaya: Alamar kiran tashar da kake aiki da ita da musayar gasa. Suna cika [TARGET] da [EXCH] a saƙonninka. Taɓa ɗaya don rubuta shi; taɓa wata alamar kira a layin mai fassara (abu na 7) don ɗauke ta a matsayin manufa.
    2. BAUD: Sauri: 45.45 (wanda aka saba), 50 ko 75. Kowane taɓawa yana zuwa na gaba.
    3. SHIFT: Tazara tsakanin sautuka biyun: 170 Hz (wadda aka saba), 200, 425 ko 850 Hz. Matatar tana bin shift ɗin.
    4. REV: Yana musanya sautuka biyun, domin tashar da take aika su a juye. Yana da launin amber muddin yana kunne.
    5. FILTER da NR: Matatar karɓa da rage hayaniya.
    6. Ma'ajiyoyi: Saƙonni da aka shirya da macro iri ɗaya kamar a CW. Taɓawa ɗaya tana aika ɗaya. Saƙon da ke da fili mara komai yana dushewa: taɓa shi don ganin abin da ya rage. Madannai (da Murya a waya da iPhone) suna ƙarshen jerin.
    7. Layin mai fassara: Rubutun tashar da ke ƙarƙashin sandar karɓa, da kore; abin da kake aikawa, da ja. Taɓa shi (ko'ina sai dai kan alamar kira) don ganin dukan tattaunawar wannan mitar a wurin spectrogram; taɓa ƙaramin spectrogram da ya maye gurbin layin don komawa.
    8. Sandar karɓa da rubutunta: Rubutun yana nuna alamar kira, sauri da shift na tashar da ke ƙarƙashin sandar. Taɓawa kusa da sigina tana ajiye sandar a tsakiyarta.
    9. Tasha mai wasu saituna: Idan tashar da ke ƙarƙashin sandar tana amfani da sauri, shift ko tsarin sautuka daban da naka, alamarta tana zama launin amber, kuma maɓallan BAUD, SHIFT ko REV da suka bambanta suna zama amber suna nuna daidaita tare da ƙimar wannan tashar. Taɓa alamar ko ɗaya daga cikin waɗannan maɓallan, qFT8 zai ɗauki dukan ƙimomin wannan tashar, don ka iya amsa mata.
    10. Sauran sigina: Ana rubuta rubutunsu tare da hanyoyinsu. Tashar da ke ƙarƙashin sandar karɓa kaɗai ce ke nuna yadda ta bambanta da saitunanka.
    11. Mita: A RTTY, saman allon yana nuna mitar mark, wato mitar da masu aiki suke faɗa kuma wadda ake rubutawa a log.

    Haka kuma a wannan allon:

    • RX / TX / RX/TX: Yana zaɓar wace sanda taɓawa a kan waterfall ko ƙafafun daidaitawa ke motsawa: sandar karɓa, sandar aikawa, ko duka biyun tare. Da RX ko TX za ka iya saurare a wata mita ka aika a wata.
    • AUTO RIT: Yana kai sandar karɓa kan tashar da ke amsa maka kaɗan a gefen mitarka (duba AUTO RIT da katunan bi).
    • The tuning scope (optional): The small round box left of the mode button, in RTTY, PSK, the MFSK family and Olivia, Packet and JTTY (not in CW). Kore: the receiver is on the signal (two crossed ellipses for the two tones of RTTY and packet; two dots for PSK, four for QPSK; a ladder of the mode's tones with the one being received lit for the multi-tone modes). Rawaya, pulsing: a signal is there but off tune, further than qFT8 follows by itself, or with another shift. Ja: no signal, only noise. Tap it to put the receive bar on the signal and, when the shift differs, take the signal's shift; the transmit bar does not move and nothing is sent. It always shows the signal being decoded under the receive bar, never a stronger one beside it; another signal inside the filter makes it yellow. It is off until you switch on Nuna na'urar nuna daidaitawa in Settings > Application, under User interface.
    • Gear yana buɗe Saituna > RTTY (ko Saituna > JTTY yayin da allon ke gudanar da JTTY), kuma QSO yana buɗe log na hannu (duba Rubutawa a log).
    • Jan layi: Press TX ON to transmit. Muddin yana bayyana, abin da ka aika samfoti ne kawai.

    A kwamfuta ana amfani da wannan allon da linzamin kwamfuta da madannai. Na'urar sarrafa nesa ta yanar gizo tana nuna rubutun da aka karɓa tare da akwati don rubuta da aika saƙo, maɓalli don dakatar da shi, maɓallin AUTO RIT da katunan bi; maɓallin TX nata shi ne na manhajar kanta.

    Aikawa

    Shirya TX, sannan taɓa ma'ajiya, ko buɗe Madannai, rubuta ka danna AIKA. A Android, Murya yana yin iri ɗaya daga magana: faɗi saƙon, abin da wayar ta fahimta yana buɗewa a akwatin Madannai domin ka gyara ka aika. Layi mai TSAYA yana nunawa yayin da rubutun yake fita. Da TX OFF, waɗannan ayyuka gwaji ne: ana kunna saƙon a lasifikarka kuma ana nuna shi da ja, kuma babu abin da ke fita iska. Haruffan da lambar RTTY ba ta da su ana cire su kuma ana jera su. Ma'ajiyar da ake aikawa tana kasancewa kore har sai ta ƙare.

    RTTY watsawa ce mai cikakken ƙarfi ba tsayawa, don haka iyakar watsawa ba tsayawa ta rediyo tana aiki: saƙon da ya fi iyakar tsawo ba a fara shi, kuma watsawa tana tsayawa idan aka kai iyakar.

    AUTO RIT da katunan bi

    Tashar da ke amsa CQ ɗinka sau da yawa tana kaɗan a gefen mitarka. AUTO RIT, kusa da RX/TX, yana kula da wannan. Yana kashe har sai ka kunna shi; yana zama kore muddin yana kunne, kuma qFT8 yana tuna shi. RTTY da JTTY kowanne yana da maɓallin kunnawa nasa. Muddin yana kunne:

    • Idan wata tasha ta gama aikawa da ke ɗauke da alamar kiranka, kuma tana cikin matatarka, sandar karɓa tana komawa kanta da kanta. CQ, saƙo tsakanin wasu tashoshi biyu da saƙonka da aka sake karantawa ba a ƙirga su. Sandar aikawa ba ta taɓa motsawa: RX/TX tana zama RX, don haka kana ci gaba da amsawa a mitarka.
    • Tashar da ke kiranka daga nesa tana samun kati a saman ma'ajiyoyi, misali Bi Q0ABC +150 Hz?. BI yana kai sandar karɓa kanta; X yana rufe katin har sai wannan tashar ta sake kira. Akwai kati uku a mafi yawa, sabon farko. Kati yana ɓacewa minti ɗaya bayan kiran ƙarshe na wannan tashar, kuma yana ɓoye muddin kana kanta.
    • Muddin tashar da kake aiki da ita tana kiranka, kowace tasha mai kira tana samun kati kawai.
    • Ba a karanta komai yayin da kake aikawa. AUTO RIT ba ya taɓa kunna TX kuma ba ya taɓa aika komai.

    A JTTY saƙo yana zuwa a cikin firam-firam, don haka ana ɗaukar aikawar tashar a matsayin ta ƙare idan firam ɗinta na ƙarshe ya iso.

    Rubutawa a log

    QSO yana buɗe log na hannu (duba Log na hannu) da yanayi, band, mitar mark, manufa da rahotanni an riga an cike su: rahoton da kake aikawa daga saitunanka ne, kuma rahoton da aka karɓa ana karanta shi daga rubutun tashar idan ta aiko da shi. QSO ɗaya a kowane danna. Ana rubuta QSO na RTTY a matsayin RTTY; na JTTY a matsayin JTTY (a fayilolin ADIF da lodawa, yanayin MFSK da ƙaramin yanayi JTTY). Muddin kunna wurin shakatawa ko kololuwa a buɗe yake, lambar ishararsa ma tana shiga QSO.

    Saituna > RTTY

    • Modem: sauri, shift, Juyawa, Jadawalin lambobi (Na duniya (ITA2) ko Amurka: sun bambanta a alamomi biyar, kuma dole tashoshin biyu su yi amfani da iri ɗaya) da Koma haruffa bayan sarari yayin karɓa da yayin aikawa (bayan sarari, rubutu yana komawa haruffa, don haka shift da ya ɓace yana ɓata kalma ɗaya kawai). Tashoshin da suke watsa rukunonin lambobi, kamar tashoshin yanayi (weather), ba sa maimaita shift bayan kowane sarari: rukunonin lambobinsu suna fitowa a matsayin haruffa muddin Koma haruffa bayan sarari yayin karɓa yana kunne, don haka kashe shi domin karanta su.
    • Aikawa: sabon layi a farko da ƙarshen kowace watsawa, haruffan cikewa idan babu abin aikawa, da sabon layi ta atomatik bayan wasu haruffa.
    • Fassara da nuni, Sa ido (lasifika), Ma'ajiyoyi tare da bayanansu (rahoto, suna, wuri, musaya) da SAKE SAITA ZUWA NA ASALI.

    Babu AUTO a RTTY ko JTTY: qFT8 ba ya gudanar da waɗannan QSO da kansa.

    RTTY da JTTY: bambance-bambance

    Zaɓi JTTY (gwaji) a mai zaɓar yanayi: wannan allon yana buɗewa da modem na JTTY, kuma maɓallin yanayi yana nuna JTTY; layin RTTY yana dawo da RTTY. Kowanne yana da mitarsa a kowane band, don haka sauyawa tsakaninsu yana motsa dial (a 20 m, misali, JTTY yana kan 14.090 MHz). JTTY sabo ne kuma yana iya canzawa. Yana aiki a waya, kwamfuta da iPhone; na'urar sarrafa nesa ta yanar gizo tana nuna shi kamar yadda take nuna RTTY: rubutun da aka karɓa, AUTO RIT da katunansa, da rubutun da aka aika.

    • Siginar: RTTY sautuka biyu ne, da sauri da shift da ka zaɓa. JTTY sautuka huɗu ne da sauri ɗaya tsayayye, faɗinsa kusan 130 Hz, a tsakiyar sandar aikawa, kuma ana iya karɓarsa yana da rauni sosai. Allon ba shi da maɓallan BAUD, SHIFT da REV a cikin JTTY.
    • Rubutun: RTTY yana rubuta kowane harafi yayin da ya iso. JTTY yana aika saƙo a firam-firam na kusan daƙiƙa 2 kuma yana rubuta shi firam bayan firam, saƙo ɗaya a kowane layi.
    • Saƙonninka: a JTTY kowane layi na ma'ajiya, ko na saƙon da aka rubuta da madannai, saƙo ɗaya ne mai haruffa 80 a mafi yawa, a cikin tsarin haruffan JTTY na kansa. Editan ma'ajiya na Saituna > JTTY yana nuna yadda ma'ajiya za ta fita.
    • Saituna: JTTY yana da shafin saituna da ƙimomi nasa (duba ƙasa), da maɓallin AUTO RIT nasa.
    • Rubutawa a log: RTTY ko JTTY, kamar yadda aka bayyana a Rubutawa a log.

    Saituna > JTTY

    JTTY (layin JTTY (gwaji) na zaɓin yanayi) yana amfani da wannan allon tare da modem na JTTY. Saitunansa suna da shafinsu, kusa da RTTY a jerin saituna; maɓallin giya na allon yana buɗe shi yayin da allon ke gudanar da JTTY. Yana da abin da JTTY ke amfani da shi kawai: Fassara da nuni, Sa ido (lasifika), Ma'ajiyoyi tare da ƙimominsu da SAKE SAITA ZUWA NA ASALI; saitunan Modem da Aikawa na RTTY ba sa shafar sa. A karon farko da aka yi amfani da JTTY yana ɗaukar abin da ka canza a Saituna > RTTY (misali ma'ajiyarka, sunanka da wurinka); abin da ba ka canza ba yana farawa da tsoffin ƙimomin JTTY, kamar gajerun ma'ajiya da kowanne ya dace da saƙon JTTY ɗaya. Bayan haka, canza ɗaya ba ya canza ɗayan.

    Ƙayyadaddun bayanai: babu ƙayyadaddun bayanai da aka buga.

    Saituna > Q65

    Q65 (layin Q65 (na gwaji) na mai zaɓar yanayi) yanayi ɗaya ne mai mai zaɓar ƙaramin yanayi kusa da shi: lokacin zagaye (daƙiƙa 15, 30, 60, 120 ko 300) da tazarar sautuka (A zuwa E), masu suna kamar 30A. Akwai ƙananan yanayi 22 (15 s: A-C, 30 s: A-D, 60, 120 da 300 s: A-E); na asali shi ne 30A. Q65 yana amfani da saƙonnin FT8 da tsarin QSO iri ɗaya (CQ, rahotanni, RR73/73, AUTO, jerin tashoshi). Ana adana QSO a matsayin MODE=MFSK SUBMODE=Q65 kuma spots suna zuwa PSKReporter a matsayin Q65.

    Ƙayyadaddun bayanai: J. Taylor, B. Somerville, S. Franke, N. Palermo, “Q65 Quick Start”, 2021.

    Amfani na yau da kullum: watsawar ionosphere a 6 m (30A, 50.275 MHz), watsawa a 2 m (60C, 144.170 MHz) da EME a 2 m zuwa sama (60A, 60B, 60C). Makada da ake bayarwa su ne 10 m (28.140), 6 m (50.275), 2 m (144.170), 70 cm, 23 cm da 13 cm (waɗannan uku suna amfani da mitar FT8 har sai an tabbatar da ta Q65). Watsawa tana ɗaukar daƙiƙa 12.75, 25.5, 51, 113.3 ko 293.8 kuma tana bayyana a saitin Iyakar watsawa ba tsayawa; ƙaramin yanayi na 300 s yana buƙatar iyaka mai isasshen girma. Ana aika Q65 a matsayin sauti kawai: qFT8 ba ya kunna shi ta yanayin sautin CAT na rediyo.

    Sashen Q65 na Saituna > YANAYIN DIJITAL yana ɗauke da:

    • Ƙaramin yanayi: lokacin zagaye da tazarar sautuka, kamar a mai zaɓar.
    • Matsakaici (a kunne): yana haɗa raunanan watsawa masu maimaitawa don a iya fassara su.
    • A priori (a kunne): yana amfani da sunan kiranka da na abokin QSO don taimaka wa mai fassara.
    • Fassara ɗaya (a kashe): a kashe yana fassara dukan maƙalli; a kunne kawai kewayen mitar karɓa.
    • Juriyar karɓa: faɗi a Hz kewayen mitar karɓa (na asali 100).
    • SHARE MATSAKAICI: yana mantawa da watsawar da aka tara har yanzu.
    • Madogara: bayanin yarjejeniyar daga marubutansu: “Quick-Start Guide to Q65” na Taylor, Somerville, Franke da Palermo, da aikin Palermo kan lambobin q-ary repeat-accumulate.

    Ba a fassara abin da ba a karɓa ba: fassarar da aka samu da bayanin a priori dole ta wuce binciken da kowace sauran ta wuce.

    Saituna > MSK144

    MSK144 (layin MSK144 (na gwaji) na mai zaɓin yanayi) an yi shi ne don watsawar meteor: hanyar meteor tana mayar da siginar na ɗan lokaci, don haka ana jin ta ne kawai a matsayin gajerun pings. Aikawa tana maimaita saƙonta a duk tsawon lokacin, kuma ana fassara kowane ping da ya isa ya ɗauke ta. MSK144 yana amfani da saƙonnin FT8 da tsarin QSO iri ɗaya (CQ, kira da grid, rahotanni, RRR/RR73/73, AUTO, jerin tashoshi). Ana rubuta QSO a matsayin MODE=MSK144 kuma spots suna zuwa PSKReporter a matsayin MSK144.

    Ƙayyadaddun bayanai: S. Franke, J. Taylor, “The MSK144 Protocol for Meteor-Scatter Communication”, QEX, July/August 2017.

    Kowace aikawa tana tsakiyar 1500 Hz na sauti, tare da sautukanta a 1000 da 2000 Hz: matsar TX ba ta shafi MSK144 ba. Ana saita mitar kira ta MSK144 ta kowace band lokacin da ka zaɓi band ɗin. MSK144 yana cikin saitin Iyakar watsawa ba tsayawa kamar kowane yanayi.

    Sashen MSK144 na Saituna > YANAYIN DIJITAL yana da:

    • Lokaci: tsawon kowane aikawa da kowane lokacin saurare (5, 10, 15 ko 30 s); 15 s shi ne zaɓin da aka saba.
    • Gajerun saƙonni don rahotanni, RRR da 73: ana aika rahotanni, RRR da 73 a matsayin gajerun saƙonni, waɗanda ke ɗauke da hash na kiran biyu: abokin hulɗarka ne kaɗai zai iya karanta su. Sun fi amfani a 144 MHz zuwa sama.
    • Juriyar karɓa: nisan da ake neman sigina a kusa da 1500 Hz (tare da matsar RX): 25, 50, 100, 200 ko 300 Hz.

    Madogarai: makalar MSK144 ta Franke da Taylor a cikin QEX (2016).

    Saituna > FST4

    FST4 (layin FST4 (na gwaji) na mai zaɓar yanayi) yanayin QSO ne don siginar da ba ta da ƙarfi a LF, MF da HF. Yana amfani da saƙonnin FT8 da irin tsarin QSO guda (CQ, rahotanni, RR73/73, AUTO, jerin tashoshi, log), da sautuka 4 (GFSK) da alamomi 160. Ana rubuta QSO a matsayin MODE=MFSK SUBMODE=FST4, kuma ana aika spots zuwa PSKReporter a matsayin FST4.

    Ƙayyadaddun bayanai: S. Franke, J. Taylor, B. Somerville, “Protocol Specifications” (FST4, FST4W), 2020.

    Tsawon lokacin aikawa da karɓa shi ne daƙiƙa 15, 30, 60, 120, 300, 900 ko 1800, ana zaɓa a ƙaramin mashaya ko a Saituna. Idan lokaci ya fi tsawo, yanayin ya fi ƙunci da ƙwarewa: kusan -21 dB a daƙiƙa 15 zuwa -43 dB a daƙiƙa 1800 (a cikin 2500 Hz). Mitoci na yau da kullum: 2200 m 136.000 kHz, 630 m 474.200 kHz, 160 m 1.8390 MHz. A lokuta masu tsawo sosai mai aikawa yana tsayawa a kunne har zuwa minti 30: saitin Iyakar watsawa ba tsayawa dole ya ba da damar hakan. FST4 na na gwaji ne.

    Sashen FST4 na Saituna > YANAYIN DIJITAL yana ɗauke da:

    • Lokaci (60 s): lokacin aikawa da karɓa, kamar a ƙaramin mashaya.
    • A priori (a kunne): yana amfani da sunan kiranka da na abokin QSO don taimaka wa mai fassara.
    • Fassara ɗaya (a kashe): a kashe yana fassara dukan maƙalli; a kunne kawai kewayen mitar karɓa.
    • Juriyar karɓa: faɗi a Hz kewayen mitar karɓa (na asali 100).
    • Madogara: S. Franke K9AN, B. Somerville G4WJS, J. Taylor K1JT: “The FST4 and FST4W Protocols for Weak Signal Communication on LF, MF and HF”, QEX (2021).

    Saituna > FST4W

    FST4W (layin FST4W (na gwaji) na mai zaɓar yanayi) yanayin beacon ne kamar WSPR: yana amfani da allon WSPR iri ɗaya (jerin spots, kaso na aikawa na beacon, aika zuwa WSPRnet). Saƙonni suna da bit 50: CALL GRID POWER, ko PFX/CALL POWER ko CALL/X POWER. Lokaci shi ne daƙiƙa 120, 300, 900 ko 1800; ƙwarewa kusan -33 dB (daƙiƙa 120) zuwa -45 dB (daƙiƙa 1800). Yana yanke lamba a cikin haƙurin karɓa (tsoho 100 Hz) kewaye da mitar karɓa (yawanci 1500 Hz). Ana aika spots zuwa WSPRnet tare da lokacin.

    Ƙayyadaddun bayanai: S. Franke, J. Taylor, B. Somerville, “Protocol Specifications” (FST4, FST4W), 2020.

    Mitoci na yau da kullum: 2200 m 136.000 kHz, 630 m 474.200 kHz, 160 m 1.8368 MHz. A manyan band-band yana amfani da mitocin da ke kusa da mitocin dial na WSPR. A lokuta masu tsawo sosai mai aikawa yana tsayawa a kunne har zuwa minti 30: saitin Iyakar watsawa ba tsayawa dole ya ba da damar hakan. FST4W na na gwaji ne.

    Sashen FST4W na Saituna > YANAYIN DIJITAL yana ɗauke da:

    • Lokaci (120 s): lokacin aikawa da karɓa, kamar a ƙaramin mashaya.
    • Juriyar karɓa: faɗi a Hz kewayen mitar karɓa (na asali 100).
    • Kashi na aikawa (20 %): ɓangaren lokutan da beacon ke aikawa.
    • Madogara: S. Franke K9AN, B. Somerville G4WJS, J. Taylor K1JT: “The FST4 and FST4W Protocols for Weak Signal Communication on LF, MF and HF”, QEX (2021).

    Saituna > JT65

    JT65 (layin JT65 (na gwaji) na mai zaɓar yanayi) yanayin QSO ne na minti ɗaya mai sautuka 65 da lambar Reed-Solomon; ƙwarewa kusan -25 dB (a 2500 Hz). Ana rubuta QSO a log a matsayin MODE=JT65 tare da ƙaramin yanayi (JT65A, JT65B, JT65C) kuma spots suna zuwa PSKReporter a matsayin JT65. Mitoci na yau da kullum (dial na USB): 160 m 1.838, 80 m 3.570, 40 m 7.076, 30 m 10.138, 20 m 14.076, 17 m 18.102, 15 m 21.076, 12 m 24.917, 10 m 28.076, 6 m 50.276, 2 m 144.120 MHz.

    • Ƙaramin yanayi (A): A, B ko C, kamar a ƙaramin mashaya.
    • Fassara kusa da mitar karɓa kawai (an kashe): kashewa yana fassara dukan band ɗin; kunnawa yana fassara kewayen mitar karɓa kawai.
    • Juriyar karɓa: faɗi a Hz kewayen mitar karɓa (na asali 100).
    • Madogara: J. Taylor K1JT: “The JT65 Communications Protocol”, QEX (2005); S. Franke K9AN, J. Taylor K1JT: “Open Source Soft-Decision Decoder for the JT65 (63,12) Reed-Solomon Code”, QEX (2016).

    Saituna > JT9

    JT9 (layin JT9 (na gwaji) na mai zaɓar yanayi) yanayin QSO ne na minti ɗaya mai sautuka 9, faɗin 16 Hz; ƙwarewa kusan -27 dB (a 2500 Hz). Ana rubuta QSO a log a matsayin MODE=JT9 kuma spots suna zuwa PSKReporter a matsayin JT9. Mitoci na yau da kullum (dial na USB): 2 kHz sama da na JT65 daga 160 m zuwa 10 m (20 m 14.078 MHz).

    • Fassara kusa da mitar karɓa kawai (an kashe): kashewa yana fassara dukan band ɗin; kunnawa yana fassara kewayen mitar karɓa kawai.
    • Juriyar karɓa: faɗi a Hz kewayen mitar karɓa (na asali 100).
    • Madogara: J. Taylor K1JT: “JT9 protocol description” (2012).

    Saituna > JT4

    JT4 (layin JT4 (na gwaji) na mai zaɓar yanayi) yanayin QSO ne na minti ɗaya mai sautuka 4 da lambar convolutional; ƙwarewa kusan -23 dB (a 2500 Hz) a JT4A. Ana rubuta QSO a log a matsayin MODE=JT4 tare da ƙaramin yanayi (JT4A zuwa JT4G) kuma spots suna zuwa PSKReporter a matsayin JT4. Mitoci (dial na USB): 6 m 50.276, 2 m 144.120, 70 cm 432.065, 23 cm 1296.065, 13 cm 2304.065 MHz; kowace mita da ba ta cikinsu da band ɗin Wani.

    • Ƙaramin yanayi (A): A zuwa G, kamar a ƙaramin mashaya.
    • Fassara kusa da mitar karɓa kawai (an kashe): kashewa yana fassara dukan band ɗin; kunnawa yana fassara kewayen mitar karɓa kawai.
    • Juriyar karɓa: faɗi a Hz kewayen mitar karɓa (na asali 100).
    • Madogara: J. Taylor K1JT: “JT4 protocol description” (2007); J. Layland, W. Lushbaugh: “A Flexible High-Speed Sequential Decoder for Deep Space Channels”, IEEE Trans. Comm. Tech. (1971).
    ↑ Koma zuwa Abubuwan da ke ciki

    PSK31 da PSK63 (gwaji)

    Zaɓi PSK a mai zaɓar yanayi da PSK31 ko PSK63 a gefen yanayi a sandar ƙasa: allo ɗaya yana buɗewa tare da modem na PSK kuma maɓallin yanayi yana karanta sunan yanayin. PSK31 shine yanayin gargajiya na madannai-zuwa-madannai: mai ɗauka ɗaya wanda fasarsa ke juyawa don ɗaukar rubutu, faɗinsa kusan 50 Hz, a baud 31.25; PSK63 iri ɗaya ne a ninki biyu na sauri da ninki biyu na faɗi. Kowace siginar PSK a cikin passband ana fassarata kuma a rubuta ta tare da hanyarta, kuma wadda ke ƙarƙashin sandar karɓa ana bin ta koyaushe. Kowane yanayi yana da nasa mita a kowane bandi (a 20 m, 14.070 MHz).

    • Allon ba shi da maɓallan BAUD, SHIFT da REV a PSK.
    • Rubutun yana riƙe manyan baƙaƙe da ƙanana. Ma'ajiya ɗaya tana fita a matsayin aika ɗaya; rubutun da aka rubuta da madannai yana fita layi ɗaya a kowane lokaci, idan ka danna Enter.
    • Rubutawa a log: log na hannu yana buɗewa da PSK31 ko PSK63 (ana rubuta shi a fayilolin ADIF a matsayin yanayin PSK da wannan ƙaramin yanayi), a mitar mai ɗaukar siginarka da ake aikawa.
    • Nau'o'i masu sauri da ƙarfi, a cikin mai zaɓe ɗaya: PSK125 da PSK250 (sau 4 da 8 cikin sauri, kuma haka cikin faɗi), QPSK31 da QPSK63 (fasa huɗu tare da gyara kuskure: rubutun yana tsira daga wani hayaniya da dushewa da ke ɓata PSK31; dole tashoshin biyu su yi amfani da iri ɗaya). Ana rubuta su a log da sunayensu.
    • Yana amfani da saitunan Saituna > RTTY (fassara, mai sa ido, ma'ajiyoyi). PSK sabo ne kuma yana iya canzawa har yanzu.
    ↑ Koma zuwa Abubuwan da ke ciki

    Olivia, MFSK16 da DominoEX (gwaji)

    The keyboard modes that send one tone at a time are in the HAM tab of the mode selector, one button per family (OLIVIA, Iyalin MFSK with MFSK, THOR, DominoEX and Throb, and so on), each marked experimental. The speed or variant is picked beside the mode in the bottom bar, and each family remembers the last one you used. The variants: OLIVIA 8/250, OLIVIA 8/500, OLIVIA 16/500, OLIVIA 32/1000, OLIVIA 4/250, MFSK8, MFSK11, MFSK16, MFSK22, MFSK32, MFSK64, MFSK128, DOMINOEX 8, DOMINOEX 11 and DOMINOEX 16. Picking one opens the RTTY screen with that modem, and the mode button reads its name.

    • Olivia (sautuna / faɗi a Hz): a hankali kuma mai ƙarfi sosai, don siginoni masu rauni da masu dushewa; yadda sautuna suke ƙaruwa, haka yake shiga cikin hayaniya sosai. MFSK16: sautuna 16 cikin kusan 316 Hz, yana gyara kurakurai, yanayi mai kyau na kullum; MFSK32 da MFSK64 iri ɗaya ne a sau biyu da sau huɗu na gudu (sun fi faɗi, suna buƙatar sigina mai ƙarfi; MFSK32 ya zama ruwan dare a cikin hanyoyin sadarwar zirga-zirgar gaggawa); MFSK128 ya sake zama sau biyu da sauri kuma faɗinsa 2 kHz. MFSK8 (sautuna 32, kusan 250 Hz) da MFSK11 sun fi MFSK16 jinkiri kuma suna karanta siginoni masu rauni; MFSK22 yana tsakanin MFSK16 da MFSK32. DominoEX: sautuna 18, ba ya damuwa da daidaita mita da kaucewa, kuma yana da sauri.
    • Ana fassara siginar da ke ƙarƙashin sandar karɓa (sandar ita ce tsakiyar siginar). Sa sandar a kan siginar; bambancin hertz kaɗan ba damuwa.
    • Allon ba shi da maɓallan BAUD, SHIFT da REV a waɗannan yanayi. Ma'ajiya ɗaya tana fita a matsayin aika ɗaya; rubutun da aka rubuta da madannai yana fita layi ɗaya a kowane lokaci, idan ka danna Enter.
    • Kowane iyali yana da nasa mita a band ɗin da ake yawan amfani da shi (a 20 m: Olivia 14.1065, MFSK16 14.0785, DominoEX 14.0715 MHz); a sauran wurare mitar RTTY.
    • Rubutawa a log: log na hannu yana buɗewa da sunan yanayin (misali OLIVIA 8/250), ana rubuta shi a fayilolin ADIF a matsayin yanayin OLIVIA, MFSK ko DOMINO da ƙaramin yanayinsa, a mitar tsakiyar siginarka.
    • Iyakar aikawa ba tare da tsayawa ba tana aiki kamar a RTTY: dogon rubutu (Olivia a hankali yake: kusan haruffa 1.5 a daƙiƙa a 8/250) yana tsayawa a iyaka.
    • Suna amfani da saitunan Saituna > RTTY (fassara, mai sa ido, ma'ajiyoyi). Waɗannan yanayi sababbi ne kuma suna iya canzawa har yanzu.
    ↑ Koma zuwa Abubuwan da ke ciki

    THOR, Contestia da MT63 (gwaji)

    Ƙarin yanayin madannai uku suna a shafin HAM na mai zaɓar yanayi, maɓalli ɗaya ga kowane iyali tare da nau'in da aka zaɓa a gefen yanayi a sandar ƙasa, kowanne da alamar (na gwaji): THOR 8, THOR 11, THOR 16, THOR 22; CONTESTIA 4/250, CONTESTIA 8/250, CONTESTIA 16/500, CONTESTIA 32/1000; MT63 1000L, MT63 2000L, MT63 500L da MT63 1000S. Suna aiki daidai kamar Olivia (duba a sama): allon RTTY tare da wannan modem, siginar da ke ƙarƙashin sandar karɓa ana fassarata, ƙwaƙwalwa ana aika ta a matsayin aikawa ɗaya, rubutun da aka rubuta layi ɗaya a lokaci guda.

    • THOR: sautukan DominoEX (ba sa damuwa da gyara tashar da ɓata) tare da gyaran kuskuren MFSK16; ɗan a hankali fiye da DominoEX a lambar sauri ɗaya, mafi ƙarfi sosai.
    • Contestia (sautuka / faɗi a Hz): danginta na Olivia mafi sauri, manyan haruffa kawai, sananne a gasa; haruffa, lambobi da alamomin rubutu na yau da kullum (ana aika sauran haruffa a matsayin ?).
    • MT63 (faɗi a Hz, L = dogon, S = gajeren interleave): masu ɗaukar sigina 64 a lokaci guda, faɗin 500, 1000 ko 2000 Hz, kusan haruffa 5 zuwa 20 a daƙiƙa; mai ƙarfi kan dusashewa da fashewar hayaniya, ana amfani da shi a nets na gaggawa. Sandar karɓa ita ce tsakiyar siginar. Dogon interleave ya fi ƙarfi, gajeren yana fara bugawa da wuri.
    • Mitoci: a 20 m THOR 14.077, Contestia 14.1095 da MT63 14.105 MHz; a sauran wurare THOR yana amfani da na DominoEX, Contestia yana amfani da na Olivia kuma MT63 yana amfani da faffadan sassan dijital (misali 7.050 MHz). Mitar da ka saita da kanka tana nan tana rinjaye.
    • Rubutawa a log: log na hannu yana buɗewa da sunan yanayin (misali THOR 16); a fayilolin ADIF ana rubuta THOR a matsayin yanayin THOR da ƙaramin yanayinsa (THOR16 ...), Contestia a matsayin yanayin CONTESTI kuma MT63 a matsayin yanayin MT63 (ADIF ba shi da ƙananan yanayoyi ga waɗannan biyu).
    • Throb da ThrobX (layuka THROB 1, THROB 2, THROB 4, THROBX 1, THROBX 2, THROBX 4; lambar ita ce alamomi a daƙiƙa guda): harafi ɗaya a kowace bugun jini, ana aika shi a matsayin sauti ɗaya ko biyu daga cikin 9 (ThrobX: 11), faɗin kusan 70 zuwa 170 Hz. A hankali kuma mai sauƙi; manyan haruffa, lambobi da wasu alamomin rubutu (ana aika sauran haruffa a matsayin sarari). ThrobX yana da ƙarin alamomin rubutu. Suna amfani da mitocin DominoEX.
    • IFKP (layuka IFKP 0.5, IFKP 1.0, IFKP 2.0) da FSQ (layuka FSQ 2, FSQ 3, FSQ 4.5, FSQ 6; lambar ita ce gudu a cikin alamomi a sakan guda): sautuka 33 faɗin kusan 390 Hz ( FSQ: 300 Hz), kowane sauti mataki ɗaya sama da na baya, don haka daidaita mitar da ƙasa da hertz kaɗan ba matsala ba ce. Ƙananan haruffa, sarari da ɗigo suna ɗaukar mataki ɗaya, sauran duka biyu: rubuta da ƙananan haruffa don aika da sauri. Ba sa buƙatar dogon farawa: rubutu yana bayyana daga haruffan farko. FSQ yana aika kowane layi a matsayin tura ɗaya kuma yana sa sunan kiranka, ɗigo biyu da duba haruffa biyu a gabansa (ab1cde:5f), kamar yadda tashoshin FSQ ke yi. Umarni biyu na kai tsaye: rubuta sunan kira sannan alamar tambaya (k1abc?) don neman rahoton sigina daga wannan tashar (tana amsawa k1abc:f3ab1cde snr=12 db), ko rubuta heartbeat don aika kan sakonka kaɗai, domin tashoshi su rubuta cewa an ji ka. Ba a bayar da relay, akwatin wasiƙa da fayiloli ba.
    • Iyakar aikawa ba tare da tsayawa ba tana aiki kamar a RTTY. Waɗannan yanayi sababbi ne kuma suna iya canzawa har yanzu.
    ↑ Koma zuwa Abubuwan da ke ciki

    Hellschreiber (Feld Hell, na gwaji)

    Hellschreiber yana aika rubutu a matsayin hoto: ana duba kowace harafi a cikin ƙuntatattun ginshiƙai kuma mai karɓa yana zana abin da yake ji, don haka kana karanta rubutun daga allo -- ba a yanke komai zuwa haruffa, kuma siginar da ba ta da ƙarfi ko mai hayaniya tana nuna haruffa da ido zai iya gane su. Zaɓi HELL (na gwaji) a ƙasan mai zaɓen yanayi: allon RTTY yana buɗewa da modem ɗin Hell.

    • Hoton da aka karɓa yana gudana a tsawon ɗan tsiri a ƙarƙashin waterfall, sababbin haruffa a dama. Ana buga kowane ginshiƙi sau biyu, kwafi ɗaya a saman ɗayan, don haka kwafi ɗaya na kowace harafi koyaushe yana nan cikakke.
    • Taɓa tsiri don ganin hoton a naɗe cikin layuka, kamar rubutun da aka karɓa, a maimakon waterfall (kwafi ɗaya, a daidaice); taɓa ƙaramin waterfall ɗin da ke cikin tsiri don komawa.
    • MODE (a maimakon BAUD) yana zagaya nau'o'in: FELD (Feld Hell, wanda aka fi amfani da shi), SLOW, X5, X9, FSK, FSK105 da HELL80. Dole tashoshin biyu su yi amfani da iri ɗaya.
    • SLANT (a maimakon SHIFT) yana miƙe hoton da ya karkata: katin sauti na ɗayan tashar yana gudu da sauri ko a hankali kaɗan. Zagaya shi har sai haruffa su tsaya tsaye.
    • Sanya sandar karɓa a kan siginar (carrier, ko tsakiyar sautuka biyu na nau'o'in FSK). Memory yana fita a matsayin watsawa ɗaya; rubutun da aka buga da madannai yana fita layi ɗaya a lokaci guda, idan ka danna Enter. Ana iya aika haruffa, lambobi da alamomin rubutu na yau da kullum.
    • Kowane band yana da mitar Hell ɗinsa inda ake amfani da ɗaya akai-akai (a 20 m 14.063 MHz); a wasu wurare, mitar RTTY.
    • Yin log: logger yana buɗewa da yanayin HELL (ko nau'in, misali HELL X5), ana rubuta shi a fayilolin ADIF a matsayin yanayin HELL tare da submode na nau'in.
    • Iyakar watsawa mai ci gaba tana aiki kamar a RTTY: Feld Hell yana aika kusan haruffa 2.5 a daƙiƙa guda, don haka ana ƙin karɓar dogon rubutu ko a dakatar da shi a iyaka. Wannan yanayin sabo ne kuma yana iya canzawa har yanzu.
    ↑ Koma zuwa Abubuwan da ke ciki

    Packet (APRS) (na gwaji)

    Packet yana aika gajerun firam na AX.25 a matsayin sautuka biyu; APRS yana amfani da su don matsayi, abubuwa, yanayi da gajerun saƙonni. Zaɓi Packet (APRS) (na gwaji) a shafin HAM na mai zaɓen yanayi: allon RTTY yana buɗewa da modem ɗin packet, kuma ana zaɓar gudu kusa da yanayin a sandar ƙasa.

    • APRS 1200: 1200 baud ta rediyon FM, a 2 m (144.800 MHz a Turai da Afirka, 144.390 MHz a Amurkawa, 145.175 MHz a yanki na 3; haka kuma ISS a 145.825 MHz). Sautukan a tsayayyu suke; sandunan ba su da muhimmanci.
    • HF PACKET 300: 300 baud a USB a HF (30 m: 10.1473 MHz, haka kuma 20 m da 40 m). Sanya sandar karɓa a tsakiyar sautuka biyun.
    • Kowane firam da aka ji layi ɗaya ne na rubutun da aka karɓa, kamar CALL>DEST,PATH:info. Tashoshi, abubuwa da tashoshin yanayi da ke aika matsayi suna bayyana a taswira ma, tare da nisansu daga locator ɗinka.
    • Kowane layi na memory, ko kowane layi da ka buga, firam ɗaya ne: BEACON [sharhi] yana aika matsayinka, har zuwa digiri, daga locator ɗinka (ba ya taɓa zama daidai fiye da locator); CALL: text yana aika saƙo zuwa wannan tasha; STATUS text matsayi; layin da ya fara da harafin APRS (! = : > ;) yana fita yadda yake.
    • Firam ɗinka suna fitowa daga sunan kiranka da SSID da aka zaɓa a Saituna > Packet (giyar allon), ta hanyar digipeater da aka saita a can (WIDE1-1,WIDE2-1 a matsayin tsoho, 2 m kawai).
    • Aikawa yana buƙatar TX ON kuma ana ƙi shi a wajen bandodin masu son rediyo; iyakar aikawa a jere tana aiki kamar a RTTY. Ana rubuta shi a log a matsayin PKT. Wannan yanayin sabo ne kuma yana iya canzawa har yanzu.
    • AUTO: idan AUTO na kunne kuma TX ON, saƙonnin da aka aiko maka ana tabbatar da su kai tsaye. Idan AUTO a kashe (tsoho, duk lokacin da Packet ya fara) babu abin da ake tabbatarwa da kansa, kuma da TX OFF babu abin da ake taɓa aikawa. Taɓa saƙon da aka karɓa wanda aka aiko maka yana tambaya “Tabbatar da karɓar saƙo daga CALL?” kuma yana aika tabbatarwa da hannu.
    • HEARD: yana jera tashoshin packet da aka ji (nisa, lokaci, rubutu na ƙarshe) kuma yana buɗe taswira.
    • A APRS 1200 qFT8 yana faɗi sau ɗaya ka saita rediyo zuwa FM da hannu: har yanzu ba ya canza rediyo zuwa FM.
    ↑ Koma zuwa Abubuwan da ke ciki

    SSTV (hotuna)

    SSTV (talabijin mai jinkirin dubawa) tana aika hoto mara motsi a matsayin sauti, layi bayan layi, cikin rabin minti zuwa kusan minti huɗu. qFT8 tana karɓar hotuna da kanta yayin da kake cikin wannan yanayi, kuma tana aika hotunanka, hoton kyamara ko ɗaya daga cikin hotunanta da aka shirya, da alamar kiranka a rubuce a kansu.

    Ƙayyadaddun bayanai: J. L. Barber (N7CXI), “Proposal for SSTV Mode Specifications”, Dayton Hamvention SSTV forum, May 2000.

    Zaɓi SSTV da mai zaɓin yanayi na sandar ƙasa. Rediyon yana komawa mitar kira ta SSTV ta band ɗin, a USB. Allon yana nuna rabi ɗaya a lokaci guda: RX, hotunan da aka karɓa, ko TX, hoton da za a aika. Maɓallai biyu na sama a hagu suna sauyawa tsakaninsu; sauyawa yana canza abin da kake gani kawai, ba ya taɓa aika komai.

    Rabin karɓa (RX)

    1. AUTO: Idan an kunna (kore, na asali) qFT8 tana gano kowane hoto da kanta, a kowane yanayi. Idan an kashe, tana karɓa koyaushe a yanayi ɗaya, a matsayin hoto mai gungurawa (karɓa da hannu). Ana tunawa da zaɓin.
    2. RX | TX: Rabin da ake nunawa. Ɗigo mai rawaya a kan RX yana nufin hoto ya shigo yayin da ake nuna rabin aikawa. AIKA yana nuna rabin aikawa da kansa, kuma allon yana komawa rabin karɓa idan hoton ya ƙare.
    3. Matsayin karɓa: ANA SAURARE (kowane yanayi, dukan zangon sauti); ANA KARƁAR tare da yanayin, nisan tashar daga mita da layin da ake zanawa; DA HANNU idan an kashe AUTO; AN DAKATA yayin da kake aikawa.
    4. Giya tana buɗe Saituna > SSTV.
    5. QSO yana buɗe log na hannu don hoton da ake nunawa (rajista).
    6. Hoton da aka karɓa: wanda ke shigowa, ana zana shi layi bayan layi, ko hoton da aka ajiye da ka zaɓa a zane. Alamar da ke kusurwarsa: zobe mai launin amber da ke cika yayin da yake shigowa (da kibiya kore a ciki idan za a adana shi a ƙarshe), alamar daidai kore idan an adana, kibiya mai launin toka muddin ba a adana ba. Taɓa hoton da aka ajiye don gyara shi.
    7. Taken hoto: lokaci (UTC) da yanayi; yayin da hoton ke shigowa, kashi da daƙiƙan da suka rage; ga hoton da aka ajiye wanda bai cika ba, "an karɓa daga layi ...", "an rasa sigina a layi ..." ko "an rasa layuka ...". A gefensa, filin alamar kira.
    8. ADANA yana rubuta hoton a cikin babban fayil na hotuna kuma ya zama kore (AN ADANA). Idan aka danna yayin da hoton ke shigowa, yana nuna ZA A ADANA IDAN YA ƘARE.
    9. GYARA yana inganta hoton da aka ajiye: haske, bambanci, launi, yanayin launi, kaifi da cire hayaniya. Ana riƙe hoton kamar yadda aka karɓe shi koyaushe: MAYAR yana soke komai, kuma ADANA KWAFI yana adana sigar da aka gyara a matsayin fayil daban.
    10. AMSA yana tambayar alamar kiran ɗayan tashar (yana farawa daga filin alamar kira) kuma yana rubuta "alamar kiransu DE taka" a kan hoton naka da za a aika, a ɗaya daga cikin tsare-tsaren amsa biyar; ba a taɓa mayar da hoton da aka karɓa ba. Yanayin yana komawa wanda hoton da aka karɓa ya yi amfani da shi. Idan iyakar watsawa ba tsayawa taka ta yi gajeru ga wannan yanayi, sanarwa tana faɗin haka; ba a taɓa canza iyakar a madadinka ba.
    11. Kwandon shara yana share hoton da ake nunawa daga qFT8, bayan tambaya. Kwafin da aka riga aka adana a babban fayil na hotuna yana zama a can, kuma tambayar tana faɗin haka.
    12. Zane: da farko hoton da ke shigowa, sannan hotunan da aka ajiye, sabo na farko (hotunan da aka karɓa).
    13. Mai zaɓin yanayi na SSTV a sandar ƙasa: yanayoyin Martin, Scottie, Robot, PD, Wraase da Pasokon, kowanne da daƙiƙan da hoto ke ɗauka. Shi ne yanayin da ake aika hotuna a ciki, kuma yanayin da ake karɓa idan an kashe AUTO. A gefensa, yankin saƙo yana faɗin abin da ke faruwa ("ana saurare", "ana karɓar" tare da kashi, "ana aika" tare da daƙiƙan da suka rage).
    14. Zangon SSTV a kan magudanar ruwa: ana aika da karɓar hotuna tsakanin 1100 da 2300 Hz, tare da gane Morse a 1000 Hz. Kowane hoton da aka ji ko aka aika yana samun akwati mai yanayinsa da alamar kiransa; taɓa akwati don nuna wannan hoton.

    Rabin aikawa (TX)

    1. Yanayin da za a aika hoton a ciki, tare da girmansa da tsarin launinsa. Ana zaɓe shi da mai zaɓin yanayi na sandar ƙasa.
    2. ƊORA yana buɗe Zaɓi hoto.
    3. Hoton da za a aika, daidai yadda zai fita a iska. Idan babu komai, yana cewa Taɓa don ɗora hoto. Taɓa shi don sake daidaita shi da siffar yanayin. Ƙaramar kibiya a kusurwarsa tana adana shi, kamar yadda za a aika, a babban fayil na hotuna.
    4. Salon alamar kira: kibiyoyi suna wucewa tsakanin salo 16 da Babu alamar kira; taɓa sunan salon yana buɗe mai zaɓin salo da launi.
    5. Faleti yana buɗe mai zaɓi ɗaya: salo a jeri da launuka takwas na rubutu. Kowane zaɓi yana bayyana a kan hoton nan take kuma yana zama don hoto na gaba.
    6. Alƙalami yana ƙara layin rubutunka a kan hoton (alƙalamin yana zama rawaya muddin akwai shi).
    7. CIRE yana cire hoton da aka ɗora.
    8. AIKA yana nuna daƙiƙan da dukan watsawa za ta ɗauka. Yayin da hoton ke cikin iska, yana zama TSAYA da daƙiƙan da suka rage, kuma ana yi wa layukan da aka riga aka aika alama a kan hoton.
    9. Layin ja: Danna TX ON don aika hotuna. Da QMX wanda manhajar cikinsa ba za ta iya aika hotuna ba, yana faɗin haka a maimakon.
    10. Hotuna masu sauri: hotuna shida na ƙarshe da ka ɗora, sannan waɗanda aka shirya, tuni a salon alamar kiranka. Taɓawa ɗaya tana ɗora ɗaya a matsayin hoton da za a aika; ba a taɓa aika shi har sai ka danna AIKA.

    Karɓar hotuna

    Idan AUTO yana kunne babu abin da za a saita. qFT8 tana gane yanayin kowace watsawa daga siginar farkonta, da kuma idan ka shigo bayan hoton ya fara (layukan da suka wuce suna kasancewa fanko). Tana saurare a dukan zangon sauti, daga kusan 1000 Hz ƙasa da sautukan da aka saba zuwa 800 Hz sama da su. Hoton da aka aika da ɗan kaucewa daga mita, ko daga tashar da lokacinta ya ɗan karkata, ana daidaita shi ta atomatik. Idan tashoshi biyu suka aika a lokaci ɗaya a mitoci daban-daban a fili, ana karɓa da ajiye hotunan duka biyu; taɓa akwati a magudanar ruwa don zaɓar wanda za a nuna.

    Karɓa da hannu (AUTO a kashe)

    Ga tashar da siginar farkonta ta wuce ka, ko wadda qFT8 ba ta kama da kanta ba, kashe AUTO. Daga nan qFT8 tana karɓar yanayin da aka zaɓa a sandar ƙasa koyaushe, a matsayin dogon hoto ɗaya mai gungurawa: sababbin layuka suna ƙaruwa a ƙasa kuma hoton yana hawa sama. Matsayin yana nuna DA HANNU tare da yanayi da kaucewa; idan aka ji siginar farko a wani yanayi, yana faɗin haka, a matsayin nuni kawai (canza yanayin da kanka).

    • Alamar karɓa: a magudanar ruwa zangon yana zama alama mai gefuna rawaya biyu. Ja shi zuwa kan siginar (da yatsa, ko da linzamin kwamfuta a kwamfuta); qFT8 tana bin siginar cikin kusan 100 Hz daga gare shi. Ana tunawa da wurin.
    • ADANA da GYARA suna fara buɗe Yanke hoton: ja gefuna rawaya biyu a kewayen hoton da kake so; suna farawa a hoto cikakke na ƙarshe. Abin da aka yanke yana zama hoton da aka ajiye a zane, sannan a adana shi ko a buɗe shi a editan.
    • Kwandon shara yana sake fara hoto mai gungurawa babu komai; ba a taɓa hotunan da aka ajiye ba.

    Hoton wane ne? Filin alamar kira

    Tashoshi da yawa suna bayyana kansu a kewayen hotonsu, da Morse kafin ko bayan shi, ko da gajeren fashewar bayanai bayansa. qFT8 tana karanta duka biyun, kuma tana rubuta alamar kiran a filin da ke gefen taken hoto. Idan ba a ji komai ba, filin yana cewa Ba a san alamar kira ba. Taɓa filin don rubuta alamar kira da kanka (sau da yawa an rubuta ta a kan hoton) ko don gyara ta; abin da ka rubuta yana maye gurbin abin da aka ji, kuma AMSA da QSO suna farawa daga gare shi.

    Hotunan da aka karɓa

    qFT8 tana riƙe hotunan da aka karɓa kwanan nan da kanta, ko ka adana su ko a'a: guda 100, sai dai idan ka canza Hotunan da aka karɓa da manhaja ke riƙewa; mafi tsufa yana faɗuwa idan sabo ya zo. Suna wanzuwa bayan sake kunnawa. Zanen da ke ƙarƙashin hoton yana nuna su, sabo na farko, kowanne da alamar an adana / ba a adana ba; hoton da ke shigowa har yanzu yana gaba. Taɓa ɗaya don nuna shi; yayin da hoto ke shigowa, taɓa na farko don sake bin shi. Idan babu abin da ke shigowa, ana nuna sabon hoton da aka ajiye.

    Babban fayil na hotuna

    ADANA (ko Adana kowane hoton da aka karɓa a na'ura ta atomatik) yana rubuta hoton a matsayin fayil na PNG, kuma sanarwa tana faɗin inda:

    • Waya ko kwamfutar hannu: Pictures/qFT8 SSTV, don haka hotunan suna bayyana a cikin hotuna. Android 6 zuwa 9 tana neman izinin ma'ajiya sau ɗaya; ba tare da shi ba, hotunan suna shiga babban fayil na qFT8 nata kuma sanarwa tana faɗin haka.
    • Kwamfuta: babban fayil qFT8 SSTV a cikin babban fayil na Hotuna naka (wanda tebur ɗinka ke amfani da shi, ko da yana da wani suna a harshenka).
    • iPhone da iPad: babban fayil qFT8 SSTV a cikin ma'ajiyar qFT8 nata.
    • Sarrafawa daga nesa ta yanar gizo: ana adana hoton a inda qFT8 ke aiki, kuma mai bincike yana sauke kwafi. Shafin da aka ba izinin kallo kawai yana nuna SAUKE a maimakon, wanda yake sauke kawai.

    Ajiyewa ta atomatik a kashe take a waya, kwamfutar hannu da iPhone, kuma a kunne a kwamfuta. A iPhone hoton da aka ajiye kuma yana zuwa Hotunanka (tsarin yana tambaya sau ɗaya). Ana sanya wa hotunan da aka ajiye suna da kwanan wata da lokaci (UTC), bandi, mita a kHz, yanayi da alamar kira, misali 2026-10-05_1431_20m_14230_M1_Q1ABC.png, ko ..._UNKNOWN.png idan babu alamar kira. Idan alamar kira ta iso ko aka rubuta ta bayan an ajiye hoton, ana sake sanya wa fayil ɗin da aka ajiye suna; ba a yin kwafi na biyu. Kwafin da aka gyara yana ƙarewa da _edit.png, kuma hoton da aka ajiye daga rabin aikawa da _tx.png. Share hoto a qFT8 ba ya taɓa share fayil ɗin da aka ajiye.

    Aika hoto

    1. Zaɓi yanayin da mai zaɓi na sandar ƙasa. Idan kana amsa wa tasha, yi amfani da yanayin da ta yi amfani da shi (AMSA yana yi maka haka). Robot 36, Martin 1 da Scottie 1 ne aka fi amfani da su.
    2. Danna ƊORA (ko taɓa hoton da babu komai). Zaɓi hoto yana nuna hotunanka (Hotuna; Fayiloli a kwamfuta), Kyamara inda take, da hotuna 59 da aka shirya (katunan CQ, tambarin qFT8, shimfidar wurare, ruɗin gani, katin QSL, 73...). Kana kuma iya raba hoto zuwa qFT8 daga wata manhaja; idan ana amfani da wani yanayi, qFT8 tana tambaya kafin ta koma SSTV.
    3. Idan hoton ba shi da siffar yanayin, Daidaita hoto yana biyo baya: DUKA yana nuna shi gaba ɗaya da gefuna, YANKE yana cika firam (ja don motsawa, matsa yatsu don faɗaɗawa), JUYA yana juya shi, kuma haske, bambanci da Bambanci na atomatik suna daidaita shi. YI AMFANI DA HOTO yana karɓe shi.
    4. Sannan Salon alamar kira yana biyo baya: hotonka a kowane ɗayan salon alamar kira 16. Taɓa ɗaya ka danna YI AMFANI; BAYA yana komawa hotuna.
    5. Kunna TX da maɓallin TX ON kuma ka danna AIKA.

    Kashe TX, danna TSAYA, ko canza band ko yanayi yana tsayar da hoton nan take; bayan TSAYA, TX yana zama a kunne, a shirye don sake aikawa. Idan hoto yana shigowa lokacin da ka danna AIKA, qFT8 tana tambaya kafin ta daina karɓa.

    Salon alamar kira

    Kwanan wata shi ne kwanan watan UTC da aka aika hoton, kuma band shi ne wanda kake a kai. Ana rubuta "CQ" a cikin salo a matsayin CQ DE da alamar kiranka.

    1. CQ + kwanan wata + band, a cikin zane a ƙasa (na asali).
    2. Alamar kira kaɗai, a cikin zane a ƙasa.
    3. Babban kwanan wata + band, ƙasa: CQ da alamar kiranka, tare da band da kwanan wata da manyan haruffa.
    4. Babban kwanan wata + band, sama: band da kwanan wata da manyan haruffa a sama, CQ da alamar kiranka a ƙasa.
    5. Zane: alamar kira + kwanan wata + band: hoton ya ɗan ƙanƙanta, a saman zane mai CQ, alamar kiranka, kwanan wata da band.
    6. Zane: alamar kira: haka nan, da alamar kiranka kawai.
    7. Babban CQ + alamar kwanan wata: babban CQ a kusurwa da ƙaramar alama mai band da kwanan wata.
    8. Alamar kira a tsakiya: alamar kiranka da manyan haruffa a tsakiya.
    9. Tikiti + kwanan wata: tikiti a kusurwa mai alamar kiranka, band da kwanan wata.
    10. Alamar kira sama hagu.
    11. Firam + kwanan wata: firam mai launi, a cikinsa alamar kiranka da band da kwanan wata.
    12. Alamar kira mai inuwa: alamar kiranka da inuwa.
    13. CQ CQ + kwanan wata: CQ CQ CQ a sama, alamar kiranka, band da kwanan wata a ƙasa.
    14. Alamar kira a zane na sama: alamar kiranka a cikin zane mai haske a gefen sama.
    15. Hatimin gidan waya: alamar kiranka a sama hagu da hatimin gidan waya mai zagaye mai band da kwanan wata.
    16. Alamar kira mai layuka da yawa: alamar kiranka da layuka da yawa a kewayen haruffan.

    Babu alamar kira ba ya rubuta komai a kan hoton (sai layin rubutunka, idan ka ƙara ɗaya). Har yanzu ana aika alamar kira da Morse ko a matsayin FSK ID idan suna kunne a saituna. Hoton amsa yana amfani da ɗaya daga cikin tsare-tsaren amsa biyar a maimakon (ɗaya a kan ɗaya, gefe da gefe, sama da ƙasa, babban zagaye, da kwanan wata + band); kibiyoyi da faleti suna wucewa tsakaninsu.

    Iyakokin watsawa

    Hoto ɗaya watsawa ce ɗaya ba tsayawa da cikakken ƙarfi: kusan daƙiƙa 40 a Robot 36, minti biyu a Martin 1, kuma fiye da minti huɗu a yanayoyin da suka fi jinkiri. Ana ƙirga komai: siginar farko, alamar kira da Morse, hoton da FSK ID, kuma daƙiƙan da ke kan AIKA da a jerin yanayoyi su ne dukan wannan lokaci.

    • Yanayin da ya fi iyakar watsawa ba tsayawa ta rediyonka tsayi yana bayyana da launin toka a jerin yanayoyi. Zaɓinsa, ko danna AIKA a cikinsa, yana bayyana dalili kuma yana ba da damar buɗe saitin; ba a aika komai.
    • A ƙarƙashin kowane zaɓi na saitin, qFT8 tana jera yanayoyin SSTV da zaɓin ke ƙarawa, don ka ga abin da ɗaga shi ke bayarwa.
    • Kashe alamar kira ta Morse ko FSK ID a Saituna > SSTV yana sa kowane hoto ya rage daƙiƙa kaɗan.
    • Ba a taɓa canza iyakar a madadinka ba, ko da ta AMSA.

    QMX ko QMX+ yana buƙatar manhajar ciki 1_04_001 ko sabuwa don aika hotuna; da tsohuwar manhajar ciki SSTV tana karɓa kawai, kuma layin ja na rabin aikawa yana faɗin haka.

    Rajistar QSO na SSTV

    Babu abin da ake rajista da kansa. Nuna hoton ɗayan tashar kuma ka danna QSO: log na hannu yana buɗewa da yanayin SSTV, band, mitar rediyo, alamar kira daga filin alamar kira, da 595 da aka aika da aka karɓa. Sanarwa tana roƙonka ka duba alamar kira, ko ka rubuta ta idan ba a sami ko ɗaya ba. Ajiye shi kamar kowane QSO; daga nan ana loda shi zuwa ayyukanka kamar sauran.

    A kwamfuta, iPhone da sarrafawa daga nesa ta yanar gizo

    • Kwamfuta: allo ɗaya, da KARƁA | AIKA a maimakon RX | TX, linzamin kwamfuta a maimakon yatsa (danna don ɗorawa, ja alamar), da Fayiloli a maimakon hotuna da kyamara.
    • iPhone da iPad: allo ɗaya da masu sarrafawa iri ɗaya kamar a wayar Android.
    • Sarrafawa daga nesa ta yanar gizo: ginshiƙan karɓa da aikawa gefe da gefe, a gefe ɗaya AUTO, alamar karɓa, zane, ADANA da AMSA, a ɗayan gefen mai zaɓin yanayi, ƊORA, salo, CIRE da AIKA. ƊORA yana zaɓar hoto a mai bincike kuma yana yanke shi a can (ja; faɗaɗa da mai zamewa ko ƙafafun linzamin kwamfuta). Gyara, sharewa da rajista ana yin su a cikin qFT8 kanta. AIKA yana aiki ne kawai idan mai aiki yana da TX ON a qFT8.

    Saituna > SSTV

    Buɗe su da giyar allon, ko daga shafin Saituna. Karɓa:

    • Adana kowane hoton da aka karɓa a na'ura ta atomatik (a kashe a wayoyi da iPhone, a kunne a kwamfuta).
    • Hotunan da aka karɓa da manhaja ke riƙewa: 25, 50, 100 (na asali), 200 ko 500.

    Aikawa:

    • Aika alamar kiranku da CW: a kashe, kafin hoto (na asali), bayan shi, ko duka biyu. Alamar kira kawai, a 20 WPM; ana ƙirga daƙiƙanta a lokacin watsawa.
    • Aika FSK ID bayan kowane hoto (a kunne): gajeren fashewar bayanai da ke ba shirin ɗayan tashar damar karanta alamar kiranka.
    • Salon rubutu: salon alamar kira da hoto na gaba zai fara da shi (salo 1 a sabon shigarwa), da Launin rubutu (fari).
    • Bambanci na atomatik da Ƙara kaifi ta atomatik (duka a kunne): ana amfani da su a kan hoton da kake aikawa.

    SAKE SAITA ZUWA NA ASALI yana mayar da duk saitunan SSTV zuwa ƙimominsu na farko.

    ↑ Koma zuwa Abubuwan da ke ciki

    DRAW (hotuna a kan waterfall)

    DRAW yana zana hoto mai launin toka kai tsaye a kan waterfall na sauran tashoshi: kowane ginshiƙin hoton sauti ɗaya ne, 6.25 Hz daga na gaba, kuma ana aika kowane layin hoton na ɗan ƙaramin lokaci, daga layin ƙasa zuwa na sama. Duk wanda ke da waterfall yana ganin hotonka yana bayyana, da qFT8 ko ba tare da ita ba. Hoto na iya samun kowane siffa: hoton kyamara, zane, ko layin rubutu kawai kamar alamar kiranka. DRAW ba yanayi ne na ma'auni ba; an yi shi ne don a gani.

    Ƙayyadaddun bayanai: ƙirar qFT8 ta kansa.

    Zaɓi DRAW da mai zaɓar yanayi na sandar ƙasa. RX da TX a saman allon suna nuna rabin karɓa ko na aikawa:

    1. Bayanai: faɗi, girman hoton a ginshiƙai da layuka, da gudu.
    2. ƊORA: “Ɗauki hoto” ko “Zaɓi hoto”. Hoton yana riƙe siffarsa.
    3. Hoton da za a aika, mai launin toka, a zane da launukan waterfall ɗinka: wannan shi ne abin da sauran tashoshi za su gani. Yayin da ake watsawa, layin rawaya yana nuna layin da ake aikawa, tare da “Ana watsawa: layi N cikin M”.
    4. Alamar kira (a kunne ta tsohuwa): alamar kiranka da manyan haruffa a ƙarƙashin hoton. Fensir yana canza rubutun farantin (babu komai = alamar kiranka). Bambanci na atomatik yana mayar da mafi duhun sashin hoton baƙi da mafi haskensa fari, da danna sau ɗaya; Bambanci da Haske naka ne ka daidaita da hannu bayan haka.
    5. CIRE yana komawa farantin alamar kira kaɗai. AIKA yana nuna daƙiƙa nawa hoton zai ɗauka, kuma ya zama TSAYA tare da daƙiƙun da suka rage.
    6. Layin ja na faɗin dalilin da ba za a iya aika hoton yanzu ba, kuma tana ɓoye idan babu abin da ya hana: "Danna TX ON don aika hotuna"; "20 s: ya wuce iyakarka ta 15 s" (hoton ya fi iyakar watsawa ba tsayawa ta rediyonka tsawo); "Za a iya aika faɗin 500 Hz kawai a 30 m"; "Aikawa za ta fita daga zangunan masu son rediyo"; ko, da QMX wanda firmware ɗinsa ba zai iya mayar da rediyo zuwa SSB ta CAT ba, cewa hotuna karɓa kawai ake iya yi.
    7. GUDU: 3.125, 6.25 (tsoho: layi ɗaya yana ɗaukar tsawon sautin FT8 ɗaya) ko layuka 12.5 a kowane daƙiƙa. Kusa da shi: nawa hoton ke amfani da shi daga iyakar watsawa ba tsayawa ɗinka. Jerin yana nuna daƙiƙun hoton a kowane gudu; gudun da ya yi tsayi da iyakarka yana faɗa idan ka taɓa shi. Mitar tana nuna misali "20 s cikin iyakarka ta 30 s".
    8. Farantin rubutu: taɓawa ɗaya tana loda “CQ” da alamar kiranka, “73” da alamar kiranka, ko alamar kiranka kaɗai, a matsayin dukan hoton.
    9. JUYA (kusa da CIRE) yana juya dukan watsawar, tare da farantin, kwata na zagaye a kowane danna, domin a karanta ta a tsaye a kan waterfall da ke gungurawa gefe. A bangaren karɓa, JUYA yana juya hoton da aka nuna (kuma aka adana) haka nan.
    10. Faɗin a sandar ƙasa: 500, 700 (tsoho) ko 1200 Hz, tare da daƙiƙun hoton a kowane faɗi. Hoto mai faɗi yana da ƙarin ginshiƙai, don haka ya fi tsabta kuma yana ɗaukar lokaci mai tsawo.
    11. Band ɗin DRAW a kan waterfall: hoton yana tsakanin 1000 Hz da 1000 Hz tare da faɗinsa.

    Karɓar hotuna

    Babu abin da za a fara: qFT8 yana saurare koyaushe muddin kana cikin wannan yanayi, har ma lokacin da ake nuna rabin aikawa.

    1. RX da TX: rabin karɓa da rabin aikawa.
    2. Layin matsayi: ana saurare, faɗin da kuma maƙallin da ake saurara (1000–1700 Hz a 700 Hz). Yayin da hotonka ke kan iska, karɓa yana tsayawa kuma layin yana faɗa.
    3. Hoton da aka karɓa: maƙallin DRAW na mintuna uku na ƙarshe a matsayin hoto ɗaya, sabon layi a sama, da pikseli masu murabba'i, cikin launukan ruwan-ruwanka: hoton da aka aika da qFT8 yana bayyana a tsaye. Kafin wani abu ya iso, yana faɗin mitocin da yake saurara.
    4. Alamar kai tsaye: yanayin, faɗin da gudun da ake zana hoton da shi.
    5. ADANA (shuɗi da zarar akwai abin adanawa) yana adana hoton da ake nunawa, an yanke shi zuwa inda ya fito daga hayaniya, cikin toka, kamar yadda aka karɓa, a matsayin PNG mai suna da kwanan wata da lokaci (UTC) da faɗin. A waya ko kwamfutar hannu yana zuwa babban fayil na hotuna, a Pictures/qFT8 DRAW; idan qFT8 ba zai iya rubutu a can ba, yana zama a babban fayil na qFT8 kansa kuma sanarwar tana faɗin inda.
    6. JUYA yana juya hoton da ake nunawa (da wanda aka adana) kwata zagaye a kowane danna; maɓallin yana nuna juyawar (90°, 180°, 270°).
    7. GUDU: saita shi zuwa gudun da ɗayan tasha ke amfani da shi (layi 3.125, 6.25 ko 12.5 a daƙiƙa); ana sake zana dukan hoton da aka karɓa da sabon gudun. Ba ya canza gudun da kake aikawa da shi.

    AGC na rediyonka: sarrafa ƙarfin sigina ta atomatik na rediyo yana rage ƙarfi a layi mai haske kuma yana ƙara shi a layi mai duhu, wanda ke sa hoton da aka karɓa ya zama mai ratsi. qFT8 tana auna hayaniya kusa da hoton a kowane layi tana gyara, kuma tana faɗa idan ta ga ƙarfin yana motsi tare da hoton: sai ka saita AGC na rediyo zuwa OFF ko SLOW (kuma ka rage ƙarfin RF kaɗan idan band ɗin yana da hayaniya).

    Aika hoto

    1. Zaɓi faɗi a sandar ƙasa da gudu da GUDU.
    2. Loda hoto, ko ka taɓa farantin rubutu. Saita bambanci, ko ka danna Bambanci na atomatik.
    3. Kunna TX ka danna AIKA.

    Kashe TX, danna TSAYA, TUNE, ko canza band ko yanayi yana tsayar da hoton nan take. Hoton da ya fi iyakar watsawa ba tsayawa ta rediyonka tsawo ba a aika shi: layin ja yana faɗin tsawonsa, kuma gudu mafi sauri, faɗi mafi kunkuntar ko ƙaramin hoto yana sa shi ya shiga cikin iyaka. Ana aika dukan sautuka a lokaci guda, don haka ka riƙe matakin watsawa kamar na FT8, da ALC na rediyo a ƙasa.

    A kwamfuta, iPhone da na'urar nesa ta yanar gizo

    Fanel ɗin iri ɗaya ne a ko'ina, da maɓallai iri ɗaya. Bambance-bambancen:

    • Kwamfuta: ƊORA yana buɗe fayil ɗin hoto; ADANA yana rubutawa a babban fayil qFT8 DRAW cikin babban fayil ɗinka na Pictures.
    • iPhone: ƊORA yana bayar da "Ɗauki hoto" ko "Zaɓi hoto", kamar a waya; ADANA yana ajiye hoton a babban fayil qFT8 DRAW cikin fayilolin qFT8 kansa.
    • Na'urar nesa ta yanar gizo tana nuna hoton aikawa iri ɗaya da hoton da aka karɓa. ƊORA yana ɗaukar hoto daga na'urar da burauza ke aiki a kai (an rage shi zuwa faɗin ginshiƙai 192 a mafi yawa, hoto mafi faɗi da DRAW ke aikawa); ADANA yana adanawa a waya ko kwamfutar da ke gudanar da qFT8, ba a burauza ba. Aikawa na buƙatar matakin samun dama na cikakken iko, kuma kamar a manhaja, yana tafiya iska ne kawai idan TX yana kunne.

    Har ina yake kaiwa?

    An yi DRAW ne don a gani, ba don a fito da sigina daga cikin hayaniya ba. Allon alamar kira a kan tsoffin saitin 700 Hz da layuka 6.25 a kowace daƙiƙa ana iya karanta shi har kusan -2 dB SNR (faɗin zango 2500 Hz), a layuka 3.125 a kowace daƙiƙa har kusan -5 dB; hoto yana da kyau daga kusan +4 dB. FT8 yana kaiwa kusan -21 dB, don haka idan ɗayan tashar da ƙyar take fassara FT8 ɗinku, ba za ta ga hotonku ba. Saurin da ya fi jinkiri da faɗin da ya fi ƙanƙanta suna kaiwa nesa. Dushewa (fading) tana rage kowace hanya kusan 6 dB. Abin da DRAW ke ba ku wanda sauran hanyoyi ba sa bayarwa: duk wanda ke kallon waterfall yana ganin hotonku, ba tare da wani shiri na musamman ba.

    Mitoci, sideband da rubutawa

    qFT8 tana daidaitawa zuwa mitar DRAW ɗinta a kowane band, wadda aka zaɓa a sassan “dukan yanayi” na tsare-tsaren band na IARU na yankuna uku, nesa da cibiyoyin aikin SSTV, na dijital da na murya (dial, USB): 160 m 1.865, 80 m 3.665, 40 m 7.190, 30 m 10.145 (500 Hz kawai), 20 m 14.335, 17 m 18.140, 15 m 21.400, 12 m 24.975, 10 m 28.520 da 6 m 50.380 MHz. 60 m ba shi da mita (dokoki sun bambanta daga ƙasa zuwa ƙasa). A 30 m tsare-tsaren band suna ba da izinin 500 Hz a mafi yawa, don haka ana aika faɗin 500 Hz kawai a can. Duba lasisinka da tsarin band na yankinka.

    Rediyon yana cikin USB a kowane band. Tashar da ke saurare a LSB tana ganin hoton a juye daga hagu zuwa dama.

    QSO yana buɗe logger da yanayin DRAW: ma'aunin rubutawa (ADIF) ba shi da DRAW, kuma hoto mara motsi da aka aika layi-layi shi ne abin da FAX ke nufi a can (ana ƙirga shi a matsayin yanayin hoto, kamar SSTV). QMX yana buƙatar firmware ɗaya da na SSTV. Rajista, fayiloli da lodawa suna rubuta shi a matsayin FAX tare da ƙaramin yanayi DRAW; wuraren shakatawa (POTA) da eQSL suna samun FAX kawai.

    ↑ Koma zuwa Abubuwan da ke ciki

    Tauraron dan Adam da kamfas ɗin taswira

    Ana zana tauraron dan Adam na masu son rediyo a kan Taswirar duniya, suna motsi a ainihin lokaci, kuma ana iya yin aiki da tauraron dan Adam daga manhajar: inda yake, lokacin da zai wuce, inda za a nuna, da canjin Doppler. Ana lissafa komai a na'urarka daga bayanan falaki da qFT8 ke saukewa kawai a lokacin da ake nuna tauraron dan Adam. Kamfas yana taimaka maka ka nufi tauraron dan Adam ko tasha.

    Maɓallin SAT da tauraron dan Adam da ake nunawa

    Maɓallin SAT a ɓangaren hagu na kayan aikin taswira yana nuna ko ɓoye duk tauraron dan Adam a taswira (yana kunne ta tsohuwa). Dawo da waɗanda aka ɓoye ba ya da tsada: bayanan zagayawa suna ci gaba da sabuntawa, don haka sake danna shi nan take ne. Ta tsohuwa taswirar tana nuna tauraron dan Adam na HF. Saituna > Taswirar duniya > Tauraron dan Adam a taswira yana zaɓar abin da take nunawa: HF, VHF, UHF da SHF, kowanne a matsayin hanya biyu (mai maimaitawa, transponder ko digipeater da za ku iya aiki ta cikinsa) ko saurare kawai (fitilu, telemetry, hotuna); da farko layuka biyu na HF ne kawai aka yiwa alama, kuma yiwa wani layi alama yana ƙara tauraron dan Adam nasa. Tauraron dan Adam ɗaya zai iya zama duka biyun, misali mai maimaitawa a VHF da fitila a HF. Tauraron dan Adam wanda ba a san mitarsa ba ba shi da layi: yana fitowa ne kawai idan an yiwa dukkan layuka alama, kuma layi a ƙarƙashin layukan yana faɗin adadinsu. Lokacin da kuka yiwa zangon VHF ko UHF (6 m, 2 m, 70 cm, 23 cm) alama a Saituna > Zaɓin Zango, qFT8 yana tambaya ko a nuna tauraron dan Adam na wannan zangon ma; Ee yana yiwa layukansa biyu alama a Saituna > Taswirar duniya. Kowane tauraron dan Adam a taswira yana ɗauke da sunansa; inda suka cunkushe, ƙara girman taswira don raba su.

    Zaɓar tauraron dan Adam: hanyarsa da yankin da yake rufewa
