    Taɓa tauraron dan Adam sai a nuna shi shi kaɗai (sauran suna dawowa idan ka taɓa wani wuri a taswira), tare da hanyarsa ta zagaye ɗaya, yankin da yake rufewa (ɓangaren Duniya da yake sama da sararin sama) da kuma hanya mafi tsawo a gaba mai alama kamar +2 h a lokuta na yau da kullum. Zaɓuɓɓuka biyu a Saituna > Taswirar duniya suna tantance yadda wannan doguwar hanyar take:

    • Hanyar tauraron ɗan adam a gaba: awa nawa na hanya ake zana, 6, 12, 24, 36 ko 48 h (24 h tun farko).
    • Alamomin hanyar tauraron dan Adam kowane: alama kowane 1, 2, 3 ko 6 h (2 h tun farko).

    Wucewa

    Taɓa guntun sunan tauraron dan Adam don ganin ɗan kati: ko yana sama yanzu (da kuma har zuwa yaushe) ko lokacin da zai fito da tsayin da zai kai, babban yanayinsa da mitoci, da wucewa ta gaba. Tauraron dan Adam mai tsayawa a wuri ɗaya ana nuna shi a koyaushe yana sama; wanda ba shi da wucewa cikin kwanaki biyu masu zuwa yana faɗin haka.

    Yin aiki da tauraron dan Adam

    YI AIKI DA SHI a kan katin yana barin wannan tauraron dan Adam kaɗai a taswira kuma yana buɗe ina za a nuna: da'irar sama mai wucewa, tauraron dan Adam, inda wayarka ke nuni, azimuth da tsayi, tazara da canjin Doppler, tare da A KAN MANUFA idan wayar tana nuni zuwa gare shi (da ƙasa da sararin sama idan bai fito ba). Guntun SAT mai shuɗi yana bayyana a kan spectrogram tare da gyaran Doppler; taɓa shi don sake buɗe ina za a nuna. KAMMALA yana dakatar da aiki da tauraron dan Adam.

    • qFT8 yana nuna mitocin da aka gyara amma ba ya sake daidaita rediyo saboda canjin Doppler, kuma ba ya taɓa canza ƙarfin rediyo.
    • Tauraron dan Adam da ba shi da abin da za a aika masa (beacon, telemetry) na karɓa ne kawai: TX yana kasancewa a kashe yayin da kake aiki da shi, kuma kai da kanka ka danna TX ON daga baya.
    • A kwamfuta, wadda ba ta da kamfas, taga ɗaya tana nuna lambobi: ka nuna eriya da su.

    Kamfas ɗin taswira

    A wayoyi da kwamfutar hannu masu kamfas, maɓalli tsakanin na saituna da na cikakken allo yana nuna jar allura mai nuni zuwa arewa ta gaskiya da alkiblar da wayarka ke nuni. Kamfas yana kunne tun farko; a kashe shi da Kamfas a taswira a cikin Saituna > Taswirar duniya, sai maɓallin, allurar da layin su ɓace kuma firikwensin ya tsaya. Yana bin allo, don haka daidai ne a tsaye da a kwance.

    • Layin: danna maɓallin don zana layi a taswira daga tasharka zuwa alkiblar da wayarka ke nuni. Yana tafiya gaba kawai, kusan zagaye duniya ta sandar arewa, yana dusashewa a ƙarshe, tare da kan kibiya da ke nuna hanya. Idan an zaɓi tauraron dan Adam ko tasha, taswira tana faɗin alkiblarsa, gajeriyar hanya ko doguwa, da nawa za a juya.
    • Daidaitawa: ! mai rawaya a kan maɓallin yana nufin ba a daidaita kamfas ba. Danna shi a lokacin yana buɗe Daidaita kamfas maimakon layin: motsa waya kamar lamba 8, kana juya ta, har sai ma'aunin ya cika. Layin yana kashe kansa idan kamfas ɗin ya rasa daidaitonsa.
    ↑ Koma zuwa Abubuwan da ke ciki

    Rediyon yaɗa labarai: FM + RDS da AM (na gwaji)

    Tare da SDR (misali RTL-SDR dongle) qFT8 kuma rediyon yaɗa labarai ne. A shafin SWL na mai zaɓen yanayi, taɓa FM + RDS ko yaɗa labarai na AM: allon rediyo yana buɗewa a saman manhajar, kuma kibiyar komawa tana rufe shi. Ba a taɓa yanayin aiki da TX ba: wannan allon yana karɓa ne kawai.

    • BINCIKA yana bincika band ɗin kuma yana lissafa tashoshin da za ka iya karɓa, tare da sunan tashar da RDS ke aikawa. Taɓa tasha don saurara; tauraro yana riƙe ta a jerin a tsakanin bincike. Kibiyoyi suna tafiya tasha ɗaya, kibiyoyi biyu suna zuwa tasha ta gaba a jerin.
    • Maɓallin sauti: FM stereo ko mono; a AM synchronous detector (ya fi tsayawa a lokacin fading), envelope detector na yau da kullum, ko synchronous a kan sideband ɗaya (USB / LSB, nesa da splatter na maƙwabci).
    • 50 µs / 75 µs: de-emphasis na FM. qFT8 yana zaɓarsa daga locator ɗinka (75 µs a Amurkawa); taɓa don canza shi.
    • REC yana rikodin tashar da kake ji zuwa fayil ɗin sauti; RECORDINGS yana lissafa su don kunnawa, rabawa ko gogewa. Rikodin suna zama a wannan na'ura.

    Yayin da allon rediyo yake buɗe yana amfani da SDR; sauran yanayi suna dawo da shi idan ka rufe shi. Rediyo ba tare da fitar da I/Q ba (mafi yawan transceivers) ba za a iya amfani da shi da wannan allon ba.

    ↑ Koma zuwa Abubuwan da ke ciki

    Rediyon yaɗa labarai na dijital: DAB da DAB+ (na gwaji)

    Tare da SDR da ke kunna Band III (174-240 MHz; dongle na RTL-SDR yana iyawa) qFT8 yana karɓar rediyon dijital. A shafin SWL na mai zaɓar yanayi, taɓa DAB / DAB+. qFT8 yana kunna tashar da kake kai na ƙarshe (karon farko: 12C) kuma, da zarar an ji ensemble, yana jera ayyukansa da sunayensu kuma yana kunna na farko. Wannan allon yana karɓa ne kawai.

    • The list shows every service heard, with its channel (7D, 12C ...) and DAB or DAB+. Tap one to listen. BINCIKA visits all the Band III channels and lists the services of every ensemble found. The arrows go to the next channel; the double arrows to the next service of the list.
    • Ana kunnawa yanzu: sunan sabis, tashar, sunan ensemble da adadin bit, da rubutun da tashar ke aikawa tare da kiɗa (sunayen waƙoƙi, labarai).

    Sauti: DAB koyaushe, DAB+ ta hanyar mai fassara na tsarinka

    Tashoshin DAB (tsohon tsari) suna kunnawa a ko'ina: qFT8 yana fassara sautinsu da kansa. Tashoshin DAB+ suna aika sautin AAC, tsari mai haƙƙin mallaka wanda qFT8 bai ƙunsa ba. qFT8 yana kunna shi ta hanyar mai fassara da ke kan na'urarka tuni:

    • Android: mai fassara na wayar kanta. Wayoyi masu Android 9 ko sabo suna da dukkan nau'o'in; tsohuwar waya na iya rasa ɗaya (misali HE-AAC v2), kuma qFT8 yana faɗin haka a allo: jerin ayyuka da rubutu suna ci gaba da aiki.
    • Linux: install fdk-aac (recommended: it plays every DAB+ station in full) or FFmpeg. When neither is there, the radio screen says so and offers SHIGAR: you choose the package, and your system asks for your password before anything is installed. FFmpeg up to version 7.0 plays DAB+ without its high frequencies (it lacks one part of the format for broadcast); qFT8 says so and recommends fdk-aac.
    • Windows da macOS: ɗakunan karatu iri ɗaya kamar a Linux, idan ka shigar da ɗaya (fdk-aac ko FFmpeg). qFT8 kuma yana gwada mai fassara na tsarin kansa (Windows Media Foundation, macOS AudioToolbox), amma a yau babu ɗaya da ke karɓar tsawon firam da DAB+ ke amfani da shi, don haka don DAB+ har yanzu kana buƙatar ɗaya daga cikin ɗakunan karatu. Suna fassara xHE-AAC (wanda tashoshin DRM ke amfani da shi): Windows 11 sigar 22H2 ko sabo, macOS 11 ko sabo.
    • iPhone: tashoshin DAB suna kunnawa. Mai fassara na iPhone kansa bai karɓi sautin DAB+ ba tukuna: DAB+ yana nuna ayyuka da rubutu, kuma layi ɗaya yana faɗin wayar ba ta da mai fassara don wannan sautin. xHE-AAC (DRM) yana kunnawa ta hanyarsa.

    A kwamfuta mai fassara yana aiki a cikin ƙaramin shiri daban wanda qFT8 ke farawa da kansa. Idan ɗakin karatu na mai fassara ya faɗi ko ya daina amsawa, wannan shirin kaɗai ke tsayawa: allon rediyo yana faɗin ana sake farawa mai fassara, jerin tashoshi da rubutu suna ci gaba da aiki, kuma sauti yana dawowa da kansa.

    Shigar da mai fassara da hannu (Linux)

    Rarraba Debian, Ubuntu, Mint
    Recommended sudo apt install libfdk-aac2
    Or sudo apt install ffmpeg
    Lura libfdk-aac2 yana cikin sassan non-free na Debian da multiverse na Ubuntu: kunna su da farko.
    Distribution Fedora
    Recommended sudo dnf install fdk-aac
    Or sudo dnf install ffmpeg
    Lura Dukansu daga ma'ajiyar RPM Fusion (kunna su da farko).
    Distribution openSUSE
    Recommended sudo zypper install libfdk-aac2
    Or sudo zypper install ffmpeg
    Lura Cikakkun sigogi suna fitowa daga ma'ajiyar Packman.
    Rarraba Arch, Manjaro
    Recommended sudo pacman -S libfdk-aac
    Or sudo pacman -S ffmpeg

    Bayan shigarwa, rufe allon rediyo kuma sake buɗe shi (ko jira ɗan daƙiƙoƙi): qFT8 yana samun mai fassara da kansa.

    ↑ Back to Table of Contents

    Rediyon dijital a gajeren zango da matsakaicin zango: DRM da DRM+ (na gwaji)

    A mai zaɓar yanayi, shafin SWL, zaɓi DRM / DRM+. qFT8 yana kunna mitar da kake kai na ƙarshe (karon farko: 11690 kHz) kuma yana samun siginar DRM da kansa: yanayin ƙarfi (A, B, C, D, ko E don DRM+), faɗin tasha, kuskuren kunnawa da kuskuren agogo. A cikin ɗan daƙiƙoƙi ayyukan tashar (har zuwa huɗu) suna shiga jerin da sunayensu, kuma saƙonnin rubutu da tashar ke aikawa suna bayyana a ƙarƙashin suna. Layin launin toka yana nuna mita, yanayin ƙarfi (misali DRM-B) da tsarin sauti da adadin bit.

    • Mai karɓa: any receiver with I/Q output that tunes the band: a KiwiSDR (its 12 kHz I/Q is enough for the usual 10 kHz DRM channels) or an SDR on long, medium and short wave; DRM+ (100 kHz channels in 87.5-108 MHz) needs an SDR. The range button cycles LW, MW, SW and DRM+. Type or step to the station's frequency; there is no band scan for DRM yet.
    • Sauti: tashoshin DRM suna aika sautin AAC ko xHE-AAC a cikin tsarin naɗewa nasu. A kwamfuta yana kunnawa idan an shigar da ɗakin karatu na fdk-aac (duba sashen mai fassara: ɗakin karatu iri ɗaya da maɓallin SHIGAR kamar na DAB+; FFmpeg ba ya fassara tsarin naɗewa na DRM). Wayoyi, iPhone da masu fassara na tsarin kansu a Windows da macOS ba sa karɓar shi: tuta tana faɗin babu mai fassara don sautin wannan tashar, kuma jerin ayyuka da rubutu suna ci gaba da aiki.
    • Karɓa kawai: qFT8 ba ya taɓa aikawa a waɗannan mitoci.

    Amfani da qFT8 don SWL (saurare)

    Ba kwa buƙatar lasisi ko na'urar aikawa don amfani da qFT8. A matsayinka na mai sauraron gajeren zango (da VHF/UHF), SWL, za ka iya karɓa da fassara kowane yanayi da qFT8 ya sani, ka ga tashoshin da kake ji a kan Taswirar duniya, kuma ka riƙe rajistar QSO da kake ji tsakanin wasu tashoshi, a shirye don aikawa a matsayin rahotannin SWL. qFT8 na masu sha'awar rediyo ne kuma na SWL: manhaja ɗaya, a waya, a kwamfuta, a iPhone da kuma a na'urar sarrafa ta yanar gizo.

    Abin da za ka iya karɓa da fassara

    • Kowane yanayin karɓa na qFT8: FT8, FT4, FT2, LQ8, JS8, WSPR, FST4, FST4W, Q65, MSK144, JT65, JT9, JT4, CW (Morse), RTTY, PSK31 / PSK63, Olivia, MFSK, DominoEX, THOR, Contestia, MT63, Throb, IFKP, FSQ, hotunan SSTV, da muryar SSB don saurare. Zaɓi yanayin a cikin zaɓin yanayi kamar yadda aka saba; sashen kowane yanayi a cikin wannan littafin yana bayyana allonsa.
    • Ta kowane mai karɓa da qFT8 zai iya amfani da shi: SDR mai karɓa kawai (duba Na'urori don saurare), ko rediyo na yau da kullum da aka haɗa kamar yadda aka saba. SDR yana ba qFT8 sautin da rediyo a cikin babban (ko ƙaramin) gefen mitar zai bayar, don haka kowane yanayi yana aiki a kansa daidai kamar a rediyo.
    • A kowane maɗaurin mita da mai karɓa ya rufe: maɓallan maɗaurin mita suna daidaita mai karɓa; SDR kamar RTL-SDR yana kuma kai wa mitocin yanayin dijital na 2 m da 70 cm.
    • Spots da taswira: ana lissafa kowace tasha da aka fassara a shafin Tashoshi kuma ana zana ta a kan Taswirar duniya tare da nisanta da alkiblarta, kamar lokacin da kake aikawa.

    SDR ba ya taɓa aikawa a qFT8: yayin da ɗaya ke zama mai karɓa, maɓallin TX yana karanta NO TX / SDR kuma babu abin da zai iya kunna aikawa. Mai karɓa da ke kunna rediyo (VHF na teku, masu karɓar sama da na sigina) shi ma yana daina aikawa yayin da yake riƙe rediyo: maɓallin yana karanta NO TX / RX kuma TX yana zama OFF bayan haka har sai ka danna TX ON. Tare da rediyon da ke iya aikawa, kunna yanayin SWL kuma qFT8 ma ba zai aika ba.

    Na'urori don saurare

    Zaɓi mai karɓa a cikin Saituna > Na'ura. Layukan SDR suna da nasu rukuni a cikin jerin; a ƙarƙashin layin da ka zaɓa, qFT8 yana nuna ƴan saituna da wannan mai karɓa yake buƙata da layin matsayi (“Ana haɗawa da ...”, “An haɗa da ... (2.4 MS/s)”, ko dalilin da yasa ba a haɗe ba).

    Dongle na RTL-SDR (RTL-SDR Blog V3, V4, V4 Lite da sauran dongle masu tuner na R820T / R820T2 / R828D) da aka haɗa ta USB
