    Ba a tallafawa: masu karɓa na I/Q na katin sauti (SoftRock, ko yanayin I/Q na QMX+: yi amfani da QMX+ a matsayin rediyo na yau da kullum, yana aiki nan take ta USB), da dongle na RTL masu wasu tuner (E4000, FC0012, FC0013, FC2580).

    Dongle na RTL-SDR a waya ko kwamfuta

    Waya ko kwamfutar hannu (Android): haɗa dongle da adaftar ko kebul na USB OTG. Android za ta ba da shawarar buɗe qFT8; ba da izinin USB. Idan Saituna > Na'ura yana kan AUTO kuma babu rediyo da aka haɗa, qFT8 yana amfani da dongle da kansa (“AUTO - SDR: RTL-SDR (USB)”); rediyo da aka haɗa tare da shi ita ce ke gaba. Cire dongle sannan a sake haɗa shi zai sake ɗauko shi. Dongle yana ɗaukar wuta daga waya: gajeren kebul da baturi mai caji suna taimakawa.

    Babu abin da za a shigar, a kowane dongle. qFT8 yana da nasa direba don dongle, a cikin manhajar: yana gane RTL-SDR Blog V4 kuma yana amfani da na'urar sauya mita ta HF da sauya shigarwa da ke ciki, don haka HF yana aiki a kan V4 ba tare da wata saitin ba. A kan V3 (ko wani dongle ba tare da mai sauya mita ba) yi alama a kan HF ba tare da mai canzawa ba (ɗaukar samfur kai tsaye) don karɓa ƙasa da 24 MHz a shigarwarsa ta HF. qFT8 ba ya taɓa kunna bias tee na dongle (wutar eriya).

    Kwamfuta, Linux: layi ɗaya, direba ɗaya da ke ciki. qFT8 yana ɗaukar dongle daga direban TV da tsarin ke loda masa da kansa. Mai amfani da kai yana buƙatar izinin buɗe dongle; idan layin matsayi ya ce “babu izini”, ƙara fayil ɗin ƙa'idodi sau ɗaya (misali /etc/udev/rules.d/20-rtlsdr.rules) da layin SUBSYSTEM=="usb", ATTRS{idVendor}=="0bda", ATTRS{idProduct}=="2838", MODE="0666" (2832 ga wasu tsofaffin dongle), aiwatar da sudo udevadm control --reload-rules sannan a sake haɗa dongle.

    Kwamfuta, Windows da macOS: qFT8 ba zai iya buɗe dongle kai tsaye ba tukuna. Gudanar da sabar sadarwa ta RTL-SDR a kan kwamfuta ɗaya (ko a kowace wata, kamar Raspberry Pi) sannan ka zaɓi SDR: RTL-SDR (network) da adireshin 127.0.0.1, ko ka bayar da dongle daga waya ko kwamfutar Linux da qFT8 bridge.

    Sabar sadarwa ta RTL-SDR da direbanta

    Sabar sadarwa (tashar 1234) tana zuwa tare da kunshin direban RTL-SDR na tsarinka. Wannan kunshin direba ne daban, ba na qFT8 ba, kuma sigarsa tana da muhimmanci:

    • RTL-SDR Blog V4: yana buƙatar direba da ya san V4. Shigar da sigar direban da mai yin dongle ya wallafa (RTL-SDR Blog, duba jagorar farawa ta V4); wannan ita ce muke amfani da ita da V4. Tsohon direba, kamar yadda wasu rabe-raben Linux da tsofaffin saukarwar Windows har yanzu suke kawowa, yana samun dongle amma bai san yadda aka haɗa tuner ɗinsa ba da mai sauya mitarsa: mitoci ba daidai ba ne ko babu abin da ake karɓa, kuma HF ba ya aiki.
    • RTL-SDR Blog V3 da dongle na gama-gari R820T / R820T2: kowace sigar direba tana aiki. Don HF yi alama a kan HF ba tare da mai canzawa ba (ɗaukar samfur kai tsaye); qFT8 yana neman sabar ta mayar da dongle zuwa shigarwarsa ta HF.
    • Linux: tsarin yana loda direban TV don dongle, wanda ke hana sabar sadarwa buɗe shi. Sababbin kunshin direba suna cire shi da kansu; in ba haka ba, toshe shi (na'urar dvb_usb_rtl28xxu) sannan a sake haɗa dongle. Ƙa'idar izini iri ɗaya da ta sama.
    • Windows: dole dongle ya yi amfani da direban gama-gari na WinUSB maimakon direban TV da Windows ke shigarwa; jagorar farawa ta mai yin dongle tana nuna yadda.
    • Raspberry Pi: wuri mai kyau ga sabar kusa da eriya; yi amfani da sigar direba ta RTL-SDR Blog don V4.

    Settings for the network row: Adireshi, Tashar haɗi, Saurin ɗaukar samfur (2.4 MS/s by default; if the status line says the data arrives in bursts, tap it to use the highest rate), Ƙarfafawa, Gyaran mitar (ppm) and HF ba tare da mai canzawa ba (ɗaukar samfur kai tsaye) and, for a server that can switch it, Bias tee (wuta a mashigar eriya; RTL-SDR Blog V3/V4). Any server that speaks the same RTL-SDR network protocol works too, including those made for other SDRs.

    Wasu SDR

    • KiwiSDR: babu abin da za a shigar. Rubuta adireshin mai karɓa (tashar 8073 a asali). qFT8 yana amfani da ɗaya daga cikin tashoshinsa, yana nuna kansa a matsayin qFT8 a jerin masu saurare, kuma yana bin iyakokin mai shi: idan duk tashoshi suna da aiki, yana sake gwadawa bayan mintuna 2; idan mai karɓa ya ƙi (kalmar sirri, haɗi ɗaya a kowane adireshi, iyakar lokaci) layin matsayi yana bayar da dalilinsa kuma qFT8 yana jira mintuna 30 ko har sai ka canza adireshin. Masu karɓa na jama'a ana raba su: ka bar su idan ka gama.
    • Hermes-Lite 2 / OpenHPSDR: bar adireshin a fanko don nemo rediyon a cikin hanyar sadarwarka. Rediyon yana bauta wa manhaja ɗaya a lokaci guda: da farko rufe duk wata manhaja da ke amfani da shi. qFT8 yana karɓa ta cikinsa kawai.
    • Airspy (da RTL-SDR) a tashar 5555: gudanar da sabar sadarwar mai karɓa a kwamfutar da aka haɗa shi da ita, tare da direban mai karɓar da aka shigar. Sabar tana zaɓar saurin ɗaukar samfur da ƙarfafawa; idan wani mai saurare yana sarrafa shi, qFT8 zai iya saurare ne kawai a inda aka daidaita shi.
    • SDRplay, LimeSDR, ADALM-Pluto, HackRF da sauransu: a kwamfutar da aka haɗa mai karɓa da ita, shigar da direban mai yin na'urar (don SDRplay API ɗinsa), sabar mai masaukin SDR ta nesa (tashar 55132) da na'urar ta don wannan mai karɓa. qFT8 yana amfani da mai karɓa na farko da sabar ta bayar. Sabar tana zaɓar gudu da ƙarfafawa.
    • Sababbin layukan SDR na gwaji (dukansu karɓa kawai; maɓallin TX yana karanta NO TX / SDR): kowanne yana nuna saitunansa kawai da layin taimako. Airspy HF+, Airspy R2 / Mini da HackRF ana buɗe su ta USB (wayar tana neman izini idan ka saka su; a kwamfuta, Linux kawai a yanzu). ADALM-Pluto ana karanta shi ta hanyar haɗinsa na hanyar sadarwa-ta-USB. Don layin I/Q na katin sauti, rubuta mitar tsakiya ta mai karɓan kansa a kHz kuma ka duba Musanya I da Q idan tashoshinsa biyu suna a juye. Don yarjejeniya 2 ta OpenHPSDR bar adireshin babu komai don samun rediyo. I/Q na TCI yana amfani da tashar jiragen ruwa 50001. Layin RTP na multicast yana buƙatar sabar mai karɓa a hanyar sadarwarka ta kanka da ke yin tasha bisa buƙata. Mai karɓa na yanar gizo a tashar jiragen ruwa 8073 ana sauraronsa cikin bandinsa ba tare da canza kunnawarsa ga sauran masu sauraro ba; wanda ke da aiki ba a sake tambayarsa har minti 2. An gina waɗannan daga bayanan yarjejeniya da aka buga kuma an duba su da sabobin kwaikwayo, don haka don Allah ka ba da rahoton yadda suke aiki da naka.

    Rediyo na yau da kullum

    Duk rediyon da qFT8 ke tallafawa kyakkyawan mai karɓa ne ma: haɗa shi kamar yadda aka bayyana a Saiti cikin Sauri (QMX, QMX+ ko IC-7300, misali, da kebul ɗaya na USB). qFT8 yana saita mitarsa ta CAT idan ka danna maɓallin maɗaurin mita. Kunna yanayin SWL don kada ya taɓa aikawa.

    Bias tee

    The device setup of an SDR whose driver can switch it (the RTL-SDR on this device's USB, RTL-SDR Blog V3 / V4, and an RTL-SDR network server) has the check box Bias tee (wuta a mashigar eriya; RTL-SDR Blog V3/V4), off by default (most other dongles have no bias tee). It puts DC voltage (about 4.5 V) on the antenna connector, for an LNA, a converter or another device made to be powered over the coax. Turning it on first shows a red warning that it can damage the SDR, the antenna or anything connected (a radio, a filter, a splitter, a DC-shorted antenna); type yes in its field, then KUNNA (CANCEL leaves it off). The web remote can only switch it off. qFT8 never switches it on by itself and switches it off whenever the SDR is closed.

    Yanayin SWL da rajistar abubuwan da aka ji

    YANAYIN SWL (SAURARE KAWAI), a ƙarshen Saituna > Taruka, yana tambayar lambar shaidarka ta SWL (misali R4-123-456) ko alamar kiranka. Yayin da yake kunne, qFT8 ba ya aikawa har abada (maɓallin TX yana nuna NO TX / SWL; TX, TUNE da kowane yanayi sun ƙi aikawa), kuma ba a taɓa rajistar QSO naka ba. Cikakkun bayanai suna cikin Abubuwan da ake tallafawa.

    Rajistar abubuwan da aka ji tana tattara QSO da kake ji tsakanin wasu tashoshi biyu:

    • Duk abin da aka karɓa ta SDR (ko ta qFT8 bridge da ke bayar da ɗaya) yana shiga cikin rajistar abubuwan da aka ji da kansa, ko ba tare da yanayin SWL ba. Da rediyo da ke iya aikawa, kawai yayin da yanayin SWL yake kunne.
    • A cikin FT8, FT4, LQ8 da sauran yanayin da ke da lokaci (har da JT65, JT9) QSO yana ƙidayuwa da zarar an ji tabbatarwarsa (RR73, RRR, ko 73 bayan rahoton amincewa). A cikin CW, RTTY, PSK da Olivia / MFSK yana ƙidayuwa idan an kwafi “A DE B” da “B DE A” duka, tare da rahotanni idan an aika su.
    • Kowane QSO da aka ji yana riƙe da alamomin kira biyu, lokaci, maɗaurin mita, mita, yanayi, rahotanni biyu, grids, duk wani nassi na shirin da aka ji (taron tsauni na SOTA, wurin shakatawa na POTA, RAZA ...) da mai karɓar da ya shigo ta cikinsa (a ƙarshen layinsa).
    • Jerin shine shafin SWL na shafin Log: canza jerin zaɓuɓɓuka na QSO / SWL a samansa zuwa SWL. Yana da sandar tacewa iri ɗaya kamar shafin QSO, kuma taɓa shigarwa yana gyara ko share ta (babu abin da ake taɓa sharewa a eQSL). Sarrafawa daga nesa ta yanar gizo ma tana nuna shi.
    • Log ɗin yana riƙe kowace shigarwa (babu iyaka) a cikin fayil da ake ƙara masa kawai, don haka log mai tsawo sosai ba ya rage saurin farawar aikace-aikacen; shafin yana karanta sabbin shigarwa kawai kuma yana nuna ƙari yayin da kake gungurawa.
    • Idan fasalolin Ham a kashe, kowace tashar FT8, FT4, LQ ... da aka ji ana rubuta ta a log (sau ɗaya a awa ga kowace tasha da yanayi); idan fasalolin Ham na kunne, cikakkun QSO da aka ji kawai ake rubutawa. Tashar da ka ji a karon farko (ba ta taɓa kasancewa a log ɗinka na SWL a baya ba) tana da alamar kiranta da launin shuɗi-kore a jerin Tashoshi idan fasalolin Ham a kashe.
    • Masu karɓar sigina suna rubuta abin da suka gane a log, sau ɗaya ga kowace tasha a kowace rana ta UTC: jiragen ruwa (AIS), jiragen sama (ADS-B, UAT), radiosondes, tashoshin NAVTEX, DSC, tashoshin lokaci, fitilun NCDXF, alamomin kira na ALE / ARDOP / PACTOR da APRS, hotunan faks na yanayi da na tauraron ɗan adam na yanayi, da tashoshin FM ta lambar RDS da suna. Suna ƙidaya don lambobin yabo na SWL; ba a taɓa aika su zuwa eQSL ba kuma ba sa cikin fitarwar ADIF (CSV yana da su, tare da sunan da aka ji a shafi na ƙarshe).
    • Lodawa tana bin doka ɗaya: asusun SWL na eQSL yana ajiye kowace tashar da aka ji ƙarƙashin wannan asusun (ana ƙin asusun eQSL na mai son rediyo); PSK Reporter da WSPRnet suna ba da rahoto ƙarƙashin alamar kiranka, idan ba haka ba ƙarƙashin id ɗinka na SWL.
    • Someone else's SDR: when you select a network receiver at an internet address (a public KiwiSDR or web receiver), qFT8 asks once Shin wannan SDR naku ne? (YES / NO). Receiving never waits for the answer. Until you answer YES, what you hear through it is marked remote (at the end of its line): it stays in the log, but the SWL awards do not count it (the awards page says how many were left out). The answer lasts until you change the receiver; a receiver on USB, on your own network or the radio itself is never asked. A tap on an entry and on its Mai karɓa na wa row changes it.
    • Your own network receiver: a receiver at an address on your own network is yours; one reached through an internet address shows the box Wannan sabar tawa ce in its rows, the same answer as Shin wannan SDR naku ne?, kept until you change the receiver. On your own web receiver server, when you move the dial outside the band it is on, qFT8 switches to the server's profile for that band: a profile it has seen cover the frequency, or one whose name says the band ("20m", "14 MHz"). A profile change moves the band for everyone listening and restarts the server's services, so qFT8 asks once for that receiver, never changes it when you connect, and waits at least a minute between changes. The own spy-type server (when it would move the device) and the receiver of your SDR program over TCI ask once the same way. Someone else's receiver is never moved: it just says the band it covers.

    Fitarwa: ADIF, CSV, SOTA da RAZA SWL

    • FITAR DA ADIF: rikodi ɗaya ga kowace tasha da aka ji, biyu ga kowane QSO, yadda ake aika rahotannin da katunan SWL: SWL=Y, lambar shaidarka a matsayin mai aiki, tashar da aka ji a CALL tare da rahotonka, tashar da take aiki da ita a saƙon katin “Heard working ...” da bayanin, nassoshin shirin (SOTA_REF, POTA_REF, SIG / SIG_INFO) da mai karɓa a MY_RIG.
    • FITAR DA CSV: layi ɗaya ga kowane QSO, mai karɓa a ginshiƙi na ƙarshe.
    • Every SWL export has the box Fitar da shigarwar da aka ji a masu karɓa na kaina kawai (masu inganci don lambar yabo), ticked when it opens: entries heard on someone else's SDR are left out. Untick it to export them too: in ADIF they say so (MY_RIG ends in “(remote)”, APP_QFT8_RX_REMOTE=Y, and the remote receiver's grid and location in MY_GRIDSQUARE / MY_CITY when it publishes them), and importing that file into qFT8 keeps them marked.
    • FITAR DA SOTA SWL (CSV): yana bayyana idan ka ji masu kunnawa a kan tsauni. Layin mai farauta ɗaya ga kowane mai kunnawa da aka ji, tare da tsaunin, a tsarin da bayanan SOTA ke karɓa; ɗora shi a can a matsayin rajistar SWL.
    • FITAR DA RAZA SWL (CSV): yana bayyana idan ka ji masu kunnawa na RAZA. Layi ɗaya ga kowane mai kunnawa tare da abin da za a aika masa (alamar kira, kwanan wata, lokaci, maɗaurin mita, yanayi, rahotonka, tashar da ya yi aiki da ita, nassinsa da lambar shaidarka), misali a matsayin katin SWL na lantarki a HAMLOG.online.
    • Share log ɗin SWL (Saituna > Fayilolin log) yana share jerin daga wannan na'urar bayan tambaya kuma bayan ka rubuta yes; log ɗinka na QSO ba a taɓa shi ba, kuma babu abin da ake sharewa a eQSL ko kowane sabis na kan layi. Yana kuma sake saita lambobin yabonka na SWL, don haka fitar da log ɗin da farko.

    Log ɗin SWL a Saituna, da eQSL

    • Saituna > Fayilolin log tana da maɓallai huɗu: SHIGO DA LOG ƊIN QSO da FITAR DA LOG ƊIN QSO (QSO ɗinka na kanka) da SHIGO DA LOG ƊIN SWL da FITAR DA LOG ƊIN SWL (log ɗin abin da aka ji). FITAR DA LOG ƊIN SWL yana bayar da fayilolin da ke sama. SHIGO DA LOG ƊIN SWL yana karanta fayil na ADIF kuma yana ɗaukar rikodin SWL ɗinsa kawai (SWL=Y, tsarin da sauran log da sabis na SWL ke amfani da shi) zuwa log ɗin abin da aka ji; rikodin guda biyu na QSO ɗaya da aka ji suna sake zama shigarwa ɗaya, shigarwar da suke nan tuni ba a ninka su, kuma kowane sauran rikodi ana tsallake shi (ba ya shiga log ɗin QSO naka har abada). Na’urar nesa ta yanar gizo ma tana bayar da FITAR DA LOG ƊIN SWL, ana sauke shi a mashigar bincike.
    • Daidaita SWL, right below Daidaita QSO in Settings: the row Daidaita SWL da eQSL works like the QSO syncing rows. eQSL is the online service with a real SWL account (an account whose profile is set to SWL; its cards read “Confirming SWL Reception”). Tap the gear to open the account's page: type that account's user name and password (not a ham account's) and tap TABBATAR. Once eQSL accepts them, the box can be ticked: every heard QSO is sent as one card per station heard, as it is logged, and the ones already heard are sent at once. Untick it to stop sending; LODA YANZU on the page sends what is waiting. A light beside the row shows green while sending, amber while switched off and red when eQSL refused; no light means it is not set up. Nothing is ever deleted on eQSL. Those cards are what many SWL award programmes accept.
    • Sauran sabis na littafin log (LoTW, QRZ, Club Log, ...) suna karɓar QSO na bangarori biyu kawai, don haka ba a taɓa aika musu QSO da aka ji ba. SOTA da RAZA suna karɓar rahotannin SWL ta fayilolinsu na kansu (a sama), waɗanda kai ne kake lodawa ko aikawa.

    Mataki-mataki

    1. Samo mai karɓa: RTL-SDR Blog V4 da eriyarsa ita ce farawa mafi sauƙi (daga HF zuwa UHF, babu buƙatar direba a waya ko Linux); idan ba ka da kayan aiki kwata-kwata, yi amfani da KiwiSDR na jama'a.
    2. Zaɓe shi a cikin Saituna > Na'ura (ko ka haɗa dongle da Na'ura a kan AUTO) kuma ka jira “An haɗa” a layin matsayinsa.
    3. Zaɓi yanayi da maɗaurin mita: FT8 a 20 m ko 40 m yana da aiki kusan a kowane lokaci kuma yana cika taswira cikin minti ɗaya.
    4. Kunna YANAYIN SWL (SAURARE KAWAI) idan kana son lambar shaidarka ta SWL a cikin fitarwar (da rediyo da ke iya aikawa, haka ma don a ci gaba da TX a kashe).
    5. Fitar da rajistar abubuwan da aka ji idan kana son aika rahotanni.

    Waya: dongle na USB ko kowane mai karɓa na hanyar sadarwa. Kwamfuta: dongle na USB a Linux, masu karɓa na hanyar sadarwa a ko'ina. iPhone / iPad: masu karɓa na hanyar sadarwa, ko mai karɓa da qFT8 bridge ke bayarwa (iPhone ba shi da mai masaukin USB don dongle). Sarrafawa daga nesa ta yanar gizo: yana nuna yanayin NO TX / SDR na mai karɓa da log ɗin SWL.

    Awards (experimental)

    qFT8 makes a diploma itself from your own logs: there is nothing to apply for. At the very top of Settings, tap the QSO button (with the Ham features on) or the SWL button (with SWL on) to open its page, Lambobin yabo na HAM or Lambobin yabo na SWL; each button shows a star and the stars you have earned. Settings > Features > Lambobin yabo turns them off (nothing is lost).

    • HAM: tebur ɗaya tare da yanayi zuwa ƙasa da bandodi a kwance; kowane yanayi da kowane bandi yana nuna taurarinsa goma, adadin tashoshinsa da manufa ta gaba, kuma kowace cell tana ƙara tauraro don tashoshi daban-daban (ana ƙidaya su ta asalin alamar kira) da ka yi aiki da su a wannan yanayi a wannan bandi. Ƙasashen DXCC da murabba'ai na grid suna da nasu akwatuna. Ana ƙidaya kowane QSO a littafin qFT8 naka, an tabbatar ko ba a tabbatar ba (ba a buƙatar LoTW ko QRZ), har da waɗanda aka shigo da su da LQ8, idan an rubuta shi da alamar kiranka a matsayin alamar kira ta tasha. Muddin shafin yana kan 0, layi a saman takardar shaidar yana faɗin dalili.
    • SWL: tashoshin masu son rediyo da ka ji (ta rukunin yanayi) da sauran ayyuka (yaɗa labarai, lokaci, fitilu, faks na yanayi, bayanan HF, teku, jiragen ruwa, jiragen sama), tare da ƙasashen DXCC da grid da aka ji. Log ɗinka na SWL da QSO naka duka suna ƙidaya, kowace tasha sau ɗaya.
    • The diploma shows your callsign (or SWL id), “has achieved N stars” and the tiles. A tap (a click on the computer) on the diploma opens it full screen: pinch or the mouse wheel zooms it, drag moves it, a double tap shows the whole page again; its exit and share buttons are at the top-right corner (the computer's diploma window also closes with Esc). SHARE, at the right end of the line above the diploma, sends it as a picture (the web remote downloads it). Daidaita alamar kirana daidai counts only QSOs logged with exactly your callsign (otherwise /P, /M and the like count too). A Harshen takardar shaida button picks the language of the diploma for viewing and sharing; Na asali na manhaja follows the app's language.
    • Inda: Settings yana buɗewa da gear, maɓalli na dama-dama na sandar ƙasa (a kwamfuta, gear ɗin da ke sandar ƙasa; a web remote, shafin gear); maɓallan QSO da SWL suna can sama-sama.
    • Rabawa: a Android, iPhone da iPad maɓallin raba yana buɗe takardar rabawa tare da difloma a matsayin hoto, a shirye don aikawa ko adanawa; a kwamfuta yana adana difloma a matsayin fayil na PNG a inda ka zaɓa; web remote yana sauke shi a matsayin fayil na PNG.

    Farawa ba tare da alamar kira ba

    The callsign card of the first start has a SWL KAWAI button and the line “No callsign? Press SWL ONLY to receive.” It asks for an optional SWL id (one button, CI GABA; an empty box means “SWL”) and warns when neither a callsign nor a locator is set (Settings > Time and location warns the same, and on the computer PICK ON MAP picks your locator on a world map). The welcome then says “You didn't enter a callsign, but I can guide you through the SWL features of qFT8.” and START TUTORIAL runs a short SWL tutorial. Without a callsign only the SWL features are on and the TX button reads NO CALLSIGN; to transmit, press it and then SAITA. The first time the callsign card is answered with SWL ONLY, the mode selector's experimental check box is switched on too, so the receivers are listed at once; after a first start with a callsign it stays off.

    Weather fax

    Layin faks na yanayi yana karɓar taswirorin faks na rediyo na HF na hukumomin yanayi da na sojojin ruwa ta kowace rediyon HF a USB, ko SDR. Maɓallin mitarsa yana bi ta watsa shirye-shiryen jadawalin faks na rediyo na duniya na jama'a (mitoci 70, tare da lokutan watsa su), yana tsallake waɗanda ba sa kan iska a wannan sa'a (UTC); ana kunna rediyo 1.9 kHz ƙasa da mitar da aka jera. Ana samun sauri (layuka 60, 90, 120 ko 240 a minti) daga layukan daidaitawa na taswirar, kuma taswira da ke kan iska tuni tana farawa da kanta bayan ɗan layuka; hoto yana ƙarewa a siginar tsayawa, ko da kansa idan taswirar ta zama hayaniya. Karɓa kawai.

    Bayanan murya ta dijital ta intanet

    Layukan bayanai na DMR, D-STAR, Fusion da M17 kuma na iya sauraro ta intanet (zaɓinsu na ƙarshe): wanda ke magana, zuwa wane talkgroup ko reflector, rubutunsu da wurinsu, daga ciyarwar ayyuka ta BrandMeister da reflectors masu buɗe (XLX, XRF, DCS, YSF, M17). Bayanai kawai kuma karɓa kawai: qFT8 ba ya taɓa aikawa cikin hanyar sadarwa, ba ya taɓa haɗa biyu daga cikinsu kuma ba ya taɓa kunna muryar DMR, D-STAR ko Fusion. Reflectors suna ganin ka a matsayin mai sauraro ƙarƙashin alamar kiranka ta kanka. Idan hanyar sadarwa ta yanke, tasha tana sake haɗawa da kanta cikin kusan daƙiƙa 15. Na gwaji.

    Saurare da doka

    • qFT8 yana karɓa ne kawai ta SDR; ba zai iya aikawa ta cikinsa ba.
    • Karɓar tashoshin masu sha'awar rediyo, na watsa labarai da na sigina lokaci ya halatta a yawancin ƙasashe. Ana iya karɓar wasu ayyukan rediyo a wasu ƙasashe amma ba a yi rikodi, ba a mika ba ko a yi amfani da su, kuma wasu ƙasashe kaɗan suna hana saurarar kanta. Ƙa'idojin ƙasar da kake saurare a ciki suna aiki: duba su kafin ka saurara a wajen maɗaurin masu sha'awar rediyo da na watsa labarai.
    • When your callsign or your location is in Germany, the United Kingdom, Belgium or Austria, qFT8 does not receive or decode aircraft data (ACARS and the like), AIS / VDES, ADS-B / UAT or weather sensors: their buttons stay in the list, in red, and say “Your country or your current location does not allow receiving or decoding this mode.” Pagers outside the amateur bands are not received anywhere. If your location is wrong, correct it with WURINA BA DAIDAI BA NE in the Signals pane.
    • Haka abin yake a Faransa, wanda ya haɗa da Corsica, sassan ƙetare (Gwadaluf, Martinik, Gini Ta Faransa, Rawuniyan, Mayoti) da ɓangaren Faransa na San Martin (alamomin kira F, TK, TM da prefix na ƙetare).
    • A wace ƙasa kuke? qFT8 yana gane ƙasarka daga mafi daidaiton abin da ya sani: da farko ainihin wurinka, sannan wurinka na kusan-kusan, sannan tsakiyar locator ɗinka ko alamar kiranka (ɗaya daga cikin biyun ƙarshe ya isa a sa takunkumi). Idan wannan ƙasar ba ta yarda da wani yanayi ba, akwatin magana A wace ƙasa kuke? yana cewa “Muna tsammanin kana …, inda ba a yarda a karɓa ko a fassara wannan yanayin ba.”, tare da jerin ƙasashe, mafi kusa da farko, sannan duka daga A zuwa Z. Ka canza ƙasar kawai idan qFT8 ya gane ta ba daidai ba: kai ne ke da alhakin bin dokar ƙasar da kake ciki. Idan ƙasar da ka zaɓa ma ba ta yarda da yanayin ba, akwatin magana yana faɗa maka. Ana tuna ƙasar da ka zaɓa (Saituna > LOKACI DA WURI, Ƙasa don dokokin karɓa) kuma za a sake tambaya ne kawai idan daga baya wurinka ya saɓa mata.
    • Kusa da iyaka qFT8 yana taka-tsantsan: idan wurinka na kusan-kusan zai iya kasancewa a cikin ƙasar da ba ta yarda da yanayin ba, yana tambaya. Idan ya gane ƙasar ba daidai ba, gyara ta a akwatin magana A wace ƙasa kuke? ko a Saituna.
    • A Birtaniya da Ireland mai karɓar TV yana nuna sanarwa, sau ɗaya a kowane zama, cewa ana buƙatar lasisin TV don kallo ko rikodin TV kai tsaye; yana ambaton ƙasar da kake ciki.
    • KiwiSDR na jama'a mallakin mai shi ne: bi iyakokin mai shi.
    ↑ Koma zuwa Abubuwan da ke ciki

    Abin da ke bayyana a taswira

    Idan qFT8 ya karɓi siginar da ke ɗauke da matsayi, yana zana mai aikawa a Taswirar duniya yayin da ake jin sa, yana motsa shi yayin da sababbin rahotanni ke shigowa, kuma yana barin shi ya ɓace a hankali idan ba a sake jin komai ba. Kowane iri yana da ƙaramin zanen sa da launin taswirar:

    • Jirage (ADS-B, 1090 MHz): jirgin sama da ake gani daga sama, yana nuni ta inda yake tashi. Taɓa shi don ganin lambar tashin jirgin, adireshi, tsayi, gudu, alkibla da lambar transponder (squawk). Jirage suna ɓacewa a hankali bayan minti ɗaya ba tare da rahoto ba kuma su ɓace gaba ɗaya bayan uku.
    • Jiragen ruwa (AIS, 162 MHz): jirgin ruwa da ake gani daga gefe, kullum a tsaye; yana kallon yamma idan jirgin yana tafiya yamma, kuma gabas in ba haka ba. Taɓa shi don ganin sunan jirgin, MMSI, nau'i, alkibla da gudu. Jiragen ruwa suna ɓacewa a hankali bayan mintuna 10 kuma su ɓace gaba ɗaya bayan 30.
    • Balan-balan na yanayi (radiosondes, 400-406 MHz): balan-balan tare da nauyin da yake ɗauke da shi da layi a bayansa da ke nuna inda ya taɓa wucewa. Taɓa shi don ganin tsayinsa, alkibla da gudu.
    • Tashoshi da abubuwan APRS: ginshiƙin eriya. Taɓa shi don ganin tsayin tashar, alkibla da gudu idan tana aika su.
    • Nodes na hanyar sadarwar mesh: siffar mai kusurwa shida da node a tsakiya.
    • Beacon, tashoshin tushe na AIS da kayan taimakon jagorar jirgi: zoben beacon da ake amfani da su a wasu wurare a qFT8.

    Ana nuna su da maɓallin SPOT na taswira: idan yana kunne za ka ga su tare da sauran spots, idan kuma yana a kashe ana ɓoye su. Kowane kati kuma yana faɗin lokacin da aka ji mai aikawa na ƙarshe, a wane mita, da kuma nisansa daga gare ka. Tashoshi da balan-balan suna zama a taswira har na tsawon awa ɗaya, suna dusashewa yayin da rahotonsu na ƙarshe ke tsufa.

    ↑ Koma zuwa Abubuwan da ke ciki

    Bayanin fasaloli

    Saituna da kayan aiki waɗanda ba su shafi yanayi ɗaya kaɗai ba: saituna biyu na kowane rediyo a TSARIN NA'URA, shafin yanar gizo domin sarrafa qFT8 daga wata na'ura, bridge wanda yake ba ka damar barin rediyo a wani ɗaki, da tsarin allo.

    Bambancin daidaitawar spectrogram

    A Saituna > TSARIN NA'URA, a ƙarƙashin Daidaitawa (wannan rediyo), Bambancin daidaitawar spectrogram yana matsar da abin da spectrogram yake nunawa har 300 Hz ƙasa ko sama. Asalinsa −100 Hz ne ga dangin QMX, −90 Hz ga IC-7300 da −50 Hz ga kowane rediyo; ƙimar da ka saita (har da 0) ana ajiye ta, daban ga kowane rediyo.

    Yawancin rediyo suna yanke ɓangaren ƙasa na sauti, don haka sigina a ɗaruruwan hertz na farko na spectrogram suna da rauni ko ba sa nan. Da bambanci na −100 Hz, misali, qFT8 tana daidaita rediyo 100 Hz ƙasa kuma tana matsar da sauti da adadi ɗaya, don haka waɗannan sigina suna komawa ɓangaren da rediyo yake ji da kyau.

    Babu wani abu daban da yake canzawa: mitar da ake nunawa, bambancin mitar tashoshi, spots, mitar watsawarka da log suna nan daidai kamar da. Allon rediyo kansa ne kawai yake nuna daban da adadin bambancin. Bambancin yana aiki a kowane yanayi kuma yana buƙatar haɗin CAT; da VOX ko lasifika ba ya yin komai.

    Iyakar watsawa ba tsayawa

    Wannan saitin tsaro ne ga mai watsawa. An gina ƙananan rediyo domin gajerun watsawa masu hutu: watsawar FT8 tana ɗaukar ƙasa da daƙiƙa 13, sannan a ƙalla daƙiƙa 15 na hutu. Wasu yanayi suna sa rediyo ya watsa da cikakken ƙarfi na dogon lokaci ba tare da hutu ba, kuma hakan yana iya sa amplifier na ƙarshe na ƙaramin rediyo ya yi zafi sosai. Iyakar watsawa ba tsayawa ita ce mafi tsawon lokacin da qFT8 take sa rediyon ka ya watsa a tafi ɗaya.

    Tana cikin Saituna > TSARIN NA'URA, a ƙarƙashin Daidaitawa (wannan rediyo). Zaɓuɓɓukan su ne daƙiƙa 20, 40, 60, 90, 120 da 150, da Babu iyaka. A ƙarƙashin kowane zaɓi, qFT8 tana jera abin da wannan zaɓi yake ƙarawa, domin ka ga abin da za ka samu idan ka ƙara ta. Ana ajiye saitin daban ga kowane rediyo.

    Na asali ga kowane rediyo

    • Daƙiƙa 60: QRPLabs QMX, QMX+ da QDX, da (tr)uSDX.
    • Daƙiƙa 120: Xiegu X6100 da X6200.
    • Babu iyaka: kowane sauran rediyo, da rediyon da ake amfani da shi ta VOX ko lasifika (qFT8 ba ta iya sanin wane rediyo ne).

    Waɗannan saitunan na asali na taka-tsantsan ne. Kai ne ka san rediyonka, saitin ƙarfinsa da sanyayarsa: canza iyakar idan sun yarda. qFT8 ba ta taɓa ƙara iyakar da kanta.

    Abin da iyakar take yi

    • Watsawar da aka san tsawonta, wadda ta fi iyakar tsawo, ba a fara ta. Shi ya sa WSPR da dogayen yanayin SSTV suke neman ka ƙara ta: watsawar WSPR tana ɗaukar daƙiƙa 111, kuma hotunan SSTV da yawa suna ɗaukar minti biyu ko fiye. Idan ka zaɓi irin wannan yanayi, ko ka danna TX ON a cikinsa, saƙo yana gaya maka daƙiƙa nawa yake buƙata da iyakar rediyonka, tare da maɓallin Buɗe saitin. Ba a shirya watsawa har sai iyakar ta yarda da yanayin.
    • Watsawar da ba ta da tsayayyen tsawo tana tsayawa idan aka kai iyakar, kuma rediyo yana komawa karɓa: TUNE, SSB (maɓallin PTT, VOX da ma'ajiyoyin murya) da RTTY. Sanarwa tana cewa an kai iyakar.
    • Ba a taƙaita CW: maɓallin yana ƙasa na wani ɓangare na lokaci ne kawai.

    QMX da WSPR: Da QMX, QMX+ ko QDX, WSPR yana buƙatar a ƙara iyakar daga daƙiƙa 60 zuwa 120. Domin tsaron rediyo, qFT8 tana neman ka yi haka sau ɗaya, da hannu. Yi la'akari da rage ƙarfin rediyo ga WSPR: yanayi ne na ƙaramin ƙarfi tun asali.

    Sabar Yanar Gizo Ta Kula Daga Nesa

    qFT8 tana da sabar HTTP a cikinta, wadda take ba ka damar duba da sarrafa manhaja daga kowane web browser a network ɗinka. Wannan yana da amfani musamman domin aiki daga nesa ko a matsayin allo na biyu.

    A karon farko da sarrafawa daga nesa ta yanar gizo ta fara a Windows, Windows tana tambaya ko za a bar Java runtime (wanda aka nuna a matsayin "OpenJDK Platform binary") ta wuce firewall. Ka bari a kan hanyar sadarwa ta sirri don sauran na'urori su iya isa shafin.

    Shafin yana kama da manhaja kanta kuma yana aiki kamar ta, kuma yana cika tagar browser a kowane girma. A taga mai faɗi, spectrogram yana hagu, allon da aka zaɓa kuma a dama; a taga mai tsayi, kamar wayar da aka riƙe a tsaye, spectrogram yana sama. Sandar da ke ƙasa tana da kayan sarrafawa iri ɗaya da na manhaja: maɓallin TX, saƙo, jeri, yanayi da zango, da maɓallan tashoshi, log na QSO, taswirar duniya da saituna. Waterfall yana motsi kai tsaye, da rubutu iri ɗaya da na manhaja. Wannan shi ne kallon tashoshi a taga mai tsayi:

    Yana ba da damar aiki da maɓallin AUTO, jerin zaɓi na sharuɗɗa, maɓallin + domin ƙara alamomin kira kai tsaye, da jerin tashoshi masu maɓallan CALL domin kiran su da hannu. Ƙaramin haske a ƙarshen dama na sandar kore ne muddin shafin yana haɗe da qFT8.

    Kuma wannan shi ne kallon log na QSO:

    Wannan kallo yana nuna QSO na ƙarshe a log. Sauran maɓallan biyu suna buɗe:

    • Taswirar duniya, kamar a manhaja. Maɓallinta na cikakken allo yana ba taswira dukkan taga; wannan maɓalli ko Esc yana dawo da sauran sassan.
    • The page's settings (the gear): your callsign, locator and CQ modifier as the app has them (shown, not changed from the page), the time offset in steps of 100 ms, how often the app sends the waterfall, and Kashi huɗu, which shows the spectrogram, the stations, the world map and the QSO log all at once. Quadrants is remembered by each browser. Bar allon a kunne keeps the phone's or tablet's screen from turning off while the page is shown (browsers allow it only on the page's secure address, https).

    Ana iya buɗe browser biyu a kan qFT8 ɗaya a lokaci guda; abin da ka yi a ɗaya yana bayyana a ɗayan a cikin daƙiƙa ɗaya. Fasalin da aka kashe a manhaja (duba Tsarin allo) ana ɓoye shi a shafin ma. Shafin ba ya ɗauko komai daga intanet: komai yana zuwa daga qFT8.

    While the page plays the radio's audio (Saurara, or Kullum kunna sautin rediyo), it keeps receiving it when the browser is in the background or the screen turns off; the waterfall pauses meanwhile and catches up when you come back. Whether a browser keeps a page running with the screen off is the browser's choice: Chrome on Android does. Where a browser stops pages with the screen off, use Bar allon a kunne.

    Network & Haɗi

    Sabar tana buɗe wani port na TCP (na asali 13497) a wayarka ko kwamfutarka. Domin shiga, dole na'urorinka su iya "ganin" juna a network:

    • Network na gida: Wayar ko kwamfutar da take da qFT8 da na'urar sarrafawarka (kwamfuta, tablet, ko waya) dole su kasance a network ɗaya.
    • Saitunan router: Wasu router suna da fasalin da ake kira AP Isolation (ko Client Isolation) a kunne tun asali. Dole a kashe shi domin na'urori su iya sadarwa da juna.
    • Hotspot na waya: Idan kana waje, kana iya buɗe Wi-Fi hotspot a waya ɗaya (mai intanet) ka haɗa wayar qFT8 da wannan Wi-Fi. Bayan haɗawa, rufe kuma sake buɗe sabar yanar gizo a saitunan qFT8; adireshin IP da aka nuna zai zama ana iya shiga daga wayar da take ba da hotspot.
    • Shiga ta intanet: Shiga sabar daga wajen gidanka (ta intanet) yana buƙatar Port Forwarding a saitunan router ɗinka (haɗa IP da port na ciki da na waje). Lura cewa galibi wannan ba ya aiki a haɗin 5G/LTE saboda iyakokin NAT na kamfanin waya.

    Matakan Shiga

    Kana iya saita matakin tsaro na nesa a allon saituna:

    • Karatu kawai: Yana ba da damar ganin spectrogram kai tsaye, tashoshin da ke aiki, da log na QSO. Ba a iya yin komai.
    • Karanta kuma ka tsayar da TX: Yanayin tsaro ne galibi. Yana ba da damar ganin dukkan bayanai da kashe shirin watsawa (tsayar da TX) idan gaggawa ta taso, amma ba ya barin a fara TX ko a canza saituna.
    • Cikakken iko: Yana ba da cikakken iko a kan manhaja, yana kunna dukkan fasaloli.

    Bayanin tsaro: Duk wanda yake da damar shiga network ɗinka, adireshin IP da port zai sami matakin ikon da ka saita. Idan an saita Cikakken iko, yana iya fara watsawa da tasharka.

    Abubuwan da take iya yi

    A yanayin Cikakken iko, fuskar yanar gizo tana ba da kusan duk abin da manhaja take bayarwa:

    • Yanayin aiki: Canzawa tsakanin yanayin AUTO da yanayin CALL da hannu.
    • Dabara: Canza sharuɗɗan AUTO (Kowa, DXCC, Grid, da sauransu) da iyakokin bincike.
    • Watsawa: Fara/tsayar da TX da hannu, zaɓi wasu saƙonnin TX, da canza jerin FT8 (0/1).
    • Mita & zango: Taɓa spectrogram na yanar gizo domin motsa bambancin mitarka, ko canza zangon rediyo.

    Ana sarrafa SSB, CW, RTTY da SSTV a qFT8 kanta. Yayin da manhaja take cikin ɗaya daga cikinsu, shafin yanar gizo yana nuna waterfall da ko manhaja tana watsawa, kuma ba ya watsawa; zaɓi yanayin dijital a shafin domin sake aiki daga can.

    Iyakar watsawa ba tsayawa ta rediyo tana aiki ga shafin daidai kamar ga manhaja. Idan ta riƙe TX a kashe (misali WSPR da iyakar daƙiƙa 60) ko ta tsayar da TUNE, shafin yana nuna sanarwa na ɗan daƙiƙoƙi mai faɗar dalili, domin kada ka tsaya kana mamaki a gaban maɓallin da ba ya yin komai. Iyakar kanta ana canza ta a manhaja kawai.

    Lura: An tsara fuskar yanar gizo domin ta ci gaba da amsawa ko da allon waya ya yi nauyi saboda RF interference, don haka hanya ce amintacciya ta sarrafawa.

    Lura: Sabar yanar gizo za ta yi aiki allo a kashe idan an kunna saitunan aiki a bango (na asali shi ne ci gaba da aiki a bango idan qFT8 tana haɗe da transceiver).

    qFT8 bridge

    qFT8 bridge yana ba ka damar barin rediyo a wuri ɗaya ka yi aiki daga wani wuri. Wata na'ura da ke kusa da rediyo, wato bridge, tana aika umarnin rediyo da sautinsa ta network ɗinka zuwa qFT8 a wata na'ura. Wannan qFT8 ɗin yana yin duk sauran: yana decode, yana rubuta log, kuma yana yanke shawarar abin da za a aika. A wurinsa, rediyon yana aiki kamar an haɗa shi da USB.

    Yi amfani da shi idan rediyo yana kusa da eriya, a soro, a garejin mota ko a rumfar lambu, kuma kana so ka yi aiki daga wuri mafi jin daɗi; ko kuma idan na'urar da ke kusa da rediyo za ta kula da rediyo kaɗai: ƙaramar kwamfuta kamar Raspberry Pi, ko tsohuwar waya ko kwamfutar hannu.

    Wannan ba ɗaya ba ne da Sabar Yanar Gizo Ta Kula Daga Nesa. A can, qFT8 yana aiki kusa da rediyo kuma kana kallonsa daga browser. Da bridge, qFT8 yana aiki a na'urar da ke gabanka, rediyo kaɗai ne yake wani wuri.

    Abin da kake buƙata

    • Na'ura kusa da rediyo, da rediyo a haɗe da ita ta USB kamar yadda aka saba: kwamfuta mai Windows, macOS ko Linux (Raspberry Pi ya isa), ko waya ko kwamfutar hannu ta Android. Wannan ita ce bridge.
    • qFT8 a na'urar da kake aiki daga gare ta: shirin kwamfuta ko manhajar Android. Kowanne daga cikinsu zai iya amfani da kowane bridge: waya za ta iya sarrafa rediyo da ke kusa da kwamfuta, kwamfuta kuma rediyo da ke kusa da waya, da sauransu.
    • Duka biyun a network na gida ɗaya. Wayar network tana aiki kamar Wi-Fi kuma ta fi tsayayye; idan za ka iya, ka ba bridge waya aƙalla. Waya ko kwamfutar hannu tana amfani da Wi-Fi ɗinta, ba data na wayar hannu ba.
    • Kalmar sirri ta bridge. Bridge yana ƙirƙirar ɗaya kuma yana nuna maka ita.

    Shirya bridge

    Akwai hanyoyi biyu na gudanar da bridge. Zaɓi ɗaya.

    1. Daga Saitunan qFT8 (shirin kwamfuta ko manhajar Android). A na'urar da ke kusa da rediyo, buɗe Saituna > qFT8 BRIDGE ka yi alama a kan Yi aiki a matsayin qFT8 bridge a port. Bar port kamar yadda yake sai dai idan wani shiri yana amfani da shi.

    Kalmar sirri tana nuna kalmar sirrin da ɗayan na'urar za ta buƙata. SABUWAR KALMAR SIRRI tana ƙirƙirar wata; taɓa kalmar sirrin kanta domin ka rubuta naka (haruffa 7 ko fiye).

    Muddin yana bridge, qFT8 a wannan na'urar ba ya yin komai dabam. Ana kashe TX, amfaninsa na kansa da rediyo yana tsayawa, kuma allon yana nuna qFT8 yana aiki a yanayin bridge kaɗai, da Adireshi da Kalmar sirri da za a shigar a ɗayan na'urar, da wanda yake amfani da rediyo (Babu wanda aka haɗa, ko sunan na'urar). A Android bridge yana ci gaba da aiki ko da allo a kashe yake, sanarwa tana nuna hakan; ka bar waya ko kwamfutar hannu a kan caja.

    KASHE YANAYIN BRIDGE yana tambaya da farko, domin ɗayan na'urar tana rasa rediyo nan take, sannan yana dawo da qFT8 kamar yadda yake, da TX har yanzu a kashe. Idan ka rufe qFT8 yayin da yake bridge, zai sake farawa a matsayin bridge a gaba.

    2. Daga layin umarni (kwamfutoci kaɗai). Domin kwamfutar da ba ta da wani aiki, har da Raspberry Pi ko sabar da ba ta da allo: fara qFT8 da --bridge. Daga nan yana aiki a matsayin bridge kaɗai, ba tare da taga ba, kuma ba ya loda wani abu na qFT8 (babu saituna, babu fassara), don haka yana buƙatar ƙwaƙwalwa kaɗan ƙwarai. Yi amfani da qFT8 da ka sauke don wannan kwamfutar, daga terminal:

    • Windows: buɗe Command Prompt a cikin babban fayil na qFT8 ka rubuta qFT8.exe --bridge --tx-volume 70. Idan Windows ya tambaya ko zai ba "OpenJDK Platform binary" izini a networks ɗinka, ka ba shi izini.
    • macOS: a Terminal, rubuta /Applications/qFT8.app/Contents/MacOS/qFT8 --bridge --tx-volume 70. A karon farko, macOS yana neman izinin makirufo: ka ba shi, ta haka ne ake karanta sautin rediyo.
    • Linux: a cikin babban fayil na qFT8, rubuta ./qFT8 --bridge --tx-volume 70.
    • Kowace kwamfuta mai Java 21 ko sabuwa, misali Raspberry Pi da Java na tsarinta: saukewar .jar ta qFT8 tana aiki a kowane tsari. Rubuta java -jar, sunan fayil ɗin .jar, da --bridge. Tana ɗauke da abin da take buƙata don amfani da serial ports a Windows, macOS da Linux (PCs da allunan ARM, bits 32 da 64).

    Terminal yana nuna kalmar sirri a farkon farawa kuma yana adana ta don na gaba. Bar terminal a buɗe: bridge yana aiki muddin yana buɗe. Ctrl+C, ko rufe terminal, yana tsayar da shi kuma yana mayar da rediyo zuwa karɓa.

    Zaɓuɓɓukan da za ka iya so, a ƙara su bayan --bridge:

    • --tx-volume N: dole: matakin sauti, daga 1 zuwa 100 %, da gada ke sanyawa a na'urar sautin rediyo don aikawa, kamar saitin ƙarar TX na qFT8 (na asali 70; rediyon QRP Labs: 100). Matakin sauti ne kawai: ba a taɓa canza ƙarfin rediyo ba.
    • --password TEXT: kalmar sirrinka maimakon wadda ya ƙirƙira.
    • --show-password: sake nuna kalmar sirrin da aka adana.
    • --new-password: ƙirƙiri sabuwa.
    • --name TEXT: sunan da sauran na'urori suke gani a jerinsu ("qFT8 bridge" ne idan ba haka ba), yana da amfani idan akwai bridge fiye da ɗaya.
    • --only TEXT: raba serial port da na'urar sauti na rediyo kaɗai, da aka ambata da wani ɓangare na sunansu (misali --only QMX); ba a bayar da wani abu na kwamfutar ba.

    --bridge --help yana lissafa sauran.

    Haɗawa daga qFT8

    On the device you operate from, open Settings > DEVICE CONFIGURATION and, in Haɗin rediyo at the very top, choose qFT8 bridge. These rows appear right under it:

    • Hanya: qFT8 bridge.
    • Bridge: yana buɗe jerin bridges da qFT8 ya samu a network ɗinka. Zaɓi naka. Sake nema yana sake duba. Idan naka ba ya cikin jerin, zaɓi Rubuta adireshi ka shigar da adireshin da bridge yake nunawa.
    • Kalmar sirri: wadda bridge yake nunawa.
    • HAƊA: akwatin da ke gefensa yana zama kore kuma yana karanta An haɗa da sannan sunan bridge.
    • Port na rediyo a kan bridge, Na'urar sauti a kan bridge (karɓa) da Na'urar sauti a kan bridge (aikawa): abin da aka haɗa rediyo da shi a ɓangaren bridge. AUTO yana samo su; zaɓa da hannu kawai idan bridge yana da su da yawa. Rediyo da ake isa gare shi ta shirinsa a kwamfutar bridge (virtual serial port da virtual audio cable) yana da port da AUTO ba ya iya ganewa: zaɓi port da hannu, ka duba cewa na'urorin sauti biyun na kebul ɗin ne.

    From here on everything is as with a USB radio: qFT8 recognises the radio, reads its frequency, receives and transmits. qFT8 remembers the bridge and its password, and a settings export carries them to your other devices. While the box is ticked, a radio plugged into the device you operate from is not used. To go back to it, choose Kai tsaye (USB / waya) in Haɗin rediyo; no restart is needed.

    A waya ko kwamfutar hannu akwatin matsayi yana karanta Babu Wi-Fi. Haɗa wannan na'ura da hanyar sadarwar bridge ɗin. idan ba ta kan network na Wi-Fi: a can kaɗai ake neman bridge.

    Bayanin haɗi

    A ƙarƙashin na'urorin bridge, layi ɗaya yana gaya maka yadda haɗi yake, misali Jinkiri 120 ms, yana daidaita kansa. Haɗi: tsayayye (jitter 3.0 ms).

    • Jinkiri: network ba ya isar da sauti daidai sosai, don haka ana riƙe sauti na ɗan lokaci kafin a kunna shi, domin a daidaita hakan. Babu abin da za a saita: qFT8 da bridge suna zaɓar jinkirin da kansu, suna ƙara shi idan sauti ya iso a makare kuma suna rage shi idan haɗi ya kasance mai tsabta na ɗan lokaci. Ba ya dagula lokacin yanayoyin dijital: watsa naka yana farawa a kan lokaci, kuma abin da kake karɓa ana sanya shi bisa lokacin da rediyo ya ji shi.
    • Haɗi: tsayayye: komai lafiya.
    • Haɗi: mara daidaito: sauti yana isowa ba daidai ba. Jinkiri yana magance shi; babu abin da za a yi sai dai idan ya ci gaba haka.
    • Da ja, Haɗin zuwa bridge yana rasa sautin aikawa: wani ɓangare na watsawa bai isa rediyo a kan lokaci ba. Ana ƙara jinkiri nan take; idan ya ci gaba da faruwa, duba Magance Matsaloli.
    • Da ja, Haɗin zuwa bridge ba ya iya bin sawu: ko mafi girman jinkiri bai isa ba kuma sautin aikawa yana katsewa. Duba Magance Matsaloli.

    Nan da nan bayan haɗawa layin yana karanta Ba a auna komai ba tukuna.

    Tsaron watsawa

    • Kai kaɗai kake kunna TX, da maɓallin TX na qFT8 da ke gabanka. Bridge ba ya taɓa fara watsawa da kansa.
    • Idan haɗi ya katse (Wi-Fi ya ɓace, na'ura ta yi barci, an rufe qFT8), bridge yana mayar da rediyo zuwa karɓa da kansa, cikin daƙiƙa ɗaya ko biyu. qFT8 yana tsayar da abin da yake aikawa, yana kashe TX kuma yana nuna jan kati wanda yake faɗin dalili, kamar Haɗin ya katse. Ana sake haɗawa... Yana sake haɗawa da kansa. Idan haɗi ya dawo cikin daƙiƙa 10 kuma TX yana kunne a gare ka, qFT8 yana sake kunna shi sau ɗaya, kamar yadda kake da shi; bayan katsewa mafi tsayi yana zama a kashe har sai ka sake danna shi.
    • Iyakar watsawa ba tsayawa ta rediyonka bridge ma yana kiyaye ta.
    • Tsayar da bridge, ta kowace hanya daga cikin biyun, ita ma tana mayar da rediyo zuwa karɓa.

    Bayanin tsaro: Kalmar sirri tana kare iko da rediyonka: ba wanda zai iya amfani da bridge ba tare da ita ba, kuma ba a taɓa aika kalmar sirrin kanta ta network ba. Ba ta sa haɗin ya zama na sirri ba: sauti da umarnin rediyo ba a ɓoye su ba. Yi amfani da bridge a cikin network ɗinka kaɗai, kuma kada ka buɗe shi ga intanet.

    Iyakoki

    • Network na gida kaɗai. Ba a yi bridge don a kai gare shi ta intanet ba. VPN da ke aika zirga-zirgar network ɗin gidanka ta ramin sa shi ma yana ɓoye bridge: kashe shi, ko ka ba da izinin shiga network na gida a cikinsa, a na'urorin biyu.
    • Na'ura ɗaya a lokaci guda. Idan na'ura ta biyu ta haɗa, ita ce take karɓa; ta farko tana karanta Wata na'ura ta karɓe bridge. Danna HAƊA don karɓo shi.
    • Kowane yanayi yana aiki ta bridge, karɓa da aikawa: FT8, FT4, FT2, JS8, WSPR, LQ8, SSB, CW, RTTY, SSTV da TUNE. Lasifikarka da makirufonka suna zama a na'urar da ke gabanka; sautin rediyo kaɗai yake ratsa network.
    • Rediyon da ake sa shi watsawa ta layin sarrafawa (RTS ko DTR) maimakon umarni ana kunna shi ta bridge ma.

    qFT8log

    qFT8log yana tara log ɗin qFT8 da dama wuri ɗaya ta cikin network ɗinka. Ɗaya daga cikin qFT8 shi ne sabar qFT8log; sauran suna aika masa kowane QSO da suka yi. Sabar tana ƙara su a log ɗinta (jerin QSO, taswira, ƙididdiga) kuma tana tura su zuwa sabis ɗin da aka saita a kanta, kamar QRZ da LoTW. Abin da ke faruwa da QSO daga baya, tabbaci ko gyara, yana komawa zuwa qFT8 da ya yi shi muddin su biyun suna haɗe.

    Kowane qFT8 na iya zama sabar da abokin ciniki a lokaci guda: shirin kwamfuta, manhajar Android da manhajar iPhone. A Android sabar tana ci gaba da aiki a bango idan Gudu a bango yana a kunne; a iPhone tana aiki ne kawai yayin da manhajar take a buɗe.

    Saita sabar

    1. A kan qFT8 da zai tara QSO, buɗe Saituna, je zuwa Ƙarin saituna > SABOBI ka buɗe giyar sabar qFT8log.
    2. Rubuta kalmar sirri. Yi amfani da irin kalmar sirri da kake amfani da ita ga qFT8 bridge: haruffa bakwai ko fiye.
    3. Bar port a 13510 sai dai idan wani abu dabam yana amfani da shi, ka kunna sabar. Shafin yana nuna adireshin sabar da qFT8 da ke haɗe da ita. Port da aka canza yana aiki nan take.
    4. Adireshin da aka buɗe a burauza (misali http://192.168.1.20:13510/) yana nuna log, don karatu kawai, bayan ka rubuta kalmar sirrin sabar (ba a taɓa aikawa da ita): duba Kallon log a cikin burauza.

    Saita abokin ciniki

    1. A kan kowane qFT8 da zai aika QSO ɗinsa, buɗe Saituna, danna gear kusa da qFT8log a sama.
    2. Danna NEMA: ana jera sabobin qFT8log da ke network ɗinka da sunayensu. Zaɓi ɗaya, ko ka rubuta adireshin sabar da port ɗinta da kanka.
    3. Rubuta kalmar sirrin sabar ka danna TABBATAR. Idan sabar ta karɓa, yi alama a qFT8log a saman Saituna.

    Daga nan abokin ciniki yana haɗawa da kansa duk lokacin da ya fara kuma yana sake haɗawa da kansa idan network ko sabar ta dawo. QSO da aka yi yayin da ba a iya isa ga sabar suna jira a cikin log (suna bayyana a matsayin ba a loda su zuwa qFT8log ba) kuma ana aika su da zarar haɗin ya dawo, har ma bayan sake kunnawa. Idan abokin ciniki ba zai iya isa ga sabar ba, layin qFT8log a Saituna yana zama ja tare da Ba a iya haɗawa ba tun da lokaci.

    Ɗauki QSO a matsayin an loda idan an loda shi nan yana a kashe ga qFT8log ta tsohuwa. Yi masa alama idan sabar ita ce wurin da QSO ɗinka ke zuwa, don alamar "ana jiran loda" ta QSO ta goge da zarar sabar ta same shi.

    Abin da ake aikawa

    • Sababbin QSO, daga abokin ciniki zuwa sabar, tare da kowane fanni da qFT8 ke adanawa: rahotanni, grids, wuraren shakatawa da kololuwa, kayan aiki, sharhi, da ko an riga an loda QSO zuwa QRZ, LoTW ko wani sabis ko sun tabbatar da shi (QSO da aka shigo da shi daga LoTW yana kasancewa an tabbatar).
    • Tabbatarwa, ta hanyoyi biyu, da zarar sun zo: tabbaci da sabar ta karɓa daga LoTW yana bayyana a kan abokin ciniki da ya yi QSO, haka kuma akasin haka.
    • Gyare-gyare da aka yi a editan QSO, ta hanyoyi biyu. Idan an canza QSO ɗaya a ɓangarorin biyu, canji na baya shi ne yake rinjaye.
    • Gogewa ba sa tafiya. Goge QSO yana gogewa a wannan qFT8 kaɗai; sauran suna riƙe nasu. Kowane qFT8 yana tuna QSOs da aka goge a kansa kuma ba ya taɓa karɓar su daga wani qFT8, ba ta shigowa ba kuma ba ta daidaitawa kai tsaye ba.

    Sabar tana karɓar QSO na kowane yanayi da kowace alamar kira: wuri ne da kake tarawa, kuma matattarar log ɗin ke zaɓar abin da kake gani. QSO da sabar ta riga ta samu (alamar kira ɗaya, lokaci, band da yanayi) ana haɗa shi da wanda take da shi, ba a taɓa ƙara shi sau biyu ba, kuma ba a sake aika shi.

    Shigo da daga sabar, da daidaitawa kai tsaye

    Abokin ciniki yana aika QSOs nasa kawai. Hanyoyi biyu suna kawo masa log ɗin sabar ma:

    • SHIGO DA QSOS DAGA SABAR, a shafin qFT8log na abokin ciniki, yana kwafar sau ɗaya kowane QSO na sabar a cikin Daga, Zuwa, Bands da Alamar kira ta fara da, na kowane yanayi. Ana tsallake QSOs da aka yi da wata alamar kira banda taka, sai dai idan an saka alama a Shigo da QSOs don alamun kira banda na yanzu. Yana aiki ma idan ba a saka alama a qFT8log a saman Saituna ba: shigowar tana haɗuwa don kanta kawai kuma ba ta aika komai.
    • Ci gaba da daidaitawa kai tsaye da wannan sabar (yana buƙatar a saka alama a qFT8log a saman Saituna): QSOs na sabar suna zuwa nan, kuma daga lokacin kowane QSO da aka rubuta, aka gyara ko aka tabbatar a sabar, ko a kowane qFT8 da aka haɗa da ita, yana bayyana a nan nan take, naka kuma suna tafiya can. Da dama qFT8 da aka saka alama a wannan akwatin suna raba log ɗaya ta sabarsu, kuma da sarƙoƙi, ta kowace sabar a cikin sarƙar.
    • Idan haɗin ya katse, babu abin da ya ɓace: kowane gefe yana cim ma abin da ya rasa idan haɗin ya dawo, har bayan sake farawa.
    • Lokutan gyare-gyare suna bin agogon sabar, don haka na'urar da agogonta ba daidai ba ba ta cin nasara, ko rasa, kowane gyara.

    Sarƙoƙi: tarawa da ajiya

    Sabar kanta na iya zama abokin ciniki na wata sabar, don haka za a iya haɗa qFT8 a sarƙa. Ba a taɓa mayar da QSO zuwa qFT8 da ya riga ya wuce ta, don haka ko da zobe na qFT8 ba ya yin zagaye. Misalai biyu:

    • Wuri ɗaya da ke lodawa. Wayoyi da kwamfutar hannu mai ɗaukuwa suna aika zuwa kwamfutar shack. Kwamfutar shack ce kaɗai ke da QRZ da LoTW a saita: tana loda komai, kuma tabbatarwar da take karɓa suna komawa zuwa qFT8 da ya yi kowane QSO.
    • Ajiya. Kwamfutar shack tana kuma aika komai zuwa wani qFT8, misali tsohon kwamfutar hannu da ke adana log kawai kuma yana nuna taswirar duniya. Ba a buƙatar saita komai a kan ajiyar sai sabarta.

    Bayanin tsaro: ba a taɓa aika kalmar sirri ta network ba: qFT8 biyun suna tabbatar da cewa sun san ta, kuma kowane saƙo bayan haka ana sanya masa hannu, don haka ba za a iya canza komai ko a sake kunnawa a hanya ba. Idan Rufe haɗin qFT8 log a kunne yake (ta asali, a kan abokin ciniki da sabar duka), ana kuma ɓoye QSO, don haka babu wani a cikin network da zai iya karanta su. Yi amfani da qFT8log a cikin network ɗinka kaɗai, kuma kada ka buɗe portinsa zuwa intanet.

    Iyakoki

    • Network na gida kaɗai, kamar qFT8 bridge.
    • Dole qFT8 biyun su yi amfani da sigar qFT8log iri ɗaya. Sabar tana ƙin abokin ciniki na wata sigar, kuma layin abokin ciniki yana faɗin wacce za a sabunta.
    • Abokan ciniki 16 a lokaci guda a kowace sabar.
    • QSO da sabar ba za ta iya karantawa ba (misali wanda ba shi da kwanan wata) ana yi masa alama a kan abokin ciniki cewa an ƙi shi kuma ana sake aikawa ne kawai bayan ka gyara shi.

    Ƙa'idar qFT8log (sigar 2)

    Wannan cikakken bayani ne na abin da qFT8 biyu ke gaya wa juna. Kowace lamba big-endian ce. Rubutu UTF-8 ne.

    Neman sabobi

    Abokin ciniki yana aika datagram na UDP guda ɗaya zuwa adireshin broadcast na network ɗinsa, port 13510: "qFT8L?" (bytes 6), sigar API (bytes 2), id na bazuwar (bytes 8). Kowace sabar tana amsa wa mai aikawa: "qFT8L!", sigar API ɗinta (2), id ɗaya (8), port ɗin TCP ɗinta (2), id ɗin instance ɗinta (lambobin hex 16 na ASCII), tsawon sunanta (1) da sunanta (bytes 64 a mafi yawa). Amsar ba ta faɗin wani sirri.

    Frames

    Ta TCP (port 13510 ta tsohuwa) komai yana tafiya a frames: nau'i (byte 1), tsawon jiki (bytes 3) da jiki (bytes 16384 a mafi yawa). Nau'i huɗu na farko suna tafiya kafin mabuɗan su wanzu; jikin kowane frame ɗaya an rufe shi: ƙidaya na bytes 8, saƙon, da bytes 16 na farko na HMAC-SHA256 (mabuɗin shugabanci) a kan nau'i, ƙidaya da saƙon. Kowane shugabanci yana ƙidaya daga 0 kuma dole frame ya ɗauki lamba ta gaba daidai: frame da aka canza, aka maimaita ko aka tsallake yana kawo ƙarshen haɗin.

    Nau'i 0x01
    Suna HELLO (client)
    Jiki "QFT8LOG1", API version (2), client random (32), client instance id (16 ASCII), name length (1), name
    Nau'i 0x02
    Suna CHALLENGE (server)
    Jiki API version (2), server random (32), salt (16), rounds (4), server instance id (16 ASCII), name length (1), name, server proof (32)
    Nau'i 0x03
    Suna AUTH (client)
    Jiki client proof (32)
    Nau'i 0x04
    Suna REFUSED (server)
    Jiki dalili (1): 1 wata sigar API, 2 kalmar sirri ba daidai ba, 3 jira (kalmomin sirri marasa daidai da yawa), 4 yana aiki, 5 ba HELLO ba, 6 instance na sabar kanta; jira a ms (4); sigar API ta sabar (2)
    Nau'i 0x10
    Suna WELCOME (server, sealed)
    Jiki sigar API (2), lokacin sabar a ms (8): abokin ciniki yana sanya lokaci a gyare-gyarensa da wannan agogon
    Nau'i 0x11 / 0x12
    Suna PING / PONG
    Jiki fanko
    Nau'i 0x13
    Suna BYE
    Jiki dalili (1): 1 ana rufewa, 2 kuskuren ƙa'ida, 3 ba a ji komai ba, 4 da yawa; tsawon rubutu (1), rubutu
    Nau'i 0x20
    Suna PUT
    Jiki lambar saƙo (4), bayanin QSO (sauran: abu na JSON)
    Nau'i 0x21
    Suna ACK
    Jiki lambar saƙo (4), sakamako (1): 0 iri ɗaya, 1 sabo, 2 an haɗa, 3 an tsallake; hash na mai karɓa na QSO (lambobin hex 16 na ASCII); bayanin mai karɓa idan ya bambanta da wanda aka karɓa (sauran, in ba haka ba babu komai)
    Nau'i 0x22
    Suna NACK
    Jiki lambar saƙo (4), dalili (1): 1 ba bayanin QSO ba ne, 2 rage gudu, 3 kuskuren ciki; tsawon rubutu (1), rubutu
    Nau'i 0x23
    Suna SUBSCRIBE (abokin ciniki)
    Jiki wani abu na JSON: mode (once, live ko off), from da to (YYYYMMDD, fanko = babu iyaka), bands (jeri), call (farkon alamar kira), station (alamar kiran tasha, fanko = kowace)
    Nau'i 0x24
    Suna BACKLOG_DONE (sabar)
    Jiki QSOs da aka aika (4), sababbi a can (4), da aka haɗa a can (4): kowane QSO da biyan kuɗin ya nema ya tafi

    Musafaha

    Bangarorin biyu sun san k = PBKDF2-HMAC-SHA256(password, salt, rounds), bytes 32. Sabar tana zaɓar salt da rounds (200000; abokin ciniki yana ƙin ƙasa da 10000 ko fiye da 5000000). Rubutun t shi ne jikin HELLO sai jikin CHALLENGE ba tare da shaidarsa ba. Idan L = rubutun ASCII "qFT8 log v1 ":

    • server proof = HMAC-SHA256(k, L + "server proof" + t); client proof = HMAC-SHA256(k, L + "client proof" + t);
    • key client to server = HMAC-SHA256(k, L + "key client>server" + t); key server to client = HMAC-SHA256(k, L + "key server>client" + t).

    Abokin ciniki yana duba shaidar sabar da farko kuma yana rufewa ba tare da amsawa ba idan ba daidai ba ce, don haka babu abin da aka samo daga kalmar sirri da ke zuwa sabar da ba ta san ta ba. Sabar tana duba shaidar abokin ciniki; ta kuskure tana kulle wannan adireshi na 1 s, tana ninkawa da kowane gwaji mara daidai a jere har zuwa 60 s. Duka bazuwar biyu sababbi ne ga kowane haɗi, don haka musafahar da aka naɗa ba ta da amfani. Dole sigogin API su zama daidai: sabar tana ƙin kowace ta daban da dalili 1, kuma abokin ciniki yana ƙin CHALLENGE na wata sigar. Sabar tana ba da damar musafaha 8 a lokaci guda, 5 s ga kowane mataki, da abokan ciniki 16; abokin ciniki da ya sake haɗawa yana maye gurbin tsohon haɗinsa.

    Bayanai

    Bayanin QSO abu ne na JSON mai sunayen fannoni da qFT8 ke adanawa (call, qso_date a matsayin YYYYMMDD, time_on a matsayin HHMM ko HHMMSS, band, mode, submode, rst_sent, rst_rcvd, gridsquare, freq, station_callsign, my_gridsquare, comment, nassoshin wuraren shakatawa da kololuwa, yanayin QRZ da LoTW app_qrzlog_logid, app_qrzlog_status, app_lotw_qsl_rcvd, app_lotw_logid, app_lotw_upload_time, da app_svc), bytes 16000 a mafi yawa. call, qso_date da time_on wajibi ne; ana karɓar kowane yanayi da kowace alamar kira. app_svc yana ɗauke da yanayin sauran sabis da bayanai uku na ƙa'idar kanta, a matsayin sassan id:key=value,... da aka raba da ;:

    • q8u: id = uid na QSO (an yi shi sau ɗaya ta qFT8 da ya fara aikawa, ana riƙe shi ta kowane gyara), t = lokacin da aka gyara abin da ke cikinsa na ƙarshe (UTC ms, babu = ba a taɓa ba);
    • q8v: id = id na instances da QSO ya wuce ta, da aka raba da digo (32 a mafi yawa);
    • mai aikawa yana barin lissafinsa na kansa: hashes na kowane abokin tarayya, yanayin lodawarsa da tutocin aikinsa na gida.

    Hash da haɗawa

    Hash na QSO shi ne bytes 8 na farko na SHA-256, a matsayin lambobin hex 16, a kan layukan name=value na abin da ke cikinsa, lokacin gyaransa da yanayin lodawa da tabbatarwarsa (ba instances da ya wuce ta ba). Kwafi biyu na QSO ɗaya suna zama ɗaya ta hanya ɗaya a ko'ina: abin da ke cikin kwafin da aka gyara daga baya yana rinjaye gaba ɗaya (lokuta daidai: babban hash na abin da ke ciki); yanayin lodawa da tabbatarwa suna tafiya gaba kawai (ba a taɓa warware tabbaci ba); instances haɗin biyun ne. Haɗawa iri ɗaya ne a kowane tsari kuma haɗawa sau biyu ba ya canza komai, don haka duk kwafi suna ƙarewa iri ɗaya.

    Wane ne ke aika me

    • Abokin ciniki yana aika wa sabarsa kowane QSO da bai wuce ta wannan sabar ba tukuna, da kowane QSO da su biyun suka musanya a baya wanda hash ɗinsa ya canza tun lokacin.
    • Sabar tana aika wa abokin ciniki QSOs da su biyun suka riga suka musanya a baya kuma hash ɗinsu ya canza (tabbatarwa, gyara). Bayan SUBSCRIBE kuma tana aika wa wannan abokin ciniki kowane QSO na log ɗinta a cikin biyan kuɗin da su biyun ba su taɓa musanyawa ba kuma bai bi ta abokin cinikin ba, sannan BACKLOG_DONE. Tare da live tana ci gaba da yin haka ga kowane QSO da za ta samu daga baya, muddin haɗin yana nan (abokin ciniki yana sake yin biyan kuɗi bayan kowace sake haɗuwa); once yana ƙarewa da BACKLOG_DONE; off yana ƙare biyan kuɗin.
    • Mai karɓa yana samun kwafinsa ta uid, idan babu to ta alamar kira, kwanan wata, lokaci (HHMM), band da yanayi. Sabar tana ƙara QSO da ba ta da shi; abokin ciniki yana ƙara ɗaya ne kawai a cikin biyan kuɗin da ya aika. Babu ɗayansu da yake ƙara QSO da aka goge a kansa (sakamako 3 a dukkan lokuta biyu). In ba haka ba ana haɗa kwafin; ACK yana ɗauke da hash na mai karɓa kuma, idan kwafin da aka haɗa ya bambanta da abin da aka aika, rikodin da aka haɗa, wanda mai aikawa shi ma yake haɗawa.
    • Kowane ɓangare yana tuna, ga kowane QSO da kowane abokin tarayya, hash da duka biyun suka yarda a kai na ƙarshe, kuma yana sake aikawa ne kawai idan kwafinsa ya bambanta da shi. Ba a taɓa aika sharewa ba.
    • PUT 16 a mafi yawa na iya jiran amsarsu. Sabar tana karɓar PUT 100 a sakan guda daga kowane abokin ciniki (fashewar 200) kuma tana amsa NACK 2 fiye da haka; bayan bayanai 50 da ba za ta iya karantawa ba tana kawo ƙarshen haɗin.
    • Ɓangare yana aika PING idan bai aika komai ba na 15 s kuma yana kawo ƙarshen haɗin idan bai ji komai ba na 45 s. Abokin ciniki yana sake haɗawa bayan 1 s, 2 s, 4 s ... har zuwa 60 s tsakani.

    Tsarin allo

    Hotunan allo a wannan jagora suna nuna tsarin na asali: a kwance, da spectrogram a hagu. A manhajar Android, Saituna > AIKACE-AIKACE > Tsarin mai amfani yana ba ka damar canza shi (manhajar kwamfuta ba ta da waɗannan saituna: tana da nata tsare-tsare, duba Tsarin allo da maɓallin tsari):

    • Fuskantarwa: A kwance, A tsaye, ko Bi wayar. A tsaye, spectrogram yana sama, shafi a ƙarƙashinsa, kuma a waya sandar ƙasa tana ɗaukar layuka biyu.
    • Mai rabawa: ja siririn layin da ke tsakanin spectrogram da shafi domin ba ɗayansu ƙarin sarari. Kowace fuskantarwa tana tuna nata wurin.
    • Kashi huɗu (tablet): spectrogram, tashoshi, taswirar duniya da log na QSO duka a lokaci guda, da Saituna yana buɗewa a dama.
    • Fasaloli: Fasalolin Ham (callsign, transmitting, the QSO log, syncing and events), Fasalolin SWL (listening: the SWL tab, the SWL log and your SWL id) and Lambobin yabo can each be turned off if you do not use them. On the phone Radio features, QSO log and Taswirar duniya can be turned off too; while one is off, an KUNNA button for it shows in Settings. Turning the Ham or radio features off also switches TX off.
    • Manhajar tana rufe: yana barin qFT8 ta zana a kan sandar navigation, wurin kyamara da sandar status, domin ƙarin ɗan sarari.
    ↑ Koma zuwa Abubuwan da ke ciki

    Rahoton matsala da sabbin sigogi

    Ba da rahoton matsala

    BA DA RAHOTON MATSALA, a ƙasan shafin Saituna, yana buɗe kati inda kake bayyana abin da ya faru, kuma, idan kana son amsa, ka bar e-mail ɗinka. Danna AIKA yana aika bayaninka tare da log na manhaja: saituna, bayanan rediyo da sauti, da abin da ya faru kwanan nan. Ba a taɓa haɗa kalmomin sirri, mabuɗan API, takardun shaida da bayanan shiga sabis na QSO naka. Kana samun lamba kamar QFT8-7K3P9 domin ambata daga baya. Idan ba a iya kaiwa sabar rahoto ba, qFT8 tana ba da damar aika rahoton ta e-mail.

    "A aika rahoto game da wannan?"

    When qFT8 notices that it stopped responding for a moment, or that decoding is taking too long on your device, it may show a small card that offers to send a report about it, with AIKA, KADA A AIKA and KADA KU SAKE TAMBAYA. Nothing is ever sent without your tap. The card appears at most once a day, and KADA KU SAKE TAMBAYA silences it until the next version. It also appears when the spectrogram keeps having trouble for minutes (it looks slow, pauses or skips), with a plain question whether to send a report; a healthy slow device never sees it. When your radio is reached through a qFT8 bridge, a report also carries the bridge's own log, fetched over the bridge link. After a crash, qFT8 asks at the next start whether to send a crash report.

    Duba sabbin siga lokacin farawa

    Idan an sa alama a wannan akwati (babban shafin Saituna, kusa da ƙasa), qFT8 tana neman sabuwar siga idan ta fara, kuma tana nuna sanarwa mai maɓallin saukewa idan akwai. Ba ta taɓa sabunta kanta.

    Bayanin aiki ba tare da suna ba

    Aika taƙaitaccen bayanin aiki na kullum ba tare da suna ba (babban shafin Saituna, kusa da ƙasa, ƙarƙashin Duba sabbin siga lokacin farawa) a kashe yake sai ka kunna shi. Idan yana kunne, kimanin sau ɗaya a rana qFT8 tana aika gajeren bayani game da saurin aikinta a na'urarka: lokacin farawa, sauƙin motsin allo, lokutan fassara, lokutan sauti da rediyo, da model na na'ura da allo. Ba ya taɓa ɗauke da alamar kiranka, grid, log, saituna ko wani abin da zai gane ka. Yana taimakawa wajen sa qFT8 ta fi sauri a na'urori irin naka.

    Bayan kowane awa 10 na amfani da shi a kashe, qFT8 na iya tambaya sau ɗaya, idan ya buɗe: A kunna rahotannin aiki? KUNNA yana saka alama a akwatin; A'A, NA GODE yana barin shi a kashe kuma qFT8 ba zai sake tambaya ba; rufe tambayar ba tare da amsa ba yana sake tambaya bayan awa 10. Sigogin gwaji ba sa tambaya, kuma qFT8 ba ya taɓa tambaya yayin aikawa.

    ↑ Koma zuwa Abubuwan da ke ciki

    Magance Matsaloli

    “haɗin rediyo ya ɓace”

    A kwamfuta (da kuma tare da rediyon USB na waya), kebul na rediyo ko adaftar USB-serial da ta daina amsawa ba ta ƙara daskarar da qFT8. Idan tashar ba ta gama umarni ba cikin daƙiƙa 4 shirin yana nuna haɗin rediyo ya ɓace, TX yana zama OFF, ana watsar da sababbin umarni maimakon tarawa, kuma qFT8 yana sake buɗe tashar da kansa sau kaɗan kuma sake idan an sake saka adaftar. TX OFF koyaushe yana gaba da duk sauran abubuwan da ke jiran rediyo. Duba kebul da adaftar, sake saka shi, kuma danna TX ON idan haɗin ya dawo: qFT8 ba ya taɓa kunna TX da kansa.

    Waya ko kwamfuta ba ta gane transceiver ko haɗin yana katsewa

    Kusan koyaushe wannan matsalar haɗin kayan aiki ce ko ta radio interference. Haɗin USB yana da saurin damuwa da girgiza, ingancin kebul, da ƙura ko zaren tufafi da suke taruwa a port na USB na waya. A Android, wani dalili da aka saba shi ne mai yiwuwa an kashe USB OTG tun asali a na'urarka (musamman idan qFT8 tana karɓar sigina amma ba ta iya watsawa, kuma umarnin CAT ba sa aiki duk da cewa ana tallafa wa na'urar sosai, tana cikin jerin na'urori, ana gane ta a yanayin AUTO, da sauransu; to wannan ne mai yiwuwa dalilin).

    Gwada:

    • A Android, kunna USB OTG a saitunan na'urarka (a wasu na'urori, OTG a kashe yake tun asali, kuma yana iya kasancewa a ƙarƙashin saitunan advanced, system, ko additional).
    • Yi amfani da kebul na USB gajere mai inganci (gajeren kebul yana iya taimakawa sosai), kuma tabbata port na USB yana da tsabta kuma haɗin ya matse.
    • A Linux, idan qFT8 ta ce ba ta da izinin buɗe port na rediyo, karɓi tayinta na gyarawa (ana saka asusunka a rukunin port ɗin), ko tambayi mai kula da tsarinka.
    • Idan matsalolin suna faruwa ne musamman yayin watsawa, radio interference ne mai yiwuwa. Saka chokes ko ferrites a kebul na USB yana iya taimakawa. Kana kuma iya gwada watsawa a wani zango, domin wasu na'urori suna damuwa da interference a wasu zanguna kawai.

    Transceiver yana ɓacewa idan wayar ta cika caji (Android)

    Wannan yana faruwa da adaftocin USB OTG masu wurin caji, waɗanda suke barin a yi cajin waya yayin da transceiver yake haɗe. Da wasu daga cikinsu, idan baturi ya kai 100%, wayar tana tsayar da haɗin USB da transceiver, kuma ba ya dawowa sai an cire caja. Sai qFT8 ta nuna transceiver a matsayin an cire shi, kuma babu abin da yake aiki: babu sauti, babu CAT.

    Gwada: Cire caja daga adafta (transceiver zai dawo), yi aiki ba tare da caji ba, ko yi amfani da wata adafta ko hub mai wutar kansa.

    qFT8 bridge: "Ba a sami bridge a wannan hanyar sadarwa ba"

    qFT8 ya tambayi network kuma babu bridge da ya amsa. Dalilan da aka saba su ne bridge ba ya aiki, na'urorin biyu ba su a network ɗaya (Wi-Fi na baƙi ana ɗaukarsa wani network dabam), ko wani abu a tsakaninsu yana toshe tambayar.

    Gwada:

    • Duba cewa bridge yana aiki: tagarsa a buɗe take, ko qFT8 a wannan na'urar yana nuna qFT8 yana aiki a yanayin bridge. Sannan Sake nema.
    • Rubuta adireshi ka shigar da adireshin da bridge yake nunawa. Bridge da bincike bai samu ba yakan amsa idan aka kira shi kai tsaye.
    • Ba bridge izini a firewall na kwamfutarsa: port na TCP da UDP 13498, da port na UDP 13499 (wanda bincike yake amfani da shi).
    • Kashe AP Isolation (ko Client Isolation) a router, kamar na Sabar Yanar Gizo Ta Kula Daga Nesa.
    • Kashe VPN a na'urorin biyu, ko ba da izinin shiga network na gida a cikinsa. VPN da ke aika duk zirga-zirga ta ramin sa yana ɓoye bridge, ko da na'urorin biyu suna gefen juna.

    qFT8 bridge: "Kalmar sirri ba daidai ba ce", ko bridge ya ƙi haɗin

    Dole a rubuta kalmar sirri daidai kamar yadda bridge yake nuna ta. Kalmar sirri da bridge ya ƙirƙira tana da ƙananan haruffa da lambobi kaɗai, kuma ba ta taɓa ƙunsar masu kama da juna i, l, o, 0 da 1. Bayan ƙoƙari da yawa marasa daidai a jere, bridge yana sa wannan na'urar ta jira kafin na gaba, har zuwa minti ɗaya.

    Gwada: Sake karanta kalmar sirri a bridge: a allon yanayin bridge na qFT8, ko ta fara saukewar bridge da --show-password. Idan kana shakka, ƙirƙiri sabuwa (SABUWAR KALMAR SIRRI, ko --new-password) ka rubuta ita.

    qFT8 bridge: an haɗa, amma babu rediyo ko babu sauti

    Haɗin yana aiki, amma bridge ba zai iya amfani da port na rediyo ko na'urar sautinsa ba. Akwatin zai iya karanta An haɗa da sunan bridge, sannan "amma ba a iya buɗe na'urar sautinsa ba".

    Gwada:

    • Zaɓar Port na rediyo a kan bridge da layukan na'urar sauti biyu da hannu maimakon AUTO.
    • Rufe duk wani shiri a na'urar bridge da yake amfani da rediyo, har da qFT8 da ba ya cikin yanayin bridge.
    • A macOS, ba bridge izinin makirufo a System Settings > Privacy & Security > Microphone.
    • A Linux, tabbatar cewa asusun da yake gudanar da bridge zai iya amfani da serial ports (yawanci ta kasancewa cikin rukunin dialout) da sauti.
    • A bridge na Android, ba qFT8 izinin amfani da rediyo idan Android ya tambaya, kamar rediyo da aka haɗa da wayar kanta.

    qFT8 bridge: "Haɗin zuwa bridge yana rasa sautin aikawa" ko "ba ya iya bin sawu"

    Network tsakanin na'urorin biyu ba shi da daidaito sosai: sautin aikawa yana isowa a makare da yawa don a kunna shi. Muddin jinkiri yana iya ƙaruwa, qFT8 yana ƙara shi kuma yana cewa haɗi yana rasa sauti; idan ko mafi girman jinkiri bai isa ba, yana cewa haɗi ba ya iya bin sawu kuma watsa yana katsewa. Wannan maganar network ce, ba ta rediyo ba.

    Gwada: Matsawa kusa da na'urar Wi-Fi, haɗa bridge (ko na'urorin biyu) da wayar network, da dakatar da duk wani abu da yake ɗora wa network nauyi.

    Maimakon karɓar sigina daga transceiver, qFT8 tana ɗaukar sauti daga makirufo

    A Android, wannan yana nuna cewa wayar tana amfani da makirufonta na ciki, domin ba ta gane transceiver a matsayin tushen sauti na waje ba. A kwamfuta, duba wane input aka zaɓa a TSARIN NA'URA a saituna: zaɓi na'urar sauti ta transceiver. Dalilan da aka fi sabawa su ne:

    • Kebul na USB ba ya ɗaukar data (kebul na caji kawai).
    • Adaftar USB (OTG) ya lalace ko bai dace ba (Android).
    • An saka kebul a juye (wasu kebul suna da alkibla).

    Gwada: Yi amfani da wani kebul na USB, wata adafta, ko juya kebul ɗin.

    Bugun rediyo bai dace da mitar da qFT8 ke nunawa ba

    Allon rediyon da kansa da qFT8 suna bambanta da ƴan Hz har kusan 100 Hz. Yawanci wannan shi ne bambancin daidaitawar spectrogram, kuma da gangan ne: qFT8 yana daidaita rediyo ƙasa kaɗan kuma yana matsar da sauti da adadin ɗaya, domin sigina a can ƙasan spectrogram su fita daga wurin da rediyo ba ya ji a ƙaramin sauti. Sakamakon decode, spot, log da mitar da qFT8 ke nunawa duk suna nan a ainihin mitar.

    Gwada: Babu abin da ya lalace, don haka babu abin gyarawa. Asalin ya bambanta bisa rediyo (−100 Hz ga dangin QMX, −90 Hz ga IC-7300, −50 Hz ga kowane rediyo). Don canza shi ko saita shi zuwa 0, buɗe Saituna > TSARIN NA'URA > Daidaitawa (wannan rediyo) > Bambancin daidaitawar spectrogram (duba Bambancin daidaitawar spectrogram).

    Ba a karɓar sigina kwata-kwata; waterfall baƙi ne ƙirin

    Idan waterfall yana baƙi gaba ɗaya ko an haɗa transceiver ko ba a haɗa ba, mai yiwuwa qFT8 ba ta da izinin da ake buƙata domin samun sauti.

    Gwada: A Android, je zuwa Settings > Apps > qFT8 > Permissions na wayarka ka tabbata an yarda da Microphone (ko Audio recording) yayin amfani da qFT8. Ko kuma, cire qFT8 gaba ɗaya ka sake shigar da ita, ka karɓi dukkan tambayoyin izini a buɗewa ta farko. A macOS, buɗe System Settings > Privacy & Security > Microphone ka yarda da qFT8. A kowace kwamfuta, duba input da aka zaɓa a TSARIN NA'URA.

    Ana ganin sigina, amma ba a fassara saƙonni

    Idan kana ganin "layuka masu lanƙwasa" na tsaye a waterfall (siginar FT8) amma babu rubutu a shafin Tashoshi, mai yiwuwa agogonka bai daidaita ba.

    Gwada:

    • Tabbata kana da intanet, ka sake buɗe manhaja domin a yi DAIDAITA NTP.
    • Idan babu intanet, gwada daidaitawa ta GPS (Android).
    • Idan wannan ma bai yi aiki ba amma ana ganin sautukan saƙonni a spectrogram, yi daidaitawar gaggawa daga saƙonni, daga menu na saitunan lokaci da wuri.
    • Tabbata rediyonka yana karɓar sigina daga iska da gaske.

    Idan babu layuka masu lanƙwasa kwata-kwata a waterfall, mai yiwuwa zango a rufe yake, ko eriyarka ba a haɗa ta ba/ba a daidaita ta ba.

    Ana fassara saƙonni a makare

    Processor na waya (ko na tsohuwar kwamfuta) yana iya zama mai jinkiri sosai wajen fassara saƙonni a kan lokaci.

    Gwada: Tabbata ka kashe Zurfin fassara a saituna, kuma kana iya matsar da lokacin fassara gaba (misali zuwa 13000ms) domin samun ƙarin lokacin fassara, ko da yake za a iya rasa wasu saƙonnin da suka makara a kowane zagaye.

    Kuskuren "SWR exceeded threshold — transmission stopped" a transceiver na QMX

    Idan qFT8 ta nuna 0W yayin watsawa, akwai dalilai da dama masu yiwuwa:

    1. Kariyar SWR

    Idan allon LCD na QMX ya nuna "S" bayan mita, kariyar SWR ta kunnu. Wannan yana nufin ba a daidaita eriyarka yadda ya kamata ga wannan zango ba, kuma QMX ya daina watsawa domin kare kansa.

    Haka kuma, qFT8 tana da nata saitin iyakar SWR a saitunan manhaja. Idan SWR da aka auna ya wuce wannan iyaka, qFT8 za ta tsayar da watsawa ta atomatik domin kare kayan aikinka. Yana kuma mayar da TX zuwa OFF: sake danna TX ON bayan ka gyara eriya.

    • Firmware 1_04_004+: qFT8 tana sake saita kariyar SWR ta atomatik a ƙarshen zagaye.
    • Firmware 1_03_000+: Kana iya saita "SWR Protection reset options" zuwa Timer ko TX->RX a menu na QMX. Wannan yana barin qFT8 ta ci gaba da aiki ba tare da ka taɓa transceiver ba.
    • Tsohon firmware: A firmware na QMX kafin 1_03_000, dole ka sake saita transceiver da hannu (kashe/kunna) ko ka shiga ka fita daga menu na TUNE a QMX domin share kuskuren.

    Lura: qFT8 tana jiran daƙiƙa 1 bayan fara watsawa kafin ta duba 0W, domin guje wa kuskuren gargaɗi yayin da QMX yake ƙara ƙarfi. Idan QMX ya farfaɗo kuma ƙarfi ya haura 1W, alamar matsala tana gushewa ta atomatik.

    2. Firmware ba ya tallafa wa auna ƙarfi

    Idan QMX bai nuna cewa kariyar SWR ta kunnu ba, kuma ya nuna cewa watsawa tana tafiya daidai, mai yiwuwa firmware tsoho ne kuma ba ya tallafa wa auna ƙarfi. Na'urori kamar QDX ma ba sa tallafa wa auna ƙarfi. qFT8 ba ta san yadda za ta fahimci 0W ba, sai ta ruɗe.

    Gwada: Idan firmware na QMX ɗinka tsoho ne, sabunta shi. Idan kana amfani da QDX, babu mafita.

    3. Siginar sauti ba ta kaiwa QMX

    Idan QMX bai nuna alamar "S" bayan mita ba, to mai yiwuwa transceiver ba ya karɓar siginar sauti da ta dace daga qFT8. Ƙarar tana iya zama ta yi ƙasa sosai (sigina ba ta isa ba) ko ta yi yawa sosai (clipping/distortion yana sa QMX ya ƙi siginar).

    Gwada: Duba alamar sigina a QMX ɗinka. Ɗigo ɗaya yana nufin babu sauti; ɗigo biyu suna nufin akwai sauti amma bai isa ba. Je zuwa sashen Saitunan FT8 ka canza saitin "Saita ƙarar tsarin zuwa [X] yayin amfani da rediyo" zuwa lambobi daban-daban (misali 70%, 80%, 90%) domin nemo matakin da ya dace da na'urarka.

    4. SWR yana ƙasa yayin tuning amma kariyar SWR tana kunnuwa

    Da alama SWR yana tashi a babban ƙarfi na ɗan lokaci yayin ainihin watsawa, wanda yake kunna kariyar SWR. Wannan alama ce ta eriya mai wuyar sha'ani (misali magloop), kebul mara kyau, haɗi mara kyau, ko matsala a tsarin.

    Gwada: saka common-mode chokes (kamar ferrite beads) ya kamata ya rage common-mode current.

    Mene ne sanarwar Android ta qFT8? (Android)

    Wannan sanarwa tana barin qFT8 ta ci gaba da sarrafa sauti da kula da QSO idan ka rage manhaja ko ka kashe allo.

    • Tun asali, tana bayyana ta atomatik idan aka saka transceiver.
    • Kana iya kashe wannan a saitunan qFT8, amma qFT8 za ta daina aiki a bango.
    • Kana iya danna TSAYA a sanarwar domin rufe qFT8 gaba ɗaya.

    qFT8 tana daina aiki idan allo a kashe yake (Android)

    Wasu masu ƙera Android suna amfani da tsauraran hanyoyin adana baturi waɗanda suke iya kashe manhajojin bango ko da ka ba da izinin farko.

    Sake duba cewa ka ba da izinin: Je zuwa Settings > Apps > qFT8 > Battery na wayarka ka zaɓi "Unrestricted" (ko "Don't optimize").

    Maɓallai suna yin abin mamaki ko allo ba ya amsawa yayin TX

    Wannan yana faruwa ne saboda RF Interference (RFI). Idan eriyarka tana kusa da waya ko kwamfuta, ko kebul ɗin ba shi da kariya mai kyau, ƙarfin rediyo yana iya damun allon taɓawa, linzami ko haɗin USB.

    • Shawara: Yi amfani da kebul na USB mai kariya (shielded) ko saka ferrite bead a kan kebul ɗin.

    qFT8 ta bambanta da hotunan allo a wannan jagora

    Ana sabunta fuskar manhaja lokaci-lokaci, don haka wasu ƙananan abubuwa suna iya canzawa tun lokacin da aka ɗauki hotunan allo. Amma idan manyan sassan fuskar sun ɓace gaba ɗaya ko launuka sun canza sosai (misali sandar ci gaba ta rawaya, masu zaɓar jeri, da sauransu), mai yiwuwa dark mode na tsarin wayarka yana canza launukan manhaja bisa kuskure.

    Gwada: Idan dark mode na tsarinka yana kawo matsala, kana iya gwada kashe shi na ɗan lokaci ko saka qFT8 a jerin waɗanda aka ware, domin dawo da daidaitaccen kama.

    qFT8 tana cewa "NO RADIO" duk da cewa an haɗa transceiver

    Mai yiwuwa ba ka ba da izinin haɗa qFT8 da transceiver ba. Wani lokaci wannan yana faruwa ne saboda ka taɓa wani wuri a wajen tambayar izini, sai ta rufe.

    Gwada: Cire transceiver ka sake haɗa shi; tambayar za ta sake bayyana. Domin sauƙi, sa alama a zaɓin "Koyaushe a buɗe qFT8 idan an haɗa transceiver", domin kada ka sake fuskantar wannan matsala.

    qFT8 tana nuna nisa a mil amma ina son kilomita (ko akasin haka)

    Wannan yana zuwa daga saitin yanki (locale) na tsarin na'ura (wato zaɓin harshe).

    Gwada: Canza wannan saitin zuwa ƙasar da ta dace (misali English (United States) ga mil, English (United Kingdom) ga kilomita) zai gyara shi.

    qFT8 tana nuna ƙaramin ƙarfin watsawa (misali 0.1W) yayin watsawa a >10W a QMX

    Wannan sanannen kuskure ne a firmware na QMX (har a ƙalla 1.03.000). Idan kana amfani da 2x4 mods (misali a QMX+) kuma ƙarfin watsawarka ya wuce 9.9W, lambar rediyon tana komawa farko. Misali, transceiver yana gaya wa manhaja 10.1W a matsayin 0.1W ta protocol na CAT na biyu.

    Gwada: Babu mafita a cikin qFT8, domin manhaja tana nuna lambar da ta karɓa kai tsaye. Kana iya barin wannan kuskuren nunawa ba tare da damuwa ba, ko rage ƙarfinka ƙasa da 10W domin lambobin su kasance daidai har sai an gyara wannan kuskure a firmware na transceiver.

    Ba a iya fitar da log na ADIF zuwa ajiyar na'ura da Raba (Android)

    Hanyar Raba ta asali tana dogara ga tsarin rabawa na Android domin fitar da log. Wani lokaci, menu na raba fayil na na'urarka ba ya ba da zaɓin ajiyewa kai tsaye a na'ura.

    Gwada: Canza Hanya ta fitarwa zuwa Ajiye a na'ura a saituna. Wannan yana amfani da mai zaɓar fayil na Android domin ajiye fayil kai tsaye a na'urarka ba tare da dogara ga menu na rabawa ba.

    qFT8 ta kasa fassara wasu saƙonni

    Gwada: Kunna zurfin fassara (deep decode) a saituna. Zurfin fassara yana fassara sau da yawa kuma yana iya fassara ƙarin saƙonni idan zango yana da cunkoso, amma yana buƙatar ƙarin ƙarfin processor. Yana kuma cire harmonics na tashoshi masu ƙarfi sosai (kwafin da ba a iya karantawa waɗanda sigina mai ƙarfi fiye da kima take bari a manyan mitocin sauti) domin nemo tashoshi masu rauni a ƙarƙashinsu. Kula da spectrogram: idan lokacin zurfin fassara ya yi yawa a na'urarka (misali fiye da daƙiƙa 1) kuma manhaja ba ta iya yanke shawara a kan lokaci, kashe zurfin fassara.

    qFT8 tana tsayawa ko tana yin abubuwa na ban mamaki

    Idan wannan yana faruwa ne kawai yayin watsawa, mai yiwuwa RFI ne mai tsanani kamar yadda aka ambata a sama. Idan yana faruwa a wasu lokuta, je ƙasan shafin Saituna ka danna BA DA RAHOTON MATSALA (duba Ba da rahoton matsala), ko yi amfani da link na "Email me to report issues and bugs".
